||śrīḥ||

mahāvīracaritam/

bhāvapradyotinī/

vande vātsalyabharitaṃ varadaṃ vīrarāghavam/

nirvighnaṃ yena nirvṛto vaiśvāmitro mahāmakhaḥ||

atha tatrabhavānbhavabhūtiḥ ``ekaḥ śabdaḥ samyagjñātaḥ suṣṭhu prayuktaḥ-'' ityādiśrutyā sādhuśabdaprayogaṃ dharmaṃ manvānaḥ, ``kāvyaṃ yaśase'rthakṛte vyavahāravide śivetarakṣataye |
sadyaḥparanirvṛtaye kāntāsaṃmitatayopadeśayuje||'' ityetena kāvyātmakaṃ pracuraśreyohetumavagacchan, ``yadi kṣuṇṇaṃ pūrvaiḥ-'' ityuktarītyā sarvaguṇābhirāmaṃ rāmameva kāvyena cikathayiṣuḥ, śrāvyaprabandhāddṛśyaprabandhasya prakarṣaṃ vibhāvya tatrābhyarhitatvānnāṭakaṃ cikīrṣauḥ, uttaracaritamuttararāmacaritena kathayiṣyan, pūrvacaritaṃ mahāvīracaritākhyena nāṭakena nibadhnan, prastāvanāṅgasya pūrvaraṅgasya pradhānāṅgaṃ nāndīṃ racayati- athetyādinā |
atha śabdo'yaṃ māṅgalikaḥ |
taduktam- aoṃkāraścāthaśabdaśca dvāvetau brahmaṇaḥ purāḥ |
kaṇṭhaṃ bhittvā viniryātau tasmānmāṅgalikāvubhau||'' iti |
ayaṃ ca mṛdaṅgadhvanivatsvarūpata eva maṅgalamiti śābdikasamayaḥ |
svasthāya ``sve mahimni pratiṣṭhitaḥ'' iti śrutyā svasminneva sthitāya |
ananyādhārāyetyarthaḥ |
nityāya utpattivināśarahitāya |
hatapāpmane jarāmaraṇaduḥkhādipāparahitāya, svasaṃbandhādanyeṣāmapi tādṛśapāpmasaṃbandhavidhvaṃsakāya ca |
``sarve pāpmāno'to nivartante'' iti śruteḥ |
kramaḥ paurvāparyam, vibhāgo'vāntarabhedaḥ, tau tyaktau yena sa tathoktastasmai |
nityatvānniravayavatvācca tyaktakramavibhāgatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
caitanyajyotiṣe |
jyotirhi prakāśakam |
caitanyātmakāya jyotiṣe |
caitanyaśabdena bhūtatṛtīyavyāvṛttiḥ |
yadvā caitanyaṃ yogāgamajanyaṃ jñānaṃ jyotiḥ prakāśakaṃ yasya tathāvidhāyetyarthaḥ |
athavā caitanyānāṃ jñānānāṃ jyotiṣe prakāśakāya |
``tacchubhraṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotiḥ'' iti śruteḥ |
evaṃbhūtāya devāya dīptiśīlāya krīḍāvate vijigīṣave stutyāya modamānāya bhagavate namaḥ |
``divu krīḍā-'' ityādinā devaśabdasyoktārtheṣu vyutpattiranusaṃdheyā |
ebhiḥ padaiḥ ``eṣa ātmāpahatapāpmā divyo deva eko nārāyaṇaḥ'' |
nārāyaṇaparo jyotiḥ-'' ityādiśrutyartho'nusaṃhitaḥ |
idaṃ namaskriyārūpaṃ maṅgalam |
taduktam- ``āśīrnamaskriyāvastunirdeśo vāpi tanmukham'' iti |
iyamaṣṭapadā nāndī |
taduktam- ``āśīrvacanasaṃyuktā nityaṃ yasmātprayujyate |
devadvijanṛpādīnāṃ tasmānnāndīti saṃjñitā/ |
padairyauktā dvādaśabhiraṣṭābhirvā padairuta/ |
iti |
atra devāyetyanena ``rāmadevaṃ nināya'' iti vakṣyamāṇo rāmadevo varṇya iti sūcitam |
hatapāpmana ityanena vālirāvaṇādivadho'tra varṇya iti sūcitam |
ata eva vīrarasapradhānamiti sūcitam |
taduktam- ``vīraśṛṅgārayorekaḥ pradhānaṃ yatra varṇyate |
prakhyātanāyakopetaṃ nāṭakaṃ tadudāhṛtam||'' iti |
caitanyajyotiṣa ityanena nāyakasya prakhyātatvamapi sūcitam |
tyaktakramavibhāgāyetyanena bayaḥkramavarṇavibhāgānākalanena jāmadagnyādyanuvartanīyatvaṃ varṇyasya sūcitam |
devāyetyanena jānakyā saha krīḍādikaṃ sūcitam |
evamatrārthataḥ śabdataśca kāvyārthasūcanaṃ kṛtam |
taduktam- ``arthataḥ śabdato vāpi manākkāvyārthasūcanam'' iti||1||

 nāndyanta iti |
nandina iyaṃ nāndī |
taduktam- ``nāndī nandīśvarapriyā'' iti |
tasyā ante avasāne sūtradhāra āhetyarthaḥ |
sūtradhāro bharatācāryaḥ |
bhagavata ityādi |
bhagavataḥ ``ajaśvaryasya samagrasya vīryasya yaśasaḥ śriyaḥ |
jñānavairāgyayoścaiva ṣaṇṇāṃ bhaga itīraṇā||'' ityuktaiśvaryādipūrṇasya |
kālapriyānāthasya kālapriyānāmikāmbikāpateḥ |
``kālapriyānāthasya'' iti hrasvāntapriyaśabdaprayuktaḥ pāṭhaḥ pracuro dṛśyate |
tatrāpyarthaḥ pūrvavat |
kṣetraviśeṣasyeśvaramūrtiviśeṣasaṃjñābhūte kālapriyānāthaśabde ``ṅyāpoḥ saṃjñācchandasorbahulam'' iti vaikalpikahrasvapravṛtteḥ |
yātrāyāṃ utsave |
āryamiśrāḥ samādiśanti samājñāpayanti |
tatprakāramāha- mahāpuruṣetyādinā |
yatra saṃdarbhe yasmingranthe gambhīro'kṣobhyo bhīṣaṇo bhayāvahaḥ |
viśeṣaṇadvayasamāsaḥ |
mahāpuruṣasya dhīrodāttanāyakasya saṃrambhaḥ stheyānprayatnaḥ |
pratipādya iti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca yatra saṃdarbhe prasannā śleṣaḥ prasādaḥ samatā'' ityatroktaprasādākhyaguṇavatī |
taduktam- ``prasiddhārthapadatvaṃ yatsa prasādo nigadyate'' iti |
karkaśā ca vipulārthā vācyavyaṅgyabhedaprabhinnaprameyabahulā bhāratī bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
yadvā prasannakarkaśā īṣanmṛdvarthasaṃdarbhā vipulārthā vyaṅgyabahulā bhāratīti bhāratyākhyavṛttirbhavatītyarthaḥ |
taduktam- ``īṣanmṛdvarthasaṃdarbhā bhāratī vṛttiriṣyate'' iti||2||

 kiṃca yatra saṃdarbhe'prākṛteṣu prākṛtabhāṣārahiteṣu pātreṣu rāmajāmadagnyādibhūmikāviśeṣeṣu sthitaḥ prayojyatayā vartamāno rasaḥ sāmājikairniratiśayānandarūpatvena svādyamāno vīraḥ sthāyībhūtaḥ śatrukṛtāparādhaprayuktamanaḥprajvalanātmakaḥ krodhaḥ sūkṣmairvasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrādirūpālambanaviśeṣeṣu gūḍhairabhivyaktai rāmajāmadagnyādyālambaneṣu prakaṭairbhedairavasthāviśeṣaiḥ pratyādhāraṃ pūrvokteṣvālambaneṣu sarveṣu vibhajyate vibhāgavānkriyate/ |
parasparavailakṣaṇyavānkriyata iti yāvat||3||

 sa saṃdarbho'bhinetavya iti samādiśantīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
atra mahāpuruṣetyanena prakaraṇādivyāvṛttiḥ |
vīro rasa ityanena śṛṅgārarasavyāvṛttiḥḥ |
prasannetyanena śṛṅgārādeḥ, bhīṣaṇa ityanena bhayānakasya cānupraveśaḥ sūcitaḥ |
mahāpuruṣa ityanena nāyakasya itivṛttasya ca prakhyātatvaṃ sūcitam |
evam- ``vīraśṛṅgārayorekaḥ pradhānaṃ yatra varṇyate |
prakhyātanāyakopetaṃ nāṭakaṃ tadudāhṛtam||'' iti ślokārthaḥ pratibimbitaḥ |
tatra viśeṣajijñāsāyāṃ nirdiṣṭaguṇasāmyātprāyeṇa śabdasāmyācca mahāvīracaritameva tādṛśaḥ saṃdarbha iti paryavasitam |
tadāha- mahāvīracaritamityādinā |
atrabhavadbhirāryamiśrairarthato'numānādādiṣṭamājñaptam |
vaśyetyādi |
vaśyavācaḥ |
svādhīnavākpravṛtteḥ kaveḥ krāntadarśino vākyaṃ tiṅsubantacayarūpaḥ śabdarāśiḥ abhinetavyamiti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca sā vaśyavākkavivākyarūpā kathetivṛttapratipādakaśabdaparipāṭī rāmāśrayā sakalaguṇābhirāmarāmaviṣayikā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca vākyaniṣyandasya vākyaprayojyaśṛṅgārādirasasya niṣpeṣa utkarṣāvagamārthavimardanaviṣaye nikaṣaḥ śāṇabhūto janaḥ sāmājikavargo labdhaḥ prāptaḥ |
asmābhiriti[śeṣaḥ]||4||

 so'hamityādi |
so'haṃ sāmājikenādiṣṭo'ham |
etadvakṣyamāṇakavikāvyādiviśeṣam |
astītyādi |
dakṣiṇāpathe dakṣiṇasyā diśaḥ pathi |
``ṛkpūrabdhūḥpathāmānakṣe'' iti samāsānto'pratyayaḥ |
śarāvatyā dakṣiṇe deśa ityarthaḥ |
taittirīyāstittiriṇā proktamadhīyānāḥ |
kāśyapāḥ kāśyapāpatyabhūtāḥ |
caraṇānāṃ śākhādhyetaṝṇāṃ guravo mahopādhyāyāḥ |
paṅktiṃ pāvayantīti paṅktipāvanāḥ bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
agryāḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu sarvapravacaneṣu ca |
śrotriyānvayajāścaiva vijñeyāḥ paṅktipāvanāḥ||'' iti manuḥ |
pañcāgnayo gārhapatya āhavanīyo dakṣiṇāgniḥ sabhya āvasathyaśceti pañcāgnayo yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
dhṛtaṃ vrataṃ ``tasya vrataṃ-'' ityādiśrutyuktaniyatānuṣṭheyadharmarūpaṃ yaiste yathoktāḥ |
somapīthinaḥ somapānavantaḥ |
udumbaranāmānaḥ |
abhijananāmedam |
brahmavādino vedāntavedyaparabrahmapravacanaśīlāḥ prativasantītyarthaḥ |
tadāmuṣyāyaṇetyādi |
amuṣya putra āmuṣyāyaṇaḥ |
naḍāditvātphakpratyayaḥ |
``āmuṣyāyaṇāmuṣyaputra-'' ityādinā ṣaṭṭhyālugabhāvaḥ |
teṣāmāmuṣyāyaṇastadāmuṣyāyaṇaḥ |
tadīyasya kasyacitputra ityarthaḥ |
devadattasya gurukulamityatrevānvayaḥ |
tathāvidhasya tatrabhavataḥ pūjyasya vājapeyayājino vājapeyeneṣṭavato mahākaveḥ pañcamaḥ pautraḥ pautrasya pautraḥ |
sugṛhītanāmnaḥ kīrtanīyanāmadheyatya |
``sa sugṛhītanāmā syādyaḥ prātaḥ sukhadaḥ smṛtaḥ'' iti |
bhaṭṭagopālasya pautraḥ |
pavitrakīrteḥ pāvanayaśaso nīlakaṇṭhasyātmasaṃbhavaḥ putraḥ śrīkaṇṭhapadaṃ lāñchanaṃ nāma yasya saḥ |
``lāñchanaṃ nāmalakṣmaṇoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
pitṛkṛtanāmedam |
padavākyapramāṇajño vyākaraṇatarkamīmāṃsābhijño bhavabhūtirnāma ``sāmbā punātu bhavabhūtipavitramūrtiḥ'' iti ślokaracanāsaṃtuṣṭena rājñā bhavabhūtiriti khyāpitaḥ |
jatukarṇīputro jatukarṇīti māturnāma tasyāḥ putraḥ |
asmākaṃ mittradheyam |
mittramityarthaḥ |
``bhāgarūpanāmabhyo dheyaḥ'' iti prakaraṇe ``mittrācca'' iti mittraśabdātsvārthe dheyapratyayaḥ |
iti vidāṃkurvantu |
jānantvityarthaḥ |
śreṣṭha ityādi |
maharṣīṇāṃ mahatāṃ satyavacasāṃ aṅgirā yathāṅgirā iva |
paramahaṃsānāmuttamāśramiṇāṃ śreṣṭho yathārthanāmārthānatikrāntanāmako jñānanidhiryasya guruḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ||5||

 tenetyādi |
tena pūrvoktena bhavabhūtinā |
dharmadruhaḥ |
dvitīyāntamidam |
dharmadrohiṇāṃ rāvaṇādīṇāṃ damayituḥ |
tṛnnantamidam |
śāsitū raghunandanasya rāmasya |
vīraścāsāvadbhutaścāsau priyaśceti viśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ |
tasya bhāvo vīrādbhutapriyatā tayoddhṛtaṃ jagattrayasya manyumūlaṃ dainyamūlaṃ kopamūlaṃ vā yena tathoktam |
``manyurdainye kratau krudhi'' iti viśvaḥ |
astoko vipulo vīro vīraraso yasmiṃstathoktam |
gurvadhikaṃ sāhasaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ karma yasmiṃstathoktam |
kiṃca |
adbhutamāścaryakaraṃ raghunandanasya caritaṃ jāmadagnyajayaprabhṛti rāvaṇavadhaparyantaṃ caritaṃ nibaddham |
prabandhena pratipāditamityarthaḥ||6||

 paripunantviti |
paripunantu pariśuddhaṃ kurvantu |
śrotriyasya samagravedādhyāyinaḥ putreṇa tena bhavabhūtinoktaṃ ca- prācetasa ityādi |
pracetaso varuṇasyāpatyaṃ munivṛṣā munīndraḥ |
``kaninyuvṛṣi-'' ityādinā vṛṣaśabdo nakārānto vyutpāditaḥ |
kavīnāṃ prathamaḥ krāntadarśināmādyo vālmīkiḥ |
pāvanaṃ pavitraṃ raghupate rāmasya yadvṛttaṃ caritraṃ praṇināya prabandhena pratipādayāmāsa |
tatra rāmacarite bhaktasya bhaktimato mamāpyatadarhasyāpi mama vācaḥ samaranta saṃgatāḥ |
``samo gamyṛcchibhyāṃ-'' iti prakaraṇe ``artiśrudṛśibhyaśca'' iti vārtikenātmanepadam |
``sartiśāstyartibhyaśca'' ityaṅ |
āgamaśāsanasyānityatvādāḍāgamābhāvaḥ |
``bahulaṃ chandasi'' iti bahulagrahaṇādacchandasyapyāḍviraho vā |
tāsu bhaktipreritāsu madvākṣu prasannamanasaḥ santaḥ sānugrahahṛdayāḥ santaḥ kṛtinaḥ paṇḍitā bhajantāṃ prītimanto bhavantu |
``prītipūrvamanudhyānaṃ bhaktirityabhidhīyate'' ityukteḥ||7||

 kṛtaprasādāḥ kṛtānugrahāḥ pāriṣadāḥ |
satāṃ sanmanttrāṇāṃ samājaḥ pariṣattatrabhavāḥ pāriṣadāḥ |
yogavibhāgātpariṣacchabdāṇṇapratyayaḥ |
apūrvatvānnūtanatvāt |
kathāyā itivṛttasya pradeśamaṃśam |
samārambhe prabandhaprārambhe/ |
dīkṣiṣyamāṇaḥ śvobhūte yāgadīkṣāṃ praveṣṭā |
ajakṣvākasyekṣvākorapatyasya |
``dāṇḍināyana-'' ityādinā ṭilopaḥ vasiṣṭhaḥ purodhāḥ purohito yasya sa tasya |
sa ca viśvāmitraśca |
vijayetyādi |
vijayasya śatruvijayasya sahajaṃ jyeṣṭhabhrātṛbhūtaṃ vīryamastrairjṛmbhakādyastrairucchrāyayiṣyannutuṅgaṃ kariṣyan, jagadupakṛterbhagavadavatāraprayojanabhūtakaṇṭakoddhāraṇādilokopakārasya bījaṃ kāraṇabhūtām |
uktaṃ ca parāśareṇa- nityānapāyinī viṣṇorjagatāmupakāriṇī'' iti |
maithilīṃ sītām |
rāmadevamityasyāpakarṣaḥ |
rāmadevaṃ prāpayiṣyan |
daśamukhakulaghātena rāvaṇavaṃśanāśanena ślāghyasya kalyāṇasya maṅgalasya pātraṃ bhājanam |
dhanuścānujaśca dhanuranujau tau sahāyau yasya tathoktaṃ rāmadevaṃ nināya prāpitavān |
svatapovanamiti śeṣaḥ |
atra kālapriyānāthasyetyanena deśaḥ, yātrāyāmityanena kālaśca nirdiṣṭaḥ |
mahāpuruṣasaṃrambha ityādinā prayojyanivedanaṃ kṛtam |
asti khalvityādinā kaveḥ, tenedamityādinā kāvyasya ca praśaṃsā kṛtā |
taduktam- ``nivedanaṃ prayojyasya nirdeśo deśakālayoḥ |
kavikāvyanaṭādīnāṃ praśaṃsā ca prarocanā||'' iti||8||

 nimantrita ityādi |
sa prasiddhaḥ |
maithilīmiti pūrvaśloke maithilaśabdena prastuto vā |
āttadīkṣaḥ svīkṛtayāgadīkṣo videhanātho janakastena viśvāmitreṇa nimantritaḥ sannāvaśyakayāgakarmaṇāhūtaḥ sanbhrātaramanujabhrātaraṃ prāhiṇotpreṣayāmāsa |
``dīkṣito na dadāti na juhoti-'' ityādiśāstreṇa dīkṣitasyāgamanādi pratiṣedhāditi bhāvaḥ |
sa janakabhrātā kuśadhvajo nāma kuśadhvaja iti prasiddha eṣa rājā purovartī rājā sītormilābhyāṃ sahito'bhyupaiti prāpnoti |
raṅgasthalamiti śeṣaḥ |
atra prayogātiśayo nāma prastāvanāṅgamuktam |
taduktam- ``eṣo'yamityupakṣepātprayogātiśayo mataḥ''[iti]||9||

 iti niṣkrāntau |
naṭaḥ sūtradhāraśca raṅgasthalamiti śeṣaḥ |
prastāvaneti |
tallakṣaṇaṃ tu- ``sūtradhāro naṭīṃ brūte māriṣaṃ vā vidūṣakam |
svakāryaprastutākṣepi citroktyā yattadāmukham/ |
prastāvanā vā tatra syāt'' iti/ |
athāṅkaḥ prastūyate |
aṅkalakṣaṇaṃ tu ``pratyakṣanetṛcarito binduvyaktipuraskṛtaḥ |
aṅko nānāprakārārthasaṃvidhānarasāśrayaḥ||'' iti |
tataḥ praviśatītyādi |
tataḥ sūtradhārādiniṣkramaṇānantaram |
kanye ceti praviśata iti śeṣaḥ |
āyuṣmatyau āśāsanīyātiśayitāyuṣke |
sītormile saṃbodhanadvivacanam |
śraddadhānena phalāvaśyaṃbhāvaniścayarūpaśraddhāyuktena praṇantavyo vandadanīyaḥ/ |
jaha ityādi |
kaniṣṭhatātaḥ pituranujaḥ |
tathāstviti śeṣaḥ |
atra śraddadhānenetyanena viśvāmitrakartṛkasītārāmapariṇayādiprayatnarūpabījanyāsādupakṣepo nāma mukhasaṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
``bījanyāsa upakṣepaḥ'' iti/ |
turīya iti |
eṣa viśvāmitrasturīyo medhyāgniścaturtho'yaṃ pariśuddhāgniḥ |
trayo'gnayaḥ prasiddhāḥ, tebhyo'pyayamatiśayito'gniḥ |
akhilābhyudayahetutvānabhibhavanīyatvatejiṣṭatvādinā nirūpaṇam |
idaṃ ca tretāgnimadabhiprāyeṇa |
hi śabdaḥ prasiddhau |
api vā |
kiṃcetyarthaḥ |
eṣa viśvāmitraḥ pañcama āmnāyaḥ |
ṛgādayaścatvāro vedāḥ, prasiddhāḥ, tebhyo'pyatiśayito'yamāmnāyaḥ |
bhramapramādādidoṣarāhityena nityaṃ hitānuśāsanādvedatvarūpaṇam |
idaṃ ca bhāratānākalaneneti mantavyam |
athaveti |
eṣa viśvāmitro jaṅgamaṃ tīrthaṃ saṃcariṣṇu puṇyasalilaṃ puṇyakṣetraṃ vā |
``tīrthaṃ śāstrādhvarakṣetropāyopādhyāyamanttriṣu |
avatārarṣijuṣṭāmbhastrīrajaḥsu ca viśrutam||'' ityabhidhānāt |
yātrādinairapekṣyeṇa svayamāgatyābhyudayapradānājjaṅgamatvanirūpaṇaṃ kṛtam |
veti |
athavetyarthaḥ |
eṣa mūrtyā saṃcaro mūrttisaṃcaraḥ |
saṃpūrvāccarateḥ pacādyac |
``kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā bahulam'' iti tṛtīyāsamāsaḥ |
dharmaḥ phalāvyavahitasādhanabhūto bhāvārthaniṣpādyo liṅgādivācyo'pūrvātmako dharmaḥ |
prasiddhasya hi dharmasya parokṣatvādanadhyavasāyāvaskanditatvānmūrtimaddharmatvarūpaṇaṃ kṛtam |
atra pūrvārdhe'nubhayarūpakadvayam |
uttarārdhe tvadhikarūpakadvayamiti vivekaḥ |
dharmo hi nirdoṣapramāṇena bodhito'nupādeyopakārakeṇa deśādinopādeyopakārakeṇāgninā copaskṛtaḥ svayaṃ sākṣādabhyudayaṃ sādhayatīti saparikaradharmasādṛśyaṃ viśvāmitrasyāstīti catvāri rūpakāṇyuktānīti draṣṭavyam||10||

 saṃkāśena nirvṛttaṃ sāṃkāśyam |
``saṃkāśādibhyo ṇyaḥ'' iti ṇyapratyayaḥ |
tasya nātheti kuśadhvajaṃ prati saṃbaddhiḥ |
na khalvityādi |
kaścidaparo mahattvena pūjyatvena viśvāmitrādṛṣerna prakṛṣyate khalu viśvāmitrādadhiko na bhavati kila |
yasyetyādi |
yasya viśvāmitrasya triśaṅkoridaṃ traiśaṅkavaṃ vasiṣṭhaśāpāccaṇḍalatāṃ prāptasya triśaṅkoḥ saśarīrasvargāropaṇam |
śunaḥ śepa iva śepo yasya sa śunaḥśepaḥ |
``śepapucchalāṅgūleṣu śunaḥ'' iti ṣaṣṭhyā aluk |
śunaḥśepasyedaṃ śaunaḥśepam |
pūrvatrātra ca ``tasyedaṃ'' ityaṇ |
mātāpitṛbhyāmupekṣitasya śunaḥkṣepasya śaraṇāgatasya mantraviśeṣopadeśena prāṇaparitrāṇam |
rambhāyāstapobhaṅgārthamāgatāyāḥ stambhanaṃ vairūpyāpādanena pratighātaścetyaparimeyamaparicchedyamāścaryajātaṃ vismayanīyacaritasamūhamākhyānavidaḥ purāvṛttapratipādakagranthajñā ācakṣate vadanti |
uttarayacchabdasya tacchabdanirapekṣatvātpūrvavākyenāsyaikavākyatvam |
tadasminnityādi |

o 12ṣṭiṣṭaṣṭatattasmādbrahmādyaiścaturmukhaprabhṛtibhistridaśagurubhirdevaśreṣṭhainārthito yācitaḥ śamaḥ kopanivṛttiryasya sa tathoktastasmin |
``nāthṛnādhṛyācñopatāpa-'' ityādinā dhātupāṭhānnāthatiyācñārthakaḥ |
``anyamindraṃ kariṣyāmi loko vā syādanindrakaḥ'' iti jagatkṣobhapravṛttasya viśvāmisya prasādaṃ brahmādayo'rthitavanta iti paurāṇikī kathātrānusaṃdheyā |
tapasaḥ kāyaśoṣaṇātmakaniyamasyaya tejasaḥ parābhibhavanasāmarthyasya ca dhāmni sthānabhūte svayaṃ svīyatapomūlakavasiṣṭhavākyenopanataṃ prāptaṃ brahma brāhmaṇyaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
asmingurau mantradraṣṭari vidyānāṃ vedādīnāṃ caturdaśānāṃ vidyānāṃ nivāse nilayabhūte'sminviśvāmitra upahitā nihitā kuṭumbavyavahṛtiḥ kanyādānādigṛhakṛtyavyāpāro yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
bhavāneva jagati gṛhavatāṃ gṛhīṇāṃ madhye ślāghyo gṛhamedhī |
``meghṛ saṃgame'' ityasmāt ``supyajātau ṇinistācchīlye'' iti ṇiniḥ |
``gṛhaṃ gṛhāśca bhūmni syādvāvimau gehabhāryayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
eṣa ṛṣiprayatnarūpabījasya bahūkaraṇātparikaro nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yaduktam- ``bījasya bahukaraṇaṃ parikaraḥ'' iti ||11||

 sūnṛtaṃ satyaṃ priyaṃ ca bhāṣase vadasi |
prakṛṣṭamatiśayitaṃ kalyāṇaṃ maṅgalamudarko bhāviphalaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ |
tathāvidhaḥ saṃgamaḥ saṃbandho yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
``eṣyatoḥ kālaphalayorudarkaḥ syāt'' iti ratnamālā |
sākṣātkṛtaṃ viśadatamajñānaviṣayīkṛtaṃ brahma paramātmā yaiste tathoktāḥ |
mahāntaśca te ṛṣayaśceti vigrahe karmadhārayaḥ |
``ṛṣayaḥ satyavacasaḥ'' iti |
atra sādhu sūtetyādinā ṛṣiprayatnarūpabījāṅgīkaraṇarūpasamādhānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
taduktam- bījāṅgīkaraṇaṃ samādhānam'' iti |
tamāṃsīti |
eteṣāṃ maharṣīṇāṃ sakṛtsaṃvāde'pi kadācidapyuktipratyuktikāyāmapi janasya tamāṃsyajñānahetubhūtāstamoguṇā dhvaṃsante naśyanti |
kiṃca bhūyānpracura upaśamo'ntarindriyanivṛttiḥ pariṇamati pravṛddho bhavati |
kiṃceha cāmutra cāsmiṃlloke parasmiṃlloke ca śubhamagarhiteṣṭārthasiddhiḥ prathate pravṛddhaṃ bhavati |
atheti vākyāntarārambhe |
eteṣāṃ pratyāsaṅga ekasthānasahāsikādisaṃsargaviśeṣaḥ kamapi mahimānamanirvācyaṃ māhātmyaṃ vitarati dadāti |
prasannānāmakasmotuto vā prasādaṃ prāptānāmeteṣāṃ vāco janasyāparimeyamaparicchedyaṃ phalaṃ sukhaṃ prasuvate janayanti |
atra mahimānaṃ vitaratīti ``brahmaṇo mahimānamāpnoti'' iti śrutiḥ sphoryate |
atra bījaguṇavarṇanādvilobhanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||12||

 haritetyādi |
harito vṛkṣādinā śyāmaḥ parisaro'bhyarṇardaśo yasya tattathoktam |
tathāvidhenāraṇyena ramaṇīyam |
kauśikyā nadyā parikṣiptaṃ parivṛtam |
siddhāśramapadaṃ nāma siddhāśramasthānamiti prasiddhaṃ tasyarṣerviśvāmitrasyāyatanaṃ sthānaṃ dṛśyate |
ātmanātṛtīyaḥ |
rāmalakṣmaṇau svayaṃ ceti trayāṇāṃ saṃpattyā viśvāmitrasyātmanātṛtīyakatvam |
``ātmanaśca pūraṇe'' iti tṛtīyāyā aluk |
kuśikanandano viśvāmitraḥ/ |
yadyevaṃ viśvāmitraścet |
āśramābhyarṇabhūmaya āśramasamīpapradeśāḥ |
nātikramayitavyā na padena spraṣṭavyāḥ |
iti prasiddhau/ |
yadājñāpayati |
tadastviti śeṣaḥ/ |
rakṣoghnānīti |
sudine śobhanadivase rākṣasopalakṣitāni pratyūhaśamanādīni rakṣāṃsighnantīti rakṣoghnāni |
``amanuṣyakartṛke ca'' iti hanteṣṭak |
maṅgalānyaṅgahiteṣṭārthasiddhihetubhūtāni karmāṇi kalpyāni kartavyāni |
kiṃca vaidehyāśca videnarājakanyāyāśca raghūdvahasya ca raghuśreṣṭhasya ca dārakriyā vivāhaḥ kalpyā niṣpādyā |
kalpyānītyasyānuṣaṅgaḥ strīliṅgaikavacanābhyāṃ vipariṇāmaśca |
kiṃca naḥ kule'smākaṃ gṛhe |
``gehe dehe janapade sajātīyaguṇe'nvaye |
kulam'' iti ratnamālā |
dīkṣāpraveśo dīkṣāyāṃ yāgakartavyatāsaṃkalpe praveśaḥ prāthamikasaṃbandho naḥ kalpyo mayā kartavyaḥ ityatra ``kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā'' iti ṣaṣṭhī |
athavā dīkṣāpraveśo dārakriyāmaṅgalāni ca naḥ kalpyāni ityekameva vākyam kalpyānītyatra ``napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyām'' iti napuṃsakaikaśeṣaḥ |
ekavadbhāvavikalpādbahuvacanam |
kiṃca tāni tāni nānāvidhāni rāmātmano daityāre rāmasvarūpasya bhagavato viṣṇoścaritādbhutāni tāṭakātāṭakeyanirasanādivṛttarūpāṇyāścaryāṇi jagatāṃ kṣemāya lokānāṃ maṅgalāya labdhāṃśaparitrāṇāya vā |
``kṣemamastrī syānmaṅgale labdharakṣaṇe'' iti ratnamālā |
āstheyānyasmābhirāvaśyakatvena pratijñātavyāni |
āpi ``kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā'' iti ṣaṣṭhī |
atha khalvathāpi vayamaham |
``asmādo dvayośca'' ityekatve bahuvacanam |
vyaprāḥ santa ukteṣu kartavyeṣu vyāpṛtāḥ santaḥ |
``vyapro vyāpṛta ākule'' iti ratnamālā |
pramodāmahe prakṛṣṭaharṣavanto bhavāmaḥ |
pramodāmahasaṃdhyaṅgamuktam||13||

 saṃdiṣṭamiti |
maithilasya rājarṣerjanakaṃ pratyasmābhirmayā saṃdiṣṭaṃ vācikaṃ preṣitam |
kimityatrāha- ācāra ityādi |
yajamāno'pi prārabdhāparisamāptayāgo'pi |
āgamanānarho'pītyarthaḥ |
ācāra iti yajñe nimantrito'si |
nimantraṇaṃ śiṣṭācārasiddhamiti hetoḥ |
ārabdhayāgastvamāgamanānarho'pi madyajñaviṣaye mayāgamanādau pravartito'sītyarthaḥ |
kiṃtu kuśadhvajaḥ sītormilābhyāṃ sahitaḥ sanpreṣitavya iti saṃdiṣṭaṃ ceti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
nimantraṇamātreṇa tvayā nāgantavyam, kiṃtu tvatsthāne sītormilābhyāṃ saha kuśadhvajapreṣaṇam |
priyasuhṛdā janakena kumārau rāmalakṣmaṇau evamabhyupagatā evaṃ priyasuhṛttvenābhyupagamamaṅgīkāraṃ prāptāḥ/ |
nimijanakasaṃbhavāḥ |
nimijanaketi mūlapuruṣavyapadeśaḥ |
videheṣu videhadeśeṣu |
teṣāmiti |
sīradhvajaḥ sīradhvajābhidhāno vṛddho jñānavayaḥśīlaiḥ prakṛṣṭasteṣāṃ dāyādo nimijanakasaṃbhavānāṃ rājarṣīṇāmapatyabhūto nṛpa idānīmasminkāle |
vartata ityarthaḥ |
yasmai yaṃ sīradhvajamujjīvayitum |
``kriyārthopapadasya ca karmaṇi sthāninaḥ'' iti caturthī |
yājñavalkyomuniḥ |
brahmaṇo vedasya |
śuklasya yajuṣa ityarthaḥ |
pārāyaṇaṃ samagrādhyayanaṃ jagau gītavān |
uttare yacchabdastacchabdaṃ nāpekṣate||1cha/ |
yasyeti |
tatprasiddhaṃ māheśvaraṃ dhanuḥ śaivaṃ dhanuryasya gṛheṣu pūjyate/ |
atha kimityaṅgīkāre |
``aṭagīkṛtau syādathakim'' ityamaraḥ |
śrūyata iti |
tatra janakagṛhe |
yaditi |
ayonijā kanyeti yadidamanyadapyāścaryaṃ tatra śrūyata ityarthaḥ/ |
ayaṃ tviti |
yajamānena vartamānayāgena tena sīradhvajenānujanmānujo'yaṃ kuśadhvajo vātsalyānmayi prītyatiśayāt |
``vatsāṃsābhyāṃ kāmabale'' iti lacpratyayāntādvatsalaśabdādbrāhmaṇāditvādbhāve vyañ |
yakṣyamāṇasya kariṣyamāṇayāgasya me gṛhaṃ preṣitaḥ |
preṣayaterdikarmakatvādgrahasyāpi karmatvam||15||

 taditi |
tasmādasminsaṃnihite rājanyaśrotriye sāṅgavedādhyetari |
jātivācino rājanyaśabdasya ``poṭāyuvati-'' ityādinā śroyiśabdena saha samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ |
``śrotriyaṃśchando'dhīte'' iti sūtreṇa chandodhyetari śrotriyaśabdo nipātitaḥ |
vatsābhyāṃ yuvābhyāṃ praśrāyeṇa priyeṇa vinayena/ |
evamityaṅgīkāre |
nirvarṇya nidhyāya |
``nirvarṇanaṃ tu nidhyānaṃ darśanālokanekṣaṇam'' ityamaraḥ |
prakṛtyeti |
prakṛtyā svabhāvataḥ |
``prakṛtyādibhya upasaṃkhyānam'' iti tṛtīyā |
puṇyalakṣmīkau puṇyaśrīkau |
``uraḥ prabhṛtibhyaḥ kap ityuraḥprabhṛtitvātkap/ |
etau kāviti svagatā jijñāsā |
tatra liṅgātsvagataṃ viśeṣanirṇayamāha- jñāyata ityādinā |
kṛtopanayanau rājanyadārakāvidatīdameva nūnaṃ jñāyate saṃbhāvanātmakajñānaviṣayīkriyate |
tuśabdo'vadhāraṇe |
mātāpitrādyantaviśeṣo na jñāyata ityarthaḥ||16||

 dvitīyasyeti |
anayoḥ kumārayormūrtidvitīyasya varṇasya ca |
``dvestīyaḥ'' iti dviśabdātpūraṇārthe tīyapratyayaḥ |
kṣatriyavarṇasya ca prathamasyāśramasya ca brahmacaryāśramasya ca nūtanasya vayasaśca bālyasya ca ramyā |
kṣayivarṇāditritayaprayuktasaubhāgyavatītyarthaḥ |
aho iti vismaye||17||

 ``rājanyadārakau nūnaṃ kṛtopanayanāviti iti pūrvoktārthe liṅgamupanyasyati- tathā hīti |
cūḍeti |
cūḍayā cumbitāni saṃyuktāprāṇi kaṅkapattrāṇi bāṇapakṣā yasmiṃstathoktaṃ pṛṣṭhato'bhitaḥ pṛṣṭhe |
dvayorbhāgayorityarthaḥ |
tūṇīdvayaṃ niṣaṅgayugalam |
bhasma bhasmamayaṃ stokaṃ svalpaṃ pavitraṃ pāvanaṃ lāñchanaṃ puṇḍraṃ yasya tathoktamuro rauravīmaiṇeyīṃ tvacaṃ dhatte |
maurvyā mekhalayā maurvyātmakaraśanayā niyantritaṃ baddhaṃ māñjiṣṭhakaṃ mañjiṣṭhayā raktaṃ vāso'dha uraso'dhastātpāṇau kārmukaṃ dhanurapāṇāvaśvatthadaṇḍa ityarthaḥ |
``aparaḥ paippalaḥ'' iti pāṭhe dhanurdaṇḍādaparaḥ |
anya ityarthaḥ |
atra ``jyā rājanyasya māñjiṣṭhaṃ rājanyasya rauravī rājanyasya'' ityāpastambavacanam'' ``āśvatthapaippalau'' iti gautamavacanaṃ ca draṣṭavyam |
rauravīṃ rājanyasyetyatra ``rururbindumānmṛgaḥ'' ityujjvalāyāmuktam |
ataḥ kṛtopanayanau rājanyādārakāviti saṃbhāvyata ityarthaḥ||18||

 saumyadarśanau khalvetau |
etau rāmalakṣmaṇau/ |
abhivādaye |
namaskaraṇenāśiṣo vācayāmītyarthaḥ/ |
diṣṭyetyānande |
``diṣṭyā samupajoṣaṃ cetyānande'' ityamaraḥ |
garbharūpaṃ garbhavadbharaṇīyam |
gṛhānāgatamasmadgṛhaṃ prāptaṃ tvāṃ kuśalinaṃ paśyāmītyanvayaḥ |
pariṣvajasva māmāliṅgetyarthaḥ |
apītyādi |
pravṛttayajñaḥ prārabdhayāgo'savādūraviprakṛṣṭo videhādhipatiḥ sīradhvajo'pi sukhī |
sukhavānkimityarthaḥ |
janakānāṃ purohito gautamo gautamaputraḥ |
``ṛṣyandhakavṛṣṇikurubhyaśca'' ityaṇpratyayaḥ |
śatānandaścāpi sukhītyanuṣajyate||19||

 āryo ṣjeṣṭhabhrātā purodhasā purohitena gautamena saha sa eva sakhī |
sīradhvaja eva sukhātiśayavānityarthaḥ |
``bhūminindāpraśaṃsāsu  nityayoge'tiśāyane |
saṃbandhe'stivivakṣāyāṃ bhavanti matubādayaḥ||'' ityatiśāyane matup |
yasya sīradhvajasyaivaṃ pūrvoktaprakāraṇe kuṭumbavṛttiṃ gṛhakṛtyavyāpṛtimanupatitāḥ |
anuprāptā ityarthaḥ/ |
paṇamāmo praṇamāvo namaskurvaḥ/ |
lāṅgaleti |
lāṅgalena sīrāgreṇollikhyamānāyāḥ kṛṣyamāṇāyaḥ |
yajñabhūmeriti pañcamī |
yā samudgatā sā sīteyamityarthaḥ||20||

 bhadramastviti |
etadṛṣyamanugraharūpaṃ bījamuktam/ |
janāntikamiti |
``arthastvekena vijñeyaḥ paścājjñāpyaḥ prasaṅgataḥ |
tajjanāntikamityuktvā kāvyabandhe niveśayet||'' ityuktyā kuśadhvajaviśvāmiādyaviditaṃ rāmamātravedyaḥ lakṣmaṇaḥ kiṃcidāhetyarthaḥ |
ārya pūjya |
adbhutasūtirvismayanīyajanmetīyadāścaryametāvadevāścaryam/ |
kimanyadapyāścaryamastītyarthaḥ |
tadāha rāmaḥ- utpattirityādinā |
devā ijyante'sminniti devayajanaṃ yajñakṣetram |
tasmādatpittirāvirbhāvaḥ |
brahmavādī ``brahma parivṛḍhaṃ sarvataḥ'' ityuktarītyā sarvaprakārato'pi sarvasmādapi parivṛḍhasya paramātmana upadeṣṭā nṛpaḥ kṣatriyavarṇāocitarājyaparipālanātmakakarmayoganiṣṭhaḥ sīradhvajaḥ pitā |
suprasannā prasādātiśayavatī mūrtivigraho'syāṃ sītāyāṃ me mama snehaṃ karotyautsukyaṃ janayati |
utpattirityādi vākyayamidam |
ataḥ karotītyekatvamupapannam |
atra gumbhālaṃkāraḥ |
``gumbhaḥ kāraṇamālā syāt'' iti lakṣaṇāt |
atra snehaṃ karotītyanenautsukyātmakārambha uktaḥ |
taduktam- ``autsukyamātramārambhaḥ phalalābhāya bhūyase'' iti |
tathā ca bhadramastu snehaṃ karotītyābhyāṃ bījārambhasamanvayānmukhasaṃdhiruktaḥ||21||

 kau tvāmityādi |
pratāpaḥ sthāna eva sthitvā śatrutāpanaśaktiḥ, vikramo'bhiyānena śatrupīḍanam |
dharmo'laukikaśreyaḥsādhanam |
dharmaṃ puraskṛtyodgatau pratāpavikramāviva sthitau tvāṃ puraskṛtyodgatau kṣatriyabrahmacāriṇau kau kasya putrau |
kiṃnāmadheyāvityarthaḥ||22||

 tasyottaraṃ rāmalakṣmaṇau |
dāśarathī iti daśarathaputrau |
ata iñ'' |
tau rāmalakṣmaṇau |
upasṛtya |
kuśadhvajamiti śeṣaḥ/ |
guro iti |
pitṛsakhatvādgurutvāoktiḥ |
śvaśurasyāpyayaṃ vyapadeśa iti dhvanyate |
bhāryāyāḥ pitā svasya piteti smṛtisiddham/ |
daśarathaprasūtirdaśarathāpatyam |
``prasūtirjanmatanayau'' iti ratnamālā |
nānyatreti |
anayo rāmalakṣmaṇayoḥ prasūtirutpattī rāghavadraghusaṃbandhino vaṃśātsaṃtānādanya na samānanurūpā |
anayo raghuvaṃśādutpattiranurūpetyarthaḥ |
candrakaustubhayorjanma dugdhārṇavādṛte kutaḥ kṣīrasamudrādanyasmātkasmāt |
na kasmādityarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntālaṃkāraḥ |
``cedbimbapratibimbatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntastadalaṃkṛtiḥ'' iti lakṣaṇāt||23||

 śruteti |
etadvakṣyamāṇaṃ karṇāmṛtaṃ karṇayoramṛtavadbhogyaṃ śrutapūrvaṃ pūrvameva śrutam |
prāptā ityādi |
kosalendeṇa dāśarathena kṛcchrādduḥkhādṛṣyaśṛṅgasyopacāraiḥ pūjābhirhetubhiḥ puṇyaśrīkāḥ pāvanalakṣmīkāḥ putrāḥ prāptā labdhāḥ |
catvāro'pi ye punardīptasya dīptiyuktasya |
jñānagabhasyetyarthaḥ |
śreyaso vedarūpasya puruṣārthasādhanasya puruṣārthasya vā pārakāmāḥ santaḥ kāṣṭhāprepsavaḥ santo brahamacaryaṃ vedārthādhyayanārthaṃ vrataṃ carantyanutiṣṭhantītyetacchrutapūrvamiti saṃbandhaḥ||24||

 taditi |
atra bhavatoḥ kumārayorariṣṭatātiṃ kṣemahetum |
``śivaśamariṣṭasya kare ityariṣṭaśabdāttātil |
``kṣemaṃkaro'riṣṭatātiḥ śivatātiḥ śivaṃkaraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``riṣṭaṃ syādaśubhakṣemanāśanistriṃśapāpmasu'' iti ratnamālā |
riṣṭaśubhaṃ tadviparītamariṣṭam |
taddheturariṣṭatātiḥ |
tamāśāsmaha icchāmahe |
``āṅaḥ śāsu icchāyām'' iti paṭhitāllaḍuttamapuruṣabahuvacanam |
siddha iti |
raghūṇāṃ raghvapatyānāṃ prasūterapatyasyotkarṣātiśayo vīryādiguṇātiśayaḥ siddha eva niṣpanna eva |
ato nāśāsanīyaṃ kiṃcidastīti pūrvoktārthākṣepo'yam |
utkarṣātiśayaṃ viśinaṣṭi- ``yānityādinā |
mitrāvaruṇayorapatyaṃ maiāvaruṇirvasiṣṭhaḥ |
``devatādvandve ca'' ityānaṅ |
bāhvāditvādiñ |
āmrāyena vedena pūte śuddhe nirdoṣe vedabodhitatvātsvayameva nirdoṣe vidhāvanuṣṭheyakarmaṇi yānyuṣmānpraśāsti prakarṣeṇa hitāhite jñāpayati |
śaśvaditi pūrvottarābhyāmanveti |
vasiṣṭho yeṣāṃ vo nityapurohita ityarthaḥ |
anyo viṣaya āśrayo yasya sa tathokto'nyaviṣayaḥ, sa na bhavatītyananyaviṣayaḥ |
rājāntarānāśrita ityarthaḥ |
viśāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ rakṣāyāṃ pālane'dhikāraḥ |
pārārthyaṃ rakṣaṇaikārthatetyarthaḥ |
yeṣu yuṣmāsu śaśvatsthitaḥ |
nityaṃ vartata ityarthaḥ |
yūyameva pradhānarakṣakāḥ |
vayamanye ca yuṣmabhyaṃ karapradā ityarthaḥ |
``sarvā pūrvamiyaṃ yeṣāmāsītkṛtsnā vasuṃdharā'' iti hyuktam |
mahīyasi pūjyatame sāvitrasya saviturapatyasya manoḥ kule vaṃśe'vāptātmanāṃ labdhaśarīrāṇām |
utpannānāmiti yāvat |
``ātmā yatno dhṛtirbuddhiḥ svabhāvo brahma varṣma ca'' ityamaraḥ |
rājñāṃ vo nirupādhikarājatvavatāṃ yuṣmākaṃ mahimā niratiśayaguṇotkarṣo vacanaprajña ānayorvāṅmanasayorjātu kadācidapi na gocaraḥ |
na viṣaya ityarthaḥ |
atra prathamapādena vidyoktā |
dvitīyena vṛttamuktam |
tṛtīyenābhijana uktaḥ |
evamabhijanajavadyāvṛttotkarṣarūpo yuṣmākaṃ mahimāyamaprameya ityarthaḥ||25||

 aśrānteti |
aśrāntānyavicchinnāni puṇyakarmāṇi nityanaimittikādisatkarmāṇi yeṣāṃ tathoktaḥ |
aśrānteti kāmyavyāvṛttiḥ |
puṇyetyabhicāraniṣiddhayorvyāvṛttiḥ |
etena mokṣasādhanakarmānuṣṭhāna iti siddham |
gītaṃ bhagavatā ``karmaṇaiva hi saṃsiddhimāsthitā janakādayaḥ'' iti |
pāvanaprāyā pavitratvapracurā kīrtirguṇavattāprathā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
prāyaśabdena kīrterbhogyatvamapyastīti labhyate |
pāvanatvabhogyatve hi saha prasiddhe |
tatra kasyacitprācuryoktāvanyasyānvayamātramanuktasiddham |
priyādapi hitamabhyarhitamiti pāvanaprāyetyuktam |
etatkīrtirlokānāṃ duritahāriṇī svayaṃ bhogyā cetyarthaḥ |
teṣāṃ raghukularājānāṃ mahābhāgyaṃ mahābhāgatvaṃ pūrvoktābhijanavidyāvṛttotkarṣarūpabhāgyātiśayaphalaṃ vidanti jānantīti mahābhāgyavidaḥ |
mahānbhāgo bhāgyaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktā mahābhāgāḥ |
``bhāgo bhāgye rūpakārthe caturthāṃśaikadeśayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
mahābhagaśabdādbrāhmaṇāditvātṣyañ |
evaṃbhūtā yūyameva teṣāṃ raghukularājānāṃ stavakṣamāḥ |
stutyarhā ityarthaḥ |
``na jātu vacanaprajñānayorgocaraḥ'' iti bhavadvacanaṃ bhavadvyatiriktaviṣayamiti bhāvaḥ||26||

 kauśikāśramasaṃstyāyaṃ kauśikāvārsaṃniveśam |
``munisthānemaṭhe brahmacaryādāvāśramo'striyām'' iti ratnamālā |
``saṃghāte saṃniveśe ca saṃstyāyaḥ ityamaraḥ |
vaikaṅkatasya chāyā vaikaṅkatacchāyām |
``chāyā bāhulye'' iti klībatvam |
tasminnāsmaha upaviśāmaḥ |
``āsa upaveśena'' īṃḍuttamaḥ |
yadvā viṅkatasyeyaṃ vaiṅkatī |
tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
vaikaṅkatī chāyā yasyeti sa tathoktaḥ |
tathāvidhe'sminpradeśe ityarthaḥ |
śeṣaṃ pūrvavat/ |
nepathye kuśīlavavargasthāne |
taduktam- ``kuśīlavakuṭumbasya sthalī nepathyamiṣyate'' iti |
``jaya jaya'' iti ādarādviruktiḥ/ |
kā punariti |
tejo'tiśayena devatāsādṛśyāddevatetyuktam |
ahalyetyādi |
ucathyasyāpatyamaucathyaḥ |
``ṛṣyandhakavṛṣṇikurubhyaśca'' ityaṇ |
aṅgiraso'patyamāṅgirasaḥ |
``ṛṣyandhaka-'' ityādināṇ |
tāmahalyām |
cakame kāmitavān |
``āyādaya ārdhadhātuke vā'' ityukterṇiṅabhāvaḥ |
tasmādahalyākāmanāvaśādgautamadārāvaskandinaṃ gautamadārānavaskandatyapaharatītyarthe avapūrvāt ``skandir gatiśoṣaṇayoḥ'' iti paṭhitātskande ``supyajātau ṇinistācchīlye'' iti ṇiniḥ |
indram |
ahalyāyaijāra iti jānanti ``ahalyāyaijāra'' iti śrutyanukaraṇam |
ahalyāyā jāra itya6ḥ |
jānantītya vedavida iti śeṣaḥ |
atha dārāvaskandanānantaraṃ bhagavāngautamo manyuṃ kopam |
tasya ahalyāyāḥ pāpmanā parapuruṣopabhogaduritena śarīraṃ kartṛ andhatāsisramandhatāḥ misrākhyanarakaviśeṣānubhāvyakāṣṭhapāṣāṇādibhāvam |
tathā ca bhāgavate- ``evamevāndhatāmisre yastu vañcayitvā puruṣaṃ dārādīnupayuṅkte ya śarīrī nipātyamāno yātanāstho vedanayā naṣṭamatirnaṣṭadṛṣṭiśca bhavati yathā vanaspatirvṛścyamānamūlastadandhatāmisraṃ tamupadiśanti'' iti |
seyamahalyā tasmādenasaḥ pūrvoktātpāpānniramucyata nirmuktā muceḥ karmakartari yaki laṅ |
karmaṇi vā yaki laṅ |
rāmabhadratejasā kartrā pūrvayojanāyā hetunā/ |
kathamityādi |
kathamiti vismaye |
aprameye'paricchedye'nubhāvaḥ prabhāvaḥ sāmaryaṃ śaktiśca yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
``anubhāvaḥ prabhāve ca'' ityamaraḥ |
vikartanasya sūryasyedaṃ vaikartanam |
``tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
tasya kulasya kumāro bālaḥ |
rāma ityarthaḥ/ |
savismayeti |
vismayo'halyāśāpavimocanāt, anurāgaḥ saundaryātiśayāt |
nirvarṇyālokya |
apavārya viśvāmitrādyavedanārthaṃ saṃvṛtya śarīranirmāṇasadṛśo'syānubhāvaḥ śarīranirmāṇasya niratiśayasaundaryayuktaśarīrasṛṣṭeḥ sadṛśo'nurūpo'sya rāmasya/ |
rāmāyetyādi |
āryo janako'pratikāryamapratinivartyaṃ tryambakasyedaṃ traiyambakam |
``tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
``nayvāyāṃ padāntābhyāṃ pūrvāai tu tābhyāmaic'' ityaijāgamaḥ |
tasya dhanuṣa āropaṇena maurvyāḥ sajjanena paṇaṃ dhanam |
dhanurāropaṇātmakaṃ kanyakāśulkadhanamityarthaḥ |
nākariṣyadyadi na kṛtavāṃścet |
tenetiśeṣaḥ |
tena janakena sītā puṇyamahase pāvanatejaskāya |
candramā ira dāśarathirdāśarathicandramāḥ |
upamitasamāsaḥ |
tasmai sadṛśāyānurūpāya |
tathā ca sadṛśāya rāmātmakāya puṇyatejaskāya candratulyāya dāśarathaye dattaivābhaviṣyat |
atrāhlādakaratvarūpasāmānyadharmasyānupādānādupamitasamāsaḥ |
pradhānasya sāpekṣasyāpi samāsa ityukteḥ |
rāmādiviśeṣaṇānāṃ dāśarathipadārthe tādātmyenānvayaḥ |
abhaviṣyadakariṣyadityanayoḥ ``liṅnimitte lṛṅkriyātipattau'' iti lṛṅ |
tathā ca raudradhanurāropaṇapaṇakaraṇābhāve rāmātmakadāśarathisaṃpradānapratibandhakamiti paryavasitam||27||

 tāpasastapaḥśīlaḥ |
śīlamityadhikāre ``chatrādibhyo ṇaḥ'' |
sarvā māyā yasya sa tathoktaḥ sarvamāyaḥ |
rājakāryādrāvaṇakāryanimittaṃ vo yuṣmānpaśyati draṣṭumicchati/ |
huṃ rākṣasaḥ |
humiti vitarke |
kimarthamihāgato rākṣasa ityarthaḥ/ |
kautukasthānaṃ harṣahetuḥ |
mātāmaheneti |
daśāsyo rāvaṇo mātāmahena mātuḥ pitrā mālyāvatā mālyavannāmnā daityena pratiṣidhyamāno nivāryamāṇaḥ sansvayaṃgrahādbalātkāreṇa grahaṇāt ``lyablope karmaṇyadhikaraṇe ca'' iti pañcamī |
balātkāreṇa grahaṇaṃ vihāyetyarthaḥ |
ayonijāṃ rājasutāṃ sītāṃ varītumudvoḍhuṃ |
``vṛtto vā'' iti jhaṭo dīrghaḥ |
maithilarājadhānīṃ sīradhvajasya nagarīṃ prati māṃ prāhiṇotpreṣayāmāsa||28||

tatra maithilarājadhānyām |
yajamāna ārabdhayāgaḥ |
sa rājā sīradhvajaḥ tadvacanātsīradhvajavacanādanugato'smyanuprāpto'smi |
iti parikrāmati |
iti vadannāḍhyena parikrāmatītyarthaḥ |
yasthāsaṃkhyamiti |
prathamanirdiṣṭo rāmaḥ prathamanirdiṣṭāṃ sītāṃ prati dvitīyanirdiṣṭo lakṣmaṇo dvitīyanirdiṣṭāmūrmilāṃ pratītyarthaḥ |
ātmagataṃ manogataṃ vakṣyamāṇam |
anusaṃdhatta ityarthaḥ |
tatkimiti |
kenāpi tādṛśena hetunetyarthaḥ |
amṛtavartirivāmṛtāñjanarekheva |
``vartirdīpadaśāgātrālepayormaṇirañjake |
vraṇabheṣajabhede ca nayanāñjanarekhayoḥ/ |
iti ratnamālā |
iyaṃ sītā ūrmilā cāpyāyayati/ |
tathaiva yathāsaṃkhyaṃ sītā rāmaṃ pratyūrmilā lakṣmaṇaṃ pratītyarthaḥ |
kimiti sajjate'smiṃllocanānande me dṛṣṭiḥ |
kimiti kuto hetoḥ sajjate lagnā bhavati |
asminrāme lakṣmaṇe cānandahetāvānandatvavyapadeśaḥ |
tatkimiyamityādinā dṛṣṭirityanatena dvādaśasu śṛṅgārāvasthāsu cakṣuḥprītirnāma prathamāvasthoktā |
taduktam- ``ādarādvīkṣaṇaṃ yattacakṣuḥprītirbhavettu sā'' iti/ |
sā bahuśo'smābhiḥ śrutapūrvādbhutākṛtirvismanīyasaundaryā sītayetyanvayaḥ |
devasya rāvaṇasya yatnaḥ svayaṃgrahādipravṛttyudyogaḥ sthāne |
ucita ityarthaḥ |
anāmayamārogyam |
``brāhmaṇaṃ kuśalaṃ pṛcchetkṣatrabandhumanāmayam'' iti smaraṇāt/ |
tau rājaviśvāmitrau |
rākṣasaṃ pratīti śeṣaḥ |
svāgatam |
tana sukhāgamanaṃ kimityarthaḥ |
ihāsyatām |
tvayeti śeṣaḥ |
apītyādi |
uttarayacchabdasya tacchabdanirapekṣatvāttasyetyasya prasiddhasyetyarthaḥ |
vaḥ prabhoryuṣmākaṃ rājño rāvaṇasyāpi kuśalamityanvayaḥ |
asau pākaśāsana indraḥ skhalatkirīṭena mūrdhnā yasya rāvaṇasya śāsanamājñāmarcayati pūjayati||29||

 kuśalaṃ svāminaḥ |
rāvaṇasya kuśalamityarthaḥ |
mahārājena rāvaṇena vo yuṣmākaṃ saṃdiṣṭam |
manmukhena kiṃcidvākyamityarthaḥ |
cakāraḥ kuśalaṃ samuccinoti |
saṃdeśaṃ viśinaṣṭi- kanyetyādinā |
yornarjanma yasya tadyonijanma |
tatra bhavatītyayonijanma kanyāratnam |
``ratnaṃ maṇau ca śreṣṭhe ca'' iti ratnamālā |
bhavatāṃ yuṣmākamāste vartate |
tataḥ kimityatrāha- vayamityādi |
vayamahamarthinaśca tadviṣaye svoddeśyakadānecchāṃ yuṣmākaṃ prakaṭayanto bhavāmaśca |
kiṃca ratnaṃ kvacidasti cecchreṣṭhavastu yatra kvacana vidyate cecchakrādapīndraṃ vihāyāpi |
``lyablope pañcamī'' |
asmāsu mayi |
pūrvatrātra ca ``asmado dvayośca'' ityekatve bahuvacanam |
``saviśeṣaṇasya pratiṣedhaḥ'' iti tu na vidheyaviśeṣaviṣayam |
pariṇamatyantato gatvāpi viśrāmyati |
``ratnahārī ca pārthivaḥ'' iti hi prasiddham |
kiṃca kanyāyāḥ parārthatā parakīyataiva matā hi śāstrakṛtāmiṣṭā hi |
``artho hi kanyā parakīya eva'' iti hi prasiddham |
atra cāyonijanmetyanena yuṣmākaṃ śarīrasaṃbandhābhāvādyuṣmākamasādhāraṇatvaṃ na bhavatītyuktam |
kṣetrajatvamātreṇa svatvābhimāne tu kaiścidarthitatve tattebhyo'vaśyaṃ dātavyam |
tatrāpi yācñā dainyaparāñcītyarthitvagandhavidhureṇa mayārthitatve'vaśyaṃ mahyaṃ deyam |
athāpi ``madabhyarthanollaṅghane ratnahāri'' iti nyāyena kathaṃcidapi madīyameva tadbhavet |
kiṃca kanyāyāḥ parārthatvādavaśyaṃ kasmaiciddīyate |
evametairhetubhistasyā ratnabhūtāyāḥ kanyāyāḥ ``daśaite rājamātaṅgāstasyaiva hi turaṃgamāḥ'' iti vṛdvṛttyekadeśasyāpi tacchabdena parāmarśaḥ |
pradānātprārthanāpūrvakatvarūpaprakarṣayuktadānena hetunāhaṃ prajāpatiprapautro'haṃ vaḥ kṣatriyāṇāṃ yuṣmākaṃ bandhurbhavitā bandhurbhaviṣyati
(ṣyāmi) |
``suvarṇaṃ kuṇḍale bhavataḥ'' itivadvikṛtikartranusāriṇī tiḍvibhaktiriti bhavaterluṭi prathamapuruṣaḥ |
pulastyapulahau praṣṭhāvagragau yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
``praṣṭho'gragāmini'' iti sūtreṇa nipātitaḥ |
``praṣṭhastrīṣvagrage vare'' iti ratnamālā |
saṃbandhino bhavitāra ityanuṣaṅgavipariṇāmau |
bhaviṣyantīṣyarthaḥ||30||

 hā dhik iti duḥkhanirvedayoḥ/ |
cintayataḥ |
etadrākṣasābhyarthanaṃ cintayataḥ |
prativacanamiti śeṣaḥ |
niśācarapate rāvaṇo devīmimāṃ prārthayate sītāṃ yācate/ |
sādhāraṇyāditi |
sādhāraṇyātsādhāraṇatvātsarveṣāṃ sāmyāt |
samāno dhāraṇo yasya sa sadhāraṇaḥ |
sadhāraṇa eva sādhāraṇaḥ |
tasya bhāvaḥ sādhāraṇyam |
brāhmaṇāditvātṣyañ |
kanyāmanyo'pi rāvaṇādanyo'pi nirātaṅkaḥ sannirbhayaḥ sanyācate prārthayate |
parameṣṭhinaścaturmukhasya prapautraḥ pautrasya putro jagatāṃ jetā jagadvijayī rāvaṇaḥ kanyāṃ yācata iti kiṃ punaḥ |
kimu vaktavyamityarthaḥ||31||

 saujanyaṃ guṇāviṣkārahetubhūtaṃ sujanatvam |
ati hyatiśayitaṃ hi |
``prakarṣe laṅghane'pyati'' ityamaraḥ |
nisargaṃ svabhāvabhūtaṃ vairaṃ vidveṣo'syātiśayenāstīti nisargavairī |
nisargavairiśabdādatiśāyanārthaṃ iti pratyayaḥ |
na karmadhārayāditi niṣedhastu bahuvrīhiṇā tulyārthatāyām |
natvatiśayādivivakṣāyām |
tasminnapi rāvaṇe'pi bahumānaḥ pūjā |
nirgavairamupapādayati- yo na ityādinā |
yo rāvaṇastrayīparidhvaṃsādvedamārgapraṇāśanāmnaḥ kṣātraṃ tejaḥ kṣatrasaṃbandhiparābhibhavanasāmarthyam |
``tejo bale pratāpe'gnau'' iti ratnamālā |
apakarṣatyabhibhavati |
kiṃca yo rāvaṇo rājānamasmākamanaraṇyamasmatkūṭasthamavadhītkila jaghāneti prasiddham |
evaṃ sādhāraṇenāsādhāraṇena cāpakāreṇa nirgavairātiśaye rāvaṇe'pi bahumānādāryasya saujanyamatiśayitamiti bhāvaḥ||32||

 kāmaṃ śatruriti vadhyaḥ syāt |
śatrutvāddhetostasya vadhyatvaṃ mayāpyanujñātamityarthaḥ |
smarābhilāṣayoḥ kāmo'nujñāyāṃ kāmamavyayam'' iti ratnamālā |
kimatrānanujñātamityatrāha- na punarityādi |
ativīryamatiśayitaparākramam |
``vīryaṃ parākrame śulke'' iti ratnamālā |
aprameyatapasamaparicchinnapataskam |
aprākṛtamapṛthagjanaṃ prākṛtavatprākṛtena tulyam |
``vivarṇaḥ pāmaro nīcaḥ prākṛtaśca pṛthagjanaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vyapadeṣṭuṃ punarvaktuṃ nārhasītyanvayaḥ/ |
nirastavīrapuruṣācārasya tyaktavīrapuruṣadharmasya/ |
mā maivam |
evaṃ tvaduktirītiḥ mā mā |
naivetyarthaḥ |
yadityādi |
atra rāvaṇa ityādi rāvaṇo vidvānapi jñātamapi tādṛśe'pyabhijane lokaśreṣṭhe |
pulastyavaṃśe jāto'pīti śeṣaḥ |
dharmyāddharmādanapetāt |
``dharmapathyarthanyāyādanapete iti yatpratyayaḥ |
api cyuto garhaṇīyatayā cyuto bhraṣṭaḥ |
``garhā samuccayapraśnaśaṅkāsaṃbhāvanāsvapi'' ityamaraḥ |
viduṣo'tikrame daṇḍarūyastvamityabhiprāyeṇa vidvānapītyuktam |
ārūḍhapatanābhiprāyeṇa ``tādṛśe'pyabhijane'' ityuktam |
cyuta iti yadatra kiṃ brūmaḥ kiṃ vadāmaḥ |
dharmamārgānna cyuta iti na vadāma ityarthaḥ |
dharmamārgādbhraṣṭatvamanyadeva |
doṣa evetyarthaḥ |
sarve guṇāḥ śauryācārādaya ekatra na vasanti |
ekasminsthāne na tiṣṭhantītyarthaḥ |
doṣastiṣṭhatu, guṇa evādaraṇīya ityāha- līletyādinā |
kiṃ tu līlānirjitaṣaṇamukhādanāyāsanirjataskandādbhagavata ajaśvaryādiguṇavataḥ śrījāmadagnyādṛte jamadajagnatanayaṃ paraśurāmaṃ vinā nirvighnapratipannaviśvavijayo nirvighnaprāptasarvavijayastādṛśo rāvaṇatulyo vīraḥ kaḥ |
na ko'pītyarthaḥ |
anantarāpatye'pi gotratvāropaṇājjāmadagnya iti yañpratyayaḥ||33||

 kimatra cintyate |
manmukhena matprabhusaṃdiṣṭe'rthe vakṣyamāṇādanyatkiṃ vimṛśyata ityarthaḥ |
drāgityādi |
bhūmeḥ sutā jagadvīrasya jagati mukhyavīrasya tasya bhartuḥ pāṇigrahīt rāvaṇasya idaṃ ca vakṣasītyanenānveti |
drāgjhaṭiti niṣpeṣeṇa saṃghaṭṭanena viśīrṇasya bhagnasya vajrasya śakalaiḥ khaṇḍaiḥ pratyuptaiḥ khacitai rūḍhānāṃ prādurbhūtānāṃ vraṇānāṃ granthibhiḥ parvabhiḥ |
``granthiḥ parvagadāntare'' iti ratnamālā |
tairudbhāsinyatyantadyotamāne |
padmarāgamaṇitulyeṣu vraṇagranthiṣu lagnāni dambholiśakalāni hīramaṇasya iva khacitā dṛśyanta iti bhāvaḥ |
bhagnaḥ prārambha eva naṣṭo moghaḥ pravṛtto'pi viphalo maghavanmātaṅgadantānāmudyamo vyāpāro yasmiṃstattathoktaṃ tasmin |
nandanadevatābhirindrodyānapālikābhirdevatābhirviracitānāṃ gumphitānāṃ srajāṃ dāmnāṃ dhāmni sthānabhūte |
``dhāma janmaprabhāsthānaprabhāvasukhasadmasu'' iti ratnamālā |
``viracitasragdāmni'' iti pāṭhe viracitāni gumphitāni srajo mālā dāmāni prālambavaikakṣyakādīni ca yasmiṃ stattathoktaṃ tasminnityartho bodhyaḥ |
tasya rāvaṇasya vakṣasi vīraśrīvira vīralakṣmīriva viśrāmyatu |
viśramaṃ prāptotvityarthaḥ |
ato'nyatkiṃ cintyata ityarthaḥ |
vīraśrīrivetyupamālaṃkāraḥ |
``drāṅniṣpeṣa-'' ityādinā ``nandanadevatā'' ityādinā ca viśeṣaṇadvayena vakṣaso viśramasthānatvaupayikaratnamālāpuṣpamālālaṃkṛtatvokteḥ parikārālaṃkāraḥ |
``alaṃkāraḥ parikaraḥ sābhiprāye viśeṣaṇe'' iti tallakṣaṇāt||34||

 yajñopanimantritā yajñārthamāhūtāḥ |
putradāraiḥ saha putrakalatraiḥ saha |
saṃpatanti samāgacchanti |
ākrandena śiśūnāṃ rodanaśabdena kalilaḥ kaluṣaḥ/ |
āntretyādi |
āntreṣu purītatsu protāni gumphitāni bṛhanti |
nalakānīva ghuṭikā iva kapālāḥ kapālanalakāni |
yadvā |
kapālāśca nālakāni ceti dvandvaḥ |
``śṛṅkhāsthi nalakam'' iti halāyudhaḥ |
krūraṃ yathā bhavati yathā kvaṇanti dhvananti kaṅkaṇāni karabhūṣaṇāni |
āntraprotabṛhatkapālanalakānyeva krūrakvaṇatkaṅkaṇāni tāni prāyo bahūni yeṣu tāni tathūktāni |
teṣāṃ preṅkhitānāṃ calitānāṃ bhūrīṇāṃ pracurāṇāṃ bhūṣaṇānāṃ ravaiḥ śabdairambaramākāśamāghoṣayantī samantādghoṣayuktaṃ kurvatī |
pītasyoccharditasyātimātratvādudvāntasya karmadamasya ghanena sāndreṇa prāgbhāreṇa samūhena |
prāgbhāraśabdaḥ samūhe rūḍha iti vyākhyātāraḥ |
ghorau bhayaṃkarāvullalantāvutpatantau vyālolāvabhitaścalantau yau stanau tayorbhāreṇa bhairavaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ vapuryasyāḥ sā tathoktā |
keyaṃ darpeṇoddhatamudagraṃ yathā tathā dhāvati śīghraṃ gacchati |
``sṛ gatau'' ityasya ``pāghrāghmāsthāmnādāṇ-'' ityādinā śīghragamane dhāvādeśaḥ |
``āntraṃ purītat'' ityamaraḥ |
``prāyo vayomṛtyutulyabāhulyānaśaneṣu nā |
prāyo'vyayaṃ ca bāhulye'' iti ratnamālā||35||

 seyamityādi |
suketoḥ suketunāmakasya yakṣasya duhitā sutā |
sundāsurasya bhāryā |
mārīcasya jananī mātā |
tāṭakā nāma ghorā bhayāvahā rākṣasīti pūrvasmādanuṣaṅgaḥ||36||

 hatāśā iti nindāyām/ |
mā bhaiṣṭaṃ bhītimatyau na bhavetam |
bhaiṣṭamiti bibheterluṅmadhyamapuruṣadvivacanam |
``na māṅyoge'' ityaḍāgamābhāvaḥ |
``adhastāccibukam'' ityamaraḥ |
adharādhodeśe rāmaṃ spṛśannityarthaḥ |
hanyatāmiyam |
iyaṃ tāṭakā hanyatāṃ vadhyatām |
tvayeti śeṣaḥ/ |
eṣa evātisukumāro rāma evātiduṣkare'smiṃstāṭakāvadhe samājñaptaḥ/ |
bhagavānviśvāmitra, iyaṃ tāṭakā strī khalu |
ato na vadhyetyarthaḥ/ |
śrutamāryayāgrajayā tvayā |
strī khalviyamiti rāmavākyaṃ śrutaṃ kimityarthaḥ |
asya rāmasya cittamedo'bhiprāyaviśeṣaḥ/ |
anyato vadhādanyasminmukhamaunmukhyaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ/ |
ajakṣvāka ikṣvākuvaṃśyaḥ |
strīvadhājjugupsamānatvādrāmabhadrasyekṣvākuvaṃśyatvaṃ satyamityarthaḥ/ |
ayamityādi |
eṣa uttālāyāstālamudgatāyāḥ |
``atyādayaḥ krāntādyarthe dvitīyayā'' iti samāsaḥ |
tāṭāyā utpātasyotpatanasya tāṭakātmakaduḥkhasūcakadurgrasya vā darśa'pyaprakampito'calitastasyāstāṭakāyāḥ pramāthāja vadhāya niyukta ājñaptaḥ |
viśvāmitreṇeti śeṣaḥ |
straiṇena strītvena |
strīpuṃsābhyāṃ nañsnañau bhavanāt'' iti strīśabdādbhāve snañpratyayaḥ |
vicikitsati |
hanyatāṃ na hanyatāṃ veti saṃdegdhītyarthaḥ ``vicikitsā tu saṃśayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
ayaṃ sa iti saṃbandhaḥ||37||

 tvarasva |
hantumiti śeṣaḥ |
saṃghātasya mṛtyuḥ saṃghātamṛtyuḥ |
ekakāle bahūnāṃ maraṇamityarthaḥ |
brāhmaṇajanasya saṃghātamṛtyumiti devadattasya gurukulamitivadanvayaḥ |
agrata uttaradakṣiṇena |
tathā ca ``tyajedekaṃ kalasyārthe'' iti nyāyena bhūyiṣṭhabrāhmaṇajanavyapekṣayaikasyā duṣṭastriyo vadho na doṣāyetyarthaḥ/ |
bhagavantaḥ ``utpattiṃ pralayaṃ caiva bhūtānāmāgatiṃ gatim |
vetti vidyāmavidyāṃ ca sa vācyo bhagavāniti||'' ityuktarītyā bhūtotpattyādivedino yūyamevaṃ pūrvoktaprakāreṇa jānanti manyante |
sarveti |
sarvadoṣāṇāṃ bhramapramādāvipralipsādivaktṛdoṣāṇāmanabhiṣvaṅgādasaṃbandhāddhetorānmayasamatāṃ gatā vedatulyatāṃ prāptāḥ |
yuṣmākaṃ bhavādṛśāmabhyupagamā aṅgīkārāḥ puṇyapuṇyāpayoḥ kṛtyākṛtyayorviṣaye pramāṇaṃ pramitihetavaḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
ato bhavanniyogāāttāṭakāṃ haniṣyāmīti bhāvaḥ||38||

 niṣkrānta iti |
vadhasya ``dūrādhvānaṃ vadhaṃ yuddham'' iti pratyakṣaprayoganiṣedhāttāṭakāvadhārthaṃ rāmo raṅgasthalānniṣkrānta ityarthaḥ/ |
utpātavātāvaliranarthasūcikā cakravatyā |
hatāśā duṣṭāśayā/ |
dhanurāsphālya dhanuṣṭaṃkṛtya |
āḥ iti kope |
``āstu syātkopapīḍayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ/ |
tātaḥ kuśadhvajaḥ |
hṛnmarmeti/ |
hṛdo hṛdayasya vakṣaḥsthalasya madhyaṃ bhindantīti hṛnmarmabhedinaḥ |
tathāvidhaiḥ patadbhirutkaṭairudagrahaḥ kaṅkapatraiḥ |
``kaṅkapatraḥ śilīmukhaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃvegena saṃbhrameṇa tatkṣaṇe patanakṣaṇe kṛto janitaḥ sphuṭatāṃ dalatāmaṅgānāṃ bhaṅgo nāśo yasyāḥ sā tathoktā |
``samau saṃvegasaṃbhramau'' ityamaraḥ |
hṛsvā kuṭī kuṭīraḥ |
``kuṭīśamīśuṇḍābhyo raḥ'' iti rapratyayaḥ |
nāsaiva kuṭīro nāsākuṭīraḥ |
tasya kuharayo randhrayoḥ |
``kuharaṃ suṣiraṃ vivaraṃ bilam'' ityādyamaraḥ |
dvayādyugmāttulyaṃ yugapannirgacchannudbudbudo'tiśayitabudbudayukto dhvanañcabdayuktaścāsṛjāṃ raktānāṃ prasaraḥ pravāho yasyāḥ sā tathoktā mṛtaiva |
tāṭaketi śeṣaḥ||39||

 ādarādviruktiḥ- priyaṃ naḥpriyaṃ na iti/ |
dṛḍhaprahāritā niṣṭhuraprahārakartṛtā |
āho |
vismayanīyetyarthaḥ/ |
ārye iti |
svayaṃ vṛddho rākṣasaḥ svāpekṣayāpi kālajyaiṣṭhyāttāṭakāmārye iti saṃbodhayati |
etanmānuṣānmaraṇam |
alābvāḥ phalānyalābūni |
``phale luk'' iti vikārapratyayasya luk |
``tumbyalābūrubhe same'' ityamaraḥ |
tāni hi śuṣkāṇi lāghavātiśayājjale prakṣiptāni plavanasvabhāvāni bhavanti |
grāvāṇaḥ śilā ambuni plavante taranti |
te hi gurutvātiśayājjale prakṣiptā majjanasvabhāvā bhavanti |
teṣāmubhayeṣāmapi pratiniyatasvabhāvaviparyāso yathātyantāsaṃbhāvyaḥ, tathaivātyantāsaṃbhāvyo'pyayaṃ mānuṣarākṣasayoḥ svabhāvaviparyāso'dyakāladoṣātsaṃbhāvita ityarthaḥ |
mānuṣasya rākṣasavadhyatvaṃ svabhāvaḥ, rākṣasasya tu mānuṣaghātakatvam |
iti svabhāvapratiniyame'pyanayoradya vadhyaghātakabhāvo viparyasta iti bhāvaḥ |
uktaṃ ca- ``mānuṣānghnanti rākṣasāḥ'' |
``apsuplavante pāṣāṇāḥ'' iti rūpakātiśayoktiriyam |
upamānabhūtenālābumajjanādinopameyasya mānuṣakartṛkarākṣasavadhasya nigaraṇāt ``rūpakātiśayoktiḥ syānnigīryādhyavasānataḥ'' iti lakṣaṇāt |
upamānaṃ cehāsaṃbhāvitamityasaṃbhāvitopamā vyajyate |
``candrabimbādiva viṣaṃ candanādiva cānalaḥ |
paruṣā vāgato vajrādityasaṃbhāvitopamā||'' iti hyuktam |
nanvityādi |
rākṣasapate rāvaṇasya pratāpaḥ śatrutāpanasvabhāvo'dya skhalito nanu |
adya bhraṣṭa evetyarthaḥ |
``praśvānadhāraṇānujñānunayāmantraṇe nanu'' ityamaraḥ |
adbhuto vismayanīyo manuṣyapotānmanuṣyaśiśoḥ paribhavastiraskāro rākṣasapaterityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
rāvaṇasyādyagata ityarthaḥ |
kiṃca sthitena svāmikāryārthaṃ sthitavatā mayā svajanapramāro rākṣasavadho dṛṣṭaḥ |
dainyaṃ yācñayā kṣīṇasattvatā jarā vayohāniśca niruṇaddhipraticikīrṣaurmā nivartayati |
kathaṃ karomi |
kiṃ karomītyarthaḥ||40||

 eṣa tāṭakāvadhaḥ sakalarākṣasasaṃhāra eva subāhuprabhṛtirāvaṇaparyantasarvarākṣasavadha eva nigamādhyayanaṃ svādhyāyādhyayaṃ tasya ``niṣkarāṇaṃ ṣaḍaṅgo vedo'dhyeyaḥ'' iti niyatvātpuruṣārthasādhanatvācca svādhyāyāyadhyayanasya rākṣasasaṃhāro'pi kṣatriyasya rāmasya nityaḥ puruṣārthasādhanabhūtaśceti bodhayituṃ tasya nimādhyayanarūpaṇaṃ kṛtam |
aoṃkāraḥ praṇavoccāraṇarūpaḥ |
``brahmaṇaḥ praṇavaṃ kuryādādāvante ca nityaśaḥ |
sravatyanaoṃkṛtaṃ brahma parastācca viśīryate||'' ityuktarītyā praṇavo yathā svādhyāyādhyayanaprārambhabhūto'dhyayanapratiṣṭhāpanaśca bhavati, tadvattāṭakāvadho'pi kariṣyamāṇasarvarākṣasavadhaprārambhabhūtastatpratiṣṭhāpakaśca bhavatīti tasyaoṃkāratvarūpaṇaṃ kṛtam/ |
asmāsu mayi matsvāminī ca |
dvitve bahuvacanam/ |
atreti |
atra kanyāratnamityāditvaduktarāvaṇasaṃdeśe viṣaye sīradhvajo janako vettā |
prativacanamiti śeṣaḥ |
``vida jñāne'' ityasmālluṭ prathamapuruṣaḥ |
kuśadhvajo'yaṃ prativaktumityatrāha- kaniṣṭha iti |
kuśadhvajaḥ kaniṣṭha iti tvamapi jānāsyevetyarthaḥ |
bhrātroḥ saṃsṛṣṭatvātkaniṣṭho'pi vaktvityatrāha- asyā iti |
sa sīradhvajo'syāḥ kanyāyāḥ pitā |
kanyādāne hi piturevādhikāro na pitṛvyāderiti bhāvaḥ |
kanyeyaṃ tasyānaurasī |
kṣetralabdhatvaprayuktapratyāsattistūbhayorapi śiṣṭetyatrāha- kuleti |
sa sīradhvajaḥ kule vaṃśe jyeṣṭhaḥ kālato guṇataścādhikaḥ |
sa eva prabhuḥ svāmī ca bhavati |
kṣatriyeṣu jyeṣṭhasyaiva bhūmisvāmyātkṣetralabdhāpīyaṃ tenaiva pratipādyeti bhāvaḥ||41||

 so'pi sīradhvajo jānāti prativacanamiti śeṣaḥ |
atastadanumate kuśadhvajaḥ prativaktveveti rākṣasābhiprāyaḥ/ |
idānīṃ viśvāmitraḥ prakārāntareṇa prativacanasya cirayati- asyetyādi |
divyāstrāṇyeva jṛmbhakāstrāṇyeva maṅgalāni teṣāṃ dānasyāsya rāmasyāyamavasaraḥ prāptakālaḥ |
maṅgale sādhurmaṅgalyaḥ |
``tatra sādhuḥ'' iti yatpratyayaḥ |
yānītyādi |
bhagavato jñānādipūrṇātkṛśāśvātkṛśāśvanāmno muneḥ |
``ākhyātopayoge'' iti pañcamī |
guroścaryā gurucaryāḥ kāṣṭhodakādyāharaṇāni guroḥ karmāṇi tā eva vratānyavaśyānuṣṭheyadharmāstairadhītasya prāptasya |
rahasyamupadeśaikagamyaṃ kavacapañjarādikam |
prayogaḥ preṣaṇam |
saṃhāraḥ pratyāvartanam |
jṛmbhakāstrāṇāṃ prayogasaṃhārau jṛmbhakaprayogasaṃhārau |
rahasyaṃ ca jṛmbhakāsyraprayogasaṃhārau ca rahasyajṛmbhakaprayogasaṃhārāḥ |
taiḥ sahitasyetyarthaḥ |
divyāstramantrāṇāṃ divyāstraprakāśakopaniṣadbhāgaviśeṣāṇāṃ pārāyaṇasyādita ārabhyāntādavicchedanādhyeyasamudāyayasya yāni vidyātattvabījāni vidyātattvasya vidyāvastuno bījāni bījaśaktikīlakādiṣu mantraśāstraparigaṇiteṣu mantrāṅgeṣu bījarūpāṇyaṅgāni |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
tāni matprasādānmadanugrahāt |
gurucaryāvratābhāve'pītyarthaḥ |
arthato vācyabhūtatattaddevatātmanā śabdātmanā ca vācakaśabdasvarūpeṇa ca prakāśantāṃ pratyakṣībhavantu |
``prauṣātisargaprāptakāleṣu kṛtyāśca'' ityanena vidhau loṭ |
brahmādaya iti |
purāṇāḥ purātanā guravo vidyopadeṣṭāro brahmādayaścaturmukhādayo brahmahitāya vedahitāya brāhmaṇahitāya vā paraḥsahasraṃ sahasrātparam |
supsupeti samāsaḥ pāraskarāditvātsuṭ |
rājadantāditvātparanipātaḥ |
``viṃśatyādyāḥ sadaikatve'' ityekatvam |
sahasrasaṃkhyādhikasaṃkhyākāḥ śaradaḥ saṃvatsarānau |
``saṃvatsaro vatsaro'bdo hāyano'strī śaratsamāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
yadyapi ``apavarge tṛtīyā'' iti phalaprāptau vivakṣitāyāmatyantasaṃyoge tṛtīyayā bhavitavyam, tathāpi yatra tṛtīyayaiva jhalaprāptirvivakṣitā tatraiva sā |
yathā ``ahnā krośena vānuvāko'dhītaḥ'' ityādau |
atra śabdāntareṇa phalaprāptervivakṣitatvādatyantasaṃyogamātre dvitīyaiva |
tapāṃsi kāyaśoṣaṇāni karmāṇi taptvā kṛtvā |
pākaṃ pacatītivannirdeśaḥ |
tapomayāni tapasa āgatāni |
``tata āgataḥ'' ityadhikāre ``mayaṭ ca'' iti mayaṭ pratyayaḥ |
svāni svakīyāni tejāṃsi balānyetāni jṛmbhakāstrāṇi |
vidheyaviśeṣaṇamidam |
adarśannapaśyan kāryakāraṇabhāvāttapasāmastrāṇāṃ cāthedadarśanam |
``dṛśir prekṣaṇe''  |
luṅtvādaṅ |
``ṛdṛśo'ṅi guṇaḥ'' iti guṇaḥ |
tapomayānītyatra svārthiko mayaṭ||42||

 diṣṭyetyānande |
devadundubhīnāṃ dhvaniḥ |
``bherī strī dundubhiḥ pumān'' ityamaraḥ |
divīti śeṣaḥ |
puṣpavṛṣṭiśca |
devānāmiti śeṣaḥ |
pūrvatra śrūyata iti, aparatra patatīti kriyāpadamadhyāhartavyam |
rājaviruddhaṃ rāvaṇānabhimataṃ dundubhitāḍanādikamanutiṣṭhari kurvanti/ |
kathamiti vismaye jhaṭitītyādi |
diśa uttaptenātyantānalasaṃyuktenāta eva drutena dravatvaguṇavatā kanakena svarṇena siktā iva kṣaritā iva jhaṭityevāsminneva kṣaṇe |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
divyāstradevatākāntibhirāvṛtatvāddiśāmevaṃvidhatvam |
divasaḥ piśaṅgatvātkapilatvāt |
kaḍāraḥ kapilaḥ piṅgapiśaṅgau'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃdhyayā saṃdhyārāgeṇāntarita eva tirohita eva nirbhāti prakāśate |
atrāpi divyāstradevatākāntayo'bhipretāḥ |
divyāstrairdivyāstradevatābhirnicitaṃ vyāptaṃ nabho'ntarikṣaṃ jvaladbhirdīptaiḥ ketuvrātairdhvajasamūhaiḥ sthagitamiva cchāditamiva |
kiṃca nairantaryeṇa nīrandhratayā pracalitābhistaḍidbhiḥ piñjaramiva piśaṅgavarṇayuktamiva |
``piñjaraṃ kanake klībaṃ pīte triṣu haye pumān'' iti ratnamālā||43||

 bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
tejobhirityādi |
diśidiśi sarvāsu dikṣu viśvataḥ sarvasmindeśe |
``sārvavibhaktikastasiḥ'' iti saptamyarthe tasiḥ |
pradīptaiḥ prakāśamānairādityadyutiṃ sūryakāntimapavidhya pratihatya visphuradbhiḥ paryāyeṇa krameṇa tvaritaṃ yathā tathā gṛhīto grasto vipramukto visṛṣṭaśca |
khañjakubjādivadviśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ |
nāyano netrasaṃbandhī |
śaiṣiko'ṇpratyayaḥ |
mayūkho raśmiḥ sāmarthyaṃ padārthagrahaṇaśaktiṃ rahayati tyajati |
``raha tyāge'' caurādikaḥ||44||

 prākṛte dviva
/
/vanābhāvāttatsthāne bahuvacanam ``siddhirlokāñca'' iti prākṛtavyakāraṇasiddham/ |
durāsadaṃ duṣprapam |
styāyati saṃghātībhavati |
``styai śabdasaṃghātayoḥ'' ityato laṭ prathamapuruṣaḥ |
yena tejasā dvanddavasaṃrambhaṃ dvandvayuddhodyogam |
sarvetyādi |
sarve samastena prāṇena balena pravaṇaṃ yathā tathā prahvaṃ yathā tathā |
``dehāntaranile prāṇo jīvātmaparamātmanoḥ |
āyurindriyasattveṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
``pravaṇaṃ kramanimnorvyāṃ prahve'' ityamaraḥ |
maghavatendreṇa muktam |
kuliśaviśeṣaṇamidam |
vakṣo rāvaṇavakṣaḥ karma |
āhatya saṃtāḍya tatsaṃghaṭṭādrāvaṇavakṣaḥsaṃmardādvighaṭitā viśakalitā bṛhantaḥ sthūlāḥ khaṇḍāḥ śakalāni yasya taṃ tathoktamuccaṇḍaṃ karālaṃ rocistejo yasya tathoktam |
kuliśaṃ kartṛ |
vidyutsahasraiḥ svaśakalātmakataḍitsahasraiḥ karaṇaiḥ |
vyoma nabhaḥkarma |
evaṃ divyāstratejaḥ piñjaritamivākarot |
kiṃca te pūrvābhūtā vatkrajvalanena mukhāgniā kapiśā dhūmraḥ bhartū rāvaṇasya roṣeṇa kopenāṭṭahāsāḥ śuṣkahāsāḥ |
``aṭṭastvaṭṭālake pūre bhṛśaśuṣke tvayaṃ triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
vyoma karma evamakurvan |
akaroditsyānuṣaṅgaḥ, vipariṇāmaśca |
ato dvandvasaṃrambhaṃ smārito'smīti saṃbandhaḥ||45||

 abhivandasvābhivādayasva |
``vadi abhivādanastulyoḥ'' |
brahmeti |
brahmā caturmaukhaḥ, indra upendra indro vā, draviṇeśaḥ kuberaḥ, rudra īśaḥ, varuṇaḥ pracetāḥ, prācīnabarhiḥ surendraḥ |
yadvā prācīnabarhirnāma prajāpatiḥ |
taduktaṃ purāṇaratne- ``prācīnabarhirbhāgavānmahānāsītgrajāpatiḥ'' iti |
marudvāyuḥ, kālo yamaḥla agniranalaḥ, ityetebhyo vyatirekiṇāmadhikānāṃ bhagavatāṃ jñānaśaktyādipūrṇānāmāmnāyāmantrāṇāmātharvaṇavedamantrāṇāmātmanāṃ prāṇabhūtānāmapratihatairakuṇṭhitaistejobhirdīptibhirbalairvā tapasāmivataiddhetubhūtatapovadutkarṣiṇāmatiśayatāmeteṣāmastrāṇāṃ madhya ekaikasyaikasyaikasya yogyatā sāmarthyaṃ jagattyasya pramathanaṃ vidhvaṃsanaṃ trāṇaṃ rakṣaṇamityete'vadhiḥ kāṣṭhā yasyāḥ sā tathoktā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ||46||

 eṣa iti prahvo'smi praṇato'smi |
divyāstrasaṃpradāyo divyāstrāṇāṃ prāthamikaṃ dānam |
upadeśa iti yāvat |
īṃkṣmaṇena sahāstu mameva lakṣmaṇasyāpyastviti bhāvaḥ||47||

 jhaṭiti |
ātmānam |
māmityarthaḥ |
vidyāyā astravidyāyāḥ prakāśanātsākṣākārājjhaṭiti śīghramunmīlitā nikhilavastvavagahinī prajñā prakaṭaṃ jñānaṃ yasya sa tathoktastam |
śaktibhirduṣkarakāryanirvāhakaiḥ sāmarthyairapratarkyamitarasajātīyatayā cintayitumanarham |
kiṃ ca jyotirmayamiva prakāśapracuramiva |
tatprakṛtavacane mayaṭ'' iti prācurye mayaṭ |
vidheyaviśeṣaṇānīmāni |
manye |
niścinomītyarthaḥ||48||

 rāma rāmeti |
``vākyāderāmantritasyāsūyāsaṃmatikopakutsanabhartsaneṣu'' iti prītyatiśaye dviruktiḥ |
viśvāmitrābhyanujñānādviśvāmitrānumaterhetorvayaṃ tvayi yattāmaha āyatāmahe |
tvadadhīnā bhavāma ityarthaḥ |
``adhīo nighna āyattaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
abhyanujñāpadena divyāstrāṇāṃ rāmāyattatvaṃ svārasikamiti gamyate |
saha bhrātreti pūrvottarānvayi |
bhrātari tvayi cāyattā ityarthaḥ |
saha bhrātrā praśādhi naḥ |
asmāṃstava bhrātā lakṣmaṇaḥ kartavyānyājñāpayatu |
tvamapi kartavyānyājñāpayetyarthaḥ |
``śāsu anuśiṣṭau ityasmātprapūrvakālloṇmadhyamaḥ||49||

 devatā mantrayanta ityāścaryam |
``matri guptabhāṣaṇe'' iti caurādika ātmanepadī/ |
bhagavanta iti |
bhagavanto jñānaśaktyādipūrṇā divyāstranikāyā divyāstrasamūhā iti saṃbodhanam |
puṇyaiḥ sukṛtairyuṣmānviśvasya mittrātsarvasuhṛdo viśvāmitrātkauśikāt |
viśvasya mitraṃ viśvāmiḥ |
``mitre carṣāai'' iti pūrvapadasya dīrghaḥ |
śabdasvabhāvātpuṃstvam |
``ākhyātopayoge'' iti pañcamī |
viśvasya mittramityavayavārtho vyutpattimāopayogī prayogasthaleṣu vaivakṣikaḥ |
yathā ``saptasaptarṣayo mālā'' iti saptatvasya pṛthagvivakṣayā sapteti pṛthakprayogaḥ |
ata eva ``gamerḍos'' iti gacchatītyarthe vyutpannasya gopadasya gacchatītyanena paunaruktyaṃ tiṣṭhatītyanena vyāghātaśca netyācāryacaraṇāḥ |
iha ca viśveṣāmasmākaṃ cāmitro viśvāmitra iti pratipakṣajanābhimatavyutpattervyāvṛttyarthaḥ |
prāpyalabdhvā |
rāmo'dya kṛtā6ḥ |
rāmavacanamidam |
ahamadya labdhasarvapuruṣārtha ityarthaḥ |
bhavadbhiḥ sarvaprakārapūjyairyuṣmābhirdhyātaiḥ sadbhistadā tadā saṃnidhānārthaṃ cintitaiḥ sadbhiḥ |
saṃnidheyaṃ saṃnidhiḥ kāryaḥ |
akarmakatvādbhāve yat |
``nityavīpsayoḥ'' iti vīpsāyāṃ siruktiḥ |
vīpsā ca vyāpakatvaṃ vyāptiśca |
kālikatayā yadā yadā mayā dhyānaṃ kriyate tadā mama yaṣmābhiḥ saṃnidhānaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ |
yūyaṃ svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ yāta gacchata |
yā prāṇe'' ityasmālloṇmadhyamabahuvacanam |
vo yuṣmabhyaṃ namaḥ |
namaḥ svasti-'' ityādinā caturthī||50||

 āryavacanādrāmavacanāt |
antaritānyantarhitāni/ |
mahādbhutanidhe mahāścaryanidhe |
jvaliteti |
jvalitāni prakāśamānāni tapāṃWsi kṛcchrādīni yasya sa tathoktastasya tejasāṃ parābhibhavanasāmarthyānāṃ rāśeḥ samudāyabhūtasyamitānyasaṃkhyātānyojāṃ si jñānāni balāni yasya sa tathoktastasya ``ājaḥ śabdaguṇe jñāne bale garvaprakāśayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
tava niravadhāvaparicchide māhābhāgye mahābhāgatve viṣaye kṛtaṃ stutisāhasaṃ stutiviṣayāsāhasapravṛttiryena tathoktaḥ |
avimṛśya stutau pravṛtta ityarthaḥ |
stotā stutikartā vāci pramitiviṣayāṃ stutyanuguṇayathārthajñānaṃsaṃbandhinī śaktiṃ samarthatāṃ na vindannalabhamānaścetasi ca manasi ca pramitiviṣayāṃ śaktiṃ vindansanpratihataḥ parispandaḥ kiṃcitpravṛttiriti yasya ca tathoktaśca san |
yadvā pratihataḥ parispando racanā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
``racanā syātparispandaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vipadya vipadaṃ prāpyaṃ jagati loke ghṛṇīyate ghṛṇāṃ prāpnoti |
``ghṛṇā jugupsā kṛpayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
yadvā māmasamīkṣyakāriṇamiti svātmānaṃ garhate |
viṣayaprabhāvamapayālocya varāko'yaṃ stutau pravṛttau duḥkhyatīti viśeṣajñaiḥ kṛpāviṣayīkriyanta iti vārthaḥ |
``ghṛṇiṅ roṣaṇe lajjāyāṃ ca'' ityasmāt ``kaṇḍvādibhyo yak'' iti yaki dīrghe ca sati ṅitvādātmanepade ca ghṛṇīyata iti rūpam |
dhātūnāmanekārthatvājjugupsākṛpayorvṛttiḥ||51||

 taditi |
tasmādyuṣyābhiranugṛhītena divyāstrapradānahetubhūtaviṣayīkṛtena rāmabhadreṇālaṃkṛtāya śobhitāya |
yuktāyeti yāvat |
rājñe daśarathāya spṛhayāmi daśarathaviṣayakaspṛhāvānbhavāmi |
``spṛha īpsāyām'' curādau kathādiḥ ``spṛherīptisataḥ'' iti saṃpradāsaṃjñāyāṃ caturthī |
vayaṃ punarvayaṃ tvāryeṇa sīradhvajena vañcitā apahṛtapuruṣārthāḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
īdṛśenāpratimaprabhāvena jāmātrā putrīpatinā na saṃyujyāmahe |
yadi saṃyuktā na bhavāmaścedduṣkaradhanurāropaṇapaṇakaraṇena hetunā sīradhvajena vayaṃ vañcitā iti saṃbandhaḥ |
``yuja samādhau'' iti daivādikāllaḍuttamabahuvacanam samityupasargavaśācca saṃyogārthakatvam/ |
adyāpi tāṭakāvadhajṛmbhakāstropadeśānantaramapīyaṃ yadi na saṃyujyāmaha iti pūrvavākyaprastutāsaṃbhāvanāsthānasaṃbhāvanā utkaṭaikakoṭikasaṃśayarūpā saṃbhāvanā |
asmāsu mayi rāmalakṣmaṇayośca/ |
nahi nahi |
asaṃbhāvanetyanuṣaṅgaḥ/ |
śaṃbhoriti |
śaṃbhorvarādrudravarādvo yuṣmākam |
anudhyānamoṇa cintanamātreṇopasthānadāyi gamanadāyi |
anucintanamātreṇa saṃjanadhānaśīlamityarthaḥ |
``supyajātau ṇinistācchīlye'' iti ṇiniḥ |
tasya śaṃbhodharnuḥ kartṛ, rāmabhadrasya purataḥ prādurbhavatu saṃnidhattām||52||

 evamastu bhavaduktaṃ syāt/ |
ebhirniśvāmiādibhiranyadevāsmadviruddhameva prastutaṃ prakrāntam |
kiyacciraṃ kiyantaṃ dīrghakālam |
anādṛto'smi prativacanānuktyā paribhūto'smi/ |
etatprativacanam/ |
sphūrjaditi |
diviṣadāṃ devatānāṃ tejobhiḥ prabhāvaiḥ kāntibhirvā |
``tejaḥ prabhāve dīptau ca'' ityamaraḥ |
iddhaṃ dīptam |
``ñiindhī dīptau'' ityasmātkartati ktaḥ |
trayāṇāṃ purāṇāṃ samāhārāstripuraṃ tasyāntaṃ nāśaṃ karotīti tripurāntakam |
raudratvāditi bhāvaḥ |
dhanuḥ kartṛ |
sphūrjatāṃ śabdatāṃ vajrāṇāṃ dambholīnāṃ sahasraṃ nirjitaṃ tiraskṛtaṃ yena tathoktam |
āhitāgnyāderākṛtigaṇatvānnirjitaśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
``nirmitamiva iti pāṭhe sṛṣṭamiva |
sthitamitya6ḥ |
rāmasyāgrataḥ prādurbhavati rāmasya purataḥ prakāśate/ |
etacchrutvā sītāvākyam- sāṃprataṃ saṃśayitāsmīti |
rāmastadāropayiṣyati na veti saṃdehavatyasmi anantaraṃ rājavākyam- kalabhena gajapotena śuṇḍāraḥ karihastaḥ |
``śuṇḍā karikare madye iti vaijayantī |
``alpe'' ityanuvartamāne ``kuṭīśamīśuṇḍābhyo raḥ'' iti śuṇḍāśabdādrapratyayaḥ |
alpā śuṇḍetyarthaḥ |
``yaṃ śuṇḍaṃ ca śuṇḍā ca śuṇḍāśceti kathyate'' iti trirūpakośaḥ |
acale yadvatparvata iva vatsena rāmeṇa bālena dordaṇḍako'lpo dordaṇḍaḥ |
``alpe'' iti kanpratyayaḥ |
tasmindhanuṣyāhita eva nihita eva/ |
etacchrutvormilāvākyam- api nāmaitadbhavediti |
etaddhanuṣi rāmabhadreṇa dordaṇḍanidhānam/ |
punaḥ rājavākyam- garjiteti |
taddhanurgarjitaḥ śabdayukto guṇo maurvī yasminkarmaṇi tadyathā tathā kaṣṭaṃ ca |
maurvyāropaṇenākṛṣṭamityarthaḥ |
etacchrutvā punarapyūrmilāvākyam hṛṣṭāṃ rāmāya svapradānāt, lajjitāṃ maugdhyāt |
diṣṭyetyānande |
vardhāmahe vṛddhiṃ prāpnumaḥ punarapi rājavākyametat- taddhanurbhagnaṃ cāmarditam |
vatsenetyanuṣaṅgaḥ |
upamānaṃ kalabhaḥ |
upameyaṃ vatsaḥ |
tadanusāreṇa śuṇḍāro dordaṇḍaka ityalpārthapratyayau prayuktau idaṃ cāścaryātiśayakhyāpanārtham||53||

 sarvaṃ kaṣatīti sarvaṃkaṣaḥ |
sarvakūlābhrakarīṣeṣu kaṣaḥ'' iti khalpratyayaḥ |
prabhāvastejaḥ |
tāṭakānirasanarudradhanurbhaṅgābhyāṃ sarvaṃ kaṣatvam |
dorlīleti |
doṣṇorbāhvorlīlayānāyāsavyāpāreṇāñcitātprāpitāñcandraśakharadhanurdaṇḍāvabhaṅgādudyata utpanna āryasya rāmasya bālacaritānāṃ prastāvanāyāḥ prādurbhavasya ḍiṇḍimastatsūcakavādyaviśeṣakadhvanibhūtaḥ |
drāgjhaṭiti paryastasya vighaṭitasya kapālasaṃpuṭamitasya kapālasaṃpuṭasadṛśasya kapālasaṃpuṭavatsvalpasya mitasya vā |
dhvanyādhikyāpekṣayā mitatvoktiḥ |
brahmāṇḍalakṣaṇabhāṇḍasyodare bhrāmyan, ata eva piṇḍitacaṇḍimā puñjībhūtakrūrabhāvaṣṭaṃkāradhvaniṣṭaṃkārātmakaśabdo'dyāpi vyavahitakālo'pi na viśrāmyati na viramati |
aho iti vismaye |
kathamiti vaiyākulyamitaḥparaṃ kiṃcitkālamayaṃ dhvaniravirataścetprapañcapralayo bhaviteti vyākulatvam||54||

 harṣo'nurūpavaralābhāt |
unmādo durghaṭatvāccittavibhramaḥ |
ehyehīti |
ādarāddviruktiḥ |
caraṇapuṣkarakadvayamalpayoścaraṇapaṅkajayordvayam |
``alpe iti kānpratyayaḥ |
vatsa raghunandaneti saṃbuddhyanuguṇaṃ cumbanamityādikam |
rāmabhadreti saṃbuddhyanuguṇamudvahāmītyādikam |
ramante yogino'nante satyānande cidātmani |
iti rāmapadenāsau paraṃ brahmābhidhīyate||'' iti rāmapadanirukteḥ |
a harṣānmūrdhni cumbanādikam, unmādāccaraṇodvahādikam |
suptānāṃ kupitānāṃ svabhāvajñānaṃ dṛṣṭamiti hi prasiddham||55||

 ativātsasyādatisnehāt/'' vatsāṃsābhyāṃ kāmabale'' iti lacpratyayāntādvatsalaśabdādbrāhmaṇāditvādbhāve ṣyañ |
prasaṅgaḥ prakṛto vāgvyāpāraḥ |
atikrāmati sīmānamiti śeṣaḥ/ |
guruḥ pitṛbhūta ityarthaḥ |
garbharūpaḥ putrabhūtaḥ/ |
rāmeṇeti |
rāmeṇa patyā rāmātmakena bhūbhartrā hetunā sītāyā yuṣmākamāśiṣaḥ pūrṇāḥ |
samagrā ityarthaḥ |
asminnevotsave rāmāya sītāpradānahetubhūtadhanurāropaṇātmakotsave mayormilā lakṣmaṇāya dattā||56||

 edrāmāya sītāpradānarūpam/ |
suṣṭutaraṃ samīcīnataram |
``avaduḥsuṣusthaḥ'' iti suśabda upapade sthādhātostarappratyayaḥ |
``suṣāmādiṣu ca'' iti ṣatvam |
rāmāgra sītāpradānaṃ suṭṭhu |
lakṣmaṇāyormilāpradānaṃ suṣṭhuriti samarthaḥ |
vaktavyaśeṣo'nyatkiṃcidvaktavyam |
``śeṣo'nanto hariḥ śeṣaṃ triṣvanyadupayuktataḥ iti ratnamālā |
vidyate hīti śeṣaḥ/ |
te ceti |
te iti dvitīyāntam |
bharataśughnābhyāmiti caturthī/ |
tapasyatastapaścarataḥ |
tapasadhātoḥ kaṇḍvāderyak/ |
vanecarasya haladantātsaptamyāḥ saṃjñāyām'' iti saptamyā aluk/ |
kṣatriyakuṭumbe kṣatriyāṇāṃ gṛhakṛtye vaiyātyaṃ dhṛṣṭatā |
viyātaśabdādbhāve karmaṇi vā ṣyañ |
``dhṛṣṭe dhṛṣṇurviyātaśca'' ityamaraḥ/ |
vicāryaṃ vimarśanīyam |
atra vastuni bharataśughnābhyāṃ māṇḍavīśrutakīrtyoḥ pradānarūpe'rthe paravānasmi |
``paratantraḥ parādhīnaḥ paravānnāthavānapi'' ityamaraḥ/ |
keneti |
paravānityanuṣaṅgaḥ/ |
tāvacchadaḥ kramārthakaḥ |
prathamaṃ bhagavataiva |
yuṣmābhirityarthaḥ |
athetyānantarye/ |
gautameva gautamaputreṇa |
``ṛṣyandhakavṛṣṇikurubhyaśca'' ityaṇ |
ahamāvedayitā |
asminnarthe yuvābhyāṃ na vimatiḥ kāryeti tayorahaṃ pratibodhayitā bhavāmītyarthaḥ/ |
idānīṃ jānāti |
arthasthitimiti śeṣaḥ/ |
janakānāmiti |
janākānāṃ karmayoganiṣṭhānāṃ janakavaṃśyānāṃ raghūṇāṃ jñānayoganiṣṭhānāṃ raghvapatyānāṃ ca saṃbandho'patyasaṃbandhaḥ kasya na priyaḥ |
sarvasyāpi priya ityarthaḥ |
vistarastvasmatkṛtāyāmuttararāmacaritavyākhyāyāṃ talasparśinīsamākhyāyāmanusaṃdheyaḥ |
ya yasminsaṃbandhe kalyāṇasya pratibhūrmadhyasthakalyāṇo maṅgalakaraḥ pratibhūriti vā |
``bhuvaḥ saṃjñāntarayoḥ'' iti kvip |
``dhanikādhamarṇayontare yastiṣṭhati viśvāsārthaṃ sa pratibhūḥ'' iti śābdikāḥ |
``syurlagnakāḥ pratibhuvaḥ, iti cāmaraḥ |
bhavān |
tvamityarthaḥ||57||

 ākāśa iti |
``apraviṣṭaiḥ sahālāpe bhavatyākāśabhāṣitam'' ityukteḥ |
śunaḥśepapātrasyāpraviṣṭatvādākāśa ityuktam |
asmadvacanānmadvacanamuccārya |
lyablope pañcamī |
vacanaprakāramāha- etā ityādinā |
mayaiva kartrā nimergṛhe sīradhvajasya gṛha etāścatasro rājasutāḥ sītormilāmāṇḍavīśrutakīrtayaścaturbhyo raghunandanebhyo rāmalakṣmaṇabharataśatrughnebhyaḥ samaṃ yugapadgautamavadbhūtvā dattā janakakulapurohitena gautamaputreṇa śatānandena tulyaṃ bhūtvā dattāḥ |
vasiṣṭhavacca bhūtvā pratīṣṭāśca raghukulapurohitena vasiṣṭhena bhūtvā pratigṛhītāśca |
abhūvanniti śeṣaḥ/ |
``iṣu icchāyām'' ityasmātpratipūrvātkarmaṇi ktaḥ |
upasargavaśena ca pratigrahārthatvam |
``pratīccha caināṃ bhadraṃ te'' iti pratigrahe prayogaḥ||58||

 tattasmāt |
upamantryāhūya |
rājñaḥ sīradhvajasya |
vitataṃ vismṛtaṃ godānameva maṅgalaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
gāvo lomāni dīyante khaṇḍyante'sminniti godānam |
``do avakhaṇḍane'' |
pariṇeṣyanti vivāhaṃ kariṣyanti |
ityasmādvacanādbrūhīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ/ |
kumārau rāmalaṅmaṇau |
priyādāvābhyāṃ sītormilayordānātpriyataramatiśayitapriyam |
bharataśatrughnābhyāṃ māṇḍavīśrutakīrtyoḥ pradānamācāryatātapādāmantraṇaṃ cetyarthaḥ/ |
avipravāso videhasthitivirahaḥ |
ekagṛhe bhrātaṝṇāṃ bhāryātvādityarthaḥ/ |
adyāpi vāgdānakāle'pi |
dharmākṣarāṇyanarthaparihārakatvātkevaladharmārthamanuśaṃsena mayā pratyuktānyakṣarāṇi |
yadvā vāgdattāyā api saptamapadātpūrvamanyasmai dānasya dharmatvāttadbādhakāni madakṣarāṇītyarthaḥ |
iyaṃ kanyā sītānyasmai dīyata iti yadeṣo'narthaḥ |
prayojanaviparītamityarthaḥ |
ana6 iti vidheyaprādhānyādeṣa iti puṃliṅgam |
kanyeyamityanenormilādiṣvanapekṣa iti dhvanitam |
iti dharmākṣarāṇi śṛṇatieti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
paulastya iti |
paulastyaḥ pulastyapautro rāvaṇo vinayenānauddhatyena yācata iti prārthayata ityasminnarthe ślāghye'pyabhinandanārhe'pi yo'nādara ādarābhāvaḥ paribhavo vā |
ya yasmiṃllokapatinā rāvaṇena saṃbandhe sati jāmātṛśvaśurabhāvasaṃbandhe sati saukhyaṃ sukhaṃ bhavati |
tatra spṛhā na tasminsaṃbandhe spṛhāvirahaḥ |
kiṃca sītayānyathā rāvaṇāya pradānābhāve'pi laṅkaniyataṃ gantavyāḥ |
niyatamiti saṃbhāvanāyām |
prāptavyaivetyarthaḥ |
``ratnahārī ca pārthivaḥ iti hyuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
iti yattattebhyo hetubhyaḥ |
``vacaneṣu ca sarveṣu yanna vyeti tadavyayam'' ityuktatvādbahutvalābhaḥ |
anādāratspṛhayālutayā gantavyatvācca hetoḥ saṃbhāvyamāna ityarthaḥ |
puraṃdarapuryāstatsthānāmapsarasāṃ bandyāḥ kārāyāḥ |
nirodhārthamiti yāvat |
prasakto vidhirbalātkāro vo yuṣmākamiha sītāyāṃ mābhūnna bhavediti ca dharmākṣarāṇīti pūrveṇa sambandhaḥ||59||

 imau kau |
kiṃnāmakau kasya putrāviti cārthaḥ |
etāviti tasyottaram||60||

 yajñapratyūho yajñavighnaḥ |
vighnahetorvighnatvavyapadehaḥ |
hetvalaṃkāraḥ |
``hetuhetumatoraikyaṃ hetuṃ kecitpracakṣate'' iti tallakṣaṇāt vikaṭaṃ viṣamam/ |
tāṭakā hi strītvānmṛtā |
puṃsoḥ subāhumārīcayorviṣaye |
kathamityarthaḥ/ |
hanteti |
sādhusaṃpannamivāsmadanukūlaṃ saṃpannamiti saṃbhāvayāmi |
vidhistaṭākāvadhadhanurbhaṅgasītāpradānarūpo'nuṣṭhīyamāno vyāpāro viparyasto bhavedanyathā bhavet |
subāhumārīcābhyāṃ rāmasya parābhave pūrvoktaṃ sarvamanyathā bhavedityarthaḥ |
yadvā vidhirbhāgyamanyathā bhavedityarthaḥ |
athavā vidhiḥ kālo'nyathā bhavedityarthaḥ |
yadvā vidhiścaturmaukho'nyathā bhavedityarthaḥ |
vidhirvidhāne niyatau kāle padmobhave'pi ca'' iti ratnamālā |
tattasmātkāryamiṣṭāniṣṭarūpaṃ paryanto vidhiryasmiṃstathoktaṃ vīkṣya mālyavati bhartuḥ kaṇiṣṭhamātāmaha upavedaye nivedayiṣye |
atra mālyavatyupavedaya ityanenottarāṅkādau pravekṣyato mālyavatpāsya sūcitatvādaṅkamukhaṃ nāmārthopakṣepakamuktaṃ bhavati |
yathoktam- ``aṅkāntapātreṇārthasya uttarāṅkasya sūcanāt'' iti||61||

 apramatto'vahitaḥ pramattamanavahitaṃ vijayasvāmibhūyāḥ |
``ji abhibhave ityasmāt ``viparābhyāṃ jeḥ'' ityātmanepadam |
pūrvaṃ subāhvabhiprāyeṇa hanyatāmeṣa iti viśvāmitreṇoktam, idānīṃ mārīcābhiprāyeṇa pramattaṃ vijayasvetyuktaṃ kuśadhvajena |
parāgata eva sahānugata e/ |
he rājankuśadhvaja, ito hyehyāgaccha |
yadyapi bhavānrājaiva tathāpi na bhavato'trāvakāśaḥ |
atastvaṃ tatra mā gā ityarthaḥ |
sahānujasyānujasahitasya |
``vopasarjanasya'' iti sahasya so vaikalpikaḥ |
rāmasyāpratimānaṃ nistulam |
``pratimānaṃ pratibimbaṃ pratimā pratiyātanā'' ityamaraḥ |
ājaḥ parākramaṃ paśya |
hi yasmādeṣa rāmaḥ savānbrahmadviṣaḥ samastānapi brāhmaṇaśūnsubāhvādīn |
karma |
ātharvaṇo'tharvaṇoktastīvra ugro'bhicāra iva hiṃsakākalpa iva nihanti |
hinastītyarthaḥ |
pañcasaṃhitātmako hyatharvaṇavedaḥ |
yathāha- vāyuḥ- ``nakṣakalpo vedānāṃ saṃhitānāṃ tathā vidhiḥ |
caturtho'ṅgirasāṃ kalpaḥ śāntikalpaśca pañcamaḥ |
śreṣṭhāstvatharvaṇāmete saṃhitānāṃ vikalpakāḥ||'' iti |
nakṣatrakalpo jyotiṣāṃśaḥ |
vedakalpo brahmayajñādividhiḥ |
saṃhitākalpo mantrabhāgavidhiḥ |
āṅgirasaḥ kalpo'bhicāravidhiḥ |
śāntikalpo'ṣṭādaśamahāmārīśāntyādividhirityarthaḥ |
iti brahmadviṣa ityanenābhicāravidhervedavaidikavirodhinibarhaṇameva prayojanamityuktaṃ bhavati||62||

 iti niṣkrāntāḥ sarve |
raṅgasthalāditi śeṣaḥ |
iti prathamo'ṅkaḥ |
prathamo'ṅkaḥ samāpta ityarthaḥ |
``iti ``hetuprakaraṇaprakārādisamāptiṣu'' ityamaraḥ||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vīrarāghavaḥ/
sa mahāvīracarite vyavṛṇodaṅkamādimam||

ityaṇṇāppaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyākhyāyāṃ bhāvapradyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ prathamo'ṅkaḥ||

dvitīyo'ṅkaḥ

idānīmaṅkāntaramārabhamāṇastadīyaviṣkambhamāha- tataḥ pratiśatītyādinā |
viṣkambhalakṣaṇaṃ tu- ``vṛttavartiṣyamāṇānāṃ kathāṃśānāṃ nidarśakaḥ |
saṃkṣepārthastu viṣkambho madhaypātraprayojitaḥ||'' iti/ |
yataḥ prabhṛti yasmātkālādārabhya |
sarvamāyātsarvamāyanāmnaḥ |
siddhāśramavṛttāntaṃ tāṭakāvadhādikam |
tadārambha |
taditi pañcamyantamavyayam |
tata ārabhyetyarthaḥ |
dūrāditi |
dharaṇīdharābhaṃ parvatasadṛśaṃ tāṭakeyaṃ tāṭakāpetyaṃ mārīcam |
``strībhyo ḍhak'' |
tṛṇavattṛṇena tulyaṃ dūrādapi davīyānatyantadūro yasminkarmaṇi tadyathā bhavati tathā tattathoktam |
yadvā davīya ityavyayam |
dūrādatidūra ityarthaḥ |
vyadhūnotprākṣipat |
sa tāṭakāriḥ subāhorapi hantā rājaputro māṃ hṛdi bādhate||1||

 tadanuplavānāṃ tasya mārīcasya sahāyānām |
``anuplavaḥ sahāyaśca ityamaraḥ |
varyeti padmayoniścaturmaukho'mṛtabhujāṃ devānāṃ vīryotkarṣairvīyātiśayaiḥ karaṇairyaddhanunirmame sṛṣṭavān |
amṛtabhujāmiti sābhiprāyam |
amṛtaṃ hi balātiśayahetuḥ |
tasya sarvadevavīryātiśayātmakasya |
śaṃbhoridaṃ śāṃbhavīyam |
``vā nāmadheyasya vṛddhasaṃjñā vaktavyā'' iti śaṃbhuśabdasya vṛddhasaṃjñāyāṃ ``vṛddhācchaḥ'' iti chapratyayaḥ |
śaṃbhusaṃbandhina ityarthaḥ |
dhanuṣo dvaidhaṃ dvidhābhāvam |
``dvitryośca dhamuñ'' iti dhamuñpratyayaḥ |
rāmo vyadhita kṛtavān |
vipūrvāddadhāterluṅi sicaḥ kittvena ``sthādhvoricca'' itītve ``hrasvādaṅgāt'' iti sico lopaḥ |
aprameyo'cintyo yo rāmaḥ kṛśāśvasya ṛkṣeḥ satyavacanasya |
``ṛṣayaḥ satyavacasaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
śiṣyādviśvāmitrādvijayajananīṃ vijayapradāṃ divyāṃ praśastāmatropaniṣadamastramantrātmakarahasyavedabhāgaṃ prapede prāptavān||2||

 prasahyeti |
prauḍhena pragalbhena |
``prādūhoḍhoḍhyeṣaiṣyeṣu ityanena vṛddhiḥ |
muninā viśvāmitreṇa kāle tāṭakāvadhānantarayogyakāle paśyato'smāddūtasya paśyantamasmaddūtamanādṛtya |
``ṣaṣṭhī cānādare'' iti ṣaṣṭhī prasahya niṣedhākṣarāṇyupalaṅghya rāvaṇadviṣṭaṃ rāvaṇāniṣṭamastradānādbhutamastradānātmakamāścaryaṃ kṛtam |
rāmāyeti śeṣaḥ||3||

 sīteti |
tasya rājñaḥ sīradhvajasya sītāyā bandīgraheṇa balātkṛtyagrahaṇena prasaktaḥ paribhāvo nirasto'gaṇītaḥ |
kiṃca surāṇāṃ devānāmasmānpratyekamukhyamasmānprati guṇabhāvaḥ |
śithilatāṃ nītaṃ prāpitam |
idānīṃ dvairājyamāsīditi bhāvaḥ |
hi yasmādrājñā suraiśca tadānīṃ nāndīdānaprabhṛti |
``vivāhe nāndīmukhaṃ kuryāt'' iti nāndīśrāddhādikam |
prabhṛtiśabdāddevadundubhidhvanyādikaṃ vivakṣitaṃ kṛtam |
tasmātsītābandītyādinā pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
sarvaḥ śakto'śakto'pi vā pratāpe parākrame bhidyamāne sati pūrvāvasthāto vailakṣaṇyaṃ prāpnuvāne sati vikṛtiṃ pūrvāvasthāviparītāvasthāṃ prāyo bāhulyena bhavati prāpnoti |
prāya iti saṃbhāvanāyāṃ vā |
``manye śaṅke dhruvaṃ prāyaḥ'' ityādyukteḥ||4||

 vatsā dauhitrītvāt |
śarpavannakhā yasyāḥ sā tathoktā |
``nakhamukhātsaṃjñāyām'' iti ṅīṣo niṣedhe ṭāp |
``pūrvapadātsaṃjñāyām'' iti ṇatvam |
rājasaṃnidhau sīradhvajasamīpe vārtāke kṛṣyādau vartane'pi ca||'' iti ratnamālā/ |
nirvṛttāni niṣpāditāni |
tatra maithilanagare |
maṅgalakāle upahārīkṛtamityarthaḥ |
māhendraṃ mahendrasaṃbandhi |
parārdhyāni śreṣṭhāni |
``parārdhyāgraprāgrahāra-'' ityādyamaraḥ |
brahmarṣibhyo rāme pariṇamanti paryavasyanti |
amoghamiti |
kṣattrasyāstraṃ kṣattrāsādhāramastraṃ brāhmaṇānāmanugrahaścāmoghaḥ |
avyabhicaritakāryakaramityarthaḥ |
amoghaścāmogha ceti sahoktau ``napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyāmanyatarasyām'' ityekavadbhāvaḥ |
kiṃca yatkṣatraṃ brahmasaṃyutaṃ brāhmaṇānugṛhītaṃ tasya kṣatrasya tejaḥ parābhibhavanasāmarthyaṃ durāsadaṃ durdharṣam |
mānuṣamātre kevalamānuṣe |
``mātraṃ kārsnye'vadhāraṇe'' ityamaraḥ/ |
utpattyeti |
rāghavo jagati loka utpattyaiva svabhāvata eva |
``prakṛtyādibhya upasaṃkhyānam'' iti tṛtīyā |
adbhutamāścaryakaraṃ kimapyanirvācyaṃ tatprasiddhaṃ bhūtaṃ nirupādhikasattāyogi paraṃ brahma |
atra mahadbhūtamanantamaparaṃ vā tasya ha vā etasya mahato bhūtasya'' iti ca śrutiḥ sphoryate |
asyaivaṃvidhasya rāmasya martyatvena manuṣyatvena hetunā kim |
na kiṃcidapyavahīyata ityarthaḥ |
yasya rāmasya caritaṃ devāsurairgīyate kiṃca tarkottarāḥ kāryākāryaviṣaye tarkeṇohāpohātmakajñānenottarā adhikā devā ṛṣayaśca vastuṣu sattāmātrayogiṣu padārtheṣu śuktaṃ duṣkarakāryanirvāhasāmarthyamādadhate niṣpādayanti |
tathā ca sahajaśaktiyoginyādheyaśaktiyogo'tiśayitakāryakara iti bhāvaḥ brahmā caturmaukhaḥ |
varapradānasamaye rāvaṇādibhyo'pekṣitārthapradānakāle martyādeva tvayā manuṣyamātra ityanādṛtānmanuṣyādabhayaṃ bhayābhāvaṃ no jagau noktavān |
``gai śabde'' liṭ |
devādibhya eva rāvaṇasyāvadhyatvaṃ dattavānityarthaḥ |
yadvā brahmā caturmaukho no'smākaṃ rāvaṇāderityarthaḥ |
pakṣaikyenāsmacchabdaprayogaḥ |
martyādeva bhayaṃ bhītiṃ jagau gītavān |
devādibhya evāvadhyapradāne pariśeṣyānmanuṣyādvadhyatvameva dattavānilabhiprāyaḥ |
tasmānmanuṣyamātra iti māvādīriti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||6||

 tarhi prabalena tena rāmeṇa sakhyameva kriyatāmityatrāha- nisargeṇeti nisargeṇa svabhāvena prakṛtyāditvāttṛtīyā |
dharmasya prayībodhitasyālaukikaśreyaḥsādhanasya goptā rakṣakaḥ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
vayaṃ rāvaṇādayo vayaṃ nirgeṇa dharmadruhaḥ pūrvoktadharmadrohiṇaṃ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
ataḥ śaktena balavatā pratiyoginā pratipakṣiṇā rāmeṇa sahārtho vastusvabhāvaprayukto virodho nāśyanāśakabhāvo no'smākaṃ jāta utpannanaḥ |
ayamāśayaḥ |
``duha jighāṃsāyām'' iti dhātodrohaḥ |
jighāṃsā ca dhvaṃsecchā |
``gupū rakṣaṇe'' ityasmāddhātorgopanaṃ rakṣaṇam |
tacca pratiyogyātmano dhvaṃsaprāgabhāvasya sthāpanam |
tathā ca dhvaṃsatatprāgabhāvayornāśyanāśakabhāvo vastusvabhāvaprayuktaḥ, natvaipādhikaḥ |
prāgabhāvasthāpanasya saṃbhavatīti pratiyogijanakakāraṇadhvaṃsanarūpatāyā prasiddhatvāt |
atastejastismirayoriva sakhyaṃ na |
uktaṃ ca kāmandakena- rājastrīsthānadeśānāṃ jñātīnāṃ ca dhanasya ca apahāro mado mānaḥ pīḍā vaiṣayikī tathā/ |
jñānārthaśaktidharmāṇāṃ vidhāto daivameva ca |
miārthasyāvamānaśca tathā bandhuvināśanam |
bhūtānugrahavicchedastathā maṇḍaladūṣaṇam |
ajakārthabhiniveśatvamiti vigrahayonayaḥ||'' iti |
atra dharmavighātaprayukto virodha ukto bhavati||7||

 dṛṣṭiviśeṣaiścakṣurvyāpāraviśeṣairapahniyamāṇalocano'ntarnīyamānalocano na viramatīti jānāmīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
rāvaṇo durmānavaśādapi vigrahameva kuryāditi bhāvaḥ |
atra māno nāma virodhaheturuktaḥ/ |
vandyā iti |
vandyāḥ pūjyā ye viśvasṛjo jagatsnaṣṭāro yugānāṃ kṛtādenāmādau guravaḥ pitaro hitopadeṣṭāro vā |
svayaṃbhuvo'patyāni svāyaṃbhuvāḥ |
``tasyāpatyam'' ityaṇ |
saṃjñāpūrvakavidheranityatvena guṇābhāvāduvaṅ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
te ca vaidehasya videharājasya saṃbandhinaḥ santaḥ kiṃ kuto hetorna priyāḥ |
priyā na bhavantītyarthaḥ |
aho iti vismaye |
vayaṃ cāhaṃ kumbhakarṇādayaśca kiṃ kutaḥ saṃbandhino na priyāḥ kutaḥ saṃbandhino na priyāḥ kutaḥ saṃbandhitvena neṣṭā bhavāmaḥ |
atra ``marīcimatryaṅgirasau pulastyaṃ pulahaṃ kratum |
vasiṣṭhaṃ ca mahātejā yo'sṛjatsaptamānasān||'' iti vacanamanusaṃdheyam |
tadviśvasṛjāmasmakāṃ ca saṃbandhitāyā aniṣṭatvamastu nāma kathaṃcidbhavatu |
durāsadena duṣkareṇa tapasā kāyaśoṣaṇātmakaniyamena dīptasya jājvalyamānasya dīptaśriyo jājvalyamānalakṣmīkasya jagatpaterjagatāmekātapatrādhipatyavataḥ paulastyasya rāvaṇasyāpi nyūnatā kanyakāpradānābhāvahetubhūtamabhijanataporūpaiśvaryavaikalyajñānaṃ hṛdi manasi kathaṃ jātā |
kathamutpannetyarthaḥ||8||

 athaveti pūrvoktākṣepe |
arthitva iti |
prabhoḥ śaktasya svāmino rāvaṇasyārthitve kanyakārthitve prakaṭīkṛte'pi kadāpyaprakaṭite sarvamāyamukhādadya prakaṭite satyapi phalaprāptirna bhavati sītārūpaphalalābho na bhavati |
anenāvamāno nāma virodhaheturuktaḥ |
pratyuteti pūrvoktaviruddhe druhyansayūthyānāṃ tāṭakādīnāṃ vadhenāpakurvanviruddhacarite rāvaṇāniṣṭadharmasthāpanarūpacaritayukto dāśarathī rāmastayā rāvaṇaprārthitayā kanyayā sītayā yuktaḥ saṃgataḥ |
atra druhyanityanena bandhuvināśarūpo virodhaheturuktaḥ |
tayā kanyayā yukta ityanenaikārthāminiveśarūpavirodhaheturuktaḥ |
kiṃca jagatpatirdṛpto daśamukhaḥ parasya śatrormānasya pūjāyāścittasamunnatervā yaśaso guṇavattāprathāyāścotkarṣamatkṛtaṃ strīratnaṃ ca kathaṃ mṛṣyate |
kathaṃ sahata ityarthaḥ |
``mṛṣa titikṣāyām'' daivādi ubhayapadī |
atra dṛpta iti madarūpavirodhaheturmānayaśasoriti mānarūpo virodhahetuścoktaḥ||9||

 nepathyārdhapraviṣṭaḥ |
raṅgasthala iti śeṣaḥ |
pratīhāraḥ |
dvāraniyuktaḥ puruṣa ityarthaḥ |
``dvāri dvāḥsthe pratīhāra'' ityamaraḥ |
vārtāharaḥ pravṛttiharaḥ |
vārtāmāharatītyarthe ``āṅi tācchīlye'' iti harateracpratyayaḥ |
tena vārtāhareṇa tamālarasena tamālapadraveṇa |
``raso rāge drave harṣe'' iti kośaḥ |
tālīpattraṃ taddhi dhavalam |
tatra tamālarasasya nīlasya parabhāgātiśaya iti bhāvaḥ |
upakṣipya samīpe nikṣipya vācayati paṭhati |
``vaca paribhāṣaṇe'' ityasmāccurādeḥ svārthe ṇic |
svastīti mahendradvīpānmahendraparvatopalakṣitadvīpe'vasthāya |
``lyablope karmaṇyadhikaraṇe ca'' iti pañcamī |
abhyarhayati saṃbhāvayati/ |
prabhupadaṃ prabhuviṣayakaṃ vākyam |
``padaṃ vyājāṅghritañcihnasthānāṇāṃśulakṣmasu |
vyavasāye suptiṅante sāyake vastuvākyayoḥ||'' iti ratnamālā |
duḥśliṣṭakramaṃ tyaktaparipāṭīkam |
``śaktau caraṇavikṣepe paripāṭhyamapi kramaḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
mahārājaviṣayakaṃ likhitvāmātyaviṣayaṃ lekhanīyam |
atra rāvaṇaṃ vihāya mālyavato lekhanāllekhyaparipāṭī parityakteti bhāvaḥ/ |
atraiva māṃ prati preṣite lekhya eva |
maheśvaro bhajanīyo'syeti māheśvaraḥ |
``so'sya bhaktiḥ'' ityaṇ |
laṅkeśvaraṃ rāvaṇamabhinandya ślāghitvā bravīti |
vākyaṃ prayuktavānityarthaḥ |
amātyābhyarhaṇe rājābhyarhaṇaṃ kaimutikam |
kiṃca sākṣāllekhye nāmanirdeśānnyakkāro bhavet |
ato rāvaṇaṃ vihāya māmeva likhitavāniti bhāvaḥ |
lekhyārthaprakāramāha- viditamityādinā |
asmābhirmayā daṇḍakāraṇyatīrthānāṃ daṇḍakāraṇyasthitānāṃ puṇyabhūmīnāṃ puṇyasalilānāṃ vopāsakebhyaḥ sevakebhyaḥ |
caturthyantamidam |
``jalāvatāro guruyonyupāyaśāstrāṇi yajño munijuṣṭatoyam |
mantrādayo'ṣṭādaśa puṇyabhūmiḥ pātraṃ jalaṃ strīkusumaṃ ca tīrtham||'' iti ratnamālā |
abhayaṃ bhayanivartanaṃ pratijñātam |
kartavyatayāṅīkṛtamiti yāvat |
etadvoviditamiti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
tatra daṇḍakāraṇyatīrthopāsakeṣvaticarantyasmatpratijñātārthaviruddhamācaranti |
tānvirādhaprabhṛtīnpratiṣidhya nivartyāsmākaṃ yuṣmāsu sthitāṃ māheśvaraprītimanurudhyantāṃ kāmayantām |
``ano rudha kāme'' ityasmāddaivādikātkartari loṭiśyan |
virādhādyanivartane yuṣmadiṣṭāsmatprītirvihanyeteti bhāvaḥ |
brāhmaṇeti |
brāhmaṇeṣvatikramasyāparādhasya tyāgo nivṛttirbhavatāmeva |
bhūtaye yuṣmākameva śreyase |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
nāsmākaṃ kṣemāyaiva brāhmaṇātikramatyāgo bhavadbhiḥ kāryaḥ |
kiṃtu bhavatāmātmahitakāmanayāpi kārya iti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ jāmadagnyaḥ paraśurāmo vo yuṣmākamevaṃ satyeva mittraṃ bhavet |
cakārādyuṣmadbhūtireva mukhyaphalam, asmanmaitrī tvānuṣaṅgikīti bhāvaḥ |
anyathaivamapi brāhmaṇātikrame jāmadagnyo durmanāyate duṣṭanāmake duṣṭamanasko bhaviṣyati |
kruddho bhaviṣyatītya6ḥ |
durmanaḥśabdādbahuvrīheḥ ``kartuḥ kvayaṅ salopaśca'' iti kvaṅ |
sakāralopo dīrghaśca||10||

 īṣanmasṛṇena kiṃcicchlakṣṇenāvaṣṭambhena kārkaśena gambhīro gahano guruko mahāṃścopanyāso vākyasaṃdarbhaḥ |
daurmanasyapratiyogino maitrasyānuṣaṅgikatvokteḥ bhūtermaukhyatvokteśca kiṃcicchlakṣṇatvam/ |
khalviti prasiddham |
vakṣyamāṇa viśeṣaiḥ prasiddha ityarthaḥ |
abhijaneti |
nijaiḥ svīyaiḥ |
``nijau svīyāvināśinau'' iti ratnamālā |
