abhijanasya vaṃśasya tapasaḥ kāyaśoṣaṇādervidyāyā devājadakāyā vīryasya parākramasya kriyāyā yāgayuddhādikāyā atiśayairutkarṣairupacitamadaḥ pravṛddhyarhagarvaḥ |
arhektaḥ |
``gajadānaṃ garvaharṣaretomṛgamadā madāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
tathāpi sarveṣāmabhijanādīnāṃ tyāgātparābhibhavahetutayānādartavyatvajñānānnirīhatayā parāvamānaviṣaye niśceṣṭatayā sthito vidyamāno'sau jāmadagnyaḥ śaivaprītyā śivabhaktatāprayuktaprītyā kathaṃcitkenāpi prakāreṇa |
pūrvoktarītyā mukhyānuṣaṅgikabhāvaviparyayeṇetyarthaḥ |
anāsthayā tatparatāviraheṇa |
``āsthāā pratijñātātparyagoṣṭhīyatnopalambhane'' iti ratnamālā |
no'smākaṃ vyapadiśati ``brāhmaṇātikramatyāgaḥ'' ityādikaṃ vadati |
kārye kārye punaḥ svābhimateṣu prabhurivātikarkaśo bhavati |
``hasanniva nṛpo hanti'' ityuktarītyā rājavadatitīkṣṇadaṇḍadharo bhavatītyarthaḥ |
yadvānāsthayā prabhurivetyatra aprabhuriva iti chedaḥ |
kārye kā itya ca ``kārye akārye iti chedaḥ |
tathā cāyamarthaḥ- anāsthayā kathaṃcidaprabhurivāśakta iva vyapadiśati |
akārye naḥ akārye punarbrāhmaṇātikramādisvānabhimatakārye'smābhiḥ kartavye tvatikarkaśo bhavatītyarthaḥ||11||

 yadītyādi |
śaṃbhordhanuḥpramāthāṃ rudradhanurbhaṅgakaro rāmaḥ śaṃbhoḥ śiṣyasya śaṃbhuśiṣyasya jāmadagnyasya prapadyeta yadi jñāyet cet |
śiṣyasyeti śeṣe ṣaṣṭhī |
śiṣyasaṃbandhijñānaviṣayo bhaveccedityarthaḥ |
śaṃbhuśiṣyajāmadagnyo na titikṣate na kṣamate |
``tijeḥ kṣamāyām iti svārthe san |
guruparibhavaṃ na kṣamiṣyata ityarthaḥ |
āyodhane yuddhe saṃrambhasya sakrodhodyasya yogātsaṃbandhādubhayorjāmadagnyadāśarathyornirghātaḥ sundopasundavanmitho vadhaḥ syāccet, tadā no'tipriyamatyantamiṣṭam |
syāditi śeṣaḥ||12||

 anyataravijaye'pyekaikasyaikaikena vijaye'pikṣatriyāntakaḥ paraśurāmo rājaputraṃ rāmaṃ vijayeta cedabhibhūyāccet, enaṃ rājaputramanamihatyāhatvā samīhitamabhilaṣitaṃ siddhaṃ niṣpannam |
ajakṣvāka iti cediti pūrvoktapakṣātirikte pakṣe |
``pakṣāntare cedyadi ca'' ityamaraḥ |
vijayamāno'bhibhavanbrahmaṇyo brāhmaṇahita ajakṣvāko rāmo brahmarṣi bhārgavaṃ nābhihanyāddhantuṃ nārhaḥ |
niḥśreyasāpanno muktaprāyo'yaṃ jāmadagnyo'paviddhamapi kuṇṭhitamapi śastramāyu3 na praṇidadhyānna cintayet |
rāmāparibhavena śastrasaṃnyāsāttapobhyupagamāñca niḥśreyasāpannatvaṃ mantavyam/ |
tato bhārgavasya rāmātparājaye sati ko viśeṣaḥ |
ubhayorāyodhane nikhātasyeṣṭatvena śatrutvāviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ/ |
tāvatprathamamāraṇyakānāmaraṇyasthamanuṣyāṇāṃ vrataṃ niyamo yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
``araṇyānmanuṣye'' iti vuñ |
punastādṛśa eva punarapyāraṇyakavrata eva |
rāmavadhamātreṇa caritārtho nāsmākamapi cikīrṣediti bhāvaḥ |
sa iti |
cediti pakṣāntare |
ślāghyo guṇādibhiḥ śreṣṭho rājaputrastu rāmastu punarutthātukāmo bhārgavavadhānantaramapyutthātukāma utkṛṣṭaṃ prāptukāmaḥ san |
``saṃtuṣṭaḥ kṣatriyastathā'' ityuktatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
utsāhaśakterāphalāntasthiraprayatnaśakteḥ |
``stheyānprayatna utsāhaḥ'' iti hyuktam/ |
saṃpadātiśayena prakṛṣṭatamamatiśayena prakṛṣṭam |
rāvaṇaḥ prakṛṣṭaḥ, kārtavīryaḥ prakṛṣṭataraḥ, jāmadagnyaḥ prakṛṣṭatamaḥ, iti viṣayanirdhāraṇe vihitasya tamapaḥ prayogaḥ |
dharmeṇa vijayinam |
anena kūṭayodhitvaṃ vyudasyate |
taṃ bhārgavaṃ vijayate cetsarve nirjarā devāstaṃ rāmaṃ vijayinaṃ sarvotkṛṣṭaṃ jānīyuravagaccheyuḥ |
rāvaṇaparākrāntyā rāvaṇasya parākrameṇa nibhṛtatūryā nirvyāpārayuddhadatūryā nirvyāpārayuddhavādyaviśeṣāḥ |
``nibhṛtakruddhāḥ'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
enaṃ rāvaṇaṃ prasahya balātkṛtyādhikuryuḥ vyāpriyeran |
hi yasmādasuvijayināṃ devānāmavamānatastiraskārātprakṛtikopo rāṣṭrakopaḥ |
``amātyarāṣṭradurgāṇi kośo daṇḍaśca pañcamaḥ |
etāḥ prakṛtayastajjñairvijigīṣorudāhṛtāḥ||'' iti kāmandakakāḥ |
nityānuṣakto nityānuvṛttastasmātprasahyādhikuryuriti saṃbandhaḥ |
paulastyeti |
yomunirbhāgavaḥ paulastyasya rāvaṇasyāpajayena parābhavena pracaṇḍacarite tīkṣṇavyāpāre kārtavīrye kṛtavīryasyāpatye'rjune viṣaye sarvakṣatrasya kṣatriyasāmānyasya kathāyāḥ śabdasya samāpanaṃ nāśanaṃ tadeva kathāsamāpanaṃ prabandhasamāptiniṣpādanaṃ tadeva vidhirvihitadharmānuṣṭhānaṃ tasya prāṅmaṅgalamavighnena parisamāptyarthamādau kartavyamāṅgalam |
madhyāntamaṅle'pi histastadvyāvṛttaye prāgiti padam |
nirvighnasamāptyarthamaṅgalasthānīyaṃ vadhaṃ prākarotprastutavān |
karmaṇo'dhikaraṇatvivakṣayā kārtavīrye iti saptamī |
tasminnapi tādṛśe jāmadagnye'pyupanītaṃ prāpitaṃ yuktaṃ yogyam |
brāhmaṇasya vadhānarhatvācchaktyākarṣarūpamityarthaḥ |
damanaṃ śikṣaṇaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ |
sāmarthye sati śaktau satyāmujjhitamasmadbhayastyaktāsmāddhetukānarthaśaṅko dharmeṇānṛśaṃsyādinā saumyaṃ ramyaṃ caritaṃ vṛttaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
saumyaḥ pumānbudhe vipre triṣu syātsomadaivate |
ramyerunugre ca'' iti ratnamālā |
rāmo viśvasya patiḥ syādekacchādhipatirbhavitumarha ityarthaḥ||13||

 āsminsaṃdehe/ |
paraśurāmasyottejanaṃ niśātanaṃ vairasaṃdhukṣaṇam/ |
pakṣāntare rāmeṇa jāmadagnyasya jame mahādoṣaḥ |
pūrvokto mahānanarthaṃ ityarthaḥ/ |
tatrāpi tasminpakṣe'pi rāmeṇa jāmadagnyasya jaye'pi śaktitaḥ śaktyanusāreṇa pratividhāsyate pratīkāraḥ kariṣyate |
asmābhiriti śeṣaḥ |
vañcanayā daṇḍakāraṇyaprasthāpanasītāharaṇādirūpaṃ pratīkāraṃ iti caturthāṅkasya cārthaḥ sūcitaḥ |
kiṃtviti dvitīyapakṣaparityāgena prathamapakṣaparigrahaḥ |
tānyeveti |
a rāmasyetyādi |
rāmasya tānyeva bhūtāni yadi losādhāraṇāni dehārambhakāṇi pṛthivyādibhūtāni yadi |
``yukte kṣmādāvṛte bhūtaṃ prāṇyatīte same triṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
tā eva śaktayo yadi lokasādhāraṇāni sāmarthyāni ca yadi |
tato rāmātparaśurāmasya parājayaṃ na pratīmo na niścinumaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ- rāmasya vigrahārambhakāṇi vīrapuruṣāntarasādhāraṇāni śaktayaśca sarvavīrapuruṣasādhāraṇyaścedrāmātparaśurāmasya parājayo na bhavedeva |
paraṃtu ``na bhūtasaṃghasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ |
na tasya prākṛtā mūrtiḥ'', ``parāsya śaktirvividhaiva śrūyate'' ityādiśāstroktarītyā rāmasya lokavilakṣaṇavigrahaśaktiyogādrāmātparaśurāmasya parābhavaḥ śaṅkanīya eveti |
yadvā paraśurāmasya tānyeva bhūtāni yadi pūrvāvasthāvasthitāni yadyanākṛṣṭāni |
akṣarāṇītyarthaḥ |
tā eva śaktayo yadi |
śaktayo'pyanākaṣṭāścedityarthaḥ |
tato rāmādityādi |
ayamāśayaḥ- rāmeṇa paraśurāmasya vigrahasāraśaktayo nākṛṣṭāścedrāmāttasya parājayo na bhavet |
rāmeṇa tā yadyākṛṣṭā bhaveyustadā rāmāttasya parājayo bhavedeveti |
kiṃ tānyevetyādi |
rāmasya dehārambhakāṇi bhūtānyeva paraśurāmasya dehārambhakāṇi |
rāmaniṣṭhā eva śaktayaḥ paraśurāmaniṣṭhāśca bhavanti khalu |
ubhayorapi bhagavadavatāratvena dharmaikyāt |
ataḥ paraśurāmasya parājayaṃ na pratīmaḥ |
``nahi svasmātsvasya parājayaḥ'' iti yuktisaṃbhavo'stītyapi dhvanyate/ |
tattasmānmithilāprasthāpanāya jāmadagnyasya mithilāprasthāpanaṃ kartumuttiṣṭhetyanvayaḥ |
jāmadangyamuttejayitumudbhūtakrodhaṃ kartuṃ mahendradvīpameva gacchāva iti saṃbandhaḥ |
tatra mahendradvīpe bhārgavo bhṛgorapatyaṃ paraśurāmo draṣṭavyaśca prakṛtanimittenāpyabhyudayārthaṃ draṣṭavya evetyarthaḥ |
gabhīra iti |
māhātmyānmahāprayatnavattvānmahādhairyavattvānmahānmanastvādabhijanata po'tiśayaprabhṛtimahāsvabhāvavatvātparamātmāṃśatvānmahājñānavatvānmahāśarīravattvācca gabhīro durjñeyaḥ |
``ātmā yatnadhṛtisvāntasvabhāvaparamātmasu |
jīvabuddhiśarīreṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
praśamena prakṛṣṭenāntarindriyanigraheṇa śuciranagho'tyantasujano'tiśayitasaujanyavān |
saujanyamānṛśaṃsyādipūrṇatvam |
sarvasya janasya puṇyānāṃ sukṛtānāṃ prasannaḥ pracaya iva phalapradānonmukhātiśaya iva sarvasya janasya sukhadaḥ saukhyaheturasau jāmadagnyo dṛṣṭaḥ sanprāṇibhiḥ sākṣātkṛtaḥ sanprabhutvasyotkarṣājjagadekasvāmitvātsarvakāryanirvahaṇānuguṇasāmarthyātiśayādvā |
tapasāṃ kāśoṣaṇādīnāṃ pariṇatyā parayā kāṣṭhayā yā viśuddhiranaghatvaṃ tato hetośca sarvasya prāṇinaḥ sattvaṃ balaṃ sattvaguṇaṃ sā3tāṃ vā |
``sattvaṃ dravye piśācādau vyasāyasvābhāvayoḥ |
guṇaprāṇabalasvāntasattāsvastrī tu jantuṣu||'' iti ratnamālā |
prabalayati vardhayati |
pāpaṃ nudati vāniṣṭahetuduritaṃ nāśayati ca |
ato draṣṭavya iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
atra prabhutvasyotkarṣāt ``sattvaṃ prabalayati ityanena ``mahānprabhurvai puruṣaḥ pravartakaḥ'' iti śrutyartho'nusaṃhitaḥ |
``pāpaṃ ca nudati ityanena ``te punantyurukālena darśanādeva sādhavaḥ'' iti smṛtyartho'nusaṃhitaḥ||15||

 miśraviṣkambhaḥ saṃskṛtaprākṛtātmako viṣkambhaḥ/ |
rājakuleṣu rājagṛheṣu carantīti rājakulacāriṇaḥ |
kailāseti |
kailāsoddhārasya kailāsaparvatoddhāraṇasya sāreṇa taddhetubhūtapalena |
``sāro bale sthirāṃśe'rthe nyāyye klībaṃ vare triṣu |
klībamambuni'' iti ratnamālā |
trayāṇāṃ bhuvanānāṃ samāhārastribhuvanam |
pātrāditvātklībatvam |
tasya vijayasya svavaśīkaraṇasyaurjityena |
ūrjito baliṣṭhaḥ ``ūrja balaprāṇanayoḥ'' ityasmāt ``gatyarthākarmakaśliṣaśīṅsthāsavasajanaruhajīryatibhyaśca'' iti kartari ktaḥ |
tasya bhāva aurjityam |
brahmaṇāderākṛtigaṇatvādbhāve ṣyañ |
tena niṣṇātaḥ sarvakāryanipuṇo dorbāhuryasya sa tathoktastasya paulastyasyāpi rāvaṇasyāpi iyaṃ ṣaṣṭhī mada ityanenānveti |
helayānāsenāpahṛto raṇamado yuddhadarpo yena sa tathoktaḥ durdamo durnigrāhyaḥ kārtavaryo yasya paraśurāmasya krodhātkuṭhāreṇa paraśunā pravighaṭitacchinnā mahāntaḥ pṛthulāḥ skandhā eva skandhāḥ |
śliṣṭarūpakam |
teṣāṃ bandhā bandhanāni yāsāṃ tathoktāḥ sthavīyasyo'tisthūlā doḥśākhā eva bāhulakṣaṇāḥ śākhā eva daṇḍaṣaṇḍāni daṇḍasamūhā yasya sa tathoktaḥ sankandairiva kulyairasthibhiḥ |
``asthi kulyaṃ syāt'' iti ratnamālā |
rahitaḥ śūnyastaruriva vṛkṣa iva purābhūtpūrvamabhūt||16||

 so'yamiti |
so'yaṃ triḥsaptavārānekaviṃśatyavasān |
``nivahāvasarau vārau'' ityamaraḥ |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
``dvitricaturbhyaḥ suc'' iti triśabdātsuc |
avikalaṃ samagraṃ yathā tathā vihitaḥ kṛtaḥ kṣattrāṇāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ tanttramanekakarmakārakeṣvekakriyā vyaktirūpaḥ prakṛṣṭo māro maraṇaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ |
tattraṃ hyanekapradhānoddeśena kriyāyāḥ sakṛdanuṣṭhānam |
yathā- darśādikriyoddeśena prayājādīnāṃ sakṛdanuṣṭhānam |
etaccādhvaramīmāṃsāyāmekādaśādhyāye bhagavatā jaimininā nirūpitaṃ tatra draṣṭavyam |
iha tvekenaivodyameva bahūnāṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ vadhamiti tantraśabdaprayogaḥ |
``tantraṃ svarāṣṭracintāyāṃ tantuvāye paricchade |
yāgaprayoge siddhānte śāstramantrauṣadheṣu ca/ |
ekasyaivobhayārthatve kuṭumbavyāpṛtāvapi||'' iti ratnamālā |
``māro nā maraṇe kāme mārī caṇḍyāṃ janakṣaye'' iti ca ratnamālā |
vīraḥ śūraḥ |
krauñcasya krauñcaparvatasya bhedāddāraṇātkṛto dharaṇitale'pūrvo nūtano haṃsānāmavatāro'varohaṇaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ |
pūrvaṃ hi haṃsā mānasasarasyeva sthitā bhūmau nāvarohanti sma |
tadanantaraṃ bhagavatā paraśurāmeṇa krauñcaparvate sarandhrīkṛte sati tebhyo randhrebhyo haṃsā mānasasaraso bhūtalamavatarantīti paurāṇikī prasiddhiratra draṣṭavyā |
herambo vināyakaḥ, bhṛṅgī bhṛṅgīnāmako rudraparivāraviśeṣaḥ, tau pramukhau pradhānau yeṣāṃ te tathoktāsteṣāṃ gaṇānāṃ pramathānām |
``saṃghāte pramathe gaṇaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
camūnāṃ senānāṃ cakrāṇi samūhāḥ santyasyetyasminnarthe cakraśabdāt ``ata iniṭhanau'' iti matvarthīya inipratyayaḥ |
``cakraṃ caye jalāvarte rathāṅge rāṣṭrasainyayoḥ |
ghaṭasādhanabhede ca klībe loke tu puṃsyayam||'' iti ratnamālā |
tārakāreḥ skandasya jetābhibhavitā |
purā khalu śasrakalahe skandaṃ paraśurāmo jitavāniti paurāṇikī prasiddhiḥ |
jāmadagnyaḥ paraśurāmaḥ svagurorharasya rudrasya dhanuṣo bhaṅgena roṣāttvāṃ pṛcchansannupaiti prāpnoti||17||

 sīteti |
praviśatītyanuṣaṅgaḥ |
sakhyaśca praviśantīti vipariṇāmaśca/ |
māhābhāgyeti |
mābhāgyasya mahābhāgatāyā mahānidhirnidhayo nava prasiddhāstebhyo'pyatiśayito nidhirbhavato devasya purāṃ dagdhuḥ śivasya śiṣya āmrāyena vedābhyāsena viśuddhānyanaghāni sattvaṃ vyavasāyaścaritāni vṛttāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
bhṛgūṇāṃ bhṛguvaṃśaprabhavāṇāṃ patiḥ śreṣṭhaḥ paraśurāmo draṣṭavyaḥ |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
sa ca māṃ didṛkṣuḥ so'pi māṃ draṣṭumicchuḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
tathā taddarśanaṃ mamābhyudayahetustadvattaddṛṣṭiviṣayatvamapi mamābhyudayaheturiti bhāvaḥ |
yathoktam- ``yasyānubhāvaparyantā dṛṣṭistattve pratiṣṭhitā |
taddvṛṣṭigocarāḥ sarve mucyante sarvakilbiṣaiḥ||'' iti |
apicetyādipūrvoktārthavirodhadyotako'picetyavyayasaṃghātaḥ |
mugdhayā |
``udayadyauvanā mugdhā lajjāvijitamanmathā'' ityuktamugdhālakṣaṇalakṣitayā sītayā saṃtrāsādbhayātiśayāddhrayaṃ lajjāṃ tyaktvābhijātyena paramaprakṛṣṭajanakavaṃśaprasūtatvena hetunā nibhṛtaḥ sthiramato'yaṃ sneho'sthānātiśaṅkāhetuḥ prītiviśeṣo mayi paraśurāmaṃ didṛkṣau tena ca didṛkṣite mayi dyotyate mukhabhaṅgīviśeṣeṇa sūcyate |
mama taddarśanocchāviruddho mayi snehasthitayā sūcyata ityarthaḥ||18||

 etatparaśurāmadarśanam/ |
paraśurāmadarśanatvarayālam |
sādhyaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ |
``gamyamānāpi kriyā kārakavibhaktau prayojikā'' iti tṛtīyā/ |
utsavā vivāhādayaḥ |
parairavadhīraṇayā paraprayuktaparibhavena vairasyaṃ svādutāvirahaṃ nārhanti |
kiṃtu parāvadhīraṇāparihāreṇaivotsavānāṃ rasyatvamityarthaḥ/ |
vāraṃ vāramavasare'vasare |
``kālādhvanoratyantasaṃyoge'' iti dvitīyā |
nivartito niṣpādito viṣayavyavasāyaḥ kṣatriyarudhireṇa pitṛtarpaṇasaṃkalpo yena sa tathoktaḥ |
brāhmaṇasya kṣatriyavadho hyakṛtyakaraṇamiti bhāvaḥ/ |
ekadeśena mahati guṇasamudāye'ntargatenaikena doṣeṇa mahājñānaninanananadheḥ sarvadoṣānabhiṣvaṅgahetubhūtatattvajñānapūrṇasya māhātmyaṃ sarvābhyarhaṇatvamapahriyate nāśyate |
``tejīyasāṃ na doṣāya vahneḥ sarvabhujo yathā'' iti hyuktam |
``eko hi doṣo guṇasaṃnipāte nimajjītīndoḥ kiraṇeṣvivāāṅkaḥ'' iti nyāyaḥ śāstre draṣṭavyaḥ |
ya eṣa yaḥ paraśurāmaḥ |
kriyābhyāvṛttigaṇane kṛtvasuc'', ``dvitricaturbhyaḥ suc'' iti sūtrābhyāṃ saptanśabdātkṛtvasucpratyayastriśabdātsucpratyayaśca |
utkhātānyunmūlitāni kṣitipālānāṃ rājñāṃ vaṃśā evānvayā eva vaṃśā veṇavaḥ |
śliṣṭarūpakam |
teṣāṃ gahanāni vanāni yāsāṃ tāstathoktāḥ |
vaṃśagahanapadena ``na svānyeva kulāni kevalamidaṃ sarvaṃ daheyurvanam'' iti nyāyena rājñāṃ duṣṭatvaṃ sūcitam |
vidheyaviśeṣaṇam |
kṛtvā vidyamāna iti śeṣaḥ |
viśrutaḥ prasiddhaḥ kārtikeyasya kṛttikānāmapatyasya skandasya |
``strībhyo ḍhak'' |
vijayo yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
kiṃca bāhvorbalācchlāghyo ya eṣa paraśurāmaḥ |
aśvamedhe kāśyapāya munaye sadvīpāṃ mahīmaṣṭādaśadvīpayuktāṃ bhūmimatha dattvā kārtsnyena dattvā |
``maṅgalānantarārambhapraśnakārtsnyeṣvatho atha'' ityamaraḥ |
śastreṇa paraśunā vyastenotsāritena samudreṇa dattamanumataṃ viṣayaṃ nivāsadeśaṃ labdhvā prāpya tapastapyate tapaścarati |
pākaṃ pacatītivat |
``tapa ajaśvarye vā'' iti śyantaṅau |
asya mahājñānanidherityādipūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||19||

 sattveti |
sattvabhraṃśena darśanamātreṇa balahānyā viṣādibhirduḥkhitaistrastairudvignairdṛṣṭivighātena  tejotiśayaprayuktacakṣuḥprasarapratighātena jihmitāni kuṭilitāni mukhāni yeṣāṃ te tathoktairvetribhiḥ kanyāntaḥpurādhikṛtairvetradhāribhiḥ puruṣaiḥ kathamapyatiprayāsena yeṣāṃ te tathoktairvetribhiḥ kanyāntaḥpurādhikṛtairvedhāribhiḥ puruṣaiḥ kathamapyatiprayāsena kṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭo'vyāhato'kuṇṭhitaḥ prakramaḥ prārambho yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
tadeva paramutkṛṣṭamidaṃ yasya tatparaḥ |
rāmānveṣaṇe tatparo rāmānveṣaṇatatparaḥ |
``saptamī'' iti yogavibhāgātsamāsaḥ |
ata eva purajanairmithilānagarasthitajanairunmukta uccairmukto hāhāravo yasminsa tathoktaḥ |
kruddho bhārgavamuniḥkanyāntaḥpurameva praviśati |
hā śabdo viṣāde |
``hā viṣādaśugartiṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
atra bindurnāmārthaprakṛtiruktaḥ |
yathoktam- avāntarārthavicchede binduracchedakāraṇam'' iti||20||

 nanvityavadhāraṇe |
eta eva etādṛśā eva śiṣṭācārapaddhateḥ śiṣṭācāraparipāṭyāḥ praṇetāraḥ kalpasūtrakārāḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
tattasmādayaṃ paraśurāmo vidvāndharmādharmaviśeṣajñaḥ sanpramādyatyanavadhānaṃ prāpnoti |
dharmavismaraṇena mahyaṃ nāpakuryādityarthaḥ |
upasarpāmi samīpaṃ gacchāmi |
viṣayaṃ yathā tathā/ |
jāmātṛketi |
anukampanīyo jāmātā jāmātṛkastasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
``anukampāyām'' iti kan |
paridevanena rodanaśabdena mukharāḥ śabdāyamānāḥ, kātarā bhītāḥ, udvignā atiśayena kampitāḥ |
``āvijī bhayacīnayoḥ ityasmāt ``gatyarthākarmaka-'' ityādinā kartari ktaḥ |
``āditaśca'' iti niṣṭhātakārasya nakāraḥ |
sama,satāḥ parijanā yasya sa tathoktaṃ rājakulaṃ rājavṛndaṃ palāyitamapasarpitam |
``aya paya gatau'' ityasmātkartari ktaḥ |
``upasargasyāyatau'' iti parāśabdarephasya lakāraḥ |
bhartṛdārike rājaputrī |
``rājā bhaṭṭārako devastatsutā bhartṛdārikā'' ityamaraḥ |
tena rājakulasyāpi palāyanena |
prākṛta ātmanepadābhāvāt ``tuvaranti ā'' iti parasmaipadam |
saṃbhāvayema |
paraśurāmasamīpagamanapratiṣedhena vardhayām ityarthaḥ |
vidhiliṅgyuttamapuruṣabahuvacanam |
tvarayā vegena viśṛṅkhalāṃ dhāvantīṃ marālavadhūmiva haṃsīmivodbhrāntagamanāmanavasthitagamanam |
``bhramu anavasthāne''/ |
prema snehaḥ |
anukampādayā |
kātarā bhīruḥ |
paryavasthāpayitavyā nirbhayā kartavyā/ |
trailokyamaṅgalaṃ trailokyamaṅgalakaram |
īṣadvibhrameṇa mugdhasvabhāvātsvalpena vilāsenopalakṣitam |
visrastasya netrakuvalayasya |
prākṛte kuvalayaśabdasya kandoṭhṭhādeśaḥ |
śobhayā viharatā vilasatā mukhapuṇḍarīkeṇa vistāritau prakāśitau snehasaṃbhramau yayā sā tathoktā/ |
svasthā satī sve svabhāve sthitā sati |
yathā punarnirbhayā satītyarthaḥ |
ātaṅketi |
madhūkasya guḍapuṣpavṛkṣasya |
``madhūke tu guḍapuṣpamadhudrumau ityamaraḥ |
puṣpāṇāṃ ruciriva kāntiriva ruciḥ kāntiryeṣāṃ tānti tathoktāni taiḥ |
lāvaṇyena kāntiviśeṣeṇa sārairvaraiḥ |
``sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca nyāyye klībaṃ vare triṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
lāvaṇyasvarūpaṃ tu- ``bhūyiṣṭhaṃ teja evādbhirbahulābhirmṛdūkṛtam |
cakṣurānandajananaṃ lāvaṇyamiti kathyate||'' inyanusaṃdheyam |
aṅgaiḥ kartṛbhiḥ |
ayamīdṛśo vācāmagocara ātaṅkaḥ saṃjvaraḥ |
``ātaṅkaḥ saṃjvare roge sādhvase murajadhvanau iti ratnamālā |
śrama āyāsaḥ sādhvasaṃ bhayameteṣāṃ vyatikareṇa melanenotkampo vepathuḥ kathaṃ sahyatām |
ebhiraṅgairayamutkampo duḥsaha ityarthaḥ |
kiṃca he priye, unnaddhābhyāmuddhābhyāṃ kuḍmalāviva stanayugmaṃ  stayugmakuḍmalau tābhyāṃ gurubhirdīrghaiḥ śvāsaiścāvabhugnasya kuṭilitasya |
dhiā valyastrivalyaḥ |
śākapārthivādivaduttarapadalopaḥ |
``valirvalī cāpyudararekhācāmaradaṇḍayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
alpāstaraṅgāstaraṅgakāḥ |
trivalya eva taraṅgakāstrivalītaraṅgakāstāñjaṣate bhajatīti trivalītaraṅgajuṭ tasya te madhyasya bhaṅgo mā bhūnna bhavet |
ato nivartasveti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||21||

 pariṣkandāḥ paricārakāḥ |
``niyojyakiṃkarapraiṣyabhujiṣyaparicārakāḥ |
parācitapariṣkandaparājātaparaidhitāḥ ityamaraḥ |
``paraśca'' iti vaikalpikaṃ ṣatvam/ |
sa eva vyāharati paraśurāma eva vadatītyarthaḥ/ |
tasya paraśurāmasyānarālenākuṭilena sāhasena śīghraprayatnena pracaṇḍakarmaṇaḥ krūrakriyasya puṣpalāvartakasya mahāmeghaviśeṣasya stanitavadgarjitavanmāṃsalā sphīto vāṅnirghoṣo varṇābhivyañjakadhvaniḥ karṇavivaraṃ karṇarandhramāpyāyayati tarpayati ``pyāyī vṛddhau'' ityasmāt ``hetumati ca'' iti ṇic/ |
dhanuṣi dhārayantī |
cāpe rāmamavalambamānā satītyarthaḥ/ |
rasāntareṇa snehātiśayena |
bāhulakāllajjadhātorāluc jitaṃ snehena |
lajjāpekṣayā sneho balavānityarthaḥ |
tarhi tvayā dhanuṣo dṛḍhadhāraṇe sati dhanurmuktvā gacchāmītyanvayaḥ |
bho bho ityādi vyākhyātam/ |
hanteti arṣe'nukampāyāṃ ca |
``hanta harṣe'nukampāyām'' ityamaraḥ |
utsiktasyeti |
utsiktasya vīryeṇa garvottarasya tapasaḥ kāyaśoṣaṇādeḥ parākramasya balasya ca nidherasya paraśurāmasyāgamādāgamanāddhetoḥ satsaṅgapriyatā ca paraśurāmarūpasatpuruṣasaṃsargeṇa priyatā ca vīrarase yo rabhaso harṣaḥ |
``rabhaso vegaharṣayoḥ'' iti kośaḥ |
tena ya unmādaścittavibhramaprāyo daśāviśeṣaḥ sa ca māmekata ekasminpārśve karṣata ākarṣataḥ |
dvivacanāntamidam |
ānandyānandahetuḥ |
ānandayaterṇijantāṇṇiniḥ |
haricandanavadinduvacca śiśiraḥ śītalaḥ snigdho masṛṇa eṣa vaidehīparirambhaścaitatsītāliṅganaṃ ca muhuścaitanyaṃ jñānamāmīlayansaṃmohayansan |
anyato'nyasminpārśve ruṇaddhi |
nivṛttagamanaṃ karotītyarthaḥ |
``rudhirāvaṇe'' ityasmāt ``rudhādibhyaḥ śnam''||22||

 hā iti duḥkhe |
eṣaḥ bhārgava ityarthaḥ |
dinakarālokaḥ sūryatejaḥ |
prabhāparikṣepaḥ prabhāpariveṣaḥ |
``dvayoḥ kuṭhāraḥ paraśuḥ ityamaroktyā paraściti na napuṃsakaliṅgam |
jaṭāprabhābhirhetubhirḍāmaraḥ |
ḍāmare vipīlave kuśalaḥ ``kṛtalabdhakrītakuśalaḥ'' iti kuśale'ṇpratyayaḥ |
bhayaṃkara ityarthaḥ |
sudūravikṣepārthamāviddhayordīrghaprasāritayorvikaṭorudaṇḍayorviṣamorukāṇḍayornirbharābhighātena dṛḍhābhighātena vihvalitavasuṃdharaḥ prakampitabhūmiḥ parāgata eva prāpta eva sakalakṣatriyāṇāṃ mahārākṣaso mahāhiṃsakaḥ/ |
ayamiti |
ayaṃ purovartī vyaktiviśeṣaḥ |
tribhuvane lokatraya ekeṣu mukhyeṣu vīrayate vikramaṃ karotīti tribhuvanaikavīraḥ |
``vīra vikrāntau'' iti paṭhitādāgarvīyādvīrayateḥ kartari pacādyacpratyayaḥ |
eko vīra iti vigrahe tu vīraika iti syāt |
sa bhṛgunandano muniḥ |
prasiddho bhṛguputro munirityarthaḥ |
ya eṣa yoāyaṃ bhārgavo munirmahānaparicchinno durāsadaḥ samīpagamanānarhaḥ |
tejasāṃ nivaha iva samūha iva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kiṃca mūrtimānvapuṣmānsphuranprakāśamānaḥ pratāpasya śatrutāpanaśaktestapasaḥ kāyaśoṣaṇādiniyamasya ca vyatikara iva militāvasthānamiva bhavati |
kiṃca piṇḍatāṃ saṃghātabhāvamupagataḥ prāptaḥ pracaṇḍo'tyantaṃ tīkṣṇo rasaḥ |
sāmājikaiścarvyamāṇatayā sūśākaṭanyāyena bhāvinīṃ vṛttimāśritya rasaśabdavācyaḥ vīra iva śatrukṛtāparādhādinā manaḥprajvalanātmakakrodha iva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
saṃnidhimātreṇa viredhanirasane prathamopamā |
saṃbandhaviśeṣeṇa virodhinirasane dvitīyā |
kāyikavyāpāreṇa virodhinīrasane tṛtīyeti vivekaḥ||23||

 puṇya iti |
ya eṣa iti pūrvasmādanuṣaṅgaḥ |
vratānāṃ tapasāṃ ni3ī rakṣaṇasthānaviśeṣaḥ |
amiteṣvananteṣu kāryeṣu śaktiḥ sāmarthyaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
yadvā amitāḥ śaktayo yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
puṇyo'pi dharmiṣṭho'pi |
``dharmāmbupuṣpasvaṇarṇeṣu puṇyaṃ dharmavati triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
bhīmakarmā bhayaṃkaravyāpāro eṣa bhṛgunandano'bhirāmaghorāṃ priyaṃkarāṃ bhayaṃkarāṃ ca |
khañjakubjādivadviśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ mūrtiṃ śarīram |
ānupūrvīmiti yāvat |
bibhraddhārayan |
atharvaṇā prokto vedo'tharvātamadhīta ātharvaṇikaḥ |
vasantāditvāṭṭhak |
dāṇḍināyanādisūtre nipātanāṭṭhilopābhāvaḥ |
ātharvaṇikasyāmnāya ārvaṇaḥ |
``caraṇāddharmāmnāyayoḥ'' iti prakaraṇe ``ātharvaṇikasyakalopaśca'' ityanenāṇpratyayaḥ ikalopaśca |
āmnāyastu pratyayasya vācyakoṭau na niviśata ato nātharvaṇo veda ityādau paunaruktyam |
nigama ivāmnāya iva cakāsti prakāśate |
``cakāsṛ dīptau'' ityasmāllaṭ |
upameye praṇatānugrahātpuṇyatvaṃ pratyarthinigrahādbhīmakarmatvam |
upamāne svarato varṇataścābhirāmatvaṃ vācyenābhicāravidhinā ghoratvamiti vibhāgaḥ||2cha/ |
ayaṃ hīti |
ayaṃ bhārgavaḥ |
asya cottarānvayaḥ kalpeti |
kalpasya brahmadinopahitasya prapañcasya |
``kalpastu pralaye nyāye śāstre brahmadine vidhau'' iti ratnamālā |
apāye nāśane praṇayi prītimatkālarudrānalatvaṃ sarvamṛtyubhūtarudratmakatvāgnibhāvaṃ vidadhatkurvāṇaḥ |
``nābhyastācchutaḥ'' iti numniṣedhaḥ |
tigmastīkṣṇaḥ nikhilabhuvanastomasya sakalajagatsamūhasya nirmāthe dhvaṃsane yogyo'nukūlaḥ kṛtasamutthānaḥ san |
``paramātmatapo vede brahma brahmābjaje dvije'' iti ratnamālā |
rāśībhūtasaṃghātībhūtaḥ |
śakteramūrtatvena rāśitvāyogādabhūtatadbhāve cvipratyayaḥ |
devadevasya tripurapīḍitairindrādibhiḥ stutasya |
ata eva saṃrabdhasya sakrodhodyamavataḥ |
yāṇāṃ purāṇāṃ samāhāstripuram |
pātrāditvāstrītvābhāvaḥ |
tasya jayino jetū rudrasya |
``ji abhibhave'' iti pāṭhitājjayateḥ ``jidṛkṣiviśyartīṇvamāvyadhābhyamaparibhūprasubhyaśca'' iti kartarīnipratyayaḥ |
sāmarthyānāṃ saṃhāśaktīnāṃ sāra ivotkarṣa iva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
utprekṣālaṃkāraḥ |
brahmacchadmetyatra tvapahnapaviśeṣo'laṃkāraḥ||25||

gapraˇˇsvācchandya svābhiprāyeṇa vartamānasya |
``abhiprāyaśchanda āśayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
sva ātmīyaśchando'bhiprāyo na tu lokaśāstravyapekṣā yasya sa svacchandastasya karmetyarthe brāhmaṇāderākṛtigaṇatvātsvacchandaśabdādbhāve ṣyañ |
tasya vaicitryaṃ vaividhyaṃ vismanīyamityarthaḥ |
jyotiriti |
abhavata ityanuṣajyate |
atrabhavataḥ paraśurāmasya kaṇṭhe jyotiṣāṃ tejasāṃ jvālānāṃ nirgatāṃśānāṃ pracayatena samūhena jaṭilo jaṭāvāniti |
picchāderākṛtigaṇatvājjaṭāśabdānmatvarthīya ilac |
kuṭhāraḥ paraśurbhāti |
kiṃca aṃsebhujaśikhare tūṇīra iṣudhiḥ |
bhātītyasyānuṣaṅgo bahuvacanāntatayā vipariṇāmaśca |
tena bhāntīti labdham |
vapuṣīti sāmānyoktāvapi sāmarthyājjaṭāyāḥ śirasi cāpasya vāmapāṇau cīrasya kaṭyāmaniyasyorasi ca bhānamanusaṃdheyam |
kiṃca valīyabhūtaṃ lolamakṣasūtraṃ rudrākṣaprotasūtraṃ yasminsa tathoktastasmin |
pāṇau dakṣiṇakare bāṇaḥ sphurati prakāśate |
yata evaṃ tata ugraśānto bhayaṃkaraḥ priyakaraśca |
khañjakubjādivadviśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ |
pūrvoktakuṭhārādibhirniṣpāditaḥ kutrima ākāro vyatikaravatīṃ vīrarasaśāntirasayormelanavatīṃ śobhaoṃ cārutāṃ tanoti |
vistārayatītyarthaḥ |
atra kuṭhāratūṇīracāpabāṇairugratvaṃ jaṭācīrānākṣasūtraiḥ śāntatvamiti vivekaḥ||26||

 eta iti pūjāyāṃ bahuvacanam |
guravaḥ |
vasiṣṭhasanāābhitvācchvaśurakulasamādhinā gurutvoktiḥ |
aavaguṇṭhanamācchādanam/ |
parāgata eva |
bhārgavaḥ samīpaṃ gataḥ evetyarthaḥ |
āryaḥ śvaśurastasya putro bharteti bhartaurvyapadeśo'yamāryaputra iti |
pariāṇamiha paraśurāmasamīpagamanābhāvarūpaṃ vivakṣitam/ |
muniriti |
ayaṃ paraśurāmo muniḥ paramātmamananaśīlo bhavati |
atheti guṇāntaraprastāve |
ayaṃ paraśurāmastādṛśo vīro lokottaro vīro bhavati |
iti yattanmunitvamahāāāvītvamelanaṃ me priyam |
yata evaṃ tato he kātare'dhīre parikampo vepathurviramatu naśyatu |
``vyāṅparibhyo ramaḥ'' iti parasmaipadam |
kiṃca kātare, tvaṃ kṣattriyāsi |
kṣattriyajātīyāsītyarthaḥ |
ato'pi parikampo na yukta iti bhāvaḥ |
``āryakṣatriyābhyāṃ vā svārthe'' iti vaikalpikaṅīṣānugāgamayorabhāve kṣatriyaśabdādyopadhāṭṭāp |
tatpriyaṃ me ityuktaṃ vivṛṇoti - tapasītyādinā |
rāghavakṣatriyo'haṃ raghuvaṃśajaḥ kaṇḍūlau nirgharṣaṇasāpekṣakṣatvarūpakaṇḍūlapracurau |
sidhmādau ``kṣudrajantūpatāpācca'' iti kaṇḍūśabdāllac |
doṣau bhujau yasya sa tathoktasyābhavataḥ paraśurāmasya paricaraṇe śuśrūṣaṇe samarthaḥ |
śakto bhavāmītyarthaḥ |
atra tapasītyādi viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ paricaraṇakarmakārake paraśurāme prayuktam |
rāghavakṣattriya iti paribaraṇakartṛkārake svasminviśeṣaṇadvayaṃ prayuktam |
asyāyamāśayaḥ |
laṅghaternalopaśca ``bālamūlalaghvaṅgulīnāṃ vā lo ratvamāpadyate'' iti sūtravārtikābhyāṃ ``laghi gatau'' ityasmādraghuśabdo nipātitaḥ |
``gatyarthā buddhyarthāḥ'' iti gatyarthānāṃ buddhyarthatvāllaṅghate jānāti |
sarvamiti raghuriti jñānādhikatvaṃ raghupadapravṛttinimittaṃ bhavati |
uktaṃ ca kālidāsena- ``śrutasya yāyādayamantamarbhakaḥ'' iti |
tathā ca mama jñānādhikaraghuvaṃśajanmānuguṇaṃ taponiṣṭhamahanīyaviṣaye paricaraṇasāmarthyam |
kṣattriyatvānuguṇaṃ tudarpakaṇḍūladoṣkatadviṣayaparitoṣaṇasamarthaśca bhavāmītyarthaḥ |
``kugatiprādayaḥ'' ityatra prādigrahaṇenaiva siddhe kugrahaṇaṃ pradisamāsasya kvācitkatāṃ jñāpayatīti vaiyākaroṇoktyā parītyasya na samāsaḥ |
``paryabhibhyāṃ ca'' ityatra vāgrahaṇānuvṛttestasilabhāvaśca |
paricaraṇasamayaṃ iti padayaṃ bodhyam |
śleṣo'laṃkāraḥ||27||

 durātmano durbuddheḥ kṣattriyabaṭoḥ kṣattriyabālakasyānātmajñatā svasvabhāvajñānaśūnyatā |
vismayanīyetyarthaḥ |
na trastamiti |
dhanuścāpaṃ vyārujatā bhagnaṃ kurvatā |
``rujo bhaṅge'' iti taudādikādārujaterlaṭaḥ śatari tudādiyaḥ śasya ṅittvādguṇābhāvaḥ |
tena kṣattriyabaṭunā rāmeṇa kartrā bhagavataḥ ṣāḍguṇyapūrṇāddevātsarvārādhyādbhūteṣu karuṇāyāḥ kṛpāyāḥ saṃtānenanāvicchinnapravāhena |
``saṃtānaṃ syuravacchittivaṃśāpatyasuradrumāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
śāntaḥ krodhādirahita ātmā hṛdayaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasmādbhavānīpateḥ pārvatīpateḥ |
bhavasya patnī bhavānī |
``indravaruṇabhavaśarvarudramṛṅahimāraṇyayavayavanamātulācāryāṇāmānuk'' iti ṅīṣānugāgamaśca |
tasyāḥ patiḥ śivastasmāt |
``bhītrārthānāṃ bhayahetuḥ'' iti pañcamī |
na trastaṃ nāma yadi trāsābhāvaḥ saṃbhāvyate yadi |
``gatyarthākarmaka-'' ityādinā trasyaterbhāve ktaḥ |
``nāma prākāśyasaṃbhāvyakrodhopagamakutsane'' ityamaraḥ |
paraduḥkhaduḥkhitvarūpadayāvatā paraduḥkhānutpādāttadadhīnānarthaśaṅkāvirahaḥ saṃbhāvito'pi yadītyarthaḥ |
tenetyanuṣajyate |
tena rāmeṇa tatputrastasya bhavānīpateḥ putro madena garveṇāndhasya kṛtyākṛtyavivekavirahitasya tārakasya tārakāsurasya vadhāddhetorviśvasya samastasya prāṇino dattotsavaḥ kṛtānandanajanakavyāpāraḥ |
dadātītya karotītyartho lākṣaṇika iti viśvaśabdānna caturthī |
skandastu pūrvoktakāruṇikabhavānīpatyapekṣayā senāpativṛttiprayuktatīkṣṇatvātiśayayuktaḥ skandaḥ |
``tu syādbhede'vadhāraṇe'' ityamaraḥ |
kathaṃ na śrutaḥ |
etādṛśaṃ skandaṃ śrutvā raudracāpabhaṅgādrāmaḥ kathaṃ na nivṛtta iti bhāvaḥ |
athavā skandādbhayābhāve skanda iva bhavānīpateḥ priya iṣṭastato'pi balādhikyāditi bhāvaḥ |
śiṣyo bhavānīpateradhītavidyo'haṃ skandādapi baliṣṭhaḥḥ kathaṃ na śrutaḥ |
skandādhikabalatvādahaṃ nāśaktaḥ |
kiṃca ``yathāśakti nigṛhṇīta devatāgurunindakān iti nyāyena gurutiraskāro mamāvaśyaṃ pariāhāryaḥ |
ataḥ kathaṃ māmanākalayannasmadguroścāpamarditavāniti bhāvaḥ||28||

 praśamasya krodhavirateḥ karkaśo'nartharūpaḥ pariṇāmaḥ phalaparyavasāyitā |
yaditi |
kṣattriyeṣvapyatadarheṣu kṣattriyeṣvādhipatyaṃ prabhutvaṃ punarmayā sarvakṣattriyavidhvaṃsanānantaramapi sthitaṃ tiṣṭhati |
kiṃca tairevātadarhaiḥ kṣattriyaireva saṃprati majjīvanakāle dhūnaṃṣi purnardhṛtāni |
kiṃca bhujamadena bhujagarveṇonmādyatāṃ vibhrāntacittānāṃ teṣāṃ kṣattriyāṇāmucchṛṅkhalānyunmaryādāni caritāni vṛttāni mayā punaḥ śrutānīti yadeṣa eva me praśamasya pariṇāma iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||29||

 akalileti |
kalilo doṣamiśro na bhavatītyakalilaḥ |
``salikalyanimahibhaḍibhaṇḍiśaṇḍipiṇḍitaṇḍikukībhūya ilac'' iti kalaterilacpratyaye sati kalilaśabdo niṣpannaḥ kalila iti mitro gahanaśceti vaiyākaraṇāḥ |
tapasaḥ kāyaśoṣaṇādestejaso māhātmyasya vīryasya balasya ca prathimā prabhutvaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasmin |
``pṛthvādibhya imanijvā iti bhāva imanic |
``raṛto halāderlaghoḥ'' iti ṛkārasya rephaśca |
yaśasaḥ pūrvokta guṇavattāprathāyā nidhāvakṣayasthāne'vitathena pūrvoktānāṃ tapaḥprabhṛtīnāṃ hetūnāṃ sadbhāvādvastubhūtena madena garveṇa dhmāte paripūrite munau paraśurāme roṣādabhidhāvati sari śīghramāgacchati sati pāṇirmama hasto'bhinavayādyaiva gṛhītayā |
pramoṣarahitayetyarthaḥ |
dhanurvidyayā dhanurviṣayakajñānātiśayena yo darpo garvastasya kṣamāyānuguṇāya karmaṇe ca yuddhāya ca pādayorupasaṃgrahaṇāyābhivādanena sparśanāya ca rabhasāddharṣādvegādvā sphurati pravartate atra tapastejobhyāṃ paraśurāmasya pādopasaṃgrahaṇaṃ vīryamadābhyāṃ yuddhaṃ ca yogyamiti bhāvaḥ |
pāṇiriti jātāvekavacanam||30||

 kiṃtviti |
ācārasya cikīrṣitasya yuddhapādopasaṃgrahaṇayoranyatarānuṣṭhānasyāviṣayo'navasaraḥ |
ayudhyamāne yuddhe doṣaḥ syāt |
yuyutsoḥ pādagrahaṇe'pi śīlanatvarūpo doṣaḥ syāt |
ato yathābhilaṣitamanuṣṭhānamadya na yujyata ityarthaḥ/ |
bho bho ityādi vyākhyātam/ |
ayamahaṃ bho ityādi spaṣṭo'rthaḥ/ |
anviṣyata iti |
jātyotpattyā viśuddhasattvaḥ pariśuddhahṛdayastvaṃ pramathanāya viśasanāyanviṣyato'pi mṛgayamāṇasyāpi mama mahyaṃ darpādvīryagarvādātmānaṃ svaṃ gandhadvipendrasya madagandhayuktagajajātiviśeṣaśreṣṭhasya kalabhaḥ potakaḥ |
``kalabhaḥ kariśāvakaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kariṇāṃ gajānāṃ kūṭānīva giriśṛṅgāṇīva |
``kūṭo'strī giriśṛṅge yantre rāśāvayodhane vyāje |
anṛte māyāyāmapi sīrāṅge niścale ca pāpe ca||'' iti ratnamālā |
kumbhāḥ śiraḥpiṇḍāḥ |
``kumbhau tu piṇḍau śiraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
teṣāṃ kuṭṭākaṃ chedanakaram |
``kuṭṭa cchedane'' ityasmāt ``jalpabhikṣakuṭṭaluṇṭavṛndaḥ ṣākan'' iti ṣākanpratyayaḥ |
pāṇireva kuliśaṃ yasya sa tathoktasya mṛgāreḥ siṃhasyātmānaṃ yasya svātmānamivārpayasi |
prāpayasītyarthaḥ |
``ṛ gatau'' ityasmāṇṇic |
``artihrīvlīrīkrūyīkṣmāyyātāṃ puṅ ṇau iti puk |
``pugantalaghūpadhasya ca'' iti guṇaḥ |
ataḥ satyamaikṣvāko'sīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||31||

 śāntaṃ pāpamiti |
paraśurāmaprayuktenāniṣṭavacanena rāmasya duritaṃ śāmyatāmityarthaḥ |
``āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacca'' iti niṣṭhā |
pratihatamamaṅgalam |
pūrvoktapāpahetukaṃ maṅgalaviparītaṃ pratihanyatāmityarthaḥ |
atrāpi pūrvavanniṣṭhā/ |
svagatamiti |
rahasyārthasya svayamanusaṃdhāne svagataśabdaḥ |
taduktam- ``sarvaśrāvyaṃ prakāśaṃ syādaśrāvyaṃ svagataṃ matam'' iti |
cañciditi |
``vañcucañcutañcutvañcumruñcumluñcumracumlucu gatyarthāḥ'' iti cañcatirgatyarthaḥ paṭhitaḥ |
pañcaśikhaṇḍāḥ śikhāmaṇḍanaṃ bhūṣaṇaṃ yasya tattathoktam |
``śikhaṇḍaḥ picchacūḍayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
mugdhaṃ navam |
bālyayuktamityarthaḥ |
pragalbhaṃ prabhāvataḥ prauḍham |
``mugdhaḥ saumye nave mūḍhe iti ratnamālā |
viśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ |
gambhīraṃ ca nipuṇairapyaparicchedyasvabhāvaṃ ca manoharaṃ ca ramaṇīyaṃ ca |
sahajāni svābhāvikāni śrīlāvaṇyalakṣmāṇi sāmudrikaśāstrapraasiddhāni mahāpuruṣalakṣaṇāni yasmiṃstattathoktaṃ rūpamākāram |
``rūpaṃ svabhāve śuklādau saundarye nāṭake'pi ca |
granthākṛtyoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
dadhadvahannasau śiśurārabdhayauvano rāmo drāgdṛṣṭo'pyadyaiva mayāvalokitaḥ sannapi saundaryasārasya saundaryaśreṣṭhasya śriyā samṛddhyā mama mana ātatāyino'pi me hṛdayaṃ harati svavaśaṃ nayati |
prītikaro bhavatītyarthaḥ |
tathāpyevaṃ prītikaratve'pyasau hantavyastu nāma mayā hantavya evetyabhyupagamyate |
``tu syādbhede'vadhāraṇe'' iti, ``nāmaprākāśyasaṃbhāvyakrodhopagamakutsane'' iti cāmaraḥ |
vīrāṇāṃ vratasya yuddhakartavyatāniyamasya krūratāṃ dhik |
krūratā nindyetyarthaḥ |
``dhiguparyādiṣu triṣu'' iti dvitīyā |
``dhiṅ nirbhartsananindayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ||32||

 prāgiti |
prākpūrvam |
prāptamanubhūtaṃ nisumbhaṃ prahāro yena tattathoktam |
nisumbhaśabdastāḍane rūḍhaḥ |
tathāvidhasya śāṃbhavadhanuṣo raudracāpasya |
śaṃbhuśabdāt ``tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
dvedhākriyayā dvidhākaraṇena |
khaṇḍadvayakaraṇeneti yāvat |
``edhācca'' iti dviśabdādedhācpratyayaḥ |
āvirbhavatādhikamutpādyamānena krodhena kopena preritasya bhīmasya bhayaṃkarasya bhārgavasya paraśurāmasya bhujastambhena stambhasadṛśabhujenāpaviddhaḥ kṣiptaḥ sa jvālo jvālāsahito'śithilo dṛḍhaḥ paraśuḥ kuṭhārastvatkaṇṭha eva pīṭhamāsanam |
``kaṇṭho vācakaśabde syātsaṃnidhāne gale dhvanau'' iti ratnamālā |
atithiḥ kṣaṇaṃ bhavatu |
atithirhyāsana upaviṣṭo bhavati tadvadayaṃ paraśustvakaṇṭhe kṣaṇaṃ tiṣṭhatvityarthaḥ |
yena pūrvoktenānena purovartinā paraśunā devo'pi jigīṣurharo rudro jagatsu lokeṣu khaṇḍaparaśuḥ khyāpyate khaṇḍaparaśunāmnā prasiddhaḥ kriyate |
``khyāprakathane'' ityasmāṇṇici pugāgame karmaṇi laṭi yak |
khaṇḍayatyahitāniti khaṇḍaḥ |
khaṇḍaḥ paraśuryasyeti vigrahaḥ ``bhūteśaḥ khaṇḍaparaśuḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
uttarayacchabdasya tacchabdanirapekṣatvāttacchabdo na prayuktaḥ |
yadvā sajvāla ityatra ``saḥ jvālaḥ'' iti padadvayam |
jvalatīti jvālaḥ |
``jvalatikasantebhyo ṇaḥ'' iti kartari ṇapratyayaḥ |
yena khyāpyate sa paraśurityādi yojayitavyam |
a ``jṛmbhitaṃ taddhanurdṛṣṭvā śaivaṃ viṣṇuparākramaiḥ'' iti śrīmati rāmayaṇe sthitam |
kālidāsenāpi ``viddhi cāttabalamojasā hareraiśvaraṃ dhanurabhañji yattvayā khātabhūtamanilo nadīrayaiḥ pātayatyapi mṛdustadrumam'' ityuktam |
bhojarājenāpi ``jīrṇacāpāttakīrteḥ'' ityuktam |
prakṛte tu ``prāgaprāptanisumbhaśāṃbhavadhanuḥ'' ityuktam |
anayorvirodhastvitthaṃ śamayitavyaḥ |
viṣṇoranyena kenāpyabhagnapūrvamidaṃ dhanurbhañjitavatastava kaṇṭhe kuṭhāro'yaṃ patatviti |
athavā prāgaprāptanisumbheti saṃbuddhyantaṃ rāmaviśeṣaṇam |
pūrvamananubhūtaprahāra he rāma, śāṃbhavaṃ dhanurityādi yojayitavyam||33||

 eṣa paraśurāmaḥ |
krodheneti śeṣaḥ/ |
ayamiti |
yaḥ saparivārasya sasainyasya kārtikeyasya skandasya vijayenābhibhavena hetunāvarjitena vaśīkṛtahṛdayena nīlalohitenārdhanārīśvareṇa |
nīlatvaṃ nāryaṃśe, lohitatvaṃ svāṃśe |
sahasrasya parivatsarāṇāṃ samāhāraḥ sahasraparivatsaram |
pātrāditvātklībatvam |
sahasraparivatsaramantevāsīti vigrahaḥ atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
supsupeti samāsaḥ |
ante vasatītyantevāsī |
``supyajātau ṇinistācchīlye'' iti ṇiniḥ |
``śayavāsavāsiṣvakālāt'' iti saptamyā aluk |
``chāāntevāsinau śiṣye'' ityamaraḥ |
prasādīkṛtaḥ |
prasādaśabdo'nugrahavācako lakṣaṇayā tatpūrvake deye vartate |
tasmādabhūtatadbhāve cviḥ, kṛño'nuprayogaḥ |
ayaṃ kilāyaṃ sa iti saṃbhāvyate |
``vārtāsaṃbhāvyayoḥ kila'' ityamaraḥ/ |
hṛdayanirvartitabahumāno manasā bahu mānayan |
niṣkampaścāsau dhīraścāsau gurukaśceti vigrahe khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ |
tasya bhāvastattvaṃ tenāpahasati |
prayāsānurūpaphalābhāvādapahāsaḥ |
bhartṛdārako rājaputraḥ/ |
vismayo rāmadhairyādinā |
asramevamapahāse kiṃ bhaviṣyatīti duḥkhena |
anya evāyaṃ prakāraḥ |
kārtavīryaskandaherambādiṣu dṛṣṭādvilakṣaṇa ityarthaḥ |
kimapītyādi |
asaṃvijñātanibandhanaṃ durnirūpākārakametanmāhātmyaṃ mahāpuruṣatvaṃ ca saujanyaṃ ca guṇagrāhatviādisujanadharmaśca kimapi |
anirvācyamityarthaḥ |
kiṃcotsāhena stheyasā prayatnena saṃrambheṇa krodhena ca gambhīro durjñeyaḥ pauruṣasya puṃstvasyāvaṣṭambhaḥ sthairyam |
``āvāñcālambanāvidūryayoḥ'' iti stambheḥ ṣatvam |
āṃ dāśarathe |
āmevamityarthaḥ |
``āmetvam'' ityamaraḥ |
ācāryapādānām |
``uttamānāṃ svarūpaṃ tu pādaśabdena paṭhyate'' |
asmadguroḥ |
śivasyetyarthaḥ/ |
asannaroṣasya praśāntakopasya/ |
astreti |
astraprayogasya khuralyā lakṣyaprabandhanasya |
``khuralī lakṣyabandhanam'' iti keśavaḥ |
kalahe'nyonyasaṃgharṣe |
gaṇānāṃ pramāthānām |
sainyabhede samūhe ca saṃkhyāyāṃ pramathe gaṇaḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
sainyaiḥ senāsamavetairvṛto'pi kumāra eva skanda eva |
``kumāro nā sute skande yuvarāje śvapācake'' iti ratnamālā |
mayā jitaḥ kumāra evetyanena na tvanyaḥ prabala ityaparitoṣo vyajyate |
priyaguṇo guṇapakṣapātī bhagavānṣāḍguṇyapūrṇo me guruḥ śiva etāvatāpyatyalpenāpyanena skandavijayarūpeṇa nimittena kṛtaprasādaḥ kṛtānugrahaḥ sanparirabhyāśliṣyemaṃ paraśuṃ prādāddattavān |
dadāterluṅ |
``gātisthāghupābhūbhyaḥ sicaḥ parasmaipadeṣu'' iti sico luk||34||

 kathamityādi |
skandavijayasya kathamatyalpatāmāhetyarthaḥ |
garvagauravasya garvādhikyasyābhogo vaipulyaṃ saṃniveśo vā |
ata etasmādrudreṇa paraśoḥ pradānādvīravādo vīra iti vādaḥ śabdo dyāvāpṛthivyordivi bhuvi ca |
dyaiśca pṛthivī ceti vigrahe dvandve ``divasaśca pṛthivyām'' iti cakārāddivo dyāvādeśaḥ vitataḥ |
vistṛta ityarthaḥ |
yenetyādi |
yena paraśunā bhagavānguruḥ pracaṇḍastīkṣṇaścaṇḍyāḥ pārvatyāḥ patistribhuvaneṣu tryavayavakeṣu bhuvaneṣu |
śākapārthivāderākṛtigaṇatvāduttarapadalopaḥ |
yadvā samāhārabahutvādbahuvacanam |
khaṇḍaparaśuḥ prarūḍhaḥ khaṇḍaparaśuriti prasiddhastārakaripoḥ skandasya vijayārjitena vijayasaṃpāditena tenaiva paraśunaiva te paraśurāma iti śrutiḥ paraśurāma ityākhyāṃ dīptiṃ gatā |
prasiddhiṃ gatetyarthaḥ||35||

 utpittiriti |
bhagavataḥ pūjyasya tava jamadagnito jamadagneḥ sakāśādutpattirjanma |
kiṃca kṣatrabrahmaṇaḥ kṣayivedasya |
dhanurvedasyeti yāvat |
tapasaśca nidherādhārabhūtasya |
bhagavataḥ pūjyasya tava sa prasiddho bhagavānṣāḍguṇyapūrṇo devo dīptiśīlaḥ pinākī śivo gururdhanurvidyopadeṣṭā |
kiṃca yattava śauryaṃ tadgirāṃ pathi na bhavati |
vācāmagocaraṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
tacchauryasya vācāmagocaratvaṃ karmabhiḥ skandavijayādivyāparairvyaktaṃ hi prasiddhaṃ hi nanvityavadhāraṇe |
kiṃca |
bhagavatastyāgaḥ svadravyāṇāṃ vīrebhyaḥ pratipādanam |
saptānāṃ samudrāṇāṃ samāhāraḥ saptasamudram |
pātrāditvātkalībatvam |
tena mudritāyā āvṛtāyā mahyā bhūmernirvyājaṃ khyātyādinirapekṣaṃ dāṃ kāśyapāya pratipādanamavadhiḥ sīmā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``avadhāne paricchede sīmni kāle bale'vadhiḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
yata evaṃ kṣatrabrahmataponidherbhagavatastava kiṃ vā pūrvokteṣvabhijanavidyāvṛtteṣu kiṃ vā lokottaraṃ na bhavati |
sarvameva lokaśreṣṭhaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
kimiti sāmānye napuṃsakam |
utpattirityabhijana uktaḥ |
bhagavāniti vidyoktā |
śauryamiti tyāga iti cābhijanavidyānuguṇamubhayavidhaṃ vṛttamuktamiti draṣṭavyam||36||

 ramaṇīyaṃ hṛdyaṃ yathā tathā mantrayitum |
``mantri guptaparibhāṣaṇe iti dhātupāṭhaḥ |
rāmā rāmeti |
rāma rāma |
saṃbuddhau dviruktiḥ |
āśayasya gurudvijabhaktyādirūpasya manovyāpārasya sadṛśīmanurūpāṃ nayanābhirāmatāṃ jñānaśaktyādyātmaguṇānāṃ ca rāmaṇīyakaṃ ramaṇīyatvaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
ramaṇīyaśabdāt ``yopadhādgurūpottamādvañ'''' iti bhāve vuñpratyayaḥ |
sarvathā rūpaguṇādibhiḥ sarvaiḥ prakārairme hṛdayaṃgamo'si |
śatrorapi mama hṛdyo'sītyarthaḥ |
khacprakaraṇe ``gameḥ supi vācyaḥ'' iti vārtikena hṛdayāśabdopapade gameḥ khacpratyaye hṛdayaśabdasya mumāgame ca sati hṛdayaṃgamaśabdo niṣpannaḥ||37||

 herambeti |
rāma rāmeti pūrvasmagādanuṣajyate |
herambasya vighneśvarasya dantamusalena musalasadṛśadantenollikhitaikabhittirekapārśvo yasya tattathoktam |
bhittiśabdo'tra bhittisadṛśapārśvavācī |
``ekabhakti'' iti pāṭha ullikhitaikabhāgamityarthaḥ |
``bhaktiḥ syādbhāgasevayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
herambasyaikadantatvādekabhāgasyollikhitatvam |
viśākhasya skandasya |
``viśākhaḥ śikhivāhanaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
viśikhānāṃ bāṇānāṃ vraṇairlāñchitaṃ cihnitaṃ me vakṣaḥ kartṛ |
adbhutasyāścaryakarasya vīrasya lābhātprāptyā romāñcaiḥ pulakaiḥ kañcukitaṃ saṃjātakañcukaṃ sa tvāṃ paribdhumicchatyāliṅgitumicchati |
satyaṃ bravīmi yathārtha vadāmi |
ivaśabdo vākyālaṃkāre||38||

 subhagasya bhāvaḥ saubhāgyam |
subhaśabdādbhāve ṣyañ |
``hṛdbhagasindhvante pūrvapadasya ca'' iti pūrvottarapadavṛddhiḥ |
śatruṇāpi hi ślāghyata iti bhāvaḥ |
mugdhātvena lajjātiśayātparāṅmukhī satyātmānaṃ svaṇīdṛśasaubhāgyānubhavavimukhaṃ karoṣītyarthaḥ |
pariśabdo varjanārthakaḥ |
karmapravacanīyānāṃ samāsaniṣedhastvanityaḥ |
yadvā sarvatobhāve pariśabdaḥ |
yato yato bharturmukhaṃ tatastato'bhimukhamukhī satyapītyarthaḥ/ |
sāsramiti |
etāvantaṃ kālaṃ maugdhyena hatāsmi |
itaḥ paraṃ paraśurāmeṇeti duḥkhādbāṣpodayaḥ |
prastutasya prakṛtasya pramathanasya pratīpaṃ pratikūlam/ |
paraśurāmoktaparirambhasyātyantāpratiṣedhādvinayaḥ |
prastutapratīpatvoktyātyantānabhyupagamāttasya dhīratvaṃ masṛṇatvaṃ kiṃcidasphuṭatvaṃ ca |
aśaktasya ca vinayo'nuguṇa iti tasya māhātmyācchobhitoktiriti draṣṭavyam |
asya rājanyapotasya rājanyabālakasya rāmasya |
potaḥ pāko'rbhako ḍimbhaḥ pṛthukaḥ śāvakaḥ śiśuḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
antaḥkaraṇaṃ manaḥ parasya guṇānāṃ śauryādīnāmutkarṣātiśayasya tattve'pi yāthārthyaviṣaye'pi parimāṇaṃ parākāṣṭhāṃ grāhayatīti parimāṇagrāhi |
ṇijantāgṛhṇāteḥ ``supyajātau ṇinistācchīlye'' iti ṇiniḥ |
tena saujanyena sujanabhāvena pūtaṃ nirdoṣam |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
yadvā pareṣāṃ sarvātiśāyināṃ guṇānāmutkarṣasyātiśayasya tattve'pi vastubhūtattve'pi parimāṇagrāhiṇā kāṣṭhāṃ prāptena |
tatra kevalādgṛhṇāterṇiniḥ |
saujanyetyādeḥ pūrvavadarthaḥ |
vinayāderākṛtigaṇatvātsvārthe ṭhak |
yadvā paramārthāya prabhavati pāramārthikaḥ |
arthakriyākārītyarthaḥ |
``tasmai prabhavati saṃtāpādibhyaḥ'' iti ṭhak |
anāropita ityarthaḥ |
tathāvidhena vinayenānauddhatyena durvibhāvyo'bhibhūto mandamatimabhirdaurjñeyo nipuṇabuddhibhirduravagāhārthavagāḍhanapaṭumatibhiḥ puruṣairgrāhyo jñeyo mahānvipulo'haṃkārasya śauryātiśāyidinibandhanacittasamunnatergranthiḥ parvaḥ |
``granthiḥ parvagadāntare iti ratnamālā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
aprākṛteti |
aprākṛtasya prākṛtajanasvabhāvavijātīyasya |
``prākatastu pṛthagjanaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
caritātiśayasya vṛttotkarṣasyādbhutairatyāścaryakarairbhāvaiḥ svabhāvaiḥ |
``bhāvaḥ padārthe sattāyāṃ kriyāceṣṭātmayoniṣu |
vidvallīlāsvabhāveṣu bhūtyabhiprāyajantuṣu||'' iti ratnamālā |
hṛtasya vaśīkṛtasya mama tathāpi vaśīkṛtatve'pyanāsthā |
etāvantaṃ kālamīdṛśāhaṃkāragranthigrahaṇe tātparyaṃ nāsīt |
adya tu tadgrahaṇaṃ samyagjātamiti bhāvaḥ |
tadvahaṇamabhinīya vadati- ko'pyeṣa ityādinā |
vīraśiśukākṛtī rāmātmanā bhāsamāna eṣa ko'pyanirvācyaḥ padārtho'prameyānāṃ durjñeyānāmaparicchinnānāṃ sāmarthyānāṃdurghaṭārthasaṃghaṭṭanaśaktīnāṃ ye sārā utkṛṣṭāṃśāsteṣāṃ samudāyamayaḥ |
saṃghātasvarūpa ityarthaḥ |
svārthe mayaṭ |
samudāyādāgataḥ, samudāyasya vikāraḥ, samudāyapracuro vetyarthaḥ |
``hetumanuṣyebhyo'nyatarasyāṃ rūpyaḥ'', ``tasya vikāraḥ'', mayaḍvaitayorbhāṣāyāmabhakṣācchādanayoḥ'', ``tatprakṛtavacane mayaṭ'' iti ca mayaṭ||39||

 saṃbhāvyeti |
asya rāmasya vapuḥ śubhākāraḥ |
``vapuḥ kāye śubhākāre'' iti ratnamālā |
saṃbhāvyo niśceyaḥ |
saptānāṃ bhuvanānāṃ samāhāraḥ saptabhuvanam |
tasmai yadabhayadānaṃ bhayasaṃsargābhāvanirvahaṇaṃ tadeva puṇyaṃ dharmastasya saṃbhāro'tiśayoo yena tattathoktam |
tathāvidhaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``rūpamevāsyaitanmahimānaṃ vyācaṣṭe'' iti śrutiratrānusaṃdheyā |
atra rāmavapuṣi lakṣmīśca lāvaṇyātmakaśobhā ca |
``lakṣmīḥ saṃpadramā śobhā'' iti ratnamālā |
sattvaṃ sattvaguṇastatra bhavaiḥ |
adhyātmāditvāṭṭak |
guṇairnirmalatvaprakāśavattvanirāmayatvasukhasaṅgitvajñānasaṅgitvādibhirjvalitaṃ viśadaṃ tejaśca |
``brahmavida iva saumya te mukhaṃ bhāti'' iti śrutiprasiddhadīptiviśeṣaśca |
sattvaguṇasya hyuktaguṇahetutvaṃ bhagavataiva gītam- ``ta sattvaṃ nirmalatvātprakāśakamānamayam |
sukhasaṅgena badhnāti jñānasaṅgena cānagha||'' ityādinā |
yadvā sattvaṃ balaṃ tatra bhavāḥ sāttvikāḥ |
sattvaṃ dravye piśācādau vyavasāyasvabhāvayoḥ |
guṇaprāṇabalasvāntasattāsvastrī tu jantuṣu||'' iti ratnamālā |
tathāvidhairguṇardhairyādibhirjvalitaṃ prakāśitaṃ tejaśca |
``adhikṣepāvamānādeḥ prayuktasya pareṇa yat |
prāṇātyaye'pyasahanaṃ tattejaḥ samudāhṛtam||'' ityuktalakṣaṇaṃ tejaśca dharmaśca śrautasmārtābhyudayasādhanaguṇaviśeṣaśca |
yadvā dharmo nyāyaḥ, ahiṃsā vā dharmaḥ |
``atha dharmo'strī puṇye sāmānyabhāvayoḥ |
cāpanyāyādhvarārhisāyajvācārayameṣu nā||'' iti ratnamālā |
māno garvaścittasamunnatirvā |
``mānaḥ strīṇāṃ kopabhede garve cittonnatāvapi'' iti ratnamālā |
yadvā mānaṃ śrutismṛtyādijanyaṃ pramitirūpaṃ jñānam |
``mānaṃ pramāṇe prasthādau'' iti ratnamālā |
vijayaḥ parābhibhavanaṃ viśiṣṭaparāvadhikotkarṣaśca |
``ji jaye'' ``ji abhibhave'' ābhyāṃ bhāve ``erac'' ityacpratyayaḥ |
tau ca parākraśca śaktiśca |
``śaktiḥ parākramaḥ prāṇaḥ ityamaraḥ |
visphuranti viśeṣeṇa prakāśante |
``yaśo vidhātuḥ kathayanti khaṇḍitam ityatreva subarthasaṃkhyāyā adhikatvātsādhutvam |
``sphuratisphulatyornirnivibhyaḥ iti vaikalpikaṃ ṣatvam |
hiśabdaḥ prasiddhārthako'syārthasya durapahnavatvaṃ vayañjayati||40||

 ayaṃ hi rāmo hi |
trātumiti |
lokānsaptalokān |
``lokastu bhuvane jane'' ityamaraḥ |
trātuṃ pālayitum |
``traiṅ pālane'' ityasmāt ``tumuṇṇvulau kriyāyāṃ kriyārthāyām'' iti tumun |
kāyavānpariṇataḥ śarīravattārūpapariṇāmamavasthāṃ prāpto'stramantra iva jṛmbhakādyastradevatāprakāśakavedabhāga iva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
mantrabrāhmaṇarūpabhāgadvayātmake vede mantrabhāgaḥ ``taccodakeṣu mantrākhye''ti bhagavatā jaimininā lakṣitaḥ |
ahitanirasanādiratra sādhāraṇadharmaḥ |
brahmakośasya brahmāṇḍasya |
``peśī kośo dvihīne'ṇḍam'' ityamaraḥ |
guptyai rakṣaṇāya |
``guptiḥ syādbhuvyudāse'pi kārāveśmani rakṣaṇe'' iti ratnamālā |
tanuṃ mūrtitam |
``striyāṃ mūrtistanustanūḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
śritaḥ prāptaḥ kṣattraḥ kṣattravarṇasaṃbandhī dharma iva |
``śauryaṃ tejo dhṛtirdākṣyaṃ yuddhaga cāpyapalāyanam |
dānamīśvarabhāvaśca kṣāttraṃ karmasvabhāvajam||'' ityuktasvabhāva iva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
sāmarthyānāmiti |
tanuṃ śrita ityanuṣajyate |
tanuṃ śritaḥ sāmarthyānāṃ sarvakārya=phalaupayikaśaktīnāṃ samudaya iva gaṇa iva |
``samudāyaḥ samudayaḥ samavāyaścayo gaṇaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
guṇānāṃ jñānādyātmaguṇānāṃ saundaryādivigrahaguṇānāṃ ca saṃcayo vā samūha iva bhavati |
``vā syādvikalpopamayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atrāpi tanuṃ śrita ityanuṣajyate prāturbhūya pratyakṣībhūya sthitaḥ |
puṇyairnirmīyanta iti puṇyanirmāṇāni |
puṇyaphalānītyarthaḥ |
``kṛtyalyuṭo bahulam'' iti karmaṇi lyuṭ |
``bhojanāḥ śālayaḥ'' iti vayyagatāṃ puṇyanirāśī ityamaraḥ |
atra sarvaotprekṣālaṃkāraḥ |
taduktam- ``yadāyamaṇupamānānāṃ lokatasiddhimicchati |
tadotprekṣaiva yenaiva śabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāparaḥ||'' iti vāśabdasyevaśabdaparyāyatvādutprekṣādyotakatavamaviruddham |
astramantrāderumānasya kāyavattvena pariṇāmādibhirviśeṣaṇairlokaprasiddhatvātpañcasvapi vākyeṣūtprekṣā |
sā ca svarūpotprekṣeti draṣṭavyam||49||

 vadhūḥ snuṣā vasiṣṭhasanābhitayā paraśurāmaḥ svasya sītāṃ śvaśuradeśīyatvamabhisaṃdhāya sītāṃ vadhūriti nirdiśati |
abhyantaraṃ gṛhamadhyam/ |
evameva |
sītāyā gṛhābhyantarapraveśo mamāpīṣṭaṣṭa ityarthaḥ/ |
sīradhvaja iti |
sīradhvajo janakaḥ |
dhanuścāpaḥ pāṇau yasya sa tathoktastathāvidhaḥ san |
``sīradhvaja iti |
sīradhvajo janakaḥ |
dhanuścāpaḥ pāṇau yasya sa tathoktastathāvidhaḥ san |
``praharaṇārthebhyaḥ pare niṣṭhāsaptamyau'' iti saptamyantasya pāṇiśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
ita evābhivartate |
asraivāyātītyarthaḥ |
śiṣṭaṃ prāgeva vyākhyātaprāyam||42||

 praviśāmaḥ |
abhyantaramiti śeṣaḥ/ |
sa eṣa iti/ |
manīṣi prājña aṅgirasenanānākṣgiraso'patyena |
pautrādirapyapatyamiti pakṣa eva mukhya ityaṅgiraḥśabdāt ``tasyāpatyam'' ityaṇpratyayaḥ |
purohitena śatānandena guptaḥ pālita eṣa janako rājā |
uddeśyasamarpakamidam |
yasmai yājñavalkyastannāmādityaśiṣyaḥ sūryaśiṣyo muniḥ paraṃ brahma paramātmānaṃ vivavre vivṛtavānsa iti vidheyaviśeṣaṇatayā yojanīyam |
a ``janako ha vaideha āsāṃcakre |
atha ha yājñavalkya āvavrāja tam ityādi vṛdāraṇyakasaṃdarbho draṣṭavyaḥ||43||

 sadvṛtaḥ sādhuśīlaḥ |
kṣattriya iti kṣattriyatvāddhetoḥ |
śiraḥśūlaṃ śirorogaviśeṣam |
``śūlamastryāyudhe roge syāddaṣṭavadhakīlake |
uparāgādidoṣe ca'' iti ratnamālā |
utkopayati prakopayati |
vardhayatīti yāvat/ |
saṃbhrāntaḥ |
paraśurāmanivāraṇe tvarita ityarthaḥ |
``saṃbhramastvarāṃ ityamaraḥ/ |
atra paraśurāmaviṣaye/ |
kimanyat |
vakṣyamāṇātirekeṇa na kiṃcitkartavyamastītyarthaḥ |
ṛṣiriti |
ayaṃ ṛṣirjāmadagnyo'tithiścecchrotriyāya vedādhyāyine'smai jāmadagnyāya viṣṭaraḥ pīṭhādyāsanaviśeṣaḥ |
``viṣṭaro viṭapī darbhamuṣṭiḥ pīṭhādyamāsanam'' ityamaraḥ |
pādyaṃ pādāya vāri |
arghyaṃ pūjārthaṃ dravyam |
atra kalpyatāmiti bhinnavākyatayā yojanīyam |
tadanu madhuparko madhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ dadhi ca kalpyatāṃ dīyatām |
atra gomadhuparkoho vedādhyāyaḥ'' ityāpatastambavacanamabhisaṃhitam |
madhuparkasvarūpamapi tatraivoktam- ``madhuparkaśca dadhimadhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ payo vā madhusaṃsṛṣṭamabhāva udakam'' iti |
atheti pakṣāntaraprastāve |
ayaṃ ripuḥ sannaniṣṭaṃ lapansan |
``rapericcopadhāyāḥ'' ityuṇādiṣu lapaterupratyaye upadhāyā ikāre ca satyaniṣṭaṃ rapatītyarthe ripuśabdo vyutpāditaḥ |
akasmānniṣkāraṇaṃ naḥ putrabhāṇḍamasmadīyaṃ rāmātmakaputrarūpaṃ mūladhanaṃ karma |
``syādbhāṇḍamaśvābharaṇe'me mūlavaṇigdhane'' ityamaraḥ |
dveṣṭi cettadviṣaye dveṣaṃ karoti cettanniṣkāraṇadveṣāddhetornayavihīne nyāyādapeta iha paraśurāme kārmaukasya kodaṇḍasyādhikāro viniyogaḥ kalpyatāmityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
atrāpi cedaṃ purā gītamevam |
``bhuvi |
pīḍākaramamitrāṇāṃ yatsyātkartavyameva tat||'' iti droṇaparvagatabhāratavacanamanusaṃdheyam||44||

 atibāṣpāyitamatyantaṃ bāṣpodvamanaṃ kṛtam |
``bāṣpoṣmabhyāmudvamane'' iti kyaṅantādbhāve ktaḥ/ |
na kiṃcit |
vakṣyamāṇādanyatra kimapītyarthaḥ |
saṃbhūyeti |
yatra tvayyālokapathāvatāriṇi locanaprasaragocaraṃ gate sati |
ālokasya panthā ālokapathaḥ |
``ṛkpūrabdhūḥpathāmānakṣe'' iti samāsāntaḥ |
sukhāni sukhabdavācyāni sarvāṇyapi saṃbhūya saṅghabhāvaṃ prāpya paramatiśayitaṃ bhūmānaṃ vaipulyam |
``bahu syādvipule stryādisaṃkhyāyāṃ ca triliṅgakaḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
``bahorlopo bhū ca bahoḥ'' iti bahuśabdādbhāve imanic |
bahorbhūrādeśe imanici ikāralopaśca |
cetasyātanvate prāpnuvanti |
sarveṣāmapyātmanāṃ sarveṣvapi deśeṣu sarveṣu na kāleṣu sarvebhyo'pi hetubhyaḥ sarvaprakārāṇi sukhāni militāni |
kṣaṇaparimitarāmavigrahālokanasukhasamānarekhārūḍhāni bhavantītyarthaḥ |
atra ca ``etasyaivānandasyānyāni bhūtāni māā mupajīvanti'' iti śrutyarthaḥ sūcitaḥ |
yadvā yatra tvayyālokapathāvatāriṇi sati sukhāni cetasi saṃbhūya paramanyaṃ bhūmānamātanvate |
sṛjantītyarthaḥ |
ayaṃ bhāvacchāndogye bhūmavidyāyām- ``yatra nānyatpaśyati nānyacchṛṇoti nānyadvijānāti sa bhūmā yo vai bhūmā tadamṛtam'' ``ssave mahimni pratiṣṭhitaḥ sa evādhastātsa upariṣṭātsa paścātsa purastātsa dakṣiṇataḥ sa uttarataḥ sa evedaguṃ sarvaṃ sa vā eṣa evaṃpaśyannevaṃmanvāna evaṃvijānannātmaratirātmakrīḍa ātmamithuna ātmānandaḥ'' ityādisarvātmyādisarvakalyāṇaguṇaniravadyasatyouruṣādiśabdābhidheyaparamātmalakṣaṇo niḥsaṅganityamuktānubhāvyaniravagrahaniratiśayānandarūpaḥ ko'pi bhūmā pratipādito ``bhūmā saṃprasādāt'' ityādinā brahmasūtreṇa nirūpitaśca |
tato'pyadhikaḥ pūrvaṃ kamapi bhūmānaṃ rāmadarśanasukhāni mama manasi nirmāntīti |
atra ca- ``akhaṇḍaikarasānandatārakabrahmavācakaḥ |
rāmāyeti suvijñeyaḥ satyānandacidātmakaḥ/ |
ityupaniṣadarthaḥ sūcitaḥ |
ratimiti yatrālokapathāvatāriṇītyetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenottaratrāpyanveti |

 13 7-6-95ṣṭiṣṭa7praˇˇratimiti yatrālokapathāvatāriṇītyetanmadhyamaṇinyāyenottaratrāpyanveti |
yatra tvayyālokapathāvatāriṇi sati netrotsavo netrānandaḥ |
kārye kāraṇavācakaśabdaḥ |
ānandajanakavyāpāro'yutsavaḥ |
ratiṃ srakcandanādijanyāṃ prītiṃ prastauti niṣpādayati |
pūrvoktabhūmavidyāgatātmakrīḍāderupalakṣaṇamidam |
srakcandanādijanyā prīti ratiḥ, udyānādijanyā prītiḥ krīḍā, strīsaṃbhavā prītirmithunam, matijanyā prītirānanda ityupaniṣadvyākhyātāraḥ |
atra ca bhūmānamityanena bhūmavākye ratimityanenātmaratirityādivākyaṃ ca sphoritam |
paramityanena bhagavataḥ paratvāvasthāto'pi rāmāvatārāvasyaiva bhogyeti sūcyate |
uktaṃ ca hanūmatā – ``snehaśca paramo rājaṃstvayi nityaṃ pratiṣṭhitaḥ |
bhaktiśca niyatā vīrabhāvo nānyatra gacchati||'' iti |
so'yamjakhilavedāntaparamarahasyārtho jāmadāgnyavākyamukhena kavināviṣkṛtaḥ – sa tvāmiti |
sa tathāvidho nūtanaḥ kaṅkaṇadharo'dyaiva vaivāhikapratisaraṃ dhārayamāṇaḥ |
nūtanatvaṃ kaṅkaṇadharatvaneti |
śrīmāṃstejasvī bhogyasamṛddhirūpasaṃpattimānvā veṣaracanāvānvā |
``śrīrveṣaracanāśobhāsaṃpatsaralaśākhiṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
cetasaḥ priya iṣṭo'smadguruṃ rudraṃ paribhūtavāndhanurbhaṅgena tiraskṛtavānsa tvaṃ hantavya iti hetoḥ prāgeva hananapūrvameva dūyāmahe |
``duṅ paritāpe'' ityasmāddivāditvācchyan |
atra guruparibhavo hananahetuḥ |
kaṅkaṇadharatvādikaṃ paritāpahetuḥ |
pūrva vīravratena hantavyatvamuktam |
adya tu paribhavena hantavyatvamiti hetubhedādapaunaruktayam||45||

 bhārgaveti |
māmanukampasa iti mayi dayāvāniti jñāyate |
maveti śeṣaḥ |
``kṛpā dayānukampā syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
atra māmanumannimittaṃ kampase vepasa iti jñāyata iti ca vivakṣitam |
``lakṣaṇetthaṃbhūtākhyānabhāgavīpsāsu pratiparyanavaḥ'' ityanuśabdasya karmapravacanīyatvāt ``karmapravacanīyayukte dvitīyā'' |
dayasa ityanuktā māmanukampasa ityuktirarthāntarābhiprāyiketyupalakṣya bhārgava āha – are, kimudbhānto'sīti |
nirbhaye bhayagrahaṇādbhāntiḥ |
tasya vahikathanādudbhāntiḥ |
amṛteti |
amṛtena sudhayādhmātaḥ pūrito jīmūto megha iva snigdhaṃ śītalatvacākacakyādiyuktaṃ saṃhananaṃ vapuryasya sa tathoktastasya |
``gātraṃ vapuḥ saṃhananam'' ityamaraḥ |
kamburiva śaṅkha iva kaṇṭho yasya sa tathoktastasya |
``śaṅkhaḥ syātkamburastriyām'' ityamaraḥ |
yadvā kambuḥ strīvalayaḥ śaṅkhaḥ sa iva kaṇṭho yasya sa tathoktastasya |
``kambuḥ strīvalaye śaṅkhe saṃkhyāyāṃ jalaśuktiṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
``rekhātrayāṅkitā grīvā kambuḥ grīveti kathyate'' iti halāyudhaśca |
tava kaṇṭhe kuṭhāraḥpatiṣyati |
spaṣṭo'rthaḥ||46||

 āḥ, karuṇayā parikṣipto'si |
āḥ iti kope |
parikṣipto'si parivṛtto'si |
satyamevetyanena vyatireko vyajate |
tvayā mama duḥkhotpādane hi paraduḥkhāsahiṣṇutvalakṣaṇā karuṇā prastāvamarhati |
tadeva tu tava duḥśakyamiti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ ceyaṃ dayā pūrvakarājeṣu kva gateti bhāvaḥ/ |
bhṛkuṭīdharaḥ kopena vikṛtabhrūrjātaḥ |
kṣatriyaḍimbha kṣatriyaśiśo, navo nūtano vadhūṭikāyā ayonijātvena mātāpitṛrahitatayā dayanīyāyā bālikāyāḥ parigraho yena sa tathoktaḥ |
``vayasi prathame'' iti vīpi vaghūṭiśabdaḥ |
tasmādanukampāyāṃ kani hrasve ca vaghūṭikāśabdaḥ |
``jīvadbhartrī kulasthā yā vaghūṭi sā rūyapāṃsinī'' iti hrasve trikāṇḍaśeṣaḥ |
sundaro manojñaḥ |
na vidyate pūrvo yasminkarmaṇi tathoktam |
upatapyate duḥkhayate |
pūrve rājāno hi naivaṃvidyāstataste nirutāpena mayā hatāḥ |
adya tvīdṛśe tvayi mama karuṇā jāyata ityarthaḥ |
suprasiddha iti |
jāmadagnyo rāmo māturmūrghānāṃ svayamacchinadityayaṃ suprasiddhaḥ pravādo lokavāda iha jagati itiha pāramparyeṇa gīyate śabdyate |
``kai gai śabde'' iti paṭhitādbhayateḥ karmaṇi laṭi yaki ``ghumāsthāgāpājahātisāṃ hali'' itītvam |
``anantāvasathetihabheṣajāṃ ñyaḥ'' iti sūtre itiheti nipātasamudāyaḥ pāramparya vartata iti vaiyākaraṇāḥ |
``pāramparyopadeśe syādaitihyamitihāvyayam'' ityamaraḥ||47||

 upakṛtyeti |
sarvabhūtaiḥsarvaprāṇibhiḥkartṛbhiḥ kṣatrasaṃtāne kṣatravaṃśe roṣātkopādgarbhānapyakiṃcitkarāngarbhapiṇḍānapyutkṛtyotkṛtya paunaḥpunyena chittvā |
``kṛtī chedane'' ityasmātrayam |
śakalayataḥ punarapi khaṇḍayata uddāmasyodīrṇasya sarvato'khiladeśeṣu rājavaṃśyānrājavaṃśabhavān |
sarvānrājavaṃśānvā |
ādyāditvāttasiḥ |
ekaviṃśatirekaviṃśo'vadhiścaramo yasmistadyathā tathā |
vidhamanakriyāviśeṣaṇam |
ekaviṃśatiśabdo lakṣaṇayaikaviṃśaparaḥ |
yathā triviṣṭapaśabde triśabdasritīyaparastadbhat |
athavaikaviṃśatyā saṃkhyayāvadhiḥ paricchedo yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktam |
``viṃśatyādyāḥ sadaikatve sarvāḥ saṃkhyeyasaṃkhyayoḥ |
saṃkhyārthe dvivahutve staḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``avadhāne paricchede sīmri kāle vale'vadhiḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
vidhamato nāśayataḥ |
``dhmā śabdāgnisaṃyogayoḥ'' ityasmāllaṭaḥ śatari pāghrādhmāsthāmrādāṇdṛśyartisartiśadasadāṃ pibajighradhamatiṣṭamanagacchapaśyarcchadhauśīyasīdāḥ'' iti dhamāveśaḥ |
upasargavaśena ca nāśanārthakatvam |
teṣāṃ rājavaṃśyānāṃ raktaiḥ pūrṇe |
``kartṛkaraṇe kṛtā bahulam'' iti samāsaḥ |
hṛde'gādhajalādhāre |
``jalādhārāstatrāgādhajalo hṛdaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
savanena srānena |
``savanaṃ somaniṣpeṣe jananasrānayorapi'' iti raktamālā |
yo mahānandaḥ pracurasukham |
mokṣatulyānandābhimānena vā mahānandoktiḥ |
``muktimokṣe mahānandaḥ'' ityabhidhānāt |
tena mandāyamāno'lpībhavankrodha evāgriryasya sa tathoktaḥ |
tasya pitryaṃ pitṛkarma |
``vāyavṛtupitruṣaso yat'' iti yat |
kurvataḥ |
tadrakteneti śeṣaḥ |
me svabhāvaḥ |
kṣatriyagarbhotkartanādirūpo na vidita iti na |
dvau navau prakṛtārtha bodhayataḥ'' |
vidita evetyarthaḥ |
khaluśabdaḥ prasiddhau||48||

 nṛśaṃsatā ghātukatā puruṣadoṣaḥ puruṣasya heyatāpādakaḥ |
ataeva hi ``ānṛśaṃsyaṃ paro dharmaḥ'' iti śāstram |
tatra nṛśaṃsattāyāṃ kā vikatthanā kā ślāghā/ |
kṣatriyavaṭo kṣatriyabālaka |
``bālo baṭurmāṇabakaḥ'' iti kośaḥ |
ati pragalbhase prakarṣeṇa dhṛṣṭo bhavasi |
``galma ghāṣṭarye'' iti dhātuḥ |
anupasargā api prādayaḥ santi tena madhye hiśabdaḥ prayuktaḥ |
cāyaṃ dhanuḥ kartṛ |
``bhayāmṛtaśakṛdbhasracāpābharaṇalāñchanam'' iti napuṃsakaśeṣedhvamarokteścāpaśabdasya napuṃsakatvamiti |
namatu śarāniḥsāraṇārthaṃ nataṃ bhavatu |
antarbhāvitaṇyarthasya namateḥ karmaṇaḥ kartṛtvavivakṣayā kartari loṭ |
yadyapi ``karmavatkarmaṇā tulyakriyaḥ'' prāptayoryagātmanepadayormadhye ``na duhatrunamāṃ yakciṇau'' iti yako niṣedhe'pyātmanepadaṃ durvāram, yathā namate daṇḍaḥ'' ityādau, tathāpi ``niraṅkuśāḥ kavayaḥ'' ityuktatvādātmanepadābhāvaḥ |
yadvā māmityavyāhāraḥ |
cāpaṃ kartṛ |
māṃ namayatvityarthaḥ |
evaṃ ca paraśurāmanamane cāpasya karaṇatvāttasya kartṛtvavivakṣāyām ``asiśchinatti'' ityatreva kartari lakāraḥ, parasmaipadaṃ ca |
athavā cāpaṃ kartṛ namatu |
visphāraśabdaṃ karotvityarthaḥ |
``ṇamu prahvatve śabde ca'' iti ṇamati śabdārthaḥ |
adya tu mukhyameva kartṛtvam |
yadvā māṃ prahara |
mayi cāpaṃ karma nama |
namayetyarthaḥ |
tuśabdaścārthakaḥ prahāraṃ samuccinoti |
tuśabdo'vadhāraṇārthako vā |
namayaivetyarthaḥ |
ahaṃ prākprahārapriyaḥ |
śatruṇā prākprahāramicchāmītyarthaḥ svayaṃ prākprahāre tu śatrorapunaraṅkuratvāttena mama prahāro durlabha iti bhāvaḥ |
tadāha – mayi tvityādinā |
mayi tu sarvavīrajanottīrṇe mayi kṛtanighāte sati kṛtaprahāre sati pareṇa |
anantarakālavācinaḥ paraśabdāt ``enavanyatarasyāmadūre'paścamyāḥ'' |
jhaṭiti śīghraṃ vitatairvistṛtairvahvayudgārairagrayudvamarnaitumirbhākhatā tejakhinā kuṭhāreṇa pravighaṭhito viyojitaḥ kaṭhoraḥ pūrṇaḥ |
``kaṭhicakibhyāmoran'' iti sūtre kaṭhoraśabdaḥ pūrṇatāyāmapīti śābdikokteḥ |
skandhasyāṃsasya |
``skandho bhujaśiro'so'strī'' ityamaraḥ |
bandho yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
kabandho'pamūrghadehaḥ |
``kabandho'strī kriyāyuktamapamūrghakalevaram'' ityamaraḥ |
kiṃ vidadhyātkiṃ kuryāt |
śaki liṅ||49||

 viśvasya paraśurāmaṃ jetuṃ śakta iti niśceyaḥ |
saṃbuddhantametat |
āskha tāvat |
adya kiṃcitkālaṃ nirvyāpārastiṣṭhetyarthaḥ/ |
abhyanujñā gurujanānumatirapekṣyāpekṣaṇīyā yena sa tathoktaḥ |
``gurośca halaḥ'' iti karmaṇyakārapratyayaḥ/ |
tvaṃ na iti |
sajjaṃ saṃmṛtamātitheyamatithiṣu sādhu viṣṭarārcyapādyādikaṃ yaiste tathoktāḥ |
``sajjau saṃmṛtasaṃnaddhau'' iti ratnamālā |
atithiśabdāt ``tatra sādhuḥ'' ityadhikāre ``pathyatithivasatikhapaterḍhañ'' iti ḍhañ |
sucaritaḥ suvṛttaḥ/ |
akamātsahasā kanyāntaḥpuraṃ praviśatā |
tvayeti śeṣaḥ |
antaḥsthitirantaḥpuramaryādā |
``sthānamaryādayoḥsthitiḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
saṃdūṣitā vikalitā/ |
parameśvarāṇāṃ prabhūṇāṃ gṛhasyāntaḥpurādervyavahārasya krameṇa praveśādivyāpārasyānabhijñaḥ |
ajña ityarthaḥ/ |
ahaṃkārasya garvasyotprakāśo vyañjako marmodghāṭanahāsaviśeṣaḥ/ |
asmatsanāthe'smatparatantre rāghavaśiśau rāme pāpaṃ karma kuṭhārapreraṇarūpaṃ kathaṃ vāñchasīcchasi |
kuṭhārapreraṇaṃ hi rāmasyāsmatsanāthatvācchiśutvācca duḥśakaṃ pāpaṃ ceti kathaṃ tadicchasītyarthaḥ/ |
kañcukī sauvidalaḥ |
kaṅkaṇamocanāya vaivāhikapratisaravisraṃsanāya |
``nirvṛtte tvatha kalyāṇe naro naddhaṃ ca kautukam |
prāpte niśāmukhe tattanmantreṇātha tatastyajet||'' iti vacanama draṣṭavyam |
militāḥ saṃgatāḥ |
pratīkṣanta iti śeṣaḥ |
varo jāmātā rāmaḥ |
``devaprasāde varaṇe jāmātari varaḥ pumān'' iti ratnamālā||50||

 ādiśantyājñāpayanti/ |
lokadharmo lokairanuṣṭhīyamānadharmaḥ |
jñātayo bandhavaḥ paśyantu tvām |
itaḥparaṃ mayā yuddhe kaṃ drakṣyantīti bhāvaḥ |
janapadeṣu deśeṣu na parīkṣeptavyo nātivāhayitavyaḥ/ |
praviśyeti |
itare janakaḥ śatānando bhārgavaśca/ |
guruvacanādvasiṣṭhaviśvāmitravacanāt/ |
pūrvāṅke'smitraṅke ca mukhasaṃdhiḥ pratimukhasaṃdhiścoktaḥ |
tatsvarūpaṃ tu – ``mukhaṃ bījasamutpattirnānārtharasasaṃbhavā |
aṅgāni dvādaśaitasya vījārambhasamanvayāt/ |
upakṣepaḥ parikaraḥ parinyāso vilobhanam |
yuktiḥ prāptiḥ samādhānaṃ vidhānaṃ paribhāvanā/ |
udbhedabhedakaraṇāni'' iti daśarūpakādāvuktam |
tatra ``bhadramastu'' ``snehaṃ karoti'' iti bījārambhasamanvaya uktaḥ |
bījārambhalakṣaṇaṃ tu ``stokoddiṣṭaḥ kāryahetubījaṃ vistāryanekaghā |
autsukyamātramārambhaḥ phalalābhāya bhūyase||'' ityuktam |
tatropakṣepādikaṃ tattadavasara eva likhitam |
śiṣṭamatra likhyate – ``ariṣṭatātimāśāsmahe'' iti bījasukhāgamarūpaprāptiruktā |
``kathamaprameya ityatra bījaviṣaya āścaryāveśarūpaparibhāvanoktā |
``rāmāya puṇyamahase'' ityatra bījasukhaduḥkhaheturūpaṃ vidhānamuktam |
``vatsa, hanyatām'' ityatra bījānuguṇaprotsāhanarūpabheda uktaḥ |
``asyāyamavasaraḥ'' ityādau bījānuguṇaprastutakāryārambharūpakaraṇamuktam |
evamanyadapi svayaṃ draṣṭavyam |
anantaraṃ pratimukhasaṃdhiḥ |
tatsvarūpaṃ tu – ``lakṣyālakṣyasya bījasya vyaktiḥ pratimukhaṃ matam |
binduprayatnāmigamādaṅgānyasya trayodaśa/ |
vilāsaḥ parisarpaśca vidhūtaṃ śarmanarmaṇī |
narmadyutipragamanaṃ nirodhaḥ paryaupāsanam/ |
vakaṃ puṣpamupanyāso varṇasaṃhāra ityapi||'' prathamamaprathamamalakṣyasya dhanurbhaṅgadivyāsradānādinā lakṣyasya ca bījasya vyaktirūpaṃ pratimukham |
tatra ca binduprayatnau vaktavyau |
tatsvarūpaṃ tu – ``avāntarārthavicchede binduracchedakāraṇam |
prayatnastu phalāvāptau vyāpāro'titvarānvitaḥ||'' iti |
atra bhārgavapraveśo binduḥ |
avāntaravicchedahetutve'pyuttaratra dhanurdānādimiracchedakāraṇatvāt |
``tapasyate''- ityādiṣu prayatnaḥ spaṣṭaḥ |
tatrāṅgāne likhyante |
``sakhyaḥ – kathayata tata'' ityatra dṛṣṭanaṣṭapadārthānusaraṇarūpaḥ parisarpa uktaḥ |
``notsavā–'' ityādāvaniṣṭavastuvikṣeparūpavidhūnanamuktam |
``kimekadeśena-'' ityādinā ratyupaśamanarūpaṃ śarmoktam |
``kumāra prekṣasva'' ityādinānurāgodghaṭanārthaprītirūpadyutiruktā |
``sakhi sasurāsura-'' ityādinā cānurāgaprakāśavacanarūpaṃ puṣpamuktam |
``ātaṅkaśrama-'' ityādinā cānurāgahetuvākyaracanārūpanyāsa uktaḥ |
``dhanuṣi dhārayantī'' ityādinā ca chadmanā hitāgamanirodhanarūpanirodha uktaḥ |
``utsiktasya'' ityādinā ca saṃbhogaviṣayamanoratharūpavilāsa uktaḥ |
``āryaputra'' ityādinā ceṣṭajanānunayarūpaparyupāsanamuktam |
``anviṣyata'' ityādinā ca pramukhaniṣṭhuravacanarūpaṃ vajramuktam |
``ayaṃ sa kila'' ityādinā parihāsavacanarūpaṃ narmoktam |
``rāma rāma nayanābhirāmatām'' ityādinā cottarottaravākyairanurāgabījaprakāśanarūpaḥ |
pragama uktaḥ |
evamanyadapi yathāsaṃbhavaṃ draṣṭavyam||

atha kavinā tṛtīyāṅkaḥ prastūyate- tataḥ praviśata upaviṣṭāvityādinā |
jāmadagnyaśatānandau ceti |
praviśata ityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrau jāmadagnyaṃ pratīti |
āhaturiti śeṣaḥ |
iṣṭāpūrteti |
a ya ityadhyāhāra uttaratra yenetyādi yacchabdayuktavākyaśailīdarśanāt |
yo daśaratha iṣṭaṃ darśapūrṇamāsajyotiṣṭomāśvamedharājasūyādiyāgaḥ |
``yaja devapūjāyām'' ityasmādbhāve ktaḥ |
pūrtaṃ devālayataṭākārāmādikaraṇam |
``ṛtukarmeṣṭaṃ pūrtaṃ khātādi karma yat'' ityamaraḥ |
iṣṭaṃ ca pūrtaṃ ceti vigrahe samāhāra itareratayogo vā  dvandvaḥ |
pṛṣodarāditvādiṣṭaśabdasya dīrghaḥ |
tayorvidheranuṣṭhānātsapatnā
ṛnāṃ śatrūṇāṃ śamanānnirasanāt |
``ripau vairisapatnāridviṣaddveṣaṇadurhṛdaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
maghona indrasya preyānatiśayena priyaḥ sakhā mittraṃ bhavati |
vīreṇa yena daśarathena vajriṇendreṇa dyauriva svarga iva |
``suraloko dyodivau dve'' ityamaraḥ |
vasumatī bhūmī rājanvatī śobhanena rājñā yuktā bhavati |
``rājanvānsaurājye'' iti pinātitādrājanvacchabdāt ``ugitaśca'' iti sttriyāṃ ṅīp |
ete vayamahaṃ viśvāmitrādayaśca yasya daśarathasyāgrataḥ purataḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
hitānuśāsanādyarthaṃ nityaṃ saṃnihitā ityarthaḥ |
aparaṃ vakṣyamāṇādanyatkim |
vaktavyamiti śeṣaḥ |
vaṃśaśca yasyeti vartate |
yasya daśarathasya vaṃśaḥ kulaṃ ca vaivasvataḥ sūryasaṃbandhī |
vivasvacchabdāt ``tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
tanayapriyaḥ priyaputraḥ |
``vā priyasya'' iti paranipātaḥ |
pariṇato vṛddho rājā mukhyarājatvavān |
``mukhyasya nirviśeṣeṇa śabdo'nyeṣāṃ viśeṣataḥ'' ityukteḥ |
so'yaṃ daśarathaḥ śamaṃ bhavataḥ krodhanivṛttiṃ yācate |
bhavantamiti śeṣaḥ |
``tasmādapi vadhyaṃ prapannaṃ prati prayacchati'' iti śrutyā śaraṇāgatavyāpādo'tyantaduṣṭa ityarthaḥ |
yenetyādi vaivasvata ityantaṃ yojayitvā so'yaṃ maghonaḥ sakhā tanayapriyaḥ pariṇato rājā śamaṃ yācata ityanadhyāhāreṇa vā yojyam |
etaduttarāṅkasya pūrvāṅkārthānusaṃgatatvādaṅkāvataraṇam |
yathoktam- ``yatra syāduttarāṅkārthaḥ pūrvāṅkārthānusaṃgataḥ |
asūcitāṅkapātraṃ tadaṅkāvataraṇaṃ matam||'' iti ||1||

 taditi |
tattasmāddhetoḥ śuṣkakalahānniṣprayojanādyuddhāt |
``kalahāsphoṭasaṃyugāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``jugupsāvirāma-'' ityādinā pañcamī |
virama nivṛtto bhava |
``vyāṅparibhyo ramaḥ'' iti parasmaipadam |
idaṃ vakṣyamāṇārtharūpam |
cakāraḥ kalahavirāmaṃ samuccinoti |
saṃjñapyata iti |
vatsatarī dvitīyaṃ vayaḥprāptā gauḥ |
``vatsokṣāśvarṣabhebhyaśca tanutve'' iti vatsaśabdātṣṭrac |
ṣittvānṅīṣ |
``damyavatsatarau samau'' ityamaraḥ |
tṛtīyaṃ vayaḥ prāptā gaurityanye |
saṃjñapyate viśasyate |
``māraṇatoṣaṇaniśāmaneṣu jñā'' iti mittvāt ṇau ``mitāṃ hrasvaḥ'' iti hrasvaḥ |
ṇijantātkarmaṇi yak |
yugāntareṣvatithīnāṃ māṃsakalpo vihitastadabhiprāyeṇetthamuktam |
kiṃca sarpiṣi ghṛte'nnaṃ pacyate saṃskriyate |
he śrotriya sāṅgavedādhyāyin, śrotriyagṛhānsāṅgavedādhyāyino gṛhāñjanakasya gṛham |
``gṛhāḥpuṃsi ca bhūmnyeva'' ityamaraḥ |
āgato'si prāpto'si |
no'smākam |
vatsataryādyātithyamiti śeṣaḥ |
juṣasva sevasva |
``juṣī pratisevanayoḥ'' ityasmānnimantraṇe loṭ |
yadvā śrotriya śrotriyeti saṃmatau dviruktiḥ |
ne'smākaṃ gṛhānāgato'si juṣasva |
prīyasvetyarthaḥ |
saṃbuddhyaikyābhisaṃdhinā netyuktiḥ||1||

 atreti atrāsminnarthe kalahaviratyātithyānnagrahaṇarūpe bhavaduktārthe |
yadi rāmaḥ prakṛṣṭavīryo'dhikabalo na syātkiṃ na kṣame |
kimartham |
krodhanivṛttiṃ na prāpnuyāmityarthaḥ |
paśyantu nirūpayantu |
rāma iti |
rāmaḥ śiśurapyadbhiutaistāṭakātāṭakeyanirasanādyāścaryaiḥ karmabhiḥ khyātaḥ prasiddhaḥ |
asahano'pi kṣāntirahito'pi na vidyate sahanaṃ yasyeti vigrahaḥ |
bhārgavastatastasmādrāmāttiraskriyāṃ gurudhanurbhaṅgarūpaṃ paribhavaṃ prāpya kasmāddhetorasthādapratikurvansthitaḥ |
tiṣṭhaterluṅ |
``gātistha-'' ityādinā sico luk |
ityasminnarthe prastute prakṛte sati gurupuruvasiṣṭhādiṣu gauravāttadvacanānatilaṅghanahetutadīyamahattvāddhetorapratikurvanbhārgavaḥ sthita iti ko vidyājjñātumarhaḥ |
arhe liṅ |
jñātā gurugauravādapratikurvansthita iti jñānavānkaścidbhavedapi |
``vistīrṇā pṛthivī janāśca vividhāḥ'' iti nyāyena saṃbhavatu nāma |
apīti saṃbhāvanāyām |
``saṃbhāvane'lamiti cetsiddhāprayoge'' iti liṅ |
vaktā punargurugauravādasthāt bhārgava iti vaktā tu na tvevāsti naivāsti |
paraparivādarasikatvāllokasyeti bhāvaḥ |
vistīrṇanyāyapravṛttiśaṅkānirāsakastuśabdaḥ |
atyantāyogyatvādatra navā pravṛttiriti bhāvaḥ |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāttathāsthitasya gurugauravādapratikurvansthita iti loke vettuṃ vaktuṃ cāviṣayatayā sthitasya |
mameti śeṣaḥ |
vīrāṇāṃ vrataṃ paribhavāsahanaprayuktapratikārakartavyatāniyamaḥ sulabhadveṣaṃ nyāyyakalahaṃ hi |
hiśabdaḥ pratiyogyabhyupagamaṃ sūcayati |
``avācyavādān-'' iti nyāyena lokeṣvavācyavādaparihārārthatvānnāyaṃ śuṣkakalaha iti bhavadbhirevopagantavyaṃ bhavatīti bhāvaḥ||3||

 yaśasīti |
viśvato viśvasmindeśe |
ādyāditvāttasiḥ |
śvetamāne dhāvalyaṃ prāpnuvati |
``śvitā varṇe'' ityasmāllaṭaḥ śānac |
śaṣo mugāgamaḥ |
kīrteḥ śvaityavarṇanaṃ kavisamayasiddham |
niravakāśe parivādapraveśārharandhraśūnya uttamānāṃ śreṣṭhānāṃ yaśasi vīryaśauryādiguṇavattāprathāyāṃ kathamapi kṛcchreṇa prayatnena ``jñātahetuvivakṣāyāmapyādikathamavyayam |
kathamāditathāpyantaṃ yatnagauravabāḍhayoḥ||'' ityutpalamālāyām |
yatkiṃcitsvalpaṃ vacanīyaṃ garhyam |
``garhyādhīnau ca vaktavyau'' ityamaraḥ |
tatparyāyatvādvacanīyaśabdo'pi tadarthakaḥ |
prāpya labdhvā |
prākṛtaiḥ pṛthagjanaiḥ |
``prākṛtaśca pṛthagjanaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kṛtā vitatirvistāro yasyāḥ sā tathoktā |
vṛddhiṃ prāpitetyarthaḥ |
``tanu vistāre'' bhāve ktin, anunāsikalopaśca |
kaśmalā mūrcchākarī |
heyeti yāvat |
``mūrcchā tu kaśmalaṃ mohaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
matvarthīyo'cpratyayaḥ |
tataḥ striyāṃ ṭāp |
kiṃvadantī lokavādaḥ |
``tribhūvahivasi-'' ityādi jhacprakaraṇe bāhulakādvaderapyauṇadike jhacpratyaye jhasyāntādeśe jhacpratyayasya pittvātideśāt ``ṣidgaurādibhyaśca'' iti ṅīṣi kiṃvadantīti rūpam |
``kiṃvadantī janaśrutiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kathaṃcitkenāpi prakāreṇa bahukālātikrame'pyakasmānniṣkāraṇaṃ pratīkārarūpakāraṇena vinā na viramati na naśyati |
ato'pi nāyaṃ śuṣkakalaha iti bhāvaḥ||4||

 evaṃ guruparivādaparihārārthatvādrāmeṇa saha mayā kalahaḥ kārya ityabhiprāyavantaṃ bhārgavaṃ vasiṣṭha āha-ayi vatseti |
brāhmaṇānarhatvenāgantukatvādavadyāvahatvācca piśācikātvarūpaṇaṃ kṛtam |
yāvajjīvaṃ jīvanakālamabhivyāpya ``yāvattāvacca sākalye'' ityamaraḥ |
``dhātusaṃbandhe pratyayāḥ'' ityadhikāre ``yāvati vindajīvoḥ'' iti ṇamul |
gurūpadiṣṭavidyāyā evāvadyāvahatvena tyājyatvādadya guruparivādaparibhavo nāvaśyaka iti bhāvaḥ |
atha yāvajjīvamāśrayaṇīyāṃ vidyāṃ prastauti- śrotriyo'sītyādinā |
ābhyāṃ viśeṣaṇābhyāṃ śastravidyānadhikāro'dhyātmavidyāyāṃ sāmānyātītādhikāraścoktau |
śrotriya iti vaiduṣyaṃ jāmadagnya iti śāstrāparyudāsaścoktaḥ |
pūtamanavadyaṃ panthānaṃ niḥśreyasopāyaṃ bhajasva |
anena śastragrahaṇamārgasyāpūtatvaṃ vyañjitam |
tathā cāpastambaḥ- ``parīkṣārtho'pi brāhmaṇa āyudhaṃ nādriyate'' iti |
āraṇyakaścāsyaraṇye bhavo'si |
anenādhyātmavidyāyāṃ viśeṣādhikāra uktaḥ |
``ye cāraṇye śraddhātapa upāsate'' iti śrūyate |
tattasmāt |
sāmānyato viśeṣataścādhikārasadbhāvādityarthaḥ |
cittaprasādanīścittanairmalyakāriṇīḥ |
``cittaprasādhanīḥ'' iti pāṭhe cittaparikarmakarīrityarthaḥ |
``parikarma prasādhanam'' ityamaraḥ |
maittrī ādiryāsāṃ tāstathoktā bhāvanāḥsaṃskārānparicinu pariśīlaya |
ādipadena karuṇā muditatopekṣā ca gṛhyante |
tatra puṇyakṛtsu maittrī, duḥkhiṣu karuṇā, sukhiṣu muditatā, pāpiṣūpekṣā ca manomalahāriṇya iti yogaśāstraprakriyā pratyāsīdati |
te pūrvoktādhikārasaṃpattiyuktasya tava viśokāśokāpahā jyotiṣmatī |
draṣṭavyatayā jyotiḥ paraṃ jyotirasyāmastīti jyotiṣmatī |
nāmeti yogaśāstraprasiddhirucyate |
yogaścittavṛttinirodhastadrūpā manovṛttiryogavṛttiḥ |
tathā ca pātañjalasūtram- ``yogaścittavṛttinirodhalakṣaṇaḥ'' iti |
pratyāsīdati pratyāsannā bhavati |
acirasiddhimatī bhavatītyarthaḥ |
krameṇaikādidvādaśaparyantasaṃvatsarasādhyānsiddhiviśeṣāñśīghrameva tava sādhayatītyarthaḥ |
tathā ca yogaśāstre- ``prathame hatarukca sarvalokapriya evātha ca vatsare dvitīye |
kavitāṃ kurute suparvavāṇyā bhujagādyairna ca badhyate tṛtīye |
caturthakenāturatāpipāsānidrādinā tarjitavarjitaḥ syāt |
dūraśravaḥ pañcamavatsare vāksiddhiḥ pareṣāṃ tanuṣu praveśaḥ/ |
ṣaṣṭhe sa vajrairapi yo na vidhyate tato'tivegī ca sudūradarśanaḥ |
bhavedbudhaḥ saptamavatsare'ṣṭadhā vibhūtayo'sya prabhaveyuraṣṭame/ |
gaganacaro digvicaro navame'bde yastu vajrakāyaḥ syāt |
sa manogo daśame syādyatrecchā tatra gacchati pramanāḥ/ |
ekādaśake varṣe sarvajñaḥ siddhimānbhavedyogī |
dvādaśake śivatulyo hartā kartā svayaṃ bhavati||'' iti/ |
tatprasādeti |
tasyā jyotiṣmatīyogavṛtteḥ prasādātpratyayāntarasaṃvalanenānāvilatvājjāyata iti tatprasādajam |
``pañcamyāmajātau'' iti ḍaḥ |
ṛtaṃ paramārthaṃ bibhartīti ṛtaṃbharam |
``saṃjñāyāṃ bhṛtaṝ-'' ityādinā khacpratyayaḥ |
ṛtapadasya mumāgamaśca |
ṛtaṃbharabhityabhidhānaṃ yasya tattathoktam |
nāma prasiddhau |
bahiḥsādhanairyogātiriktalaukikavaidikasādhanairupadheyaṃ saṃnidhāpyaṃ tanna bhavatītyabahiḥsādhanopadheyaṃ sarvārtheṣu sarvaprayojaneṣu sāmarthyaṃ yasminnastīti tathoktam |
apaviddhāḥ pradhvaṃsitā viplavāḥ krodhamohamātsaryādaya uparāgāḥ kāmalobhamadādayo'vidyāsmitādayaśca yena tattathoktam |
ūrjasvalaṃ baliṣṭhamanapāyi |
antarjyotiṣo hṛdayāntarvartinaḥ parasya jyotiṣaḥ paramātmano darśanaṃ sākṣātkāraḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
taduktaṃ yogaśāstre- ``jyotiḥpuñjaṃ nirākāraṃ lakṣayenmuktidaṃ bhavet |
jyotiḥsvarūpamamalaṃ hṛdi bāhyahīnaṃ paśyanti ye ta iha saṃyaminaḥ kṛtārthāḥ'' |
iti sarvārthaśabdena ``siddhayo'ṣṭādaśa proktā dhāraṇāyogapāragaiḥ |
tāsāmaṣṭau matpradhānā daśaiva guṇahetavaḥ||'' ityārabhya ``upāsakasya māmevaṃ yogadhāraṇayā muneḥ |
siddhayaḥ pūrvakathitā upatiṣṭhantyaśeṣataḥ||'' ityantaṃ bhāgavatagatabhagavadvacanamanusaṃdheyam |
abahiḥsādhanopadheyetyatra ``janauṣadhitapomantrairyāvatīriha siddhayaḥ |
yoegenāpnoti tāḥ sarvā nānyairyogagatirvrajet||'' iti vacanaṃ draṣṭavyam |
apaviddhaviplavetyatra ``kleśānāṃ tu kṣayakaraṃ yogādanyanna vidyate'' ityādikaṃ parāśaravacanamanusaṃdheyam |
yata iti |
yena prajñānena karaṇenāpamṛtyumakālamṛtyuvatprāṇaharaṃ paramātmānubhavalakṣaṇaprāṇasaṃbandhahānikaraṃ pāpmānaṃ pāpaphalabhūtasaṃsāram |
``astrī paṅkaṃ pumānpāpmā'' ityamaraḥ |
tarati laṅghate |
saṃsārānmuktobhavatītyarthaḥ |
tādṛśaṃ prajñānaṃ phalato'nuṣaṅgato viṣayataśca prakṛṣṭaṃ jñānaṃ yato yasmādabhisaṃbhavati samyagupapadyate tatprajñānasādhanaduścaritaviratatvādikaṃ brāhmaṇenācaritavyam |
hiśabdo vaisikaprasiddhiṃ sūcayati |
tathāca śrutiḥ- ``nāvirato duścaritānnāśānto nāsamāhitaḥ |
nāśāntamanaso vāpi prajñānenainamāpnuyāt||'' iti |
anyatra prajñānasādhanādanyatra brāhmaṇasya śāstraḥ pratiṣiddhe śāstraparigrahādāvabhiniviṣṭo'si baddhaśraddho'si |
``eṣvartheṣvabhiniviṣṭānām'' iti samarthasūtre bhāṣyaprayogāt ``abhiniviśaśca'' iti karmasaṃjñāvirahādanyatreti saptamī |
pariṣaditi |
ṛṣīṇāmiyaṃ pariṣadgoṣṭhī |
``samajyā pariṣadgoṣṭhī'' ityamaraḥ |
vīro yudhājitkuśadhvajo romāṇi pādayoryasya sa tathokto vṛddhaḥ sthaviraḥ |
ativārdhake hi pādeṣu romāṇi rohantīti lokaprasiddhiḥ |
amātyaiḥ siddhārthādibhirmantribhiḥ saha nṛpatirdaśaratho'viratayajñaḥ saṃtatānuṣṭhīyamānamokṣārthadarśapūrṇamāsajyotiṣṭomādiyajñaḥ purāṇaścirantano brahma paramātmānaṃ vadatīti brahmavadī |
tācchīlye ṇiniḥ |
ayaṃ janakānāṃ prabhurapi sīradhvajo'pītyete'druhaḥ santaḥ |
na druhyantītyadruhaḥ |
``satsūdviṣadruhaduha-'' ityādinā kartari kvip |
tvayyanaparādhina eva santaste tava yācakāḥ |
yācanta iti yācakāḥ |
kartari ṇvul |
śamamiti śeṣaḥ |
kṛtāparādhairapi yācanāyāmanugrahaḥ kāryaḥ |
``prasādaṃ pitṛhantaṝṇāmapi kurvanti sādhavaḥ'' iti hi śāstram |
kiṃ punarakṛtāparādhairuttamairyācñāyāmiti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā te tavādruho drohābhāvasya |
``druha jighāṃsāyām'' bhāve kvip |
yācakā bhavanti |
tava rāmaviṣaye jighāṃsānivṛttiṃ yācanta ityarthaḥ||5||

 etadbhavadbhirucyamānaṃ sarvam |
kiṃtviti pūrvoktārthavyatireke |
śatrumūlamiti |
śatrūṇāṃ mūlaṃ punaḥprarohahetutvānmūlatvārūpaṇaṃ, kṣatriyatvātsvaṃ prati śatrumūlatvaṃ, dhanurbhañjakatvātsvācāryaṃ prati śatrutvamiti bhāvaḥ |
anutkhāyānuddhṛtya |
``khanu avadāraṇe'' ityasmāllyapi ``ye vibhāṣā'' ityātvam |
tryambakaṃ tryakṣamācāryaṃ dhanurvidyopadeṣṭāramācāryastrīmācāryanīṃ pārvatīṃ ca |
``ācāryādaṇatvaṃ ca'' iti ṅīṣānugāgamau ṇatvābhāvaśca |
punardraṣṭuṃ notsahe na vyavasyāmi |
``utsāho vyavasāyaḥ syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
yadyapi tau kāruṇikau tathāpi nāhaṃ tadaparādhinamahatvāt taddarśanārha iti bhāvaḥ||6||

 guruṣvanurudhyase snehavānbhavasi yadi |
``ano rudha kāme'' iti divādiḥ |
tatastasmādgurvanurodhāddhetorimāvapi vaśiṣṭhaśatānandāvapi kiṃciccetayasva |
anuroddhavyatvena kṣaṇaṃ jānīhītyarthaḥ |
``citī saṃjñāne'' curādiḥ |
gurutvamupapādayati- hiraṇyagarbhāditi |
te trayo vasiṣṭhabhṛgvaṅgirasa ṛṣayo hiraṇyagarbhāccaturmukhādbabhūvurutpannāḥ |
teṣvityadhyāhāraḥ |
pūrvayacchabdasya tacchabdaniyatatvāt |
prasiddho hi yattadornityasaṃbandhaḥ |
ayaṃ teṣu sa vasiṣṭhaḥ |
teṣāṃ madhye prasiddho vasiṣṭha ityarthaḥ |
tvaṃ teṣu bhṛgunandanaḥ |
teṣvanyatamasya bhṛgorvaṃśya ityarthaḥ |
eṣo'pi śatānando'pi teṣu tasyāṅgirasaḥ prapautrasteṣvanyatamasyāṅgirasaḥ pautrasya putraḥ |
sa tvaṃ tasyāṅgirasa iti tacchabdayacchabdau pratinirdeśau na bhavataḥ |
bhṛguviṣaye tacchabdādarśanāt |
tanmātraviṣayatacchabdādhyāhāre tu bhṛgunandana iti samāso na syādatasteṣvityadhyāhāra āvaśyaka iti draṣṭavyam |
atra ``athābhidhyāyataḥ sargaṃ daśa putrāḥ prajajñire |
bhagavacchaktiyuktasya prajāsaṃtānahetavaḥ/ |
marīciratryaṅgirasau pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kratuḥ |
bhṛgurvasiṣṭho dakṣaśca daśamastatra nāradaḥ||'' iti bhāgavatavacanamanusaṃdheyam |
``prāṇādvasiṣṭhaḥ saṃjāto bhṛgustvagjaḥ karātkratuḥ |
aṅgirā mukhato'kṣṇo'triḥ'' ityetadapīha vivakṣitam |
hiraṇyagarbhātkūṭasthādaṅgirāstasmāducathyastasmādgautamastasmācchatānandaḥ |
evaṃ kūṭasthāddhiraṇyagarbhādbhṛgurbhṛgordhātā vidhātā ca |
tataḥ krameṇarcīkastasmājjamadagnistasmātparaśurāmaḥ |
evaṃ saṃkhyādhikye tatsthānenāvaraṃ paraśurāmaṃ prati śatānandaḥ sthānenādhikaḥ |
vasiṣṭhastu kaniṣṭhaprapitāmahatvādvṛddhatvācca sthānavayobhyāmadhika iti tayoḥ paraśurāmaṃ prati gurusthānīyatvam |
kiṃca śatānando jyeṣṭhasya śreṣṭhāvayavabhavasyāṅgiraso'patyatvādapi paraśurāmaṃ prati gurusthānīya iti tāvapyanuroddhavyāviti bhāvaḥ||7||

 prāyaścittamiti |
atra viśvāmitreti saṃbuddhntamupaskāryam |
he viśvāmitra, pūjyānāṃ gauravyāṇāṃ vo yuṣmākaṃ vyatikramātpratilomyakaraṇānnimittādyuṣmadyācitaśamākaraṇanimittātprāyaścittaṃ pratyavāyaparihārakaṃ karma cariṣyāmi |
śastragraha eva mahāvrataṃ mahābalasādhanam |
avaśyakartavyatvena saṃkalpitadharmaṃ tu naiva dūṣayiṣyāmi naiva lopayiṣyāmi |
``duṣa vaikṛtye'' ityasmāṇṇic |
``doṣo ṇau'' iti dīrghaḥ |
atra pūrvoktarītyā paraśurāmaṃ prati vasiṣṭhaśatānandayoḥ pūjyatvaṃ viśvāmitrasya tu paraśurāmaṃ prati piturmātulatvācca pūjyatvamiti draṣṭavyam |
``prāyaḥ pāpaṃ vijānīyāccittaṃ tasya viśodhanam'' iti smaraṇātprāyaścittaśabdaḥ pratyavāyanivartakadharmavācakaḥ |
``prāyasya citticittayoḥ'' iti suḍāgamaḥ |
atra ``putrādijanakaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ guruṃ cāpi na pīḍayet |
ekaśabdena nāmoktvā tvaṃkāraṃ huṃkṛtiṃ ca vā/ |
taddoṣaparihārārthaṃ nāciketavrataṃ caret |
kṛcchrāndvādaśarātraṃ caritvā sacailasnāto guruprasādātpūto bhavati'' iti skāndavacanamabhipretam |
pīḍayedityaniṣṭakaraṇamiṣṭākaraṇaṃ ca vivakṣitam |
yadyapi ``nāstikyaṃ prati lopaśca'' iti yājñavalkyasmṛtyā śastragrahavratatyāge satyupapātakaṃ vrajati, tathāpi guruvacanāttatyāge garuvyatikramaprayuktapratyavāyo na bhavati |
``aṣṭau tānyavrataghnāni āpo mūlaṃ ghṛtaṃ payaḥ |
havirbrāhmaṇakāmyā ca gurorvacanamauṣadham||'' iti smaraṇādvratalopadoṣañca na bhavet |
ato guruvacanānurodhācchastragraha eva tyājya itīmāṃ śaṅkāṃ nivartayituṃ tuśabdaḥ śastragrahavrataparipālane mahatastatphalasyāvāptiriti tadeva paripālanīyam |
guruvyatikramadoṣastu prāyaścittenaiva nivartayituṃ śakya iti bhāvaḥ |
kiñcoktarītyā vasiṣṭhādayo janmanaiva guravaḥ, bhagavānsa tryaṃbakastu janmanā vidyayā ca guruḥ, so'pi hiraṇyagarbhānmarīcyādibhiḥ pūrvameva jātaḥ |
taduktaṃ bhāgavate- ``dhiyā nigṛhyamāṇo'pi bhruvormadhyātprajāpateḥ |
sadyo'jāyata tanmanyuḥ sa rudro nīlalohitaḥ||'' iti |
``atho vedopadeśācca vidyayā guruḥ'' ityubhayaprakāragurostryambakasyānurodhāpekṣayā janmanaiva gurūṇāṃ yuṣmākaṃ vyatikramo'kiṃcitkara ityabhiprāyeṇa mahāvratamiti mahacchabdaḥ |
tathā coktarītyā gurulaghuvicāre phalāphalavicāre ca gurutvātphalavattvācca śastragrahavratameva paripālanīyam |
laghutvātpratyavāyaparihārātiriktaphalaśūnyatvātpratyavāyasya prāyaścittenāpyapanodayituṃ śakyatvācca vasiṣṭhānurodhastyājya iti paraśurāmasyābhiprāyaḥ||8||

 bhavatvevam, tathāpi pūrvoktayogānuṣṭhānena saṃsārataraṇalakṣaṇaniratiśayaphale yatasva |
śastragrahavrataphalaṃ hi tadapekṣayātyalpameva tathā ca mokṣopāyāṅgatayā śamādisaṃpādakatvena śastragrahatyāga eva kriyatāmityatrāha- yata iti |
yato yena nisargeṇa svabhāvaviśeṣeṇa |
``saṃsiddhiprakṛtī tvime |
svarūpaṃ ca svabhāvaśca nisargaśca'' ityamaraḥ |
vimuktermokṣāt |
apiśabdena bandhubhyo yuṣmacca |
``pañcamī vibhakte'' iti pañcamī |
mānasya śauryavijayādyutkarṣaprayuktapūjātiśayasya rakṣaṇaṃ paripālanaṃ me priyamiṣṭaṃ bhavati tathā tathābhūtaṃ svabhāvaṃ paśya |
vimukteḥ svataḥsundaratve'pi na kevalaṃ vastusaundaryamicchāyāṃ hetuḥ, kiṃtu ``annaṃ bhojyaṃ manuṣyāṇāmamṛtaṃ tu divaukasām'' iti devamanuṣyatvādi svabhāvabhedena bhojyavyavasthānena bhogādṛṣṭasahakṛtabhoktṛsvabhāvo'pi |
tatra kāraṇaṃ nirūpayasveti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃca yūyaṃ mānavisraṃsanārthino bhavantaḥ sanābhayaḥ |
``sapiṇḍāstu sanābhayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``jyotirjanapada-'' ityādinā samānasya sabhāvaḥ |
śarīraghaṭitaparaṃparāsaṃbandhena saṃbandhino bahiraṅgāḥ, śarāsanajyāyāścāpamaurvyāḥ |
``jyā maurvībhūmimātṛṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
kiṇāni vraṇapadāni lāñchanāni yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
karkaśaḥ sāhasikaḥ |
``syātkarkaśaḥ sāhasikaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
bhujo bāhurmānarakṣaṇārthayojano bāhurayaṃ śarīratvātsākṣātsaṃbandhī sannantaraṅga ityarthaḥ |
tathā ca ``antaraṅgabahiraṅgayorantaraṅgo balīyaḥ'' iti bhujābhimatamānarakṣaṇameva prabalam |
tattu bhavadabhimatamānavisraṃsanamiti bhāvaḥ |
pūrvokto mama nisargaśca |
mama bhujalāñchanaireva vābhiviśada iti cābhiprāyaḥ||9||

 saṃpūjitamiti |
saṃpūjitaṃ praśastaṃ māhātmyaṃ dhṛtimattvādiprakarṣaṃ pade pade sthāne sthāne sulabhadveṣaṃ hi vīravrataṃ śatṛmūlamanutkhāya natveva dūṣayiṣyāmi |
vimukterapi mānarakṣaṇaṃ priyamiti bahuṣu sthaleṣvityarthaḥ |
udgirantyaḥ prakāśayantyo vācaḥ paraśurāmavacanāni marmāṇi hṛdayāni svalpena hetunāpi maraṇahetubhūtāni śarīrasthānani |
mriyateḥ ``sarvadhātubhyo manin'' iti maninpratyayaḥ |
