vidhyanti ghnantīti marmāvidhaḥ |
kartari kvip |
``nahivṛti-'' ityādinā pūrvapadasya dīrghaśca |
rāmabhadravyāpārādagarbhatvādduḥkhahetubhūtāḥ satyo'pītyarthaḥ |
māṃ vīravāsanāvāsitaṃ māṃ romāñcayanti romāñcavantaṃ kurvanti |
``prātipadikāddhātvarthe bahulamiṣṭhavacca'' iti romāñcavacchabdāṇṇic, matupo lopaśca |
uktipratyuktisauṣṭhavena vīrarasodgāreṇa ca vīravāsanāvāsitaṃ māṃ harṣayantīti satyamityarthaḥ||10||

 brahmeti |
visiṣṭhamiśrāḥ |
miśraśabdo bahuvacanaṃ ca pūjāyām |
tathā ca pūjyo vasiṣṭha ityarthaḥ |
brahmaṇi brāhmaṇyocitaśamadamādiṣvekatānamananyavṛtti mano yeṣāṃ tathoktā bhavanti |
``ekatāno'nanyavṛttiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
yuddhādiṣūdāsīnā ityarthaḥ |
hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau |
vīrāṇāṃ cariteṣu mānarakṣaṇādiṣu purāṇaścirantano gururupadeṣṭā tvaṃ brūhi vada |
vakṣyamāṇārthanirṇayamiti śeṣaḥ |
yena mayā viśuddhimati dṛḍhavratatvādiśuddhibhūyiṣṭhe bhṛgoḥ |
bharjayatyaniṣṭamiti bhṛguriti vyutpattyāniṣṭatāpanaikasvabhāvasya bhṛgorvaṃśe janitvā śastraṃ gṛhītaṃ tasya me |
atra śastragrahaṇatatsanyāsaviṣaye kiṃ yuktam |
athaśabdaḥ praśnārthakaḥ |
kiṃ yuktaṃ pṛcchyata ityarthaḥ |
vaṃśabhāvādapi śastragrahavrataṃ pālanīyamiti bhāvaḥ |
``prathimradibhrasjāṃ saṃprasāraṇaṃ salopaśca'' iti bhrasjateḥ kupratyayaḥ saṃprasāraṇaṃ salopo nyaṅkvāditvātkutvaṃ ca |
``bhṛjjati tapasā bhṛguḥ'' iti śābdikāḥ |
``bhrasjo pāke'' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ||11||

 kāmamiti |
eṣa paraśurāmo guṇairvīryaśauryadṛḍhavratatvādibhirmahānatiśayitaḥ |
kāmamityanujñāyām |
asya guṇamahattvaṃ māmanumatamevetyarthaḥ |
``smarābhilāṣayoḥ kāmo'nujñāyāṃ kāmamavyayam'' iti ratnamālā |
punaḥśabdastuśabdārthakaḥ |
``punaraprathame bhede'' ityamaraḥ |
kiṃtu prakṛtyā svabhāvataḥ |
``prakṛtyādibhya upasaṃkhyānam'' iti tṛtīyā |
asura evāsuraḥ |
prajñāderākṛtigaṇatvātsvārthe'ṇpratyayaḥ |
asurasvabhāvabhūyiṣṭha ityarthaḥ |
tatra hetumāha- utkarṣāditi |
hi yasmātsarvataḥ sarveṣu prakāreṣu vṛttirvartanaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasmādutkarṣātiśayāddhetoḥ sarva ākārā mānasatvavācikatvakāyikatvādayaḥ prakārā yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktaṃ yathā tathā dṛpyati garvavānbhavati |
``darpo'haṃkārakastūryoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
``dṛpa harṣamohanayoḥ'' iti divādiḥ |
śauryavīryābhijātyādyatiśayaprayuktadarpāddhetoreṣa āsuraprakṛtirityarthaḥ |
darpo hyāsurasvabhāvaḥ |
tathā ca bhagavatā gītam- ``dambho darpo'bhimānaśca krodhaḥ pāruṣyameva ca |
ajñānaṃ cābhijātasya pārtha saṃpadamāsurīm||'' iti dambhādayaḥ svabhāvā ityarthaḥ||12||

 vatseti |
bhaginīpautratvādvatsetyāmantraṇam |
etadvakṣyamāṇaṃ bravīmi vadāmi |
tvaṃ brūhīti |
tyokto'hamidaṃ bravīmītyarthaḥ |
ekavyaktīti |
tvaṃ prākpūrvakāla ekayā vyāktyā kṣattriyatvajātyāśrayeṇa kenacidaparādhāddhomadhanuharaṇādirūpāniṣṭācaraṇādyaḥ kopastena vikṛtaḥ sanvikṛtiṃ prāptaḥ sannādhārāṇāṃ kṣattriyatvajātyādhārabhūtavyaktīnāṃ niranvayapramathanānniranvayavināśajanānādapunaḥprarohārhadhvaṃsotpādanāt |
nāśo dvividhaḥ- sānvayavināśaḥ niranvayavināśaśceti |
tatra dīpāderuttarottarajvālodayaśiraskaḥ pūrvajvālāvināśaḥ sānvayavināśaḥ |
nirvāpaṇādinā sajātīyajvālodayānarhavināśastu niranvayavināśaḥ |
tathāvidhaniranvayavināśajanānātkṣatrajāteḥ kṣattriyatvajāterucchedameva sarvadeśasarvakāleṣu vilayamevākaroḥ kṛtavānasi |
ekavyaktyekāśritāyā jātervyaktināśe nirādhārasthityasambhavāduccheda eveti phalitārthaḥ |
kiṃca vipraśukrajamapi kṣatraṃ viprebhyaḥ kṣattriyāsūtpannā api kṣattriyāḥ |
``śukraṃ tejoretasī ca'' ityamaraḥ |
triḥsaptāvadhīti pūrvottarānvayi |
triḥsaptāvadhyucchedamakaroḥ |
triḥsaptāvadhyuddhṛtamityanvetavyam |
sapta saptatvasaṃkhyāvadhiḥ paricchedikā yasyā uddharaṇakriyāyā iti kriyāviśeṣaṇamidam |
saptaśabdaḥ saptatvasaṃkhyāparaḥ śaktyā lakṣaṇayā veti pakṣadvayamapi śābdikaiḥ sūcitam |
tririti sucpratyayāntam |
tadarthaścoddharaṇakriyāyāṃ saptatvāvacchedenānvetītyekaviṃśatikṛtva iti labhyate |
sujarthasya kriyaikānvayitvasvābhāvyena saṃnihitenāpi saptatvenānvayāsaṃbhavāt |
tathaiva sākṣātkṣattriyavadevoddhṛtam |
dhvaṃsitā ityarthaḥ |
tvayetyanuṣaṅgavipariṇāmau |
kiṃca |
vṛddhaiḥ sthānavayaḥprabhṛtinādhikaiścyavanādibhiḥ svairjñātibhiḥ |
``svo'strī dhane pumāñjñātau triṣvātmīye svamātmani'' iti ratnamālā |
niyamitaḥ sanniyantritaḥ sankrodhātpūrvoktaikavyaktyaparādhakopajanitātkopādvyaraṃsīrvirato'bhūrityetadbravīmīti pūrveṇānvayaḥ |
pūrvameva śastragrahavratasya samāpitatvātpūrvoktaphalāphalavicāro'trānavasaragrasta iti bhāvaḥ |
vyaraṃsīrityatra ``vyāṅparibhyo ramaḥ'' iti parasmaipadam |
``yamaramanama-'' ityādinā sagiṭau||13||

 vyaraṃsiṣamiti |
pitṛvadhaprayuktāttanmūlakātkṣattriyāṇāṃ vadhaviṣayakamahādhikārādvyaraṃsiṣaṃ virato'bhūvam |
tathā ca nimittabhedātpunaḥ kopaḥ śastragrahaṇaṃ cādya nyāyyameveti bhāvaḥ |
atra pitṛvadhaprayuktakopavirāme nihnavo'palāpaḥ |
``apalāpastu nihnavaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
paraśuriti |
aśanivaccaṇḍastīkṣṇaḥ paraśuḥ priyamapi kṣatraghātaṃ kṣattriyavadhaṃ vihāya tyaktvā samidhāṃ prādeśaparimitānāṃ palāśādidaṇḍakhaṇḍānāmidhmānāṃ taddviguṇānāṃ palāśādīndhanakhaṇḍānāṃ vraścanaśchedanakaraṇaṃ na jātaḥ kim |
jāta evetyarthaḥ |
``vraśca chedane'' ityasmāt ``karaṇādhikaraṇayośca'' iti lyuṭ |
yadyapyamareṇa samididhmaśabdayoḥ ``idhmamedhaḥ samitstriyām'' iti paryāyatvamuktaṃ tathāpi ``samitpavitramedhaśca trayaḥ prādeśasaṃmitāḥ |
idhmaṃ tu dviguṇaṃ kāryaṃ triguṇaḥ paridhiḥ smṛtaḥ||'' iti smṛtyanurodhādaparyāyatvamiti draṣṭavyam |
cāpadaṇḍo'pi dhanurdaṇḍaśca |
nibhṛtā niścalā viśikhā eva bāṇā eva daṃṣṭrā yasya sa tathoktaḥ sanpraśamito nirvāpito viṣameva vahniryasya sa tathoktastathāvidhasyāśīviṣasya sarpasya sāmyamaupamyaṃ na dhatte kim |
dhatta evetyarthaḥ |
kiṃnaśabdayoranuṣaṅgaḥ |
ato'tra kiṃ nihnava iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
yadyapi viśikhadaṃṣṭreti rūpakaṃ prakramya sāmyaṃ dhatta ityupamāyāṃ samāpitam, tathāpi rūpakopamayoranatyantabhedānna doṣaḥ |
taduktam- ``upamaiva tirobhūtabhedo rūpakamiṣyate'' iti||14||

 evamiti |
ślokadvayamekavākyam |
cyavanādīnāṃ vākyairhetubhiḥ mayā kartrā |
evaṃ pūrvaślokoktaprakāreṇa niyamito nivartitaḥ kopānalaśca paraśuścetyetau raghusutena rāmeṇa saṃprati devasya rudrasya dhanuṣo mathanena bhaṅgena punaḥ satyaṃ yāthārthyena yathotthāpitau yena prakāreṇotthāpitau bhagavatastathā tena prakāreṇa kṛtaṃ cāpalaṃ durvinītatvaṃ yena sa tathoktasya |
``capalaḥ pārade kīśe pumānstrīṣu tu cañcale |
durvinīte drute nīce'' iti ratnamālā |
ekasya rāghavaśiśo rāmasya śiraḥ prasahya haṭhāt ``prasahya tu haṭhārthakam'' ityamaraḥ |
kṛttvā chittvā |
``kṛtī cchedane'' āgamaśāsanasyānityatvādiḍāgamābhāvaḥ |
mayi vanāya |
``kriyārtha-'' ityādinā caturthī |
vanaṃ gantuṃ punaḥ prayāte sati raghavo janakāśca svasthā nirupadravāścirāya ciraṃ santu |
atiprasaṅgo gurutiraskārādidurvinayaprastāvaḥ punaḥ kathañcinmābhūnna bhavet |
atraikasya janakā raghavaścetyanena ``tyajedekaṃ kulasyārthe'' iti nyāyaḥ sūcitaḥ||15||

16||

āḥ, śaktirityādi |
``āstu syātkopapīḍayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
paramapuruṣārthabhūtabhagavadviṣayavidveṣiṣu ``kopaḥ sarvātmanā kāryaḥ'' ityayamartho'tra kavinā śatānandādimukhenāviṣkṛta iti draṣṭavyam |
yājyasya yājayitumarhasya |
yajerṇyantāt ``aco yat'' iti yatpratyayaḥ |
preyaso'tiśayena priyasya cchāyāmapyanātapamapi |
samīpadeśamapīti yāvat |
avaskanditumākrandituṃ kasya śaktirastītyanvayaḥ |
``skandir gatiśoṣaṇayoḥ'' ityasmāttumun |
jāmātaramavaskandituṃ kasya śaktiriti kiṃ punarvaktavyamityarthaḥ |
``jāmātā duhituḥ patiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vayamiti |
sucaritānyevaṃ śrutismṛtyācārapramāṇakāni satkarmāṇyeva guravaḥ stambhā mahatyaḥ sthūṇā ādhārāḥ patanapratirodhakā yasya tattathoktaṃ tasmin |
``stambhau sthūṇājaḍībhāvau'' ityamaraḥ |
atra ``śatrubhaṅgaḥ suvṛttatā'' ityetadabhipretaṃ gṛhamedhināṃ janakadaśarathādigṛhasthāśramaniṣṭhānāṃ gṛhe gehe |
``gṛhaṃ grahāśca bhūmni syāddvāvimau gehabhāryayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
vayamivāhaṃ vasiṣṭhādayaścaiva vahniḥ gṛhyo vaśyaḥ |
pakṣimṛgavadgṛhāsakto vā bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``karmāvabodhake gṛhyamayaṃ svavaśage triṣu |
gṛhyaṃ śākhāpuraṃ gṛhyā gṛhāsaktā mṛgāḥ khagāḥ||'' iti ratnamālā |
``padāsvairibāhyāpakṣyeṣu ca'' iti gṛhṇāteḥ kyap |
kiṃ ca vayaṃ tathā ciraṃ sthitāstathaiva vahniściraṃ sthitaḥ |
tatra ``gṛhamedhināṃ gṛhe ghaṭo'sti tamānaya'' ityatreva tacchabdaḥ pūrvaparāmarśī |
anyasmātparibhavo'nyahetukatiraskāro yadyupaiti prasaktaścettattadā priyamapi prāṇebhyo'pīṣṭaṃ tapa upavāsādiniyamajātam |
``niyameṣu tapaḥśabdaḥ'' ityāpastambaḥ |
dhigastu |
tapaḥkarmikā nindā syādityarthaḥ |
dhikśabdārthasyāstikriyāyāṃ kartṛtvam |
yadvāstvityavyayamasūyārthakam, asūyā ca tapaḥprabhṛtiṣvakiṃcitkaratvābhisaṃdhinā kriyata iti draṣṭavyam |
``asūyopagame'stu ca'' ityamaraḥ |
brāhmaṇyaṃ vedādhyayanādyupayuktam |
brāhmaṇavarṇaṃ dhik |
aṅgirasaḥ kulaṃ dhik |
`` aṅgirā mukhato jajñe'' iti viśiṣya brahmaṇo mukhajātasyāṅgirasaḥ kulaṃ dhik |
abhijanavidyāvṛttāni dhigityarthaḥ ||17||

 gautama gautamaputra |
kṛtāni kṛtyāni yena sa tathoktaḥ |
svayaṃ phalārthavyāpārarahito'pi tvadīyavyāpāreṇa phalabhāgityarthaḥ |
neti |
brāhmaṇo mukhyabrāhmaṇyavān |
``brāhmaṇo'sau ṣaṭkarmā yāgādibhiryutaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vidvānsadā tvadīyābhyudayasādhanāvamarśanaparaḥ |
``vidaḥ śaturvā'' iti kvasuḥ |
tvaṃ yasya sīradhvajasya |
rāṣṭraṃ janapadaṃ gopāyati rakṣatīti rāṣṭragopaḥ |
``gupū rakṣaṇe'' ityasmātkarmaṇyaṇ |
``rāṣṭraṃ viṣaya uptāte'' iti ratnamālā |
tathāvidhaḥ purohito bhavasi |
na kevalaṃ śrautasmārtakarmaṇi bhaviṣyasi taddarśī |
kiṃtu rājyaviṣaye'pīti bhāvaḥ |
tasya tathāvidhasya sīradhvajasya rāṣṭraṃ na vyathate kutaścidapi na bibheti |
yadvāna navyathate prakṛtiviparyāsaṃ na bhajate |
``vyatha bhayasaṃcalanayoḥ'' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ |
saṃcalanaṃ nāma prakṛtiviparyāsa ityuktam |
na riṣyati na hinasti |
parasparamiti śeṣaḥ |
``ruṣa riṣa hiṃsāyām'' iti ca dhātupāṭhaḥ |
yadvā preraṇāṃśatyāgādduḥkhāśrayo na bhavatītyarthaḥ |
hiṃsā hi duḥkhotpādanam |
na jīryatyakāle śaithilyaṃ na prāpnoti |
``jaṝṣ jhaṝṣ vayohānau'' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ||18||

 kṣattriyapurohitaiḥ kartṛbhirbrahmatejasā brāhmaṇyaprayuktaparābhibhavanasāmarthyarūpaprabhāveṇa karaṇena sphuritamāsītsphuraṇamabhūt |
kiṃcidvyāpāro'bhūditi yāvat |
kiṃtvevaṃ sphuraṇe prākṛtāni pṛthagjaneṣu bhavāni |
``prākṛtaśca pṛthagjanaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``tatra bhavaḥ'' ityaṇ |
atrāṅke niyatāptiprakarīyogātmako vimarśasaṃdhiruktaḥ |
taduktam-``garbhasaṃdhau prasiddhasya bījārthasyāvamarśanam |
hetunā yena kenāpi vimarśaḥ saṃdhiriṣyate/ |
niyatāptiprakaryukteraṅgānyasya trayodaśa |
tatrāpavādaḥ saṃpheṭo vidravadravaśaktayaḥ/ |
dyutiḥ prasaṅgaścalanaṃ vyavasāyo nirodhanam |
prarocanaṃ vicalanamādānaṃ ca trayodaśa||'' iti |
tathā- ``apāyābhāvataḥ kāryaniścayo niyatāptikā |
avyāpinī prakarikā'' iti ca paraśurāmaprayuktāpāyābhāvena nirvighnarāmaśreyorūpakāryaniścayasya tadupayuktaśatānandādyavyāpikathāṃśasya ca yogādbījārthāvarmaśanarūpavimarśasaṃdhiḥ |
asyāṅgānyuttaratra darśayiṣyante/ |
anaḍvangarvoddhata |
gauṇavṛttyā nirdeśaḥ |
vṛṣabho hi darpastimito bhavati |
puruṣādhama parābhyudayāsahiṣṇo |
parasamṛddhisahiṣṇutvaṃ hyuttamapuruṣatvam, tadvyatirekaḥ puruṣādhamatvam, niraparādhetyādi |
homadhenvapaharaṇādinā kasyacidaparādhitve'pyanyasyānaparādhitvāditi bhāvaḥ |
kadana vaiklavyakārinvaikalyakārinniti vārthaḥ |
``kadi kradi kladi vaiklavye'' |
``vaikalye'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
trayo'pyanidita iti nandī |
tena numabhāvaḥ |
mahāpātakin |
mahāpātakaśabdo brahmahatyādiṣu yogarūḍhaḥ |
tasmādiniḥ |
yadvā mahāpātakaṃ brahmahatyādirūpamatiśayenāstītyatiśayavivakṣāyāṃ karmadhārayādiniḥ |
atiśayo hi na bahuvrīhilabhyaḥ |
nātaḥ ``na karmadhārayāt'' iti niṣedhaḥ |
atiśayitabrahmahatyā ca kārtavīryavadharūpā |
taduktaṃ bhāgavate jamadagninā- ``rājño mūrdhābhiṣiktasya vadho brahmavadhādguruḥ||'' iti |
aśiṣṭavedaprāmāṇyābhyupagamarahita |
brāhmaṇasyāyudhagrahaṇapratiṣedhātikramāditi bhāvaḥ |
vikṛtaveṣa |
paraśudhanurbāṇādibrāhmaṇyaviruddhavastudhāraṇācchastrakṣatakiṇadhāraṇāceti bhāvaḥ |
bībhatsyāni jugupsanīyāni karmāṇi yasya sa tathoktasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
``vadheścittavikāre'' iti jugupsāyāṃ vadheḥ san |
tataḥ karmaṇi ``aco yat'' iti yat apūrvapāṣaṇḍa nūtanapāṣaṇḍa |
``bhavavratadharā ye ca ye ca tānsamanuvratāḥ |
pāṣaṇḍinaste'' ityukteriti bhāvaḥ |
``tasmāttu pāpmano liṅgaṃ pāṣaṇḍamiti kathyate'' ityuktapāpaliṅgadharetyarthaḥ |
``pāṣaṇḍāḥ sarvaliṅginaḥ'' ityamaraśca |
kāṇḍā bāṇā asya santīti kāṇḍīrastasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
``sāyakaḥ pradaraḥ kāṇḍakaṅkapatraśilīmukhāḥ'' ityamaraśeṣaḥ |
``kāṇḍāṇḍādīrannīracau'' iti matvarthīya īranpratyayaḥ |
``syātkāṇḍavāṃstu kāṇḍīraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kāṇḍapṛṣṭha āyudhajīvin |
``śastrājīve kāṇḍapṛṣṭhāyudhīyā yudhikāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
āyudhajīvitvaṃ brāhmaṇasya niṣiddham |
pragalbhase dhārṣṭyaṃ karoṣi |
re iti nīcasaṃbodhanam |
nanu ceti virodhoktiḥ |
tvaṃ kiṃ brāhmaṇa evāsi kasmindeśe kīdṛśabrāhmaṇyavānasi |
kṣepoktiriyam/aho iti |
brāhmaṇasyācāraḥ |
vismayanīya ityarthaḥ |
māturiti |
evakāraḥ prādhānyaparaḥ |
strīsāmānyavadhe mahāndoṣaḥ, kiṃ punaḥ pradhānabhūtāyā māturvadha iti bhāvaḥ |
yadvāvadhāraṇārthako guruvacanānurodhānmāturvadho na doṣāyeti śaṅkāvāraṇārthakaḥ |
guruvacanānurodho'pi mahāpātakavyatiriktaviṣaya eva yuktaḥ, na tu patanīyaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ |
tathā cāpastambaḥ- ``ācāryādhīnaḥ syādanyatra patanīyebhyaḥ'' iti, ``amuṃ mamārātiṃ brāhmaṇamitthaṃ vyāpādayetyācāryeṇokte evamādi na kuryāt'' iti tadviṣayā hyujjvalā |
tathā ca- ``piturdaśaguṇaṃ mātā gauraveṇātiricyate'' iti pituratijayena gauravyāyā māturvadhaḥ pitṛvacanamūlako'pi na yukta eva |
garbhāṇāmapakartanaṃ ca garbhiṇībhiḥ saha cchedaśca |
savanasthānāṃ yāgādidīkṣitānāṃ rājñāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ brahmahatyāsamo vadhaśceti brāhmaṇasyācāro vismayanīya iti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
brahmahatyāsama ityanena brahmahatyātideśo vivakṣitaḥ |
tathā ca manuḥ- ``hatvā garbhamavijñātametadeva vrataṃ caret |
rājanyavaiśyāvījānāvātreyīmeva ca striyam||'' iti |
ījānau yāgadīkṣāmadhyavartinau |
ātreyyatrigotrajā garbhiṇyṛtumatī ceti tadvyākhyātāraḥ |
viṣṇurapi- ``yāgasthakṣatriyasya vaiśyasya rajasvalāyāścāntarvatnyāścātrigotrajāyā avijñātagarbhasya śaraṇāgatasya ghātāni brahmahatyāsamāni'' iti |
kāmandakastu- ``karaṇe brahmahatyāsamatvaṃ kaimutikasiddham'' |
atra guṇajyeṣṭhasya mūrdhāvasiktasya vadho bhūyasā brahmavadhādguruḥ |
rājamātrasya yāgamadhyasthasya vadho brahmahatyāsamaḥ, yāgadīkṣārahitasya rājamātrasya vadho brahmahatyāyāsturīyāṃśasamaḥ |
tathā cāmaraḥ- ``turīyo brahmahatyāyāḥ kṣatriyasya vadhe smṛtaḥ'' iti||19||

 āḥ iti krodhe |
svastivācanena jīvati svastivācanikaḥ |
``vetanādibhyo jīvati'' ityanena ṭhakpratyayaḥ |
duṣṭa doṣayukteti saṃbuddhyantam |
sāmantaviśeṣaṇaṃ vā |
sāmantasya pārśvarājasya |
sārvabhaumatāśūnyasyeti yāvat |
purohita |
re iti nīcāhvāne |
ahalyāyāḥ putra |
indrāticārābhiprāyeṇāhalyāputratvoktiḥ |
``ṣaṣṭhyā ākrośe'' ityaluk |
ahaṃ tava kāṇḍapṛṣṭhaḥ |
tvadabhiprāyeṇāyudhajīvītyarthaḥ |
duṣṭeti |
durmukha paruṣavadana |
bhugṛprasavānāṃ bhṛguvaṃśyānāṃ pāṃsanāvadyāvaha |
avadyaparātpāṃsuśabdānmatvantāṇṇic |
matublope sati nandyāditvātkartari lyuḥ |
rājāna iti |
rājāno daśarathajanakādayaḥ |
guravo vasiṣṭhādayaḥ |
mahimnaiva mahāttvenaiva mahakṣamā āgaḥsahanarūpakṣamābhūyiṣṭhāḥ |
kṣamantāṃ nāma paraśurāmapāruṣyaṃ sahantāṃ nāma |
mahatāṃ kṣamāsvabhāvatvātteṣāṃ kṣamā yuktetyarthaḥ |
śatānandastu tadvatkṣamāprayojakamahadbhāvavirahito'hamityarthaḥ |
evaṃ vasiṣṭhādivannaiva kṣamiṣyate |
uttarakṣaṇa evāparādhānuguṇaṃ daṇḍaṃ prayojayāmītyarthaḥ |
atra śatānandajāmadagnyābhyāmanyonyadoṣaprakhyāpanādapavādo nāma vimarśasandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``doṣaprakhyāpanamapavādaḥ'' iti |
atraivānyonyaṃ roṣasaṃbhāṣaṇātsaṃpheṭo nāma sandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``roṣasaṃbhāṣaṇaṃ saṃpheṭaḥ'' iti||20||

 kamaṇḍalūdakenopaspṛśatyācamanaṃ karoti |
``upasparśatvācamanam'' ityamaraḥ |
karmaṇaḥ karaṇatvavivakṣayā tṛtīyā |
``ācāntena kartavyam'' iti niyamāditi/ |
prasādyatāmiti |
dhavitreṇa mṛgacarmaracitavyajanena |
``dhavitraṃ vyajanaṃ tadyadracitaṃ mṛgacarmaṇā'' ityamaraḥ |
nirdhūtaḥ samadhikajvalanārthamutkampito'bhipraṇītaḥ kṛtasaṃskāraḥ |
``praṇītaḥ saṃskṛto'nalaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
viśiṣṭavācakānāṃ padānāṃ viśeṣyavācakapadasamabhivyāhāre viśeṣaṇamātraparatvam |
pṛṣadājyasya dadhimiśrājyasya |
``pṛṣadājyaṃ sadadhyājye'' ityamaraḥ |
abhighāreṇābhitaḥ secanena |
``gṛ ghṛ secane'' ityasmādbhauvādikādbhāve ghañ |
ghoraḥ caṭacaṭātkāravisaṃṣṭhulajvālatayā bhayaṅkarastanūnapādagniriva |
``jātavedāstanūnapāt'' ityamaraḥ |
samidhyamānaṃ saṃyagdīpyamānam |
antarbhāvitaṇyarthāt ``indhī dīptau'' ityasmātkarmaṇi laṭaḥ śānac |
yaddāruṇaṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ brahmaṇo varcastejo brahmavarcasam |
``brahmahastibhyāṃ varcasaḥ'' ityac |
tadeva jyotiryasya sa tathoktaḥ |
brahmavarcasajyotiḥśabdayoḥ sāmānyaviśeṣabhāvānna punaruktiḥ |
yadvā brahmavarcasasya sadvṛttādhyayanasamṛddherjyotistejo yasyeti vigrahaḥ |
``syādbrahmavarcasaṃ vṛttādhyayanardhiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
prasādyatāṃ nivṛttakopaḥ kriyatām |
prasādavacchabdānnijantāt ``prātipadikāddhadhātvarthe'' ityādinā matublope karmaṇi loṭ/ |
sasaṃrambhamamiti |
saṃrambhaḥ sakrodhayatnaḥ |
śāpasya parāniṣṭupayikakrośasyodakamabhivyañjanakaṃ jalam |
sabhāyāṃ sīdantīti sabhāsadaḥ |
``satsūdviṣa-'' ityādinā sīdateḥ kartari kvip |
sakrodheti |
parasyāreḥ paraśurāmasya |
``paro'riḥ paramātmā ca'' iti ratnamālā |
abhighātātparibhavarūpahiṃsāddhetoḥ sakrodhaprasabhaṃ sakopavegam |
``prasabho vegaharṣayoḥ'' iti kośaḥ |
udbhūtotpannā drutagatiḥ śīghravyāpāro yasya tathāvidho'haṃ vo yuṣmākamātatāyinaṃ vadhodyatam |
``ātatāyī vadhodyataḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anena ātatāyinamāyāntaṃ hanyādevāvicārayan'' iti vacanaṃ sūcyate |
utpātena prāṇināmaśubhayogena hetunā kṣubhitena sañcalitena marutā pravātena vighaṭyamāno bhajyamāno vajrāgniraśanivahnirdrumamiva bhasmasātkaromi kṛtsnamevāmuṃ bhasma saṃpadyamānaṃ karomi |
``vibhāṣā sātikārtsnye'' iti sātipratyayaḥ |
atra vadhodyogādvidravo nāma sandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``vadhabandhādividravaḥ'' iti||21||

 prasīda prasādaṃ prāpnuhi |
gṛhānupagate |
paraśurāma iti śeṣaḥ |
``na kañcana vasatau pratyācakṣīta'' iti śruteriti bhāvaḥ |
durāsadaṃ durdharṣaṃ tapaḥprayuktaṃ parābhibhavanasāmarthyaṃ tejaḥ praśāmyatu praśāntaṃ bhavatu |
gṛhānupagata iti pūrvāparānvayi |
ślāghya iti |
paraśurāma ityādiḥ |
paraśurāmo guṇaiḥ śrotriyatvādibhiḥ ślāghyaḥ praśaṃsanīyaḥ |
kiṃcāyaṃ dvijavaro brāhmaṇaśreṣṭhaḥ |
kiṃcāyaṃ tava nijo bandhuḥ |
svīyaḥ sapiṇḍa ityarthaḥ |
``nijau svīyāvināśinau'' iti ratnamālā |
``sagotrabāndhavajñātibandhusvasvajanāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
gṛhānupagate tasminyuṣmadgṛhāgate tathāvidhaparaśurāmaviṣaya etadbhasmasātkaraṇaṃ sadṛśamanurūpaṃ kim |
nānurūpamityarthaḥ |
eṣa paraśurāmo vidvānapi jñātā sannapi |
atra ``viduṣo'tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvam'' ityetadvivakṣitam |
mārgādbrahmaṇyānuguṇācārarūpātpracalita iti yat, tatra vinayāya śikṣaṇāya kṣatraṃ hi kṣatravarṇo hi |
yogyamiti śeṣaḥ |
tuśabdaḥ śaṅkādyotakaḥ |
sā ca śaṅkā paraśurāmo'yamācāravyatikramāddaṇḍanīya ityākārikā |
tatra kṣatriya eva yogyo na brāhmaṇa iti parihāraḥ |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāddhe śatānanda, tvaṃ brāhmaṇottamastvaṃ śamaṃ krodhanivṛttiṃ bhaja prāpnuhi |
atra virodhaśamanācchaktirnāmasandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``virodhaśamanaṃ śaktiḥ'' iti||22||

 yathāha tathaivāstviti śeṣaḥ |
yaditi |
yatkalyāṇamagarhiteṣṭārthasiddhirūpaṃ rāmasya śreyastanmanasā vayamahaṃ viśvāmitrādayaśca vartayāma āśāsanena niṣpādayāmaḥ |
tvaṃ jābālyādityuktaḥ sannagnāvadhyagni |
vibhaktyarthe'vyayībhāvaḥ |
śāntimaniṣṭaśāntihomaṃ kuryāḥ kuru |
pūrvatrātra ca phalasya kartṛgāmitvavirahānnātmanepadam |
sa bhagavān |
``taddhyetatpaśyannṛṣirvāpadevaḥ pratipede'' iti śrutiprasiddhajñānādiṣāḍguṇyapūrṇo vāmadevo'smacchiṣyaiḥ saha sahitaḥ sanparānabhibhavituṃ jaitrāñjayahetubhūtān |
karaṇasya kartṛtvavivakṣayā tṛc |
jetaiva jaitraḥ |
prajñāderākṛtigaṇatvātsvārthe'ṇpratyayaḥ |
sūktāni śrīsūktapuruṣasūktādīni, sāmāni rathantarādīni, anuvākān ``śukrapravidhya'' ityādikān ``āśuḥ śiśānaḥ'' ityādikāṃśca japannupāṃśūccārayangṛṇātu paṭhatu |
japatiratropāṃśuprayatnamātravacano gṛṇātinoccāraṇasyābhidhānāt |
atra kāryasaṃgrahaṇarūpamādānaṃ nāma sandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``kāryasaṃgrahaṇamādānam'' iti||23||

 paśyeteti |
baṭorityanena śatānandasya paraśurāmato vayasā nyūnatvaṃ sūcitam |
pūrvoktaṃ gurutvaṃ tu sthāneneti mantavyam |
kṣatriyāvaṣṭabdhasya janakādikṣatriyāvalambinaḥ |
``avāccālambanāvidūryayoḥ'' ityavapūrvakasya stambheḥ ṣatvam |
kosalavideheśvarayordaśarathajanakayoḥ prasādamanugrahamupajīvantītyāśritya jīvantītyarthe ``supyajātau-'' iti ṇiniḥ |
saptadvīpāḥ kulaparvatāśca gocarā nivāsadeśā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
idaṃ pūrvottarānvayi |
tapo vetyādi |
vo yuṣmākaṃ madhye yo brāhmaṇo vā kṣatriyo vā kaścidiha daśarathajanakaviṣaye tapo vā śāpaprayojakaṃ tapo vā, śastraṃ vā yuddhopakaraṇamāyudhaṃ vā |
brāhmaṇastapaḥ kṣatriyaḥ śastramubhāvapyubhayaṃ veti yathāyathaṃ vikalpasamuccayau draṣṭavyau |
vyavaharatyasmadabhiyogopakaraṇatvena vadati |
darpāduddāmaḥ sa darpādhikaḥ sa brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vā tviṣamasmadīyaṃ tejo'sahamānaḥ sannamṛṣyamāṇaḥ saṃstviṣamasmadīyaṃ tejaḥ skhalayatu bhraṃśayatu |
``praiṣātisargaprāptakāleṣu-'' iti kāmacāre prāptakāle loṭ |
``skhala saṃcalane'' ityasmāt ``hetumati ca'' iti ṇic, ``dalivaliskhaliraṇidhvanitrapikṣapayaśca'' iti bhojarājamatena mittvātskhalayatviti hrasvaḥ |
kāmacārārthakaloṭā na naḥ kiñcidaniṣṭamiti sūcyate |
paraśurāmo'rāmāmavidyamānarāmām |
``naño'styarthānāṃ vācyo vā cottarapadalopaḥ'' iti vidyamānaśabdasya lopaḥ |
jagatīṃ bhūmim |
``jagatī ratnagarbhā ca'' ityamaraḥ |
niḥsīradhvajadaśarathīkṛtya daśarathajanakaśūnyāṃ kṛtvā |
``kṛbhvastiyoge-'' ityādinā cviḥ |
``ūryādicviḍācaśca'' iti gatisaṃjñāyām ``kugati-``ityasmātsamāse ``samāse'nañpūrve'' ityādinā lyap |
atṛptaḥ saṃstāvatāpyalaṃbuddhirahitasaṃstatkulyānapi janakadaśarathayorvaṃśe gṛhe ca sthitānmanuṣyānapi śamayati namayiṣyati |
``vartamānasāmīpye-'' iti bhaviṣyadāsanne laṭ |
atrodvejanāddyutirnāma sandhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``tarjanodvejane dyutiḥ'' iti |
tarjanaṃ kāyikamudvejanaṃ vācikamiti bhedaḥ||24||

 avalipyase garvavānbhavasi / |
asmadavalepāyāsūyati |
asmadgarvaviṣayakakrodhaprayuktadoṣāviṣkaraṇaṃ karotītyarthaḥ |
``krudhadruherṣyāsūyārthānāṃ yaṃ prati kopaḥ'' iti caturthī |
nāmeti kutsāyām |
durbalasyāsūyā kutsanīyetyarthaḥ |
``nāma prākāśyasaṃbhāvyakrodhopagamakutsane'' ityamaraḥ |
sasaṃrambhaśca/ |
praviśyeti |
āheti śeṣaḥ |
śatrudhvaṃsāditi |
vijayasahajaṃ vijayena sahotpannaṃ yatkṣātraṃ kṣattriyavarṇāsādhāraṇaṃ tejaḥ parābhibhavanasāmarthyaṃ kartṛ śatrudhvaṃsātpurā devatāprasādasahakṛtabhujabalena śatrūṇāṃ niḥśeṣavidhvaṃsanāt |
kiṃ ca pariṇativaśājjarānurodhāt |
``vaśa kāntau'' |
kāntiricchā |
kiṃ ca gṛhyasyāpastambādipraṇītakarmāvabodhakagranthasya ``karmāvabodhake gṛhyam'' iti ratnamālā |
tantrāṇāṃ tadbodhitaprayogarūpāṇāṃ tantraṃ svarāṣṭracintāyāṃ tantuvāye paricchade |
yāgaprayogasiddhāntaśāstramukhyauṣadheṣu ca ||'' iti ratnamālā |
vratānāmupavāsādiniyamānām |
``niyamo vratamastrī'' ityamaraḥ |
nairantaryādvicchedavirahāt |
kiṃ ca paramasya mukhyasya brahmaṇo brahmaśabdavācyasya bhagavatastattvasyānāropitarūpasyopalambhādyogadṛṣṭyā sākṣātkārānmuktātmanām |
pradhānasya vedādeva gauṇyā vṛttyā brahmaśabdaviṣayatvāttadvyāvṛttyarthaṃ parameti |
śrūyate ca- ``deva brahma paramaṃ kavīnām'' iti |
smaryate ca- ``paramabrahmabhūtasya vāsudevasya '' iti ``vede bhūriprayogācca guṇayogācca śārṅgiṇaḥ |
tasminneva brahmaśabdo mukhyavṛtto mahāmune/ |
iti ca |
``bṛhati bṛṃhayati tasmāducyate paramaṃ brahma'' |
bṛhanto'sminguṇāḥ ``bṛhattvādbrahmaṇatvācca brahmeti paripaṭhyate'', ``brahma parivṛḍhaṃ sarvatra'' ityādinā ca brahmaśabdo niruktaḥ |
tattvopalambhādityanena ``mano brahmetyupāsīta'' ityādinūoktānāmarvācīnaphalakānāṃ brahmopalambhānāṃ vyudāsaḥ |
anena mokṣahetubrahmasākṣātkārāditi phalitam |
evaṃ ca hetucatuṣṭayena vyaraṃsīdyuddhādikarmaṇo viratamabhūttadidaṃ kṣāttraṃ tejaḥ kartṛ |
punaḥ pratyudbhūyodbhūtatāṃ prāpya |
prakāśāvasthāṃ prāpyeti yāvat |
kārmukaṃ kodaṇḍaṃ karmaṇe yuddharūpakarmaṇe tvarayati saṃbhramayati |
``ñi tvarā saṃbhrame'' ghaṭāditvānmittvaṃ mittvāddhrasvaḥ |
karmaṇe tvarayatītyanena ``karmaṇa ukañ'' iti sūtroktā ``karmaṇe prabhavati kārmukam'' iti vyutpattiḥ smāritā||25||

 tvamiti |
bho janaka, tvaṃ brahmaṇyaḥ kila |
vedavaidikādiviṣaye sādhuriti prasiddhiḥ |
``brahma tattvaṃ tapo vedā brahmā vipraḥ prajāpatiḥ'' iti kośaḥ |
brahmaśabdāt ``tatra sādhuḥ'' iti yat |
kiṃca pariṇato'si |
kiṃca dharmeṇa yukto'si |
``dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati'' ityuktamokṣasādhanadharmeṇa nityayukto'si |
kiṃca tvāṃ sūryaputro ṛṣiryājñavalkyaḥ |
``putrakā iti hovāca jñānena parigṛhya tān,'' ``garīyānbrahmadaḥ pitā sa hi vidyātastaṃ janayati'' ityādinā śiṣyasya putratvapratīteḥ sūryaputra ityuktam |
vedānteṣūpaniṣatsvacaramaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ bṛhadāraṇyakaṃ śaśāsa bodhitavān |
``dahyāc-'' ityādinā śāserdvikarmakatvam |
bṛhadāraṇyakasamākhyayaiva tasya vedānteṣu śraiṣṭhyamavagatamityabhiprāyeṇācaramapadena tasya nirdeśaḥ |
itiśabdo hetau |
brāhmaṇyatvādihetucatuṣṭayena |
``ācārātteṣāṃ tvayāsanena praśvasitam'' ityādiśrutyuktācāranimittaṃ mayā tvaṃ praśrayeṇa vinayenopajuṣṭo'si yadi tattatto hetormohātsvaparabalābalabalājñānādaviditabhayaḥ sannajñātabhāvyanarthaḥ sankarkaśāni kaṭhorāṇi bravīṣi vadasi |
``syātkarkaśaḥ sāhasikaḥ kaṭhoramasṛṇāvapi'' ityamaraḥ||26||

 aṇḍabhedanaṃ kriyata iti |
auddhatyeneti śeṣaḥ |
praśrayaśceti |
kīrtyata iti śeṣaḥ |
aṇḍabhedanapraśrayakathanayormitho vyāhṛtiriti bhāvaḥ |
śṛṇuteti |
yūyamiti śeṣaḥ |
bhṛgoriti |
ayaṃ paraśurāmo bhṛgorvaśe jātaḥ |
kiṃca tapasi sthita iti hetorasmābhirmayā dviṣatyapi dveṣavatyapīha paraśurāme titikṣaivāparādhānuguṇadaṇḍābhāvasaṃkalpa eva ciramiyantaṃ kālaṃ kṛtā |
hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau |
yadeti |
ayamityanuṣajyate |
ayaṃ paraśurāmo'nibhṛtaḥ sannavinītaḥ san |
``nibhṛtavinītapraśritāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
nibhṛto na bhavatītyanibhṛta iti vigrahaḥ |
yadā yena hetunā bhūyobhūyaḥ punaḥpunastṛṇavattṛṇena tulyamavadhūnoti kampayati |
asmāniti śeṣaḥ |
tadā tasmāddhetorvipre'pyasminparaśurāme dhanuḥ kartṛ namatu nataṃ bhavatu |
karmaṇaḥ kartṛtvavivakṣā |
tadabhāve hetuḥ pūrvamevoktaḥ |
anyā gatirupāyāntaraṃ nāsti |
daṇḍaikasādhyatvādasyeti bhāvaḥ||27||

 roṣaḥ krodhaḥ, hāsaḥ smitam, ākṣepo'vamānanam |
kimāttha kiṃ bravīṣi |
dhanurdhanuriti dvitvaṃ kārmukaśabdārthābhiprāyeṇa |
aho āścaryamiti śabdadvayaṃ vismayātiśayadyotakam |
aho ityetatsaṃbodhane vā |
ahośabdasya ``āt'' iti pragṛhyasaṃjñāyām ``plutapragṛhyāḥ-'' iti prakṛtibhāvaḥ |
kṣattreti |
yājñavalkyasyānurodhātpremṇā hetunā mayā mithyādhmāto'smadguṇodgāreṇāpūrito'ta eva dattotsekaḥ sañjātasmayaḥ jarasā jarayā jarjaraḥ śithilaḥ kṣattrabandhuḥ kṣattriyāpasadaḥ |
``brahmabandhuradhikṣepe'' ityamarasiṃhavacanaṃ kṣattrabandhaśabdasyāpyupalakṣaṇam |
brahmakṣattraśabdasamabhivyāhṛtabandhuśabdo brāhmaṇādhamaṃ kṣattriyādhamaṃ cābhidhatte |
janaka iti śeṣaḥ |
kṣattrasya kṣattriyavarṇasyālokena darśanena kṣubhitasya prakṛtiṃ gatasya hutabhujo vahneḥ prasphuliṅgā eva prakṛṣṭakiraṇā evāṭṭahāso mahāhāso yasya tathoktam |
``aṭṭahāso mahānhāso mukharāgādikṛt'' ityabhidhānavidaḥ |
ripuśirorūpeṣu śāṇeṣu nikaṣopaleṣu |
``śāṇastu nikaṣaḥ kaṣaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
śātaṃ niśitam |
``atha niśitakṣṇutaśātāni tejite'' ityamaraḥ |
kuṭhāraṃ paśyannapi hāsyaḥ sankimapi pralapati |
anarthakaṃ vaco vadatītyarthaḥ |
atra kṣatrabandhurityādināvamānanāccalanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktaṃ bhavati |
yathoktam- ``avamānanaṃ calanam'' iti||28||

 sāvegaṃ sasaṃbhramam |
bahunā kim |
vākyeneti śeṣaḥ |
kriyākevalamuttaramiti bhāvaḥ |
jyātihvayeti |
jyā maurvī saiva jihvā tayā valayite saṃjātavalaye utkaṭe udagre koṭī evāgre eva daṃṣṭre yasya tattathoktam |
yadvā jihveva jyā jyājihvetyupamitasamāsaḥ |
daṃṣṭre iva koṭī koṭidaṃṣṭre upamitasamāsaḥ |
valayite utkaṭe koṭidaṃṣṭre yasyetyupamitasamāsabahuvrīhyāśrayaṇāddaṃṣṭrāśabdasya na pūrvanipātaḥ |
pūrvayojanāyāṃ rūpakamatra tūpamā |
udgāro'syāstītyudgārī |
avicchinnodgata ityarthaḥ |
``udbhūri'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
bhūyānityarthaḥ |
ghoro bhayaṃkaro ghanastāro ghargharaghoṣo yasya tattathoktametaccāpaṃ grāse jagadbhakṣaṇe prasaktasya vyāpṛtasya hasato'ntakasya yamasya yantrasadṛśavaktrasya jṛmbhāṃ viḍambayatyanukarotīti jṛmbhāviḍambi vikaṭaṃ danturamudaraṃ madhyaṃ yasya tattathoktaṃ tathāvidhamastu bhavatu||29||

 dhanurāropayati |
jyāmiti śeṣaḥ |
avirataṃ saṃtataṃ yajñeṣu jyotiṣṭomādiyāgeṣu vitīrṇāni gavāṃ dhenūnāṃ sahasrāṇi yena sa tathoktaḥ |
``dakṣiṇācāreṇa kartavyam'' iti dakṣiṇapāṇiniyamāditi bhāvaḥ |
palitena jarātiśayaprayuktakeśādiśauklyena nirantaro nibiḍaḥ purāṇaścirantano dhanurdhara iti vigrahe ``pūrvakāla-'' ityādinā samānādhikaraṇasamāsaḥ |
tava pāṇirdvije'sminparaśurāme |
pṛṣatkaṃ bāṇam |
``pṛṣatkabāṇaviśikhāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kathaṃ spṛśati |
prayoktumiti śeṣaḥ |
atrāpi virodhaśamanācchaktirnāma sandhyaṅgamuktam||30||

sakhe iti |
``pūrvasaṃbandhinaṃ jñātvā tataḥ pūrvaṃ bravīmi te'' ti rāmāyaṇe vasiṣṭhoktyā janakasya daśarathaḥ pūrvameva mitramiti sakhe ityuktam |
``sarvā pūrvamiyaṃ yeṣāmāsītkṛtsnā vasuṃdharā'' iti tatroktyā janakasya narapatitvamātram, daśarathasya tu mahārājatvamiti mahārājetyuktam |
asmāniti |
ayamityādikamayaṃ paraśurāmo'smānadhikṣipatvasmadviṣaye duruktāni vadatu |
kāmacāre loṭ |
nāmetyabhyupagame |
``nāma prākāśyasaṃbhāvyakrodhopagamakutsane'' ityamaraḥ |
etatparaśurāmakṛtādhikṣepaṇaṃ na kiṃcitkiṃcidapi na bhavati |
leśo'pi duḥkhakaraṃ na bhavati |
stutinindayoraviśeṣāditi bhāvaḥ |
dvije paruṣavādini sati kasya |
brahmaniṣṭhasyāpīti bhāvaḥ |
cittabhedaḥ krodharūpamanovṛttiviśeṣaḥ |
brahmaniṣṭhasya tu mama brāhmaṇakṛtādhikṣepe kopo nāstyeveti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ tu pāpoduṣkarmakārībaṭurbālo'yaṃ paraśurāmo vatsasya rāmasya maṅgalasya kalyāṇasya viruddhaṃ viparītaṃ kaṭu paruṣavākyaṃ karṇe raṭañśrotrasamīpe dhvanansankathaṃ nu viṣahyaḥ kathaṃ marṣaṇīyaḥ |
``parinivibhyaḥ-'' iti sahateḥ ṣatvam |
``raṭa paribhāṣaṇe'' ityasmāllaṭaḥ śatari raṭanniti rūpam |
paribhāṣaṇaṃ sanindopālambhaḥ |
iha tu kaṭupadena karmaṇa uktatvādraṭateḥ śabdamātraparatvam |
anena titikṣāyā ātmādhikṣepaviṣayatvam |
amarṣaṇasya tu bhagavadadhikṣepaviṣayatvamiti vibhāgo darśitaḥ||31||

 ā iti krodhe |
durātmandurbuddhe kṣattriyāpasada kṣattriyādhama, ``nihīno'pasado jālmaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atra prakaraṇe janakādīnāṃ bahūnāṃ krodhasaṃrabdhānāmanyonyākṣepakathanānnirodhanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``krodhasaṃrabdhānāmanyonyākṣepo nirodhanam'' iti |
uttiṣṭheti |
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭha |
yuddhārthamiti śeṣaḥ |
krodhasaṃbhrameṇa kriyāyā dviruktiḥ |
taduktaṃ vaiyākaraṇaiḥ- ``saṃbhrameṇa pravṛttau yatheṣṭamanekadhā prayogaḥ prāyeṇa jñāyasiddhaḥ'' iti |
paraśuḥ kuṭhāraḥ |
viśakalitāni viśeṣeṇa khaṇḍitāni |
yakṛtkālakhaṇḍam |
``kālakhaṇḍayakṛtī tu same ime'' ityamaraḥ |
klomaṃ tilakam |
``tilakaṃ klomamastiṣkam'' ityamaraḥ |
vṛkkāgramāṃsam |
``vṛkkāgramāṃsam'' ityamaraḥ |
asraṃ raktam |
``aśruśoṇitayorasram'' iti ratnamālā |
yakṛtklomavṛkkāsrāṇi yasya tattathoktaṃ viśakalitayakṛtklomavṛkkāsraṃ gātraṃ yena sa tathokta iti paraśurāmaviśeṣaṇam |
pṛthakpadaṃ bahuvrīhigarbhaṃ bahuvrīhirūpam |
kiṃca snāyūnāṃ snasānām |
``athavā snasā |
snāyuḥ striyām'' ityamaraḥ |
granthibhiḥ |
``granthiḥ parvagadāntare'' iti ratnamālā |
asthīnāṃ śalkaiśca śakalaiśca |
``śalke śakalavalkale'' ityamaraḥ |
vyatikaritā yuktā jaratī jīrṇā |
``jīryateratṛn'' iti ``jaṝṣ vayohānau'' ityasmādatṛnpratyaye tasyogittvāt ``ugitaśca'' iti ṅīp |
yā kaṃdharā grīvā tasyāṃ grīvāyām |
``śirodhiḥ kaṃdharetyapi'' ityamaraḥ |
dattaḥ khaṇḍaḥ khaṇḍanaṃ yena sa tathoktastathāvidhaśca san |
mūrdhacchedācchiraśchedādudañcatodgacchatā |
idaṃ bhāraviśeṣaṇam |
galasya kaṇṭhasya |
``kaṇṭho galaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
dhamanīṣu mahānāḍīṣu śirāsu kṣudranāḍīṣu |
yadyapi ``nāḍī tu dhamaniḥ śirā'' iti paryāyavācakau dhamaniśirāśabdau tathāpi sahaprayogādavāntarabhedau draṣṭavyau |
saktena saṃbaddhena ḍiṇḍīrapiṇḍaprāyeṇa phenasaṃghātabahalena |
``ḍiṇḍīro'bdhikaphaḥ phenaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
raktasyaivāvasthāviśeṣopalakṣaṇamidam |
asṛgbhāreṇa raktasamūhena ghoraṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ tvāṃ paśumiva vyāghrādikamiva parvaśaḥ parvaṇi parvaṇi |
``parva granthau pañcadaśyāmutsave viṣuvādike'' iti ratnamālā |
granthau granthāvityarthaḥ |
``saṃkhyaikavacanācca vīpsāyām'' iti śaspratyayaḥ |
yadyapi parimāṇaśabdādevaikavacanāntācchas māṣaśo dadātītyuktam, tathāpi jātiśabdādapyarthaprakaraṇānekārthavṛttervīpsāyāṃ śasbhavati |
khaṇḍaśo dadāti, vanaśaḥ praviśatīti vāmanamate jātiśabdādapi śasaḥ svīkārātparvaśa iti śasbhavatyeva |
``kārakāt'' itītyanuvṛtte'dhikaraṇakārakācchas |
parvaśabdo'yamaṅgulirekhāvācī sanparimāṇaśabdo bhavatīti pakṣāntare'pi śasbhavatyeva |
tvāṃ parvaparimitaṃ śṛṇātvityarthaḥ |
adya |
karmakārakācchas |
yāvacchṛṇātu |
hinastvevetyarthaḥ |
``śaṝ hiṃsāyām'' kryādiḥ |
``pvādīnām-'' iti hrasvaḥ |
``śṛṇāti'' iti pāṭhe hiṃsiṣyatītyarthaḥ |
``yāvatpurānipātayoḥ-'' iti bhaviṣyati laṭ |
pūrvapāṭhe tu yāvacchabdo'vadhāraṇārthakaḥ |
``yāvattāvacca sākalye'vadhau māne'vadhāraṇe'' ityamaraḥ||32||

 antare janakaparaśurāmayormadhye |
eṣa iti |
eṣo'smākaṃ mittraṃ narapatirjanako yathā sthitastathā te svaṃ śarīramapi sthitam |
ubhayorapyanyonyavākparibhavena śarīrāvayaveṣu na kaścidapi cheda iti bhāvaḥ |
tatra janakaviṣaye kṛtervāparibhavairhatubhirvayaṃ sarvathaiva sarvaprakāreṇāpi duḥkhamāsmahe duḥkhaṃ yathā tathā varttāmahe |
nanvityavadhāraṇe |
yadyapi janako brahmaniṣṭhatvātparairadhikṣepe'pi na khidyate tathāpi tadviṣaye parairatikṣepo'smākaṃ duḥkhaheturbhavatīti bhāvaḥ||33||

 tataḥ kiṃ duḥkhāsahane kim/ |
tataśca na kṣamyate adhikṣepo duḥkhakaratvānna mṛṣyate'smābhirityarthaḥ/ |
tvamapīti |
tvamapītyapiśabdena janakaḥ samuccīyate |
aparaḥ prabhaviṣṇurivānyaḥ prabhurivāvaskandayasi |
santāpayasītyarthaḥ |
``skandirgatiśoṣaṇayoḥ'' |
kṣepoktiriyam |
niyāmyastvam |
niyāmako'sītyarthaḥ |
cetayasva jānīhi |
kimityatrāha- nityeti |
prakṛtyaiva svabhāvata eva nityaniravagraho nityaniṣpratibandho jāmadagnyo rāmo'smi |
kṣattriyāntako'smītyarthaḥ |
kṣattriyaśca bhavān |
ato vadhyaghātakabhāvo'nuktasiddha ityarthaḥ/ |
atastava brāhmaṇatvānmama kṣattriyatvācca vimārgastvaṃ mayā nopekṣyase, kiṃtu damyasa ityarthaḥ |
durdāntānāmiti |
durdāntānāṃ duḥkhena damayitavyānām |
atyuddhatānāmityarthaḥ |
damanavidhayaḥ śikṣāvidhānāni kṣattriyeṣvāyatante kṣattriyāyattā bhavanti |
āṅpūrvo yatatirāyattayattatāyāṃ vartate/``adhino nighna āyattaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
tvaṃ durdāntaḥ kṣattriyā vayamapi te tava sāsitāro damayitāraḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
sadyo'sminneva kṣaṇe |
``sadyaḥ sapadi tatkṣaṇe'' ityamaraḥ |
śānto nivṛttakopo bhava |
aparaṃ kimaṇyatkim |
tvaṃ śāntiṃ na gacchasi cedvakṣyāmāṇādanyanna kimapi, kiṃtu vakṣyamāṇameva tava bhavatītyarthaḥ |
kiṃ tadityatrāha- daṃyasa iti |
daṃyase śikṣyase |
śikṣāheturaticāra ityatrāha- kva brahmāṇa iti |
praśamanaṃ prakṛṣṭā śāntiḥ paramutkṛṣṭaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktā brahmāṇo brāhmaṇāḥ |
``brahmā vipraḥ prajāpatiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kva |
kasminprakāre sthitā ityarthaḥ |
kṣatradhāryaṃ śastraṃ kva kasminprakāre sthitam |
brāhmaṇatvaśastragrāhitvayorvyāghāta ityarthaḥ |
ataḥ śastragrahaṇāddurdāntastvaṃ kṣatriyairasmābhirdamyasa ityarthaḥ||34||

 sanātho nāthavān |
vinetāraḥ śikṣakāḥ |
kṣattriyā rājānaḥ pālakāḥ |
kṣepoktiriyam |
nāhaṃ nāthavānnāpi yūyaṃ vinetāra ityarthaḥ/ |
atra tavāsmākaṃ ca śikṣyaśikṣakabhāvaviṣaye bhrāntiranyathājñānaṃ vyatsāsena śikṣyaśikṣakatvajñānam |
ajña ityādi |
ajño vā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalatadupāyaviṣayapramitirūpajñānānudayavānvā |
viparyayamasaddharmaviṣayakatvaṃ saddharmiviṣayakatvaṃ ca gataṃ prāptaṃ jñānaṃ yasya sa tathoktastathāvidho yadi vā |
yadi ceti nipātasamudāyo vāśabdasamānārthakaḥ |
bhramasya hi pītaḥ śaṅkaḥ sthūlo'hamiti dharmabhramatvadharmibhramatvābhyāṃ dvaividhyaṃ prasiddham |
tadubhavidabhrāntijñānavānvā |
bhrāntijñānaṃ ca dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalatadupāyaviṣayakaṃ vivakṣitam |
atheti kalpāntaraprastāve |
``maṅgalānantarāmbha'' ityādyamaraḥ |
saṃdehamekasmindharmiṇi viruddhanānādharmaprakārakaṃ jñānaṃ dharatīti saṃdehadhṛt |
``anyebhyo'pi dṛśyate'' iti kvip |
``hrasvasya-'' iti tugāgamaḥ |
saṃdehajñānamapi dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalādiviṣayakaṃ vivakṣitam |
yaḥ puruṣaḥ |
``śāstraṃ tu manuṣyādhikāratvāt'' ityuktarītyā ityuktarītyā tiryagvyāvṛtto dehītyarthaḥ |
dṛṣṭamaihitapaśuputrapuṣṭyannāyurindriyādiphalam, adṛṣṭamāmuṣmikasvargamokṣarūpaṃ phalam, tayorvirodhi nāśakaṃ viparītaphalapradaṃ ca karma kurute svānubhāvyaphalahetutayānutiṣṭhati |
``svaritañitaḥ-'' ityādinā kartṛge phale taṅ |
tasya pūrvoktānudayasaṃśayaviparyastapuruṣasya gururajñānanivartaka ācāryaḥ |
``guśabdastvandhakāre syādruśabdastannirodhakaḥ |
andhakāranirodhitvādgururityabhidhīyate||'' ityukteḥ |
goptopadeśādinānudayādinirasanenaihikāmuṣmikapuruṣārthatatsādhanaprayojakastadviparītanivartakaśca bhavatītyarthaḥ |
``gupū rakṣaṇe'' kartari tṛc |
ūditvādveṭ |
rakṣaṇamiṣṭaprāpaṇamaniṣṭanivartanaṃ ca |
jñāne niḥsandehaviparyaye sati sandehatvaviparyayatvābhyāṃ nirgate sati |
bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ |
dṛṣṭādṛṣṭādiṣu pramityātmake satyapītyarthaḥ |
viruddhā dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaviruddhā kriyā vyāpāro yasya sa tathoktastaṃ puruṣaṃ punaḥ puruṣaṃ tu |
``punaraprathame bhede'' ityamaraḥ |
rājā na śāsti cettattasmādrājaśāsanavirahādayaṃ prajāviplavaḥ prajopadravaḥ prāptaḥ, atastvaṃ śikṣya eveti nātra śikṣyaśikṣakabhāvavyatyayabhrāntiḥ kāryeti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
``indriyadaurbalyādvipratipannānāṃ śāstā nirvedamupadiśet |
tasya cecchāstramatipravarteranrājānaṃ gamayet |
balaviśeṣeṇa vadhadāsyavarjaṃ niyamairupaśoṣayet'' ityāpastambavacanamanusaṃdheyam |
atra gurukīrtanātprasaṅgo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktamapha |
yathoktam- ``gurukīrtanaṃ prasaktaḥ'' iti||35||

 mahārājo daśarathaḥ |
anutpannamiti |
atra bhārgavetyādiḥ |
he bhārgava tava jñānaṃ parvoktadṛṣṭādṛṣṭaviṣayādiviṣayakapramityātmakaṃ jñānamanutpannaṃ yadyanuditaṃ cedvasiṣṭhasya caraṇau paricara śuśrūṣasva |
``śuśrūṣayā vā parayā'', ``tadviddhi praṇipātena paripraśnena sevayā'' ityuktadiśā guruśuśrūṣayā tava samyagjñānamudeṣyatīti bhāvaḥ |
tava jñānaṃ saṃdehena samānaviṣayakasaṃśayena vidhuramanuṣṭhānaśūnyaṃ yadi vasiṣṭhasya caraṇau paricara |
tatprasādādarthasaṃdeho nivartiṣyata ityarthaḥ |
saṃdehatvenānuṣṭhāpanaśaktiśūnyaṃ yaditi, bhāvapradhāno nirdeśa iti vā vyākhyeyam |
tavajñānaṃ viparyastaṃ vā syātpūrvoktadvividhabhrāntyātmakaṃ yadi syāttatho'pi vasiṣṭhacaraṇau paricara |
tava tatprasādādbhrāntijñānaṃ nivartiṣyata ityarthaḥ |
nanu kathaṃ saṃpratipannajñāne paraśurāme jñānānudayādyupanyāsa ityatrāha- dhruvamiti |
jñāne doṣo dhruvam |
anudayasaṃśayaviparyayatvarūpo doṣo'sya jñāne'numīyata ityarthaḥ |
atra durācāro heturabhipretaḥ |
tathā cāyaṃ jñānadoṣavāndurācārātsaṃpratipannavadityanumānaśarīram |
atrāprayojakatvaśaṃkāṃ nirasyati- kathamiti |
aparathā jñānadoṣarūpasādhyābhāve durvyavahṛtirdurācāraḥ kathaṃ syāditi śeṣaḥ |
vyavapūrvako haratiḥ kāyikavyāpāre vācikavyāpāre ca vartate |
atra ca samānaviṣayatvādisaṃbandhena jñānavyāpārayoḥ sāmānyataḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaḥ |
tenaiva saṃbandhena saṃyagjñāna saṃyagvyāpārayorduṣṭajñānavyāpārayośca viśeṣataḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvaśca dṛṣṭa iti duṣṭajñānadurvyāpārayoḥ kāryakāraṇabhāvabhaṅgaprasaṅga evāprayojakatvaśaṅkānivāraka iti bhāvaḥ |
viśuddhāviti |
jñāna ityanuṣajyate |
jñāne viśuddhau pūrvoktasaṃśayatvādidoṣābhāve'pi pāpaṃ carasi cecchāstrapratiṣiddhaṃ karmānutiṣṭhasi cennṛpatayo na sahante |
na mṛṣyantītyarthaḥ |
viduṣo'tikrame daṇḍabhūyastvādrājānastādṛśaṃ daṇḍayeyuranyathā tatpāpaṃ teṣāṃ bhavet |
yathoktaṃ manunā- ``rājā tvaśāsanātsatyaṃ tadevāpnoti kilbiṣam'' iti |
tatho rājāno na mṛṣyantīti bhāvaḥ||36||

 dharma iti |
bhagavānīśo rudro dharme vedaikasamadhigamyaśreyaḥsādhane brahmaṇi dharmārādhye paramātmani kārmuko dharmavirodhanirāsasamaye dhanuṣi ca viṣaye |
adhikaraṇatvarūpasya viśeṣasya vivakṣaṇānna dvitīyā kiṃtu saptamī |
me mama śāsitā bodhayitā |
hiśabdaḥ prasidau |
ato mama jñānadoṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
sarvakṣatranibarhaṇasya samastakṣatriyajātyānāṃ saṃharturmama nibarhaṇam |
pramāpaṇamityarthaḥ |
prakṛte tu nandyāditvātkartari lyuḥ |
vinayaṃ śikṣaṇaṃ kṣatriyāḥ kathaṃ kuryuḥ kathaṃ kartuṃ śaknuyuḥ |
śaki liṅ |
ato na sahante nṛpataya ityuktam |
vasiṣṭhamiśraviṣaye saṃbandhaḥ pūjyatamavasiṣṭhaprātiyogikasaṃbandho jarāyāṃ mānyaḥ |
jaretyupalakṣaṇam |
sthāna ityapi |
sthānavayobhyāmādhikye sati taccaraṇaparicaraṇahetutayā puraskāryaḥ |
tuśabdaḥ prapitāmahatvavṛddhatvaprayuktaśaṅkādyotakaḥ |
spardhāyāṃ tu jñānatapaḥprayuktasaṃgharṣe sati tu na mānyo na puraskāryaḥ |
kuta ityatrāha- adhika iti |
anyaḥ ko vasiṣṭho vā tvamanyo vā ko jñānena tapasā cādhikaḥ samo vā mamāstītyarthaḥ |
ato jñānārthaṃ na taccaraṇaparicaraṇaṃ kāryamiti viśvāmitroktaṃ vākyatrayamapi pratyuktam |
atra gurutiraskṛtirūpo dravo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``gurutiraskṛtirdravaḥ'' iti||37||

 bhṛguprasavādbhṛguvaṃśyātparājayastiraskāro nyūnabhāvo bhavatīti yattadasmākamiṣṭamityarthaḥ |
bhṛgorjyeṣṭhatvāttadvaṃśyena pautrasthānīyena cānena paribhavaḥ priya eva |
``putrādicchetparājayam'' iti smaraṇāditi bhāvaḥ |
asmābhiriti |
asmābhiḥ pālyasyānucchedena rakṣaṇīyasya praśastatvādabhyarhitatvānno'smākaṃ priyasya purāṇasya cirantanasyācārasya brāhmaṇāsādhāraṇānityatvā
(
ṛ)dimūlasadācārasyāsmadgṛha eva viplavaṃ hāniṃ paśya |
bhṛguvasiṣṭhayoḥ sanāditvādasmadgṛha ityuktam |
bhārgavo'yaṃ māmadhikṣipatīti na dūye kiṃtu dharmapravartakānāmasmākaṃ vaṃśe dharmahānirjāteti dūya iti bhāvaḥ||38||

 janaketyādi |
āhuriti śeṣaḥ |
anāryāśiṣṭa |
nirmaryāda vaidikavyavasthābhañjaka |
jagaditi |
jagatāṃ sanātanagurau nityagurau |
``nityasadātanasanātanāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vasiṣṭhe'pi brahmaṇaḥ prāṇājjātatvātprāṇavattadabhimate vasiṣṭhe'pi niraṅkuśo nirbhayastvaṃ niraṅkuśo'ṅkuśānniṣkrānto vyāladvipa iva duṣṭagaja iva |
``vyālaḥ sarpe duṣṭagaje śvāpade nāśake triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
viśiṣṭavācakānāṃ padānāṃ viśeṣyavācakasamabhivyāhāre viśeṣaṇamātraparatvādduṣṭamātraparo'tra vyālaśabdaḥ |
asmābhirbahubhirbrāhmaṇakṣatriyairupakṛṣyānunīya damyase śikṣyase |
``damu upaśame'' ityasmāṇṇyantātkarmaṇi laṭ |
atra vasiṣṭho'yaṃ saptavyāhṛtiṣu ṛṣitvājjagato nityaguruḥ |
yadyapi dharme brahmaṇīti tvaduktarītyā rudrastava dharmādau gururastu, tathāpi brāhmaṇyanidānasarvābhyarhitasaptavyāhṛtiṛṣitvāttavāyaṃ mukhyagururiti tadviṣayaspardhā tava brahmahatyā samā |
tathā ca yājñavalkyaḥ- ``gurūṇāmadhyadhikṣepo vedanindā suhṛdvadhaḥ |
brahmahatyāsamaṃ jñeyamadhītasya ca nāśanam||'' iti |
``dharme brahmaṇi kārmuke ca bhagānīśo hi me śāsitā'', ``mānyo jarāyāṃ na tu spardhāyām'' iti tvaduktaṃ duruktam |
bahūnāṃ pālyādbrāhmaṇasyāpi viśvāmitrasya tatrānupraveśādgurvadhikṣepeṇa paraśurāmasya tejovaikalyasaṃbhāvanayā cāsmābhistvaṃ damyasa ityuktam |
tena ``sarvakṣatranibarhaṇasya'' ityādikamapi pratyuktam||39||

 avadhūto'smyadhikṣipto'smi |
antariti |
yo mama manyuḥ krodho vṛddhānāṃ cyavanādīnāṃ vacanāddhetordhairyasya krodhaniyamanasāmarthyaprayojakadhṛtiviśeṣasya bhareṇātiśayena hetunā saṃpīḍya vīraraṇapratibandhakapiṇḍībhāvānukūlavyāpāraṃ kṛtvā piṇḍīkṛtaḥ |
saṃhatatāṃ prāpto'ta eva hṛnmarmāśritaśalyavaddhṛdayadeśātmakavimardāsahadehapradeśagatabāṇakhaṇḍena tulyaṃ paridahansannekatra sthito'pi paritastāpaṃ kurvannantaḥ śarīrāntaściraṃ sthitaḥ sa eṣa manyurmama nyakkāreṇa janakādikṛtadhikkāreṇa bhinnasthitiścinnamaryādaḥ san |
``maryādā dhāraṇa sthitiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kalpasya brahmadinasyāpāye'vasāne marutā vāyunā |
``saptamī'' iti yogavibhāgātsamāsaḥ |
prakīrṇamudgataṃ payo yasya sa tathoktasya sindhoraurvānala iva vaḍavānala iva |
``aurvastu vāḍavo vaḍavānalaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atra vāḍavasya svarūpaviśeṣaṇamātraparatvamaurvaśabdasyānalapadasamabhivyāhārāditi draṣṭavyam |
saṃprati sphūrjati vajrasakalavadvyāpriyate |
vegavattvadurnivāratvādinā sādṛśyam |
``ṭuāsphūrjā vajranirghoṣe'' iti paṭhitasya lakṣaṇayoktārthaparatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
evakāro dārḍhyadyotakaḥ||40||

 diṣṭyetyānande |
nikāramiti |
nikāraṃ janakādikṛtaṃ paribhavam |
``nikāro viprakāraḥ syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
prāpto'yaṃ me paraśurmṛtyuriva jvalati śatrudahanānukūlajvalanavānbhavati |
divyaparaśorabhimāni devatādhiṣṭhānānnikāraprāptyuktiriti mantavyam |
pṛthivyāṃ daśarathakule ca rājānaḥ pṛthivīsthā daśarathagṛhasthāśca rājānaḥ sīradhvajakuśadvajādayo daśaratharāmalakṣmaṇādayaścopāgatāḥ santi |
yasmādevaṃ tasmāprakupitasya kopātiśayavataḥ kṛtāntasya yamasyotsavakara ānandajananaśīlaḥ |
``kṛño hetutācchīlyānulomyeṣu'' iti ṭaḥ |
dvyadhikāviṃśatirdvāviṃśatiḥ |
``dvyaṣṭanaḥ saṅkhyāyāṃ'' ityādinā dviśabdasyātvam |
dvāviṃśateḥ pūraṇo dvāviṃśaḥ |
``tasya pūraṇe ḍaṭ'', ``tiviṃśaterḍiti'' iti tiśabdalopaḥ |
pūrvamekaviṃśatiḥ kṣatriyavadhā āsan, ayamanyo dvāviṃśa ityarthaḥ |
apiśabdaḥ pūrvasamuccaye |
pralaya iva prapañcapralaya iva ghoro mamāpi bhayaṅkaraḥ kṣattrasya kṣattravarṇasāmānyasya parimaraḥ, parimriyante'sminvīrā iti parimaraḥ saṅgrāmaḥ |
paripūrvānmriyateḥ ``puṃsi saṃjñāyāṃ ghaḥ prāyeṇa'' ityadhikaraṇe ghapratyayaḥ |
punarapyastu |
prāptakāle loṭ||41||

 kāmamiti |
eṣa paraśurāmo no'smākaṃ svajano bandhurbhavati |
``bandhuḥ svasvajanāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kāmamityanujñāyām |
tathāpi svajanatve'pyeṣa darpādghoraṃ bhayaṃkaraṃ karma vyavasyati kartavyatvena niścinoti |
rāmabhadrādiṣviti śeṣaḥ |
eṣo'vaśyo'smākamaparatantraḥ kathaṃ nu bhavetkathaṃ bhavet |
sarvathāsmākaṃ svādhīna eva kāryaḥ |
anyathā vyavasāyānuguṇaṃ vyāpriyetetyarthaḥ |
tatra svādhīnakaraṇopāyastu na tāvatsāma bhaveddṛptatvāt |
nāpi dānaṃ viraktatvāt |
na ca bhedo'sahāyatvāt |
kiṃtu daṇḍaḥ pariśiṣyate |
so'pi saṃbandhavaśena kāruṇikasya māmanumata ityabhiprāyeṇāha- saṃdūṣiteneti |
eṣa paraśurāmaḥ saṃdūṣitena krodharūpavikṛtiṃ prāptena |
``duṣa vaikṛtye'' iti paṭhitāṇṇic |
preraṇāṃśatyāgādakarmakatvena |
``gatyarthākarmaka-'' iti kartari ktaḥ |
vaikṛtyaṃ vikṛtiḥ |
yadvā saṃdūṣitena ``mānyo jarāyāṃ na tu spardhāyām'' iti doṣodbhāvanenādhikṣiptena mayā sakṛdekavāramīkṣitaścettadīkṣaṇaṃ vatsasya prapautratvasvarūpasaṃbandhena lālanīyasya bhārgavaśiśorasmajjyeṣṭhabhrātṛvaṃśajasya jñānatapovayobhiralpatvāddayanīyasya duritamaniṣṭakaraṃ syāt |
hi yasmādevaṃ tasmātkaṣṭamiti saṃbandhaḥ |
atra bhāgavatā vasiṣṭhenāpakāriṇyapi titikṣutvakāruṇikatvādisvaguṇāviṣkaraṇādvicalanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``svaguṇāviṣkaraṇaṃ vicalanam'' iti||42||

 abrahmavarcasamiva śāpānugrahasāmarthyahetusadvṛttasvādhyāyādhyayanasamṛddhiśūnyamiva |
``syādbrahmavarcasaṃ vṛttādhyayanarddhiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
bhraṃśitamadhaḥpatitaṃ śastrasāmarthyamāyudhaparigrahaśaktiryasya sa tathoktamiva |
``śastramāyudhacāpayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
jīvalokaṃ prāṇināṃ nivāsabhūtaṃ bhuvanam |
``jīvo bṛhaspatiprāṇajīvanātmadrumeṣu nā |
maurvyaā vasāyāṃ jīvantyāṃ jīvājīvavati triṣu||'' iti ratnamālā |
``lokastu bhuvane vane'' ityamaraḥ |
brahmakṣattreti |
jāmadagnya, tvaṃ saṃbandhato'smadbhāgineyajamadagniputratvarūpeṇa saṃbandhena hetunā pālyo'pyasmākaṃ rakṣaṇīyo'pi brahmakṣattrasamājaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ kṣattriyāṇāṃ ca tapodhyayanādinā sadharmiṇāṃ samūham |
``paśūnāṃ samajo'nyeṣāṃ samājo'tha sadharmiṇām'' imyamaraḥ |
ākṣipasyadhikṣipasi |
kiṃca vatse rāme ghorāśayaḥ krūrābhiprāyo'si |
``abhiprāyaśchanda āśayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
iti yat, yena brahmakṣattrasamājādhikṣeparāmajighāṃsāsamuccayātmakenātikramaṇena vedavaidikamaryādātilaṅghanenāsmānduḥkhayasi duḥkhavataḥ karoṣi |
duḥkhavacchabdāt ``tatkaroti-'' iti ṇici ṇāviṣṭavattvānmatublopaḥ |
yadvā no'smākaṃ duḥkhayati duḥkhaṃ karoṣi |
``sukhaduḥkha tatkriyāyām'' iti ṇic |
iti yat |
ātaḥ ataḥ |
ataḥśabdavadātaḥśabdo'pi dīrghādirhetvarthako'sti |
viṣu ekapade vṛt āta ityavyayeṣu pāṭhāt |
tvāṃ prati kopanasya tvadviṣayakakrodhayuktasya mama dakṣiṇaḥ pāṇistaralaḥ saṃstvāṃ prati śāpodakamanviṣyati |
itaraḥ pāṇirvāmaḥ karaḥ prāksaṃskāravaśena tapovasiṣṭhavākyābhyāṃ brahmarṣitvādhigamātpūrva kṣattriyatvaprayuktadhanurvidyāpāṭavavāsanānurodhena |
``graho grāhe vaśaḥ kāntau'' ityamaraḥ |
kāntiricchā |
cāpamanviṣyati |
śāpacāpābhyāṃ vartamānabhūtabrāhmaṇatvakṣattriyatvānuguṇābhyāṃ nigṛhṇāmītyarthaḥ |
``ṛṣi gatau'' iti daivādikasyopasargavaśenecchārthakatvam||43||

 tvamiti |
tvaṃ vartamāno brahmavarcasadhano yadi syāḥ |
vartamānatvaṃ brahmavarcasadhanatvadvārānveti |
etatkālavartibrāhmaṇatvānuguṇavṛttādhyayanasamṛddhyupakaraṇako bhavasi cedugreṇa tapasā brāhmaṇatvānuguṇena tava tapo brāhmaṇatvānuguṇaṃ dahāmi bhasmīkaromi |
``daha bhasmīkaraṇe'' |
yadveti kalpāntare |
svajātisamayena kṣattriyātkṣattriyāṇyāṃ svajanmato'nurūpeṇa samayenācāreṇa |
``samayāḥ śapathācārakālasiddhāntasaṃvidaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
dhanurdharaḥ syāścet |
pakṣāntare dhanurupakaraṇatvarūpaparigrāhyakalpaviśeṣe |
``pakṣa parigrahe'' iti paṭhitātpakṣateḥ pakṣaśabdo niṣpannaḥ |
sadṛśamanurūpaṃ yuddhātmakaṃ karma paraśuḥ karotu |
kāmacāre loṭ |
tapaso yadyapi kartṛtvena madapekṣā syātparaśostu na manapekṣeti nirdeśabhedābhiprāyaḥ |
atra svaśaktipraśaṃsanādvyavasāyo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``svaśaktipraśaṃsanaṃ vyavasāyaḥ'' iti||44||

 kauśikāntevāsī viśvāmitraśiṣyaḥ |
``chātrāntevāsinau śiṣye'' ityamaraḥ |
yuddhaprastāvādvasiṣṭhavyapadeśino'pi svasya rāmeṇa kauśikāntevāsīti vyapadeśaḥ kṛtaḥ |
paulastyeti |
paulastyavijayena rāvaṇavijayenoddāmasyoddhatasya kārtavīryārjunasya dviṣaṃ śatruṃ kṣattravīryasyānyeṣāṃ ṣattriyāṇāṃ vīryasya jetāramabhibhavitāraṃ paraśurāmaṃ vijayeya vijetuṃ śaknomi |
``śaki liṅ'', ``viparābhyāṃ jeḥ'' iti taṅ |
gurusaṃnidhau śaktyāviṣkaraṇadoṣasya prāyaścittaṃ carati-namo'stu va iti |
yadvā na māṃ yūyaṃ premātiśayenāsthānaśaṅkayā nivārayadhvamityanunayati- namo'stu va iti |
atra vālirāvaṇayorapi mayā prepsito vijayaḥ sukaraśceti rāmābhiprāyaḥ sūcitaḥ |
etena śiṣṭacatuṣṭayāṅkārthaḥ sūcitaḥ||45||

 kathaṃ prāptaḥ |
kathamiti saṃbhrame |
etadbālena rāmeṇa kṣattriyāntakanirasanam |
praśastaṃ samyak |
hanta harṣe |
abhyanujānītānujñāṃ kuruta |
vijayatāṃ rāmabhadraḥ |
paraśurāmamiti śeṣaḥ |
ayamiti |
ekavīraḥ sajātīdvijātīyaśūnyo vīraḥ |
bāhulakātsamāsaḥ |
jagatpatirayaṃ rāmo dṛptānāṃ vinetā damayitā |
tṛjantamidam |
dṛptānāmiti bahuvacanena vālirāvaṇayorapi vineteti sūcitam |
vasiṣṭho dhaureyo nirvāhako yoṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
``dhuro yaḍḍhakau'' iti ḍhak |
sarve vayamatra rāmasya vinetṛtve pratibhuvo lagnakāḥ |
``syurlagnakāḥ pratibhuvaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atra ``ayaṃ nivetā dṛptānām'' iti siddhavadbhāviśreyaḥkathanātprarocanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
tathoktam- siddhavadbhāviśreyaḥkathanaṃ prarocanam'' iti||46||

 nanvityādi |
nanvityāmantraṇe |
``praśnāvadhāraṇānujñānunayāmanttṇe nanu'' ityamaraḥ |
rāmabhadraḥ prathitayaśasāṃ prakhyātakīrtīnāṃ yadyapi yaśa eva khyātistathāpi khyāteḥ khyātirvivakṣitā |
``kīrteḥkīrtiḥ'' iti hyuktam |
guṇavattāprathārūpakīrteḥ |
guṇavattāprathā hi vastusaṃnibandhanatvarūpaguṇaprasiddhirūpā |
``puṇyairyaśo labhyate'' iti dṛṣṭanibandhanaśūnyamapi hi yaśaḥ kvaciduktam |
atastadvyāvṛttaye prathitayaśasāmityuktam |
ūḍhaṃ dhṛtaṃ rakṣāvrataṃ jagadrakṣaṇaniyamo yaiste tathoktāsteṣām |
kiṃca vo yuṣmābhiryājyānāṃ yājayitumarhāṇām |
``kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā'' iti va iti ṣaṣṭhī |
tathāvidhānām |
ikṣvākūṇāmiti śeṣaḥ |
guṇavati gṛhe guṇabhūyiṣṭhe gṛhe'dyaiva sujātaḥ |
pūrvaputrakāmeṣṭyā jāto'pi jāto na bhavati, idānīmeva bhāviparaśurāmādivijayarūpaśreyaskatayā bhavadbhiranugṛhītatvācchobhanajanmavānityarthaḥ |
yadvā nanviti janakaṃ pratyāmanttraṇam |
rāmabhadraḥ prathitayaśasāmūḍharakṣāvratānāṃ yājyānāṃ vo gṛhe'dyaiva sujātaḥ |
nāsmadgṛhe pūrvaṃ prajāta ityarthaḥ |
yadyasmājjñānajyotiṣā jñānaprāśena parigatāḥ samyagjñātā bhavatāṃ vartamānānāṃ bhūtānāmatītānāṃ bhavyānāṃ bhāvināṃ ca prabhāvāḥprakṛṣṭasvabhāvā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
``bhavyageya-'' ityādinā bhavateḥ kartari yat |
``bhavyaṃ satye śubhe klībe triṣu syādyogyabhāvinoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
brahmāṇo vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrādayo brāhmaṇottamāḥ |
śiśuke'pyatrātyantabāle'pi rāme kimapi madvyapekṣayā manasāpyullalitumanarhaṃ paraśurāmādivinetṛtvaṃ saṃvedayante saṃbhūya jānanti |
``vida vedanākhyānanivāseṣu'' iti curādau paṭhyate |
atrādyaiva jāta ityādināpāyābhāvataḥ kāryaniścayarūpaniyatāptiruktā |
śatānandādyavyāpikathārūpaprakarī coktā |
ato'sminnaṅke samagrāṅgo vimarśasaṃdhiruktaḥ||47||

 ehimanye rājaputra, jāmadagnyaṃ vijeṣyase |
he rājaputra, jāmadagnyamahaṃ vijeṣya iti manyase, ita ehīti yojanā |
``prahāse ca manyopapade manyateruttama ekavacca'' iti manyopapadāddhātoruttame prayoktavye madhyamaḥ prayoktavyaḥ |
manyatermadhyame  prayoktavye uttamaḥ prayoktavyaḥ prahāse gamyamāna ityukteratra manye vijeṣyasa ityuttamamadhyamayoḥ prayogaḥ |
ehīti ca prahāsadyotakam |
ataeva ``ehimanye ādanaṃ bhokṣyase'' ityudāhṛtam |
tatra hyodanasyānyopayuktatvamajānantaṃ bubhukṣamāṇaṃ pratihāsaḥ |
kṛte'pi kṣattriyāntakasya kṣattriyabālakavijayaḥ prahāsaheturityabhipretyātra putretyuktam |
tamimaṃ prahāsaṃ kaṇṭhata evāha- sasmitam |
nahi vijeṣyase |
durdānto hi reṇukātanayastvadantaka iti ``viparābhyāṃ-'' iti jayaterātmanepadam |
kṛtteti |
brahmastambānāṃ brahmāṇḍānām |
``stambo gulme'pyaśākhe'drau'' iti ratnamālā |
stambaśabdenordhvatvādisādṛśyādgauṇyā bhuvanāṇḍānyucyante |
teṣāṃ nikuñjeṣu kuñjeṣu |
``nikuñjakuñjau'' ityamaraḥ |
brahmāṇḍāni kuñjopapādakalatāsthānīyāni bhavanti |
tena brahmāṇḍagaṇeṣviti phalitam |
puñjito rāśīkṛto'ta eva ghano jyāghoṣastena ghoraṃ bhīṣmametaddhanuḥ kartṛ kṛttāśchinnāḥ kṣattriyāṇāṃ kaṇṭhā eva kaṃdarāstābhyaḥ saradbhirniṣkrāmadbhiḥ kīlālai raktaiḥ |
``śoṇite'mbhasi kīlālam'' ityamaraḥ |
nirvāpitāḥ śamitāḥ pratyudbhūtāḥ śikhānāṃ kalāpāḥ saṃghātā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
``kalāpo bhūṣaṇe barhe kāñcayāṃ saṃhatitūṇayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
tathāvidhasya hutabhujo'gnerjhaṅkārā eṣu santīti vigrahe ``ata iniṭhanau'' inipratyayaḥ |
tathāvidhairmārgaṇaiḥ |
``kalambamārgaṇaśarāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
ghasmarasya jagadbhakṣakasya |
``bhakṣako ghasmaro'dmaraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kālarudrasya pralayakālarudrasya kavalavyāpāraṃ grāsakriyāmabhyasyatu punaḥpunaḥ karotu |
``yogyāguṇanikābhyāsaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``upasargādasyatyūhyorvāvacanam'' ityātmanepadasya vaikalpikatvātparasmaipadam |
atra padārthavṛttirūpanidarśanāviśeṣo'laṃkāraḥ ``padārthavṛttimapyeke vadantyanyāṃ nidarśanām'' iti tallakṣaṇāt |
atra ca kuṭhāraṃ vihāya dhanuṣaḥ kīrtanena tasmādapi dhanuṣaḥ priyatvāvagamāduttarāṅke rāmajitena bhārgaveṇa prītyā rāmāya dhanuṣaḥ pradānaṃ kariṣyamāṇaṃ sūcyate||48||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vīrarāghavaḥ/
sa mahāvīracarite tṛtīyāṅkaṃ vyavīvarat||
asmadgururjayatu saṃprati yo nivāso lakṣmyā hi saṃtatamaśeṣagurūpasevyaḥ/
śrīraṅgarājaśiśuneha suputravānsa dūrīkṛteśaguruśeṣaturaṅgakaṇṭhaḥ||

ityāṇṇāppaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyādhyāyāṃ bhāvapradyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ tṛtīyo'ṅkaḥ||

caturtho'ṅkaḥ/

idānīṃ kaviḥ punarapi mukhāntareṇa garbhasaṃdhiṃ ca turīyapañcamaṣaṣṭhairaṅkairvakṣyanprathamaṃ turīyāṅkasya viṣkambhamupakṣipati- bho bho ityādi |
vimānena vyomayānena carantīti vaimānikāḥ |
``tena carati'' iti ṭhak |
maṅgalāni puṣpavarṣādīni śubhakarmāṇi pravartantām |
kartari loṭ |
ko'tra heturityāha- kṛśāśveti |
kṛśāśvasya jambhakādidivyāstrāṇāṃ prathamaṃ draṣṭuḥ kṛśāśvanāmno brahmarṣerantevāsī śiṣyo bhagavānkauśiko munirjayati sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate |
``śiṣyaprakarṣo yaśase gurūṇām'' iti bhāvaḥ |
adhunā sasrāṃśorvaṃśe sūryavaṃśe kṣattraṃ kṣattravarṇo jayati |
kuta ityatrāha- vineteti |
kṣattrāreḥ paraśurāmasya vinetā damayitā jagadāmabhayadānavratameva dhanaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
labdhaparipālanādalabdhasya labhyatvācca dhanasādṛśyam |
jagatkaṇṭakavālirāvaṇādivadho'pīha sūcitaḥ |
lokānāṃ śaraṇyaḥ śaraṇe rakṣaṇe sādhuḥ |
``śaraṇaṃ rakṣaṇe gṛhe'' iti viśvaḥ |
``tatra sādhuḥ'' iti yatpratyayaḥ |
jagadabhayetyādinā traivargikaphalapradānaṃ tatpratyanīkavidhvaṃsanaṃ ca vivakṣitam |
śaraṇya ityādinā ca bhayanivartanaṃ mokṣapradānaṃ ca vivakṣitam |
anena sugrīvavibhūṣaṇābhyāṃ kiṣkindhālaṅkaiśvaryapradānaṃ tatparipanthinorvālirāvaṇayorvadhaśca vakṣyamāṇaḥ sūcitaḥ |
evaṃ jaṭāyuvālinormokṣapradānaṃ ca sūcitam |
evaṃbhūtau dinakarakulenduḥ sūryavaṃśānandakaro rāmo vijayate sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate |
dinakaretyanena saṃtāpakarasyāpyuṣṇakiraṇasya candravattāpaharatvaṃ rāmasya vyajyate |
atra paraśurāmavijayena dṛṣṭasyottaratra vanapravāsenādṛṣṭasya rāmaśreyohetiviśvāmitrayatnarūpabījasyānveṣaṇādgarbhasaṃdhiḥ |
taduktam- ``garbho dṛṣṭasya naṣṭasya bījasyānveṣaṇaṃ muhuḥ'' iti |
asmiṃśca saṃdhau patākārūpārthaprakṛtiḥ prāptāśārūpācārthaprakṛtirvaktavye |
tatsvarūpaṃ tu- ``upāyāpāyaśaṅkābhyāṃ prāptyāśākāryasaṃbhavaḥ |
pratipādyakathāṅgaṃ syātpatākāvyāpinī kathā||'' ityuktam |
vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrānugrahādyupāyaśaṅkayā mālyavanmanttrādyapāyaśaṅkayā ca kāryasaṃbhāvanāyā uktatvātprāptyāśoktā |
pratipādyarāmakathāṅgasya vyāpinyā lakṣmaṇakathāyāścoktyā patākā varṇitā |
asya ca garbhasaṃdheraṅgāni dvādaśa |
taduktam-``abhūtāharaṇaṃ mārgo rūpodāharaṇakramāḥ |
saṃgrahaścānumānaṃ ca toṭakādibale tathā |
udvegasaṃbhramākṣepā dvādaśāṅgānyanukramāt||'' iti |
eteṣāṃ svarūpāṇi svāvasareṣveva darśayiṣyante||1||

 viṣkambhakamāha- tataḥ praviśata ityādinā |
saṃbhrāntau |
atra saṃbhramavacanāttoṭakaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``roṣasaṃbhramavacanaṃ toṭakam'' iti |
svottejitasya paraśurāmasya parājayātsaṃbhrāntavacanam |
vaimānikapravṛttyavagamāya vimānapraveśaḥ/ |
ekamayanamadhikaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktā anekāyanāḥ saṃpadyanta ityasminnarthe bhavatiyoge cirapratyayaḥ |
bhavateśca bhāve ghañpratyayaḥ |
ekāyanībhāvaḥ |
rāmātmakaikādhikaraṇakatvamityarthaḥ |
``ayanaṃ nilaye mārge dakṣiṇodaggatau raveḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
dṛṣṭaḥ kimiti kākvā yojanīyam |
indrādayaḥ |
ādipadādvahnyādiparagrahaḥ |
banditvaṃ stutipāṭhakatvam |
``bandinaḥ stutipāṭhakāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kṛśāśvāntevāsītyādiślokāpekṣayedaṃ banditvamuktam |
svataḥ svecchayopagatā iti yat, ayamekāyanībhāva ityanvayaḥ/ |
nirūpitaṃ vicārya nirgītaṃ vastu na visaṃvadati nānyathā bhavati |
``vipralambho visaṃvādaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
sarve taṃ vijayinaṃ nirjarā jānīyurityādinā dvitīyāṅkaviṣkambhe mālyavatā nirūpaṇādyuṣmābhirnirūpitaṃ śūrpaṇakhayoktam |
eṣa saṃcitārthaprāptirūpaḥ kramo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam-``saṃcitārthaprāptiḥ kramaḥ'' iti |
sāṃpratamutkampitāsmi |
sāṃprataṃ paraśurāmaparibhavānantaram |
tatkimatra kāryam |
atra rāmaviṣaye |
tatrāpi śaktitaḥ pratividhāsya iti mālyavatā sāmānyataḥ pratividhānaṃ paraśurāmaparibhavapakṣe pratijñātam, tadviśeṣato'dya śūrpaṇakhayā pṛcchyate kimatra karaṇīyamiti/ |
yā seti |
pratiśrutaṃ pratijñātaṃ varadvayam |
vriyete prārthyete iti varau |
vṛṅ saṃbhaktau'' saṃbhaktiḥ prārthanam, tayordvayaṃ yasyai sā tathoktā |
``aṅgīkārābhyupagamapratiśravasamādhayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kekayasyāpatyaṃ strī kaikeyī |
``kekayamitrayupralayānām'' ityādinā yāderiyādeśaḥ |
vārtāhāriṇī pravṛttiprāpikā |
ayodhyāto'yodhyāyāḥ |
``apādāne ca'' ityādinā pañcamyāstasiḥ |
mithilāmiti |
preṣayateḥ ``gatibuddhi-'' ityādinā dvikarmakatvānmithilāmiti dvitīyā |
preṣitā |
``prayojyakarmaṇyanyeṣāṃ ṇyantānāṃ lādayo matāḥ'' ityukteḥ karmaṇi ktaḥ |
cārairgūḍhapuruṣaiḥ |
``cārastu gūḍhapuruṣaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
tasyā mantharāyāḥ |
karṇe kathayatīti |
``sarthastvekena vijñeyaḥ paścājjñāpyaśca yo bhavet |
karṇe evamivetyuktyā kāvyabandhe tamīrayet||'' ityuktatvādrahasyārthasya karṇe kathanamuktam |
atra ca varadvaye'pyekena rāmasya vanapravāsanamapareṇa bharatasyābhiṣeka ityuttaratra sphuṭībhaviṣyati |
atra prastutopayogicchadmācaraṇādabhūtāharaṇaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam- ``abhūtāharaṇaṃ prastutopayogicchadmācaraṇam'' iti/ |
rāmaḥ kimevaṃ kariṣyati vanaṃ pravekṣyati kim |
anyathā kariṣyati kim |
vanapraveśaṃ vihāya svayaṃ rājyaṃ kariṣyatītyarthaḥ |
atra vitarkapratipādanādrūpaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``vitarkapratipādanaṃ vākyaṃ rūpam'' iti/ |
ikṣvākugṛheṣu sadvṛttaṃ mātṛpitṛvākyakaraṇarūpasamīcīnavṛttaṃ na bhidyate na bhinnaṃ bhavati |
na tyaktaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
tatra heturvijigīṣoriti |
vijigīṣulakṣaṇaṃ kāmandakenoktam- ``saṃpannastu kṛtibhirmahotsāhaḥ kṛtaśramaḥ |
jetumeṣaṇaśīlaśca vijigīṣuriti smṛtaḥ||'' iti |
prakṛtayo'pi tatraivoktāḥ- ``amātyarāṣṭradurgāṇi kośo daṇḍaśca pañcamaḥ |
etāḥ prakṛtayastajjñairvijigīṣorudāhṛtāḥ||'' iti |
sadvṛttahāniramātyarāṣṭrāṇāmaprītiheturiva sā tyājyeti bhāvaḥ |
tādṛśo'smītyanena daśavargayuktatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
daśavargaśca- ``duṣṭānāṃ nigraścaiva śiṣṭānāṃ pragrahastathā |
drutacāranisargaśca rañjanaṃ balarāṣṭrayoḥ/ |
dharmārthayostathā kāryaṃ tathā sainyasya vetanam |
nityakarma kriyā ceti daśa karma pracakṣate||'' ityuktaḥ |
tatra sadācārahānau balarāṣṭrarañjanadharmayorhāniḥ prasajyediti bhāvaḥ |
pitṛvacanapālanam ``jīvatorvākyakaraṇāt'' ityādibhyo dharmo'vagataḥ |
atra prastutotkarṣābhidhānādudāhṛtirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``prastutotkarṣābhidhānamudāhṛtiḥ'' iti/ |
tataḥ kim |
rāmeṇa sadvṛttapālanena pitṛvacanakaraṇāddhetoḥ kiṃ phalaṃ bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ/ |
tato'nenetyādi |
tataḥ |
pitṛvacanaparipālane rāmeṇa kṛte satītyarthaḥ |
anena mayā tava karṇe kathitena yogānāṃ sāmadānabhedadaṇḍātmakānāmupāyānāmācārasyānuṣṭhānasya nyāyena deśakālādyānuguṇyādyupāyeṣu tyājyopādeyavivekapūrvakānuṣṭhānahetvaucityanirūpaṇena |
``yogaḥ saṃnahanopāyadhyānasaṃgatiyuktiṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
``abhreṣanyāyakalpāśca'' iti cāmaraḥ |
yadvā yogācāro nāma buddhiśiṣyeṣvanyatamastaduktena nyāyena saṃvaraṇākhyena |
māyāprayogātmanā daṇḍaviśeṣeṇetyarthaḥ |
rakṣasāmaṅkaṃ samīpaṃ sthānamupanītasya prāpitasya |
``aṅko nāṭakavicchede saṃjñāyāṃ yudhi bhūṣaṇe |
samīpe rūpake sthāne matāvutsaṅgacihnayoḥ||'' iti ratnamālā |
vindhyakānrāreṣu vindhyaparvatavaneṣvadeśajñasya vicarato'deśajñasya sato vicarataḥ |
deśadoṣamajñātvā saṃcarata ityarthaḥ |
avaskandanāni pīḍanāni sukarāṇyeva syuḥ sukhena kartuṃ śakyānyeva bhaveyuḥ |
``īṣadduḥsuṣu kṛcchrākṛcchrārtheṣu khal'' iti karmaṇi karoteḥ khalpratyayaḥ |
virādheti |
tīkṣṇāḥ krūrā daṇḍakāraṇyasatreṣu daṇḍakasya rājño deśaḥ śukraśāpenāraṇyamāsīditi daṇḍakāraṇyasaṃjñakavaneṣu |
``satraṃ yajñe yajñabhede sadādāne vaneṃśuke'' iti ratnamālā |
saṃjñāśabdatvādapunarūktiḥ |
luptā nāśitā prabhuśaktiḥ kośadaṇḍajatejorūpasāmarthyaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasya |
pūrvameva rājyabhraṃśavanavāsābhyāṃ kośadaṇḍajatejorūpasāmarthyaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasya |
pūrvameva rājyabhraṃśavanavāsābhyāṃ kośadaṇḍajaśaktirūpaprabhuśaktivikalasyetyarthaḥ |
rāmasyeti śoṣaḥ |
utsāhaśaktiṃ kartavyeṣu stheyānprayatna utsāhastatra śaktiḥ sāmarthyaṃ tāṃ chadmanā māyāprayogarūpagūḍhaniṣṭakaraṇenātisaṃdhātuṃ vañcanena nāśayituṃ śaktāḥ |
samarthā ityarthaḥ |
atisaṃpūrvako dadhātiriha vañcanayā nāśane prayuktaḥ |
anivartanīya iti |
rāvaṇasya sītāsvākāragrahaḥ sītāsvīkāranirbandhaḥ |
``nirbadhoparāgārkādayo grahāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anivartanīyaḥ prakārāntareṇa nivartayitumaśakyaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
sa ceti |
evaṃ māyāprayoge satyeva sa sītāsvīkāranirbandha iṣatkaro'nāyāsena kartuṃ śakyaḥ, saprayojanaśca sītālābharūpaviṣayasiddhimāṃśca |
bhavediti śeṣaḥ/ |
mālyavatā śūrpaṇakhāyāḥ karṇe kathite vākyasaṃdarbhe ``sītālakṣmaṇamātrakātparijanādanyo na cānuvrajet'' iti sthitam |
tatra lakṣmaṇasyāpi vañcanayā pratyākarṣaṇe kiṃ phalamiti śūrpaṇakhā pṛcchati- atha lakṣmaṇasahāyatve kiṃ prayojanamiti |
athoti praśne/ |
vīra iti |
vīro naisargikaśaktiyukto'strāṇāṃ pāramadhyayanasamāptiparākāṣṭhāṃ gacchatīti gacchateḥ kartari ḍaḥ |
[``]pāra tīra karmasamāptau'' ityasmādbhāve ghañ |
evaṃ rāmo yathā cintyo bhayasthānatvena śaṅkanīyo bhavati, tathaiva vīro'strapārago lakṣmaṇobhayasthānatvena cintanīya ityarthaḥ |
lakṣmaṇena sahāstviti hi pūrvaṃ rāmeṇa prārthitam |
chadmadaṇḍaprayogo gūḍhadamānuṣṭhānam |
``daṇḍo'strī damahiṃsāsenālaguḍeṣu śāsane rājñām'' iti ratnamālā |
ekasminnekatra yathā |
kārya iti śeṣaḥ |
tathā dvayoḥ kāryaḥ |
rāme lakṣmaṇe ca kārya ityarthaḥ||2||

 mama tviti |
apratisamādheyaṃ strīvairaṃ prakārāntareṇānivartyaṃ strīprayuktavairam |
prākṛte streśabdasya ``itthiyā'' ādeśaḥ |
anābaddhavairasya nimittāntaraprayuktavairarahitasya yatsaṃnidhānakaraṇaṃ yacca strīvairametaddvayamiti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
vairaṃ hi pañcavidham |
taduktam- ``sāpatnaṃ vastujaṃ strījaṃ vāgjātamaparādhajam |
vairaprabhedanipuṇairvairaṃ pañcavidhaṃ matam||'' iti |
sa tāvaditi |
tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthakaḥ |
saṃnidhānaṃ yuktameveti prathamaṃ nirūpyata ityarthaḥ |
sa rāmo bhūmerānantaryato bhūmyanantaratvāddhetoḥ pratyāsanna eva saṃnihita eva |
rāvaṇāpekṣayā bhūmyanantaratvaṃ vivakṣitam |
tacca svamaṇḍalasaṃnivṛṣṭamaṇḍalavartitvam |
taduktamamarasiṃhena- ``viṣayānantaro rājā śatrurmitramataḥparam'' iti |
viṣayānantaro bhūmyanantaraḥ |
``nīvṛjjanapadau deśaviṣayau'' iti viṣayaśabdasya deśavācidvāddvādaśavidheṣu rājasu bhūmyanantaro nāma kaścit |
tathāhi- madhyato vijigīṣuḥ, tasyāriḥ, mitram, arermitram, mitraśatruḥ, śatrumitramitraṃ ceti purovartinaḥ pañca |
pārṣṇigrāhaḥ, ākrāntaḥ, pārṣṇigrāhāsāraḥ, pārśvastho madhyamaḥ, eteṣāṃ bahiravassithata udāsīnaśceti rājāno dvādaśavidhā bhavanti |
tadidamuktaṃ kāmandakena- ``saṃpannastu prakṛtibhirmahotsāhaḥkṛtaśramaḥ |
jetumeṣaṇaśīlaśca vijigīṣuriti smṛtaḥ/ |
arirmittramarermittraṃ mittramittramataḥparam |
athārimitramitraṃ ca vijigīṣoḥ puraḥsarāḥ''/ |
``pārṣṇigrāhastataḥ paścādākrantastadanantaram |
āsārāvanayoścaiva vijigīṣostu pṛṣṭhataḥ/ |
areśca vijigīṣośca madhyamo bhūmyanantaraḥ |
anugrahe saṃhatayorvyastayornigrahe prabhuḥ||'' iti |
atra rāvaṇo mālyavatpakṣeṇa vijigīṣuḥ, tathāririndrādidevavargaḥ, mittramasuravarga ityādi yathāyogyaṃ vijigīṣvādikalpanāyāṃ rāmo bhūmyanantaraḥ saṃpadyate |
evaṃ saṃnidhānaṃ nirūpitam |
atha vairasadbhāvaṃ nirūpayati- sānucaretyādinā |
sānucarayoḥ sundopasundaputrayoḥ subāhumārīcayorupaplavādekasya vadharūpādanyasya samudraprakṣepaṇarūpāccopadravāddhetorbhūmyanantarastāṭakī rāmaḥ kathamanābaddhavaraiḥ |
tāṭakāritvātsubāhumārīcopaplavācca rāmo bhūmyanantaraḥ |
baddhavaira evetyarthaḥ |
tathā coktam- ``rājyastrīsthānadeśānāṃ jñātīnāṃ ca dhanasya ca |
apahāro mado mānaḥ'' ityārabhya ``iti vigrahayonayaḥ'' iti |
itarathā pratividheyam |
chadmadaṇḍaprayogeṇa vinā durnivartyamityarthaḥ |
tadeva vairasya durnivartyatvamāha- paśyetyādinā |
munirūpatvābhiprāyeṇa paśyeti vidhiḥ/ |
pālyamiti |
jagattasya rāmasya pālyaṃ rakṣaṇīyam |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
vayaṃ ca rāvaṇādayo vayaṃ tu jagato lokasya nityaṃ haṭhena balātkāreṇādeśinaḥ śāsitāraḥ |
bhavāma ityarthaḥ |
``prasabhastu balātkāro haṭhaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
jagatpālanatatprasabhayorvyāghātāditi bhāvaḥ |
tataḥ kimatrāha- sāmeti |
evaṃ sati rāmasyāsmākaṃ ca jagatsukhaduḥkhatatparatve sati śaśvatsadā viruddhātmanā viparītabuddhinā'priyakṛtā vipriyakāriṇā rāvaṇena kartrā rāmaviṣaye sāmātyarthaṃ madhuravākyaṃ kīdṛk |
kathaṃbhūtamityarthaḥ |
vipriyakṛto vākyamapi vipriyameveti sāmasvarūpameva durghaṭamiti bhāvaḥ |
``sāma sāntvamune same'', ``atyantaṃ madhuraṃ sāntvam'' ityamaraḥ |
anena sāmopāyo na vairapratividhānaheturityuktaṃ bhavati |
tadevāha- tasmāddānamapīha nāstīti |
iha vairapratividhau nāsti |
hetutayeti śeṣaḥ |
iha rāme vā |
tasyaiva devaiḥ patitvena vṛtasyaiva rāmasya bhidā medopāyo naḥ sādhanamasmākaṃ pratividhiheturna bhavati |
vetanena sainyasaṃgrahe'pyadhikavetanapradānapralobhena sainyādīnāṃ pṛthakkaraṇaṃ na ghaṭate |
teṣāṃ svārthatayā svārasikarāmapakṣapātena bhedo durghaṭa iti bhāvaḥ||3||

 daṇḍaḥ paramavaśiṣyate, māyopekṣayostatraivāntarbhāvādindrajālasya bhede'ntarbhāvācca |
saptopāyapakṣānabhyupagamenopāyacatuṣṭayasyaivābhyupagamāt |
taduktaṃ kāmandakena- ``sāma dānaṃ ca bhedaśca daṇḍaśceti catuṣṭayam'' iti |
so'yaṃ daṇḍo dvividhaḥ- prakāśadaṇḍaḥ, tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍaśceti |
tatra prakāśaḥ senābhigamanādinā vadhabandhanādiḥ |
tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍastu gūḍhavadhādiḥ |
ayameva chadmadaṇḍādiśabdena vyavahriyate |
tatra prabale prakāśadaṇḍo na yuktaḥ |
tasmāttūṣṇīṃdaṇḍa evocita ityāha- daṇḍo'pīti |
abhyadhike'tyantādhike |
adhikaḥ kārtavīryaḥ, tato'pyadhikaḥ paraśurāmaḥ, tato'pyadhiko rāma ityabhiprāyeṇābhyadhika ityuktam |
śatrau rāme prakāśo daṇḍaḥ senāyā abhigamanādinā vadhādirna praśasyate na guṇavānbhavati |
kiṃtu prayokturdurbalatvena svarūpocchedakaratayā doṣavāneva bhavatītyarthaḥ |
``śaṃsu stutau'' karmaṇi yaki nalopaḥ |
stutirguṇiniṣṭhaguṇābhidhānamabhyadhike śatrau tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍastu kartavyaḥ kartumarhaḥ |
tuśabdaḥ prakāśadaṇḍavailakṣaṇyadyotakaḥ |
tacca prayoktuḥ svarūpocchedakaratvābhāvarūpam |
ayaṃ tava mantharāśarīrapraveśādistasya tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍasyopakramo buddhipūrvārambhaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``jñātvārambha upakramaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atra prastutopayogisāmadānavacanātsaṃgraho nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
tathoktam- ``prastutopayogisāmadānavacanāsaṃgraho nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
tathoktam- ``prastutopayogisāmadānavacanātsaṃgrahaḥ''||4||

 tathā sati vanavipravāsādau sati sītāpahārahetoḥ kimaparaṃ kuryātkimanyatkartuṃ śaknuyāditi vakṣyamāṇādanyat kimapi kartuṃ na śaknuyāditi bhāvaḥ |
śaki liṅ |
hṛtajāniriti |
atra rāma ityādiḥ |
rāmo'rātibhiḥ śatrubhiḥ |
``abhighātiparārātipratyarthiparipanthinaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
yadi hṛtajānirhṛtadāro bhavedyadi, tadā rāmaḥ salajjo bhavet |
lokamukhanirīkṣaṇānarhatayā yuddhādikāryākṣamo bhavedityarthaḥ |
athavā tasya rāmasya mṛtyurmaraṇaṃ śaraṇaṃ rakṣakaṃ bhavediti śeṣaḥ |
tasyetyapi śeṣaḥ |
prāṇebhyo'pi garīyasyāstasyāḥ kṣaṇavirahāsahatayā tatkṣaṇa eva mriyateti bhāvaḥ |
``śaraṇaṃ gṛharakṣitroḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anyathā tu tatkṣaṇamaraṇābhāve'pi mradito mṛdūkṛtaḥ |
san |
mṛduśabdāttatkarotītyarthe ṇici ṛkārasya rephādeśe ca sati karmaṇi ktaḥ |
mṛto bhavet |
kṣīṇabalaḥ sankrameṇa mriyetetyarthaḥ |
anyathā tvityanuṣajyate |
anyathā tu krameṇa maraṇābhāve'pi niṣpratāpaḥ pūrvameva rājyabhraṃśātkośadaṇḍajatejovirahitaḥ |
``sapratāpaḥ prabhāvaśca yattejaḥ kośadaṇḍajam'' ityamaraḥ |
paritaptaḥ saṃstaptaḥ sansaṃdhau ghaṭeta ceṣṭeta |
``ghaṭa ceṣṭāyām'' |
ceṣṭā hitāhitaparispandaḥ |
yadivetyayamavyayasaṃghāto yadvetyarthe vartamānaḥ sarveṣu kalpeṣvanvetavyaḥ |
saṃdhirnāma vijigīṣorvyavasthākaraṇalakṣaṇamaikyam |
sa ca ṣāḍguṇye prathamaḥ |
``saṃdhirnā vigraho yānamāsanaṃ dvaidhamāśrayaḥ |
ṣaṅguṇāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
tacca ṣaṅguṇaṃ niruktam |
yathā vijigīṣorvyavasthākaraṇalakṣaṇamaikyaṃ saṃdhiḥ, virodho vigrahaḥ, vijigīṣorariṃ prati yātrā yānam, tayormithaḥ pratibaddhaśaktyoḥ kālapratīkṣayā tūṣṇīmavasthānamāsanam, durbalaprabalayordviṣorvācikamātmasamarpaṇaṃ dvaidhībhāvaḥ, ariṇā vidhyamānasya balavadāśrayaṇaṃ samāśraya iti |
etādṛśe saṃdhau vyāpriyetetyarthaḥ |
atra śatruviṣaye vṛttacatuṣṭayaṃ nītisiddham |
tacca- ``ucchedanaṃ cāpajayaḥ pīḍanaṃ karśanaṃ tathā'' ityuktam |
tatra salajja ityapajayaḥ, mṛtyurityucchedanam, mṛta iti pīḍanam, paritapta iti karśanaṃ coktam||5||

 pakṣāntaramāha- uttiṣṭhetetyādi |
atrāpi rāma ityādiḥ |
rāmaḥ paribhavapreddhena sītāpahārarūpatiraskārapradīptena pūrvamevendrādipīḍanena manyuḥ, munipīḍanena tasya dīptatvam, sītāpaharaṇena tu pradīptatvamiti bhāvaḥ |
manyunā krodhena hetunā śatrukṛtāparādhādinā manaḥprajvalanaṃ krodho no'smākaṃ vadhāya |
na ityetanmadhyamaṇinyāyena vadhaparibhāvābhyāmanvetavyam |
uttiṣṭheta cedudyuṅkte cet |
``udo'nūrdhvakarmaṇi'' ityātmanepadam |
``utthānaṃ pauruṣe tantre saṃniviṣṭodgame'pi ca'' ityamaraḥ |
udadhiḥ samudrastasya rāmasya prasaramasmātpratiprasthānam |
``syādāsāraḥ prasaraṇam'' ityamaraḥ |
prasaraṇaśabdaḥ prasaraśabdaśca paryāyau |
niroddhuṃ nivartayituṃ neṣṭe na śaknoti |
``īśa niyamane'' śapo luk |
hi yasmātsa rāmastigmāṃśuvīryaḥ sūryasadṛśapratāpaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``vīryaṃ bale prabhāve ca'' ityamaraḥ |
yadyapi rāmeṇotthāne'pyasmākaṃ jaladurgaḥ samudro'styeva |
yathoktam- ``audakaṃ pārvataṃ vārkṣamairiṇaṃ dhānvanaṃ tathā |
śastaṃ praśastamatibhirjaladurgopacintakaiḥ||'' iti |
``teṣāṃ madhye sapta mahādurgāṇītyaparaṃ jaguḥ |
giridurgaṃ vanāhnaṃ ca salilaṃ paṅkadurgakam/ |
ajariṇaṃ devadurgaṃ ca miśrākhyamiti saptadhā||'' iti ca, tathāpi rāmasya tadakiṃcitkaram |
sūryavattasya jalaśoṣaṇasamarthatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
evaṃ śūrpaṇakhābhipretaṃ samudradurgapakṣaṃ dūṣayitvā svapakṣamāha- kiṃtviti |
prākpratipannarāvaṇasuhṛdbhāvena pūrvameva svīkṛtarāvaṇasakhyena bhīmaujasā rāvaṇasyāpi bhayahetubalayuktena |
``ājaḥ śabdaguṇe jñāne bale garvaprakāśayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
ghoreṇa rāmasyāpi bhayasthānabhūtena vajradharātmajenendraputreṇa hariṇā vānareṇa |
vālineti phalitam |
śatrū rāmo ghāniṣyate haniṣyate |
``hana hiṃsāyām'' ityasmātkarmaṇi lṛṭ, ``syasic-'' ityādinā ciṇvadbhāvādvṛddhiriḍāgamaśca |
``aciṇṇaloḥ-'' ityukteściṇi takārādeśābhāvaḥ kutvaṃ ca |
atra cendrātmajasya kathamiṇdrapakṣarāmavipakṣatvamiti śaṅkāvāraṇāya hariṇetyuktam |
tasya kāpeyātpitṛpakṣavipratipakṣatvaṃ saṃbhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
``etadapyasya kāpeyaṃ yadarkamupatiṣṭhati'' iti nyāyo'trābhipretaḥ |
nanu durbalasya tasya kathaṃ rāmapratyanīkatvamiti śaṅkāvāraṇāya bhīmaujaseti |
tādṛśabalādhikye ko heturiti śaṅkāvāraṇāya ghoreṇeti |
samudgatānāṃ sarveṣāmapi balākarṣaṇenopacīyamānabalatvaṃ ghoratvam, ato rāmabalamapyākarṣediti hṛdayam |
nanu tathāpi rāvaṇakāryaṃ kutaḥ sa kariṣyatīti śaṅkāvāraṇāya pratipannarāvaṇasuhṛdbhāveneti |
``śarīraṃ vasu vijñānaṃ mittrārthaṃ saṃparityajet'' ityuktermittrakāryaṃ kariṣyatyeveti bhāvaḥ |
nanu tathāpi rāmeṇa vālino mittrīkaraṇe sa kathaṃ rāmaṃ haniṣyatīti śaṅkāvāraṇāya prāgiti |
pūrvameva vālino rāvaṇena mittrīkaraṇāttasyāgramittrāriṇā rāmeṇa mittrīkaraṇameva na ghaṭata iti bhāvaḥ |
ato nirdoṣo'yaṃ pakṣa iti hṛdayam |
evaṃ ṣāḍguṇye caramoddiṣṭaḥ samāśrayaḥ kartuṃ yukta ityuktam |
samāśrayo hyariṇā pīḍyamānasya balavadāśrayaṇarūpaḥ |
atreṣṭārthopāyānusaraṇādākṣepo nāma saṃdhyaṅgatamuktam |
yathoktam- ``iṣṭārthopāyānusaraṇamākṣepaḥ'' iti||6||

 evaṃ parapakṣabhaye'pahṛte'pi rāvaṇasya svapakṣabhayaṃ yadīdamityāśaṅkya pariharati- anena prasaṅgenetyārabhya nopekṣetyantena |
anena prasaṅgena |
parapakṣobhayopanyāsaprayuktopasthitiviṣayatve satyupekṣānarhatvarūpeṇopasthitasyopekṣānarhatvaṃ hi prasaṅgaḥ |
bahuvistareṇa vakṣyamāṇamarthajātamanusaṃdhātavyaṃ nirūpayitavyam/ |
rāvaṇapriyeti |
kāryaṃ jānātīti kāryajñā |
``āto'nupasarge kaḥ'' iti kaḥ |
niḥśaṅkaṃ parebhyaḥ kathanaśaṅkārahitaṃ yathā tathā hṛdayakheda āvedyate kathyate |
tubhyamiti śeṣaḥ |
atra mitraṃ trividham- kṛtrimaṃ sahajaṃ prākṛtaṃ ceti |
evaṃ śatrusttrividhaḥ- kṛtrimaḥ sahajaḥ prākṛtaśceti |
tatropakārātmakakriyayā nivṛttaṃ kṛtrimaṃ mittram |
ekaśarīrajātaṃ mittraṃ sahajamittram, tacca mātṛṣvaseyapaitṛṣvaseyādirūpaṃ prakṛtyā svabhāvena siddham |
pūrvoktakṛtrimamittratvarahitaṃ mittraṃ prākṛtamittram |
tacca viṣayānantararājarūpam |
evamapakārakriyayā nirvṛttaḥ śatruḥ kṛtrimaḥ, ekaśarīrajātaḥ pitṛvyatatputrādirūpaḥ sahajaśatruḥ, viṣayānantaraḥ prākṛtaśatruḥ |
tatra rāmo rāvaṇasya na sahajaśatruḥ kṣattriyatvāt, kiṃtu viṣayānantaratvātprākṛtaḥ śatruḥ |
apakartṛtvādinā ca kṛtrimaśatrurbhavati |
vibhīṣaṇastu rāvaṇasya sahajaśatrutvādrāmasya tu guṇataḥ pratyāsannaḥ rāvaṇasyāpakuryāditi bhayasthānamityāha- kṣiterānantaryādityādinā |
kṣattriyo rāmaḥ kṣiterānantaryātpūrvoktabhūmyanantaratvāddhetoḥ prakṛtiniyataḥ svabhāvasiddhaḥ |
prākṛta iti yāvat |
kiṃcāpakṛdapakṛtyaścetyapakartāpakāryaśceti |
kṛtrimaśceti yāvat |
dvidhā śatruḥ prākṛtatvakṛtrimatvarūpaprakāradvayayuktaḥ śatruḥ |
rāvaṇasya bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kṣattriyapadaṃŸ
ṛ sahajaśatrutvābhāvavivakṣayā kṣattriyo rāmaḥ kṣiterānantaryātprakṛtiniyata ityapakṛdapakṛtyaśceti dvidhā śatrurbhavatīti yojanā |
rajanicaranāthasya sahajo ripuḥ |
bhrātṛtvāditi śeṣaḥ |
me tṛtīyo naptā mama tṛtīyo dauhitraḥ |
vibhīṣaṇa ityarthaḥ |
pratyāsatterguṇato rāmapratyāsatteḥ, deśato dāyādataśca rāvaṇapratyāsatteḥ saṃnikarṣādahirivaikāśrayāśritaḥ sarpa iva no'smākaṃ bhayaṃ bhāvyanarthaśaṅkāṃ janayatyutpādayati |
ataḥ svapakṣaprabhavabhayaṃ vartata iti bhāvaḥ |
ahirivetyanenāhibhayaśabdo'heriva bhayamiti vyutpattyā svapakṣaprabhavabhaye nirūḍha iti dhvanitam |
``mahībhujāmahibhayaṃ svapakṣaprabhavaṃ bhayam'' ityamaraśca |
atra śaṅkātrāsayorukteḥ saṃbhramo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``śaṅkātrāsau ca saṃbhramaḥ'' iti||7||

 nanu kumbhakarṇaḥ kathaṃ na śaṅkyate, kathaṃ vibhīṣaṇādbhayaṃ tena na nivartyetetyatrāha- kumbhakarṇastviti |
kṛtrimasvāpavyasanādbrāhmavarātmakakriyayā nirvṛttanityanidrāduḥkhādavinayācca kiñcijjāgare cātyāhārādikaraṇena madotkaṭatvācca sannapyasatsama ityanvayaḥ |
vibhīṣaṇasya bhayahetutvaṃ vivṛṇoti- vibhīṣaṇastvityādinā |
vibhīṣaṇastu |
tuśabdaḥ kumbhakarṇādvyāvṛttidyotakaḥ |
abhigamāya prabhavantītyābhigāmikāḥ |
``tasmai prabhavati saṅgrāmādibhyaḥ'' iti ṭhak pratyayaḥ |
amātyādikartṛkābhigamahetubhūtā ityarthaḥ |
tathāvidhairātmaguṇaiḥ saṃpannaḥ samṛddha iti hetorenaṃ pūrvoktaṃ vibhīṣaṇam |
anvādeśe idama enādeśaḥ |
prakṛtayo'mātyā anuratāḥ |
anuraktā ityarthaḥ |
evamiti ``lakṣaṇetthaṃbhūta-'' ityādinānoḥ karmapravacanīyasaṃjñāyām |
``karmapravacanīyayukte dvitīyā'' iti dvitīyā |
ābhigāmikaguṇavargaśca kāmandakīye darśitaḥ- ``suśīlatvaṃ vayasyatvaṃ dākṣiṇyaṃ kṣiprakāritā |
avisaṃvāditā satyaṃ vṛddhasevā kṛtajñatā/ |
daivasaṃpannatābuddhirakṣudraparivāratā |
śakyasāmantatā caiva tathaiva dṛḍhabhaktitā/ |
dīrghadarśitvamutsāhaḥ śucitā sthūlalakṣatā |
vinītatā dhārmikatā guṇāḥ sādhvābhigāmikāḥ/ |
guṇairetairupaitaḥ sannuvyaktamabhigamyate||'' iti |
``amātyarāṣṭradurgāṇi kośo daṇḍaśca pañcamaḥ |
etāḥ prakṛtayastajjñairvijigīṣorudāhṛtaḥ||'' iti prakṛtayo'pi tatraiva darśitāḥ |
kharadūṣaṇaprabhṛtayastviti |
saṃghena vṛttirjīvanaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
pratyekaṃ buddhisāmarthyaśūnyā ityarthaḥ |
rājānaṃ svāminamupatiṣṭhante bhajanti |
tiṣṭhateḥ ``vā lipsāyām'' ityātmanepadam |
yato yasmāddhetostena saṃghena |
vatsenaiva dhenumiti śeṣaḥ |
rājānamarthānduhanti |
duhirdvikarmakaḥ |
dhenorvatsamiva rājñaḥ saṃghaṃ darśayitvā dhenoḥ sakāśātpaya iva rājñaḥ sakāśātprayojanāni saṃpādayanta ityarthaḥ |
prayojanārthināṃ teṣāṃ svāminiyamo na vivakṣita iti bhāvaḥ |
yadvā tena vibhīṣaṇena vatsenevetyādipūrvavat |
sahajaripumimamevāvaṣṭabhya kadācitpṛthagbhaveyuriti śaṅkamānādrājñaḥ svaprayojanāni labhanta iti bhāvaḥ |
tarhi mukhāntareṇa prakṛtayo vibhīṣaṇādbhedyantāmityatrāha- upajāpitā iti |
prakṛtayo'mātyādaya upajāpitāḥ satyaḥ pratyupajapanti |
bhedakāneva vibhīṣaṇānuguṇyena bhindyurityurthaḥ |
``bhedopajāpau'' ityamaraḥ |
vibhīṣaṇaguṇagrāhiṇyaḥ prakṛtayo rāvaṇadoṣānusandhānādbhedakāneva bhindyuriti bhāvaḥ |
``alabdhavetano lubdho mānī cāpyavamānitaḥ |
kruddhaśca kopito'kasmādabhītaścāpi bhīṣitaḥ/ |
yathābhilaṣitaiḥ kāmairbhindyādenāṃścaturvidhān||'' ityuktaprakāro hi bhedopāyaḥ |
tatra vibhīṣaṇasya sthūlalakṣyatvalakṣaṇābhigāmikaguṇasaṃpannatvādatra nālabdhavetanaḥ kaścitsaṃbhavati |
akṣudraparivāratvācca nāsya parivāro lubdhaḥ saṃbhavati |
vinītatvāccāsya nāvamānito'nena kaścitsaṃbhavati |
dākṣiṇyasaṃpannatvāccāsya na kopito'nena kaścidasti |
dhārmikatvāccāsya nāsmādbhītaḥ kaścidapi parivāro'stīti tatrāopajāpo durghaṭa iti |
kiṃtu tatpakṣīyaiḥ pratyupajāpa eva saṃbhāvita iti hṛdayam |
tadidamiti |
tattasmāddhetorantarbhedena svajanairmitho vaimatyena jarjaramantaḥ śithilaṃ rājakulaṃ rāvaṇagṛhaṃ rāmeṇābhiyuktamātraṃ yuddhāyābhiyātameva sat |
na tūpajāpitamityarthaḥ |
bhidyate svayameva bhinnaṃ bhaviṣyati |
karmakartari prayogaḥ |
vispaṣṭavaimatyatatpūrvapṛthagbhāvādityuktaṃ bhavediti bhāvaḥ |
asminnarthe nītiśāstrasaṃvādamāha- yathoktamityādinā |
laghvapi svalpamapi vyasanapadaṃ bhraṃśasthānaṃ kāmakrodhaprabhavadoṣasthānaṃ vā |
``vyasanaṃ vipadi bhraṃśedoṣe kāmajakopaje'' ityamaraḥ |
vyasanasthānamityantarbhedo'bhipretaḥ |
abhiyuktasya pareṇābhiyātasya |
idaṃ pūrvottarānvayi |
abhiyuktasya vyasanapadamabhiyuktasya kṛcchrasādhyaṃ bhavati duḥkhena pratikāryaṃ bhavati |
``sādhanamanugamagatyoḥ śephasi siddhau nivṛttidāpanayoḥ |
mṛtasaṃskāre hetau dhanahiṃsāyātanāsu senāṅge||'' iti ratnamālā |
sādhayatiratra nirvṛtau ca vartate |
tatra bhede sati vibhīṣaṇāvagrahasya vibhīṣaṇapṛthagbhāvasya padacchedavācino'vagrahaśabdasyātra pṛthagbhāvamātraparatvam |
pratividhānaṃ pratīkāraḥ |
tatra na tāvajjyeṣṭhena kaniṣṭhe sāmaprayoga ucitaḥ |
nāpi dānaṃ saṃsṛṣṭitvāt |
bhedastu na ghaṭata iti pūrvamevoktamityabhiprāyeṇāha- sa tu prakāśadaṇḍa iti |
vidheyaprādhānyātpulliṃgatā |
saḥ |
pratividhānamityarthaḥ |
pratividhānaśabdābhiprāyeṇa vā sa itira puṃstvam |
``uddeśyapratinirdeśyayorekatvamāpādayanti sarvanāmāni tattalliṅgamupapādayanti kāmacāre'' iti śābdikokteḥ saṃrodhanānvayitve'pi puṃliṅgatvam |
apasāraṇaṃ nagarānnirvāsanam |
tatra teṣu prakāśadaṇḍādiṣu caturṣau prakāśaṃ prakāśadaṇḍamabhinnasaṃbandhāḥ samānasvasvāmitādisaṃbandhāḥ kathaṃ titikṣeran saheran |
``tijeḥ kṣamāyām'' iti san |
tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍo'pi prājñairūhāpohakuśalairanumīyamāno liṅgena jñāyamānaḥ sanprakṛtīnāmamātyādīnāṃ kopako manaḥprajvalanahetuḥ |
``tatparayojakaḥ-'' iti bhāṣyasūtrayoḥ prayogāt ``tṛjakābhyāṃ kartari'' iti samāsaniṣedhasyānityatvātprakṛtikopaka iti ṣaṣṭhīsamāsaḥ sādhuḥ |
śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā vā samāsaḥ |
rāme'bhiyoktari sati senayābhiyāte sati durantaḥ syādapratividheyaḥ syāt |
``anto'strī niścaye nāśe surūpo'gre'ntike'ntare'' iti ratnamālā |
atra liṅgādyūhanokte'numānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``liṅgābhyūhanamanumānam'' iti |
saṃrodhana iti |
saṃrodhane vihite sati tvabhibhavāddhetoridaṃ vikuryurityatrānveti |
tatra hetuḥ- padaikamatyāditi |
tena vibhīṣaṇena sahābhinnamatatvātkharaprabhṛtayo'pi vikuryuḥ krodhādirūpāṃ vikṛtiṃ prāpnuyuḥ |
atastanna yuktamiti bhāvaḥ |
ataḥ kharādīnāṃ prakāśadaṇḍādicatuṣṭayaparipanthitvāttata eva purataścintyā ityāha- tasmāditi |
pura eva pūrvameva cintyāḥ |
bhayasthānatvena śaṅkanīyā ityarthaḥ||8||

 tatra kharādiviṣaye rāmasyopayogaḥ |
cikīrṣitakāryahetutvaṃ syāditi śeṣaḥ |
kenacidapaśabdena kharādiṣu rāmeṇa vyāpāditeṣu vibhīṣaṇasyāpasārādikaṃ suśaṅkamiti prathamamasmābhirupāyato rāmeṇa kharaprabhṛtayo ghātayitavyā iti  rāmasyāsmaccikīrṣitopayoga iti bhāvaḥ |
atra kharaprabhṛtīṣṭajanātisaṃdhānādatibalaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``iṣṭajanātisaṃdhānamatibalam'' iti / |
anujīvitvasyāmātyabhāvena rāvaṇāpekṣajīvikāyāḥ |
gurveva gurukam |
yadvā kutsitaṃ gurukam |
``kutsite'' iti kan |
bandhuṣu vaiṣamyaprayojakatvātkutsitatvam |
gurukasya bhāvo gurukatā |
aho |
vismayanīyetyarthaḥ |
tulye'nyonyasaṃbandhe |
tvayeti śeṣaḥ |
mātāmahadauhitrādisaṃbandhe samāne satītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ chadmanā kharādivadhaṃ mātāmahaścintayati |
tvaṃ cintayasītyarthaḥ/ |
kulaputrakāṇāṃ sadvaṃśajātānāmatyantabālānāmapyācāro'nuṣṭhānamīdṛśaḥ khalu |
svāmikāryaikatatpara iti prasiddhamityarthaḥ / |
kharapramukhairvinā |
