teṣāmabhāve vibhīṣaṇasya kaḥ kartavyādhyavasāya ityarthaḥ/ |
prājñaḥ khalūhāpohakuśalaḥ kilāsau vibhīṣaṇaḥ svayamevokṣitavikāro'numitāsmadviruddhābhiprāyaḥ sannapasarpet |
nagarāditi śeṣaḥ |
upekṣaṇīyastvasmābhiḥ |
tuśabdaḥ pakṣāntaradyotakaḥ |
athavāsmābhirupekṣaṇīyaḥ |
nānugrāhyo na nigrāhyaścetyarthaḥ |
na caivaṃ mantavyamaurasādbhayamiti |
svayamavekṣitāsmadvikāratayāpasarpato'pyasmādaurasādbandhubhūtādekākino vibhīṣaṇādbhayamakiñcitkaramiti ca mantavyamevetyarthaḥ |
tatra hetumāha- yata iti |
bālyāditi |
eṣa vibhīṣaṇo bālyātprabhṛti bālyādārabhya nirūḍhasakhyaṃ pravṛddhamaittrīkaṃ bālinā prasādīkṛte'nugrahatvena datte bhūmibhāge bhūmerekadeśe |
kumārairbhujyata iti kumārabhuktiḥ |
bhujeḥ karmaṇi ktin |
kumārabhuktirnāma kumārāṇāṃ rājabhiḥ kalpitā vṛttiḥ sugrīvasya vālinaṃ pratyanujatvāt ``jyeṣṭhabhrātā pitṛsamaḥ'' iti nyāyena sugrīvasya taṃ prati kumāratvāttaddattabhūbhāgasya kumārabhuktitvoktiḥ |
dhāvanaṃ vyājamātraṃ prasādenaiva vālī sugrīvasyarṣyamūkamanumanyata iti hṛdayam |
ṛṣyamūke sthitaṃ sugrīvamāśrayeta |
saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ |
dhruvamityapi saṃbhāvanāyām |
jyeṣṭhabhrātrā virodhasyobhayoraviśiṣṭatvācca sugrīvāśrayaṇaṃ saṃbhāvitamityarthaḥ||9||

 evamapi nāsmākaṃ kṣatirityāha-tatrastho vālinā ghāniṣyate |
ciṇvadbhāvādvṛddhiḥ |
durbalamimaṃ sugrīvamivopekṣeta vālītyatrāha- rāmeti |
rāmo hṛtabhāryaḥ kadācidekākī maitryā tatrāgacchet, taṃ ca sugrīvaḥ samāśrayet, vibhīṣaṇo vā sākṣādāśrayet |
tataśca prabalaṃ rāmaṃ prati saṃdhāya prāgeva vālī vibhīṣaṇaṃ sugrīveṇa saha hanyātsvamittrarāvaṇāritvāt, svaśatrusugrīvamitratvāt, svamittrarāvaṇaśatrurāmamittratvācceti bhāvaḥ |
rāmopāśrayeṇa sākṣādeva rāmāśrayaṇena rāmopaśleṣeṇa sugrīvadvārā tadāśrayaṇena hetunā nopekṣeta |
upekṣituṃ nārhati |
tamiti śeṣaḥ/ |
evaṃ mālyavatā svapakṣaprabhavaṃ bhayamāśaṅkya parihṛtam |
tatra śūrpaṇakhā prakārāntareṇa śaṅkate- atheti pakṣāntaraprastāve |
vyāpādayati hanti |
``vyāpādo drohacintanam'' ityamaraḥ |
``druha jighāṃsāyām'' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ |
paraśurāmamivetyanena śaṅkābījamupanyastam |
rāmo vālinaṃ jeṣyati |
vālino'dhikaparaśurāmajiṣṇutvāditi ca tadvījam |
evaṃ paraśurāmo vālino'dhiko rāvaṇajetṛtayā vālitulyakārtavīryajetṛtvāditi ca śaṅkāṅgaśaṅkāntaramityabhipretam |
rāghava iti svābhiprāyaśaraṇāgatarakṣaṇavratatayā prasiddharaghuvaṃśaprabhavo rāmaḥ śaraṇāgatau vibhīṣaṇasugrīvāvapekṣya vālinaṃ haniṣyatītyabhiprāyeṇa rāghava ityuktam |
atrāpyanumānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ |
ya iti |
yo vālinaṃ  hanti tena ca vayaṃ hatāḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
dhruvamiti saṃbhāvanāyām |
atra yo hanti tena hatā iti sāmānyoktyā rāmo rāvaṇādīnasmānhaniṣyati rāvaṇajetṛvālihantṛtvādityanumānaṃ vivakṣitam |
tena rāmeṇa sarvanāśe sarveṣāṃ rāvaṇādīnāṃ vadhe sati sarveṣāṃ nāśa iti śeṣaṣaṣṭhyā samāsaḥ |
``karmaṇi ca'' ityubhayaprāptau ṣaṣṭhyāḥ samāsaniṣedhāt |
kulatanturnaṣṭasya vṛddhihetuḥ sūkṣmāṃśa ekaḥ sa vibhūṣaṇo jīvyātsaprāṇo bhūyāt |
āśiṣi liṅ |
asmai vibhīṣaṇāya dharmamayo dharmapracuro rāmaḥ śriyaṃ laṅkaiśvaryaṃ dadātu |
āśiṣi loṭ |
atra tatvārthānukīrtanānmārgo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``tatvārthānukīrtanaṃ mārgaḥ'' iti||10||

 evamapi tāvadbhavatu |
apiśabdo'nukalpatvadoṣaḥ |
rāvaṇamṛtirmukhyaḥkalpaḥ tadalābhe'pi vibhīṣaṇāmṛtiranukalpo'pyāśāsyata ityarthaḥ/ |
yatra preṣitāsi |
mithilāyāmiti śeṣaḥ |
sukaraṃ sukhena kartuṃ śakyametatprayojanaṃ mantharāśarīrāveśādikam |
yadi janaketyādi |
yadi na syātāṃ tadā sukaramiti saṃbandhaḥ |
anena cottarāṅke praveśatāṃ janakādīnāṃ caturṇāṃ madhye vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayornirgamaḥ sūcitaḥ |
atrāṅkasya muktamiti draṣṭavyam/ |
hā ambeti |
duḥkhaṃ prekṣitavyam |
vibhīṣaṇamātrapariśeṣasya bhāvitvāditi bhāvaḥ/ |
hā vatsā iti |
he vatsāḥ kharadūṣaṇaprabhṛtayaḥ |
yūyamityadhyāhāraḥ |
pāpasya bandhuvadhaprayojakakuṭilanayaniṣṇātatāhetuduritapracurasya me mayā |
``kṛtyānāṃ kartari vā''iti ṣaṣṭhī |
vadhyā hantavyā stha bhavatha |
astermadhyamapuruṣabahuvacanam |
hā duḥkhyate |
``hā viṣādaśugarteṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
vatsa vibhīṣaṇa, tvamapi kāryeṇa rāvaṇakāryeṇa me pāpena mayā sthiteḥ sthānāt |
``sthānamaryādayoḥ sthitiḥ''iti ratnamālā |
heyastyājyaḥ |
bhavasīti śeṣaḥ |
hā hā iti dviruktirviṣādātiśaye |
mayi vatsala prītiman |
``vatsāṃsābhyāṃ kāmabale'' iti vṛttiviṣaye prītivācakādvatsaśabdānmatvarthīyo lacpratyayaḥ |
vatsa |
dauhitratvādvatsaśabdaḥ |
te mahatsaṃkaṭaṃ duḥkhasaṃbādham |
``saṃkaṭaṃ nā tu saṃbādhaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
paśyāmi nyāyadṛṣṭyā niścinomi |
vatse, jyeṣṭhabhrātuḥ putri |
kekasīti rāvaṇamāturnāma |
hatāsi |
``putrotpattivipattibhyāṃ na paraṃ sukhaduḥkhayoḥ'' iti prasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ |
hā duḥkhyase nacirācchīghrameva trīnputrakānkharakumbhakarṇarāvaṇān |
kharaḥ kekasyāḥ svasṛputraḥ |
anukampāyāṃ kan |
drakṣyasi |
rāmeṇa hatānpretya tvatsamīpagamanāditi bhāvaḥ ||11||

 miśraviṣkambhaḥ |
saṃskṛtaprākṛtātmakaviṣkambhaḥ/ |
athāṅkaḥ prastūyate- tataḥ praviśata ityādinā |
rājānau janakadaśarathau |
pariṣvajyāliṅgya |
diṣṭyetyānande |
vardhase śreyasvī bhavasi |
putraviṣayājjāmātṛviṣayācca śreyasvitvam |
īdṛśaḥ paraśurāmasyāpi vinetā te vatsaḥ |
jāmāturapi putraprāyatvājnakaṃ prati vatsatvam |
jāmātā hi śvaśurasya śiṣyatvātputrasthānīyaḥ |
tadāha bhāgavate dakṣaḥ- ``eṣa me śiṣyatāṃ prāpto yanme duhituragrahīt |
pāṇiṃ viprāgnimukhataḥ sāvitryā iva sādhuvat||'' iti |
aprākṛtānīti |
aprākṛtāni divyāni guṇairjñānādibhiḥ śauryādibhirvinayādibhiḥ saundaryādibhiśca nirantarāṇi nibiḍāni lokottarāṇi sarvābhyarhitāni phalaiśca mahodayāni mahāphalajanakāni saṃpratyasaṃnikarṣāttasyetyuktam |
caritādbhutāni caritarūpāṇyāścaryāṇyasmākamevāvayoreva maṅgalāni bhavantīti na |
kiṃtu jagatāmapi maṅgalāni bhavantītya6ḥ |
agarhiteṣṭārthasiddhirmaṅgalam ||12||

 asmābhiriti |
asmābhiḥ |
āvābhyāmityarthaḥ |
rāmasya mahimānvayo mahimno vibhūtiguṇādyatiśayasyānvayaḥ saṃbandho'nāśāsyaḥ |
kuta ityatrāha- yaditi |
tena rāmeṇa vayaṃ kṛtinaḥkṛtāḥ kṛtārthāḥ, gurutvalakṣaṇena svasaṃbandhena paramapuruṣārthabhāginaḥ kṛtāḥ, bhuvanāni ca kṛtārthāni kṛtāni |
kṛtina ityāderanuṣaktasya liṅgavipariṇāmaḥ |
tadadhīnābhyudayābhyāmāvābhyāṃ na tadabhyudaya āśāsanīyaḥ, evamanyeṣāmapi tadadhīnābhyudayatvena tadabhyudayakārakatvaṃ na saṃbhavatītyarthaḥ||13||

 eṣa mahimā rāmasya mahimā prakṛṣṭo'tiśayitaḥ puṇyaparipākaḥ puṇyasya phalapradānonmukhībhāva upādānaṃ kāraṇaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``puṇyānāmapi puṇyo'sau'' ityukteḥ svayameva rāmaḥ prakṛṣṭapuṇyātmakaḥ |
tato'nanyādhīno'yaṃ rāmasya mahimeti bhāvaḥ |
etāvataḥ paraśurāmavijayaparyantasya prakṛṣṭasyātiśayasya ke vayam |
āśāsanena niṣpādayitumaśaktā ityarthaḥ/ |
mā maivam |
ke vayamiti vaktuṃno citamityarthaḥ |
ādityā iti |
maṅgalānāṃ nidhirbhavānnaḥ prasanna iti yadasmāsu kṛtānugraha iti yat, ayameva pāka iti yojanā |
ādityāḥ sūryavaṃśyāḥ |
``dityadityāditya-'' ityādinādityaśabdādapatyārthe ṇyapratyaye ``halo yamāṃ yami-'' iti yakāralopaḥ |
pūrve mattaḥ prācīnā dilīpādayo nṛpāḥ |
kuladevatāmiva śrīraṅganāthamiva tejorāśiṃ prabhāvanidhimṛṣiṃ satyavacasam |
``mukhyasya nirviśeṣeṇa śabdo'nyeṣāṃ viśeṣataḥ'' iti nyāyena mukhyamṛṣibhūtamarundhatīpatim ``nidhirapi pavitrasya mahasaḥ'' ityuktāyā arundhatyāḥ patiṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ bhaktyārādhyatvajñānenārādhayannapūjayan |
atra ``yasya deve parā bhaktiryathā deve tathā gurau'' iti śrutiranusaṃhitā |
iti yattasya ca bhūritapasāṃ pracuratapasāṃ satyāśiṣāmamoghāśāsanānāṃ yā āśiṣaḥ santi tāsāmapi tātaḥ phalapradānonmukhībhāvaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
vidheyaprādhānyādayamityuddeśyavācakasya puṃliṅgatvam||14||

 īdṛśaḥ daśarathoktamāhātmyayukta ityarthaḥ |
yadvācāmityādi |
yanmahatvaṃ vācāṃ cetasāṃ vā girāṃ manasaśca |
``vā syādvikalpopamayorivārthe'pi samuccaye'' ityamaraḥ |
viṣayaṃ deśam |
``deśaviṣayau'' iti cāmaraḥ |
atītyātikramya |
``parāvarayoge ca'' iti ktvāpratyayaḥ |
``samāse nañpūrve
ṛ ktvo lyap'' iti lyabādeśaḥ |
vāṅmanasāgocara ityarthaḥ |
yanmahattvamatiśāyanasya viśvādhikasya prakarṣasya |
atipūrvāccheterbhāve lyuṭ |
``atiśāyane tamabiṣṭhanau'' iti sūtre'tiśāyana iti nipātanāddīrghaḥ |
paryāyādatiśayāt |
``paryāyo'vasare krame''ityamaraḥ |
kramavācinā paryāyaśabdenātiśāyanārthaśabdāntaravācyo'tiśayo gṛhyate |
tathā ca viśvāvadhikaprakarṣāvadhikaprakarṣādityarthaḥ |
param |
atiśayitamityarthaḥ |
viśvāvadhikaprakarṣo yastadavadhiko'pi yaḥ prakarṣastatadavadhikaprakarṣayuktaṃ ca yadbhavatīti bhāvaḥ |
``yadvācām'' ityādinā pramāṇādhikatvamuktam |
tatra vāṅmanasayoḥ saṃkucitatvaviṣayatvasvābhāvyātprāptasya saṃkocasya vyudāso'pi prameyasya svataḥ saṃkocaḥ saṃbhāvyeta |
ataḥ prameyasvābhāvyādapi saṃkoco nāstīti bodhanāya paryāyātparamityādi prakarṣasyātyantaniravadhikatvavivakṣayā cottarottarāvadhinirdeśaḥ |
tattejobhiḥ prabhāvaiḥ |
``tejaḥ prabhāve dīptau ca'' ityamaraḥ |
samiddhaṃ samyagdīptam |
``indhī dīptau'' ityasmāt ``gatyartha-'' ityādinā kartari kto nakāralopaśca |
aprameyamaparicchedyam |
atra paryāyātparamityanvayoktiḥ |
aprameyamiti vyatirekoktiḥ |
ato na punaruktiḥ |
anena ca mahattvasya prāyikatvavyudāso durāsado māhātmyātiśayāt |
asmābhirapi samīpamāsādayitumaśakte brahmaṣag svatapasā prāptamaharṣitvayukte viśvāmitre mahattvamutkarṣo jvalati dīpyate |
``jvala dīptau'' ||15||

mitrāvaruṇayorayaṃ maitrāvaruṇaḥ |
``tasyedam'' iti saṃbandhasāmānye'ṇpratyayaḥ |
vasiṣṭhasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
sanatkumāreti |
sanatkumārāṅgirasoḥ svajyeṣṭhabhrātrorapi sanatkumārāṅgirasorgaururācāryo vidyayādhyātmavidyayā tapasā kāyaśoṣaṇaniyamena brahmabhāvanāniṣṭhasya sanatkumārasya vidyopadeṣṭā |
karmabhāvanāniṣṭhasyāṅgirasastapasāmupadeṣṭeti bhāvaḥ |
tvamiti śeṣaḥ |
evaṃvidhastvaṃ māṃ stauṣi cedguṇavantaṃ kathayasi cetstutya evāsmyabhidheyaguṇavānevāsmi |
hi yasmātte giraḥ satyā amoghāḥ svaviṣayasadbhāvaupayikāḥ śuddhāḥ pūtā jñātāṃśeṣvayāthārthyarahitāḥ |
khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
guṇiniṣṭhaguṇābhidhānaṃ hi stutiḥ |
sā yadyapi guṇaśūnye mayi na ghaṭate, tathāpi stāvakasya tava sūkteramoghatayā mama brahmarṣitvavanmāhātmyarūpeṇāpyata eva niṣpadyata iti stutyatvamupapadyata iti bhāvaḥ |
atra ca devahūteḥ pracetasāṃ ca svasvaputraṃ kapilādikaṃ pratīva sanatkumārāṅgirasorapi svānujaṃ vasiṣṭhaṃ pratyupapannaṃ śiṣyatvam |
tadidaṃ purāṇeṣvanusaṃdheyam |
ata eva ``na ca hi vayo jātiḥ prakṛṣṭānām'' ityetadanyaviṣayaṃ vyasthāpyam ||16||

 rāmabhadramāhātmyaṃ na matprayuktaṃ bhavadvacanenedānīmeva māhātmyaniṣpatteḥ, kiṃtu piturdaśarathasya saṃbandhaprayuktamityāha- rāmabhadre tviti |
etanmāhātmyaṃ nāścaryaṃ nāpūrvam |
``āścaryamanitye'' iti sūtreṇāpūrvavastunyāścaryaśabdo nipātitaḥ |
hi yasmāddaśaratho'sya janayitā pitā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
ataḥ pitṛprabhāvādidaṃ rāmamāhātmyaṃ nāpūrvamityarthaḥ |
sākṣāditi |
sākṣātpuṇyasamucchrayā iva pratyakṣatāpannasukṛtarāśaya iti saṃbhāvanārhāḥ |
ivaśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām |
taduktam- ``yadāyamupakramāṃśo lokato'siddhimṛcchati |
tadotprekṣaiva yenevaśabdasaṃbhāvanāparaḥ||'' iti |
upamānapuṇyasamucchrayāṇāṃ svarūpeṇa lokaprasiddhānāmapi pratyakṣatāpannatvena lokeṣvasiddhatvādutprekṣālaṅkāraḥ |
``kartarikri yātādātmyasaṃbhāvanotprekṣā'' iti kecidālaṅkārikāḥ prāhuḥ |
atra puṇyakartṛṣu puṇyatādātmyotprekṣeyam |
tvadapekṣitena tvadiṣṭena |
``avekṣitena'' iti pāṭhe tvayā dṛṣṭenetyarthaḥ |
vidhinā śāstroktavidhānena prajā janāngopāyitāro rakṣitāraḥ |
prajārakṣaṇaśīlā ityarthaḥ |
``gupū rakṣaṇe'' ityasmāttācchīlikastṛnpratyayaḥ |
ata eva ``na loka'' ityādinā ṣaṣṭhīniṣedhātprajā iti karmaṇi dvitīyā |
pavitrāṇi smaraṇādinā lokapāvanāni caritānyanuṣṭhānāni yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
pra thame pūrve |
``prathamacarama-''ityādinā prathamaśabdasya sarvanāmasaṃjñāyāṃ jasaḥśībhāvātprathamābahuvacanāntamidam |
ye rājāno vaivasvatasya vivasvadapatyasya manoranvaye vaṃśe bhūtā jātāḥ abhavanniti śeṣaḥ |
teṣāṃ dhūrdharastadbāhyabhāravoḍhā teṣāṃ śreṣṭho vā vīraḥ kṣattriyapuṃgavaḥ |
kṣattriyaśreṣṭha ityarthaḥ |
``syuruttarapade vyāghrapuṃgavarṣabhakuñjarāḥ |
siṃhaśārdūlanāgādyāḥ puṃsi śreṣṭhārthagocarāḥ/'' ityamaraḥ |
dharitryā ayaṃ ślāghyaḥ patirdaśaratho guṇānāṃ rāmamāhātmyahetuguṇānāṃ nidhirakṣayasthānaviśeṣaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ ||17||

 ariṣṭa iti |
sa prasiddho maghavendraḥ |
``śvannukṣan-'' ityādinā maghavacchabdo nipātitaḥ |
jambhadamanaḥ sannapi jambhāsurahantā sannapi, tvāṣṭrasya devaśilpinaḥ putrasya praśamanavidhau hananakriyāyāmariṣṭaḥ sannapyaśubharahitaḥ sannapi |
``riṣṭaṃ kṣemā'śubhābhāve'' ityamaraḥ |
viśveṣāmīśo'pi trailokyādhipatiḥ sannapi, marutāṃ devānāṃ saptamarutāṃ vā |
``marutau pavanāmarau'' ityamaraḥ |
nikāyasya samūhasya |
``syānnikāyaḥ puñjarāśī'' ityamaraḥ |
patiḥ sannapi |
madhyamaṇinyāyenāyamapyubhayatrānveti |
senāṃ vinetāraṃ vyūhādikriyāsu senāśikṣaṇaśīlamasurānapahantāramasuravadhaśīlam |
satatamityubhayānvayi |
tṛnnantayogātsenāsuraśabdābhyāṃ dvitīyā |

``vṛñ varaṇe ityasmātkartari liṭ |
``svaritañitaḥ-'' ityādinātmanepadam |
sahāyatveneti śeṣaḥ ||18||

 sa iti |
īdṛśa indreṇa varaṇīyo daśaratho'nīdṛśaṃ rāmaṃ visadṛśaṃ kathaṃ prasūte janayati |

kathamatrāścaryaṃ nāmeti |

yata evaṃ tataḥ sarvamanena jitamiti nigamayati- marutvantamityādinā |
yo daśamukha iha bhuvane bhagavantaṃ māhātmyavantaṃ vīryātiśayavantaṃ vā |

trayāṇāṃ bhuvanānāṃ samāhārastribhuvanam |
pātrāditvātstrītvābhāvaḥ |

nirdhāraṇe saptamī |

yadityādi |

 kimaparāddham |
ko'parādhaḥ kṛta itya6ḥ |
nanūpakṛtam |
upakāra eva kṛta ityarthaḥ |
tadevāha- puṇyeti |
he vatsa rāma, caitanyasya samīcīnajñānasya mātrāmapi leśamapi |
``atha mātraṃ syādavadhāraṇakārtsnyayoḥ |
mātrā paricchade varṇamāne karṇādibhūṣaṇe/ |
saivālpaparimāṇe ca'' iti ratnamālā |
haratā nāśayataikenāsahāyena darpāmayena puṇyā dharmiṣṭhā |
``puṇyaṃ dharmavati triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
brāhmaṇajātirbrāhmaṇāḥ |
``jātiśchando nāma gotraṃ svavyāhatikarottaraḥsāmānyaṃ mālatī ca'' iti ratnamālā |
anvayasya bhṛguvaṃśasya guṇo'mānitvādyātmaguṇavargaḥ |
brāhmaṇavaṃśeṣu hi bhṛguvaṃśaḥ praśastaḥ |
ata eva pravarādhyāye ``bhṛgūṇāmevāgre vyākhyāsyāmaḥ'' ityupakrāntam |
ślāghyaṃ me caritraṃ mayānuṣṭheyaṃ vṛttajātyanvayānurūpaṃ cetyamūni jātiguṇacaritrāṇi |
asau cāsau cādaśceti sahoktau ``tyadādīni sarvairnityam'' ityekaśeṣe tyadāditaḥ śeṣe puṃnapuṃsakato liṅgavacanāni'' iti śāstreṇa paratvānnapuṃsakatvaśeṣaḥ |
hṛtāni nāśitāni |
abhavanniti śeṣaḥ |
hṛtā ca hṛtaśca hṛtaṃ ceti sahoktau ``napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyām'' iti napuṃsakaikaśeṣaḥ |
ekavadbhāvasya vaikalpikatvādbahuvacanam |
prasiddhasya hi rājayakṣmāderāmayasya vātapittaśleṣmātmakadhātutrayanāśakatvamiti darpāmayasyāpi jātyāditrayanāśakatvamuktam |
so'yaṃ tathāvidho'yamekaḥ sannapyasahāyaḥ sannapi bhūridoṣaiḥ krodhamātsaryādibhirheyasvabhāvaviśeṣaiḥ svaprayuktairgahano durāsadaḥ |
na kevalaṃ guṇahānikaraḥ, kiṃtu doṣāvahaścetyarthaḥ |
darpa eva śauryādyatiśayaprayuktāhaṃkāra evāmayo vyādhiḥ |
``rogavyādhigadāmayāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
brāhmaṇeṣu vatsalena prītimatā preyasā viśiṣyaiva mayi prītimatā tvayā kṣemāya maṅgalāya labdharakṣaṇāya |
``kṣemamastrī syānmaṅgale labdharakṣaṇe'' iti ratnamālā |
kṣemaśabdo'tramokṣarūpakṣemavācī |
tathā cāpastambaḥ- ``prāyaścittabuddheḥ kṣemaprāpaṇam'' iti |
śamito nāśitaḥ |
itaḥparaṃ jātiguṇacaritravṛddhirnirvighāteti bhāvaḥ |
bhūridoṣagahanaïtyanenarājayakṣmaṇo bahurogasamūhātmakatvavaddarpasya bahudoṣasamūhātmatvamiti sūcyate |
caitanyamātrāṃ harateti caitanyanāśakatvādapyasya vyādheśca tulyatvam |
uktaṃ ca- ``rājayakṣmeva rāgāṇāṃ samūhaḥ'' iti |
``svasthe citte buddhayaḥ saṃbhavanti'' iti ca |
brāhmaṇavatsalenetyatra ``guruvipratapasvidurgatānpratikurvīta bhiṣaksvabheṣajaiḥ'' ityetadanusaṃdheyam||22||

 kathaṃ nāparāddham |
aparādhaḥ kathaṃ na kṛta ityarthaḥ |
āyudhaparigrahaṃyāvadāyudhaparigrahaparyantam |

duryogaḥ |

ucita ityarthaḥ |

tadāha- asādhyamiti |
vaidyo bhiṣak |
``bhiṣagvaidyaścikitsake'' ityamaraḥ |
śarīriṇo dehino doṣaṃ vraṇarūpaṃ karmānyathā śastragrahaṇādanyenopāyenāsādhyamanivartya niścitya |
``sādhanamanugamagatyoḥ śephasi siddhau nivṛttidāpanayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
yathā śastrapāṇiḥ śastraṃ pāṇau yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
``praharaṇārthebhyaḥ pare niṣṭhāsaptamyau'' iti pāṇiśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
vraṇarūpadoṣanivartanārthamiti śeṣaḥ |
bhaviṣyati tathā rājā durdamanādhikṛto rājā śarīriṇaḥ śāstravaśyatvārhasya doṣamakṛtyakaraṇakṛtyākaraṇarūpam |
anyathā śastragrahaṇātiriktaguṇopadeśādyupāyenāsādhyamanivartyaṃ paricchidya niścitya śastrapāṇirduṣṭadamanārthaṃ gṛhītāyudho bhaviṣyati |
paricchedakriyāpekṣayā bhaviṣyattvam |
doṣasya prakārāntareṇānivartyatvaniścayottarakṣaṇaprabhṛtiśastramādatta ityarthaḥ |
``śastrakṣurāgnikarmāṇi prakaroti yathā pitā'' ityādikamatrānusaṃdheyam||23||

 ka iti |

śāntam |
śāmyatvityarthaḥ |
``āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacca'' iti niṣṭhā |
maitrāvaruṇakauśikau vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrau staḥ |
āvayorvidyātapobhirādhikyāttena tayorupagantavyatvaṃ nyāyyamiti bhāvaḥ/ |
idaṃ pūrvaṃ mayā kṣiptayordaśarathajanakayorvasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayorvā samīpagamanamidānīṃ mama paribhavakāle'śakyam |
lajjāvaśādduṣkaramityarthaḥ |
anatikramaṇīyo rāmanirdeśaḥ |
tathāpi rāmājñā duratikrametyarthaḥ |
``nideśaḥ śāsanaṃ ca saḥ |
śiṣṭiścājñā ca'' ityamaraḥ |
sa eṣa iti |
jaitraṃ jayaśīlaṃ yasya rāmasya śāsanamājñā jāmadagnye kṣattriyāntake paraśurāme'pi pratiṣṭhitaṃ saṃjātagauravaṃ kāryanirvāhasāmarthyayuktaṃ vā |
jātamiti śeṣaḥ |
``pratiṣṭhā tu samāptau gaurave'pi ca |
kāryanirvāhasāmarthye caturthe chandasi sthitau||'' iti ratnamālā |
saumyatvātsomavadāhlādakaratvādacaṇḍo'tīkṣṇaḥ sa eṣa rāmaścaṇḍaśāsanastīkṣṇājñaḥ |
ājñāyāstīkṣṇatvamativartināmātmahāniparyantaṃ durgatiprayojakatvam||24||

 atīti |
saujanyasya namaskārasya paryāyaḥ |
kramaprāptasyanatipāta ityarthaḥ |
``paryāyo'vasare krame'' ityamaraḥ |
``parāvanupātyaya iṇaḥ'' iti ghañ |
guruviṣaye praṇāmasya vihitatvādidānīṃtadanuṣṭhānāttasyānatipāta iti bhāvaḥ / |
pariṣvajanta āliṅganti / |
kauśikena sārdhamatrabhavato viśvāmitrasahitānyuṣmāñjamadagniputra iti viśeṣaṇena mayi duṣṭe'pi matpitā bhavadbhiravekṣaṇīya iti vyajyate |
vijñāpanaprakāramevāha- vṛddheti |
guravo bhavantaḥ kartāraḥ |
rāmeṇa dāntasya damitasya  |
śikṣitasyeti yāvat |
``vā dāntaśānta-'' ityādinā ṇici niṣṭhānto dāntaśabdo niṣpāditaḥ |
me mama vṛddhānāṃ bhavatāmatikrameṇātilaṅghanena saṃbhṛtasya saṃpāditasya mahataḥ pāpmano brahmahatyāsāmyānmahāpātakasya nirṇiktaye nirṇejanāya |
``ṇijir śauce'' ityasmādbhāve striyāṃ ktin |
śuddhaye prāyaścetanaṃ prāyaścittaṃ taponirṇayaṃ  pāpanāśakaṃ karma vā diśantūpadiśantvājñāpayantu vā |
yadyapi tapovācinaḥ pāpavācino nāśavācino vā prāyaśabdasyākārāntasya nirṇayaśodhanasaṃdhānānyatamavācini  cittiśabde cittaśabde vā pare ``prāyasya citticittayoḥ'' iti suḍāgamo vihitaḥ,tathāpi pāraskarāderākṛtigaṇatvāccitticittaśabdasamānārthakacetanaśabde'pi parataḥ prāyaśabdasya suḍāgamaḥ sādhuḥ |
evam ``prāyaścetayāṃcakre'' iti murāriprayogo'pi pāraskarāderākṛtigaṇatvādevasādhuḥ |
``prāyo nāma tapaḥ proktaṃ cittinirṇaya ucyate |
taponiścayasaṃyogaṃ prāyaścittaṃ vidurbudhāḥ/ |
prāyaḥ pāpaṃ vijānīyāccittaṃ tasya viśodhanam ||'' iti coktam |
``citī saṃjñāne'' caurādikaḥ |
anuṣṭheyānirṇayasyānarthaparyavasānaniyamādanuṣṭhānamarthasiddham |
cetayaterviśodhanasaṃdhānārthakatvaṃ tu dhātūnāmanekārthatvātprāyaśabdasya nāśavācitve tu naṣṭarūpabrāhmaṇyādeḥ saṃdhāyakaṃ karma prāyaścittādiśabdenocyata ityāhuḥ |
prāyaḥśabdasyāvyayatvādavyayānāmanekārthatvāttapaḥpāpārthakatvam |
tataśca prāyaścetanaṃ prāyaścetayāṃcakre ityanayoḥ sādhutvam |
evaṃ prāyaścittiśabdaśca sādhureva |
``prāyasya citticittayoḥ'' ityetattu prāyaścittyādeḥ suḍāgamarahitasya vāraṇārthamityapi draṣṭavyam |
he guro vasiṣṭha, bhavanta eva dharmasyālaukikaśreyaḥsādhanasya vedaikasamadhigamyasya vārthasya prāgdraṣṭāra āsanprathamadarśino'bhavan, manvādayaḥ paraṃ manvādayastu pravacanairbhavatkṛtopadeśairjñānairdharmapramitiṃ labdhvā dharmamanekadhā prāṇayangranthaiḥ pratipāditavantaḥ |
yadvā manvādayo bhavadbhyo dharmapramitiṃ labdhvānekadhā pravacanairupadeśarūpairgranthairdharmaṃ prāṇayannabodhayan |
prapūrvo nayatiḥ pratipādane vartate |
hi yasmātprāṇayaṃstasmādbhavanta ādiśantviti saṃbandhaḥ ||25||

 vatseti |
adyānutāpena prāyaścittānveṣaṇakāle |
durvinīta iti |
he vatsa jāmadagnya, tvayi durvinīte sati sajjanocitācārāticaraṇena samuddhate sati vayaṃ kulavṛddhāḥ duḥkhitāḥ saṃjātaduḥkhāḥ |
abhūmeti śeṣaḥ |
anyathā tvayi vinīte sati sukhinaḥ saṃjātasukhāḥ |
bhavāmeti śeṣaḥ |
eṣa svavaṃśyeṣu durvinīteṣu duḥkhitatvaṃ vinīteṣu sukhitvaṃ cetyayam |
vidheyaprādhānyātpuṃstvamekatvam |
vṛddhānāṃ jñānavayaḥśīlaiḥ prakṛṣṭānāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ nisargaḥ svabhāvaḥ |
hiśabdaḥ prasiddhau |
yacchreyastattathaiva tvaduktarītyā sānutāpasya tava prāyaścittaṃ sādhyamevāsti ||26||

 vatseti |
he vatsa jāmadagnya , te pāpmānaṃ śastragrahaṇādiprayuktaṃ tvatpāpaṃ rāmabhadreṇāpahataṃ vināśitaṃ vidmo jānīmaḥ |
yato yasmāddharmācāryā dharmopadeṣṭāraḥ |
``mantravyākhyākṛdācāryaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
prāyaścitta iva kṛcchracāndrāyaṇādipāpanāśakakarmaṇīva rājadaṇḍo'pi rājñāṃ śikṣaṇe'pyenasaḥ pāpasya niṣkrayaṃ śuddhimāmananti bahuśo vadanti |
``pāghrā-''ityādinā manādeśaḥ |
``rājabhirdhṛtadaṇḍāstu kṛtvā pāpāni mānavāḥ |
nirmalāḥ svargamāyānti santaḥ sukṛtino yathā||'' iti manuvacanam, ``daṇḍye yaḥ pātayeddaṇḍaṃ daṇḍyo yaścāpi daṇḍyate |
kāryakāraṇasiddhāthag tāvubhau nāvasīdataḥ||'' iti śrīrāmāyaṇavacanaṃ ca draṣṭavyam |
kiṃ punariti |
atrabhavān pūjyaḥ |
atrabhavattatrabhavacchabdau pūjārthāviti vaiyākaraṇāḥ |
vasiṣṭhaḥ prajāpālanasannidhāvityatra ``ṛgvedavidyajurvedavicca sāmavideva vā |
tryavarā pariṣajjñeyā dharmasaṃśayanirṇaye||'' iti vacanamanusaṃdheyam |
atrabhavānityatra ``prabrūte yaṃ sa dharmaḥ syādeko vādhyātmavittamaḥ'' ityetadanusaṃdheyam/ |
etānīti |
etāni paripūta evetyādīni praśāstītyantāni |
bhagavatām |
``utpattiṃ ca vināśaṃ ca bhūtānāmāgatiṃ gatim |
vetti vidyāmavidyāṃ ca sa vācyo bhagavāniti ||'' ityuktabhagavacchabdapravṛttinimittavatāṃ sākṣātkṛtabrahmaṇāṃ pratyakṣīkṛtaparamātmanāṃ pratyakṣitaviprakīrṇavedānāṃ vā ṛṣīṇāṃ satyavacasāṃ vacanāni vākyāni prasannāni saṃśayacchedakāni gambhīrāṇyakṣobhyāṇi pāvanāni śravaṇamātreṇāpi pāpaharaṇāni ca |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
nimnonnatādivadviśeṣaṇasamāsaḥ |
bhagavatāmityādiviśeṣaṇadvayena yathārthadṛṣṭitvamuktam |
ṛṣīṇāmityanena yathārthavaktṛtvamuktam |
anenāptatvamuktaṃ bhavati prasannapadena saṃdehanirṇayārthaṃ mīmāṃsāpekṣaśrutivacanebhyo vyāvṛttirucyate |
gambhīrapadena viprakīrṇavedamūlatvoktyā vipralambhakasmṛtibhyo vyāvṛttirucyate |
pāvanapadena ca mandānāmapi tattvārthapramitiprabandhakapāpanivartakatvamucyate/ |
bhagavanniti |
nisargata iti |
bhagavajjāmadagnya, nisargataḥ svabhāvataḥ pavitrasya svayaṃ pūtasya sato'nyeṣāmapi pāvayitustavānyatpāvanaṃ pāvanāntaraṃ kim |
kimarthamityarthaḥ |
tīrthodakaṃ ca ṛṣijuṣṭodakaṃ ca |
ṛṣijuṣṭāmbhovācinastīrthaśabdasyodakaśabdasamabhivyāhārādṛṣijuṣṭatvarūpaviśeṣaṇamātraparatvam |
vahniśca pāvakaścānyataḥ śuddhiṃ svavyatiriktavastuhetukāṃ śuddhiṃ nārhataḥ |
na kṣametetyarthaḥ |
ataḥ prāyaścetanāmādiśantviti bhavaduktirna yukteti bhāvaḥ |
dṛṣṭāntālaṅkāraḥ |
``cedbimbapratibimbatvaṃ dṛṣṭāntastadalaṅkṛtiḥ'' iti lakṣaṇāt |
``tejīyasāṃ na doṣāya vahneḥ sarvabhujo yathā'' iti vacanābhiprāyeṇa vahneḥ kāṣṭhānirdeśaḥ||27||

 bhagavatīti |
randhradānena prāyaścittādinā parājitapāpasyāpyasaṃvyavahitakāryasya mama viviktadeśavāsārthaṃ svasminvivarapradānena vivarapradānārtham |
``adhyayanena vasati'' itivatprayojane tṛtīyā |
prasīda prasādaṃ kuru / |
bhagavannityādi |
yadi prasanno'si tvamasmāsu kṛtaprasādaścettadā visrabdhaṃ yathā tathā ime'smākamanatiriktā iti viśvāsayuktaṃ yathā tathā |
``samau visrambhaviśvāsau'' ityamaraḥ |
upaveśanādāsanānno gṛhānasmadgṛhān paripunīhi paritaḥ pāvaya |
etadityaṅgulyāsanaṃ nirdiśati- yaditi |
sūryaśiṣyasya yājñavalkyasyāntevāsine śiṣyāya rājanyaśrotriyāya kṣattriyāya chando'dhyetre |
``śrotriyādhyāpakadhūrtairjātiḥ'' iti samāsaḥ |
``rucyarthānāṃ prīyamāṇaḥ'' iti caturthī |
yadabhirucitaṃ yatprīyamāṇam |
tadastviti śeṣaḥ/ |
sarve upaviśanti |
upaveśaṃ nāṭayantītyarthaḥ |
janapadeti |
atra jāmadagnyetyādi |
he jāmadagnya, yūyam |
pūjāyāṃ bahuvacanam |
janapadebhyaḥ kosalādibhyo bahirbhūte vane nitarāṃ tiṣṭhantīti janapadabahirniṣṭhāḥ bhavatheti śeṣaḥ |
yūyamaraṇyaikaniṣṭhā bhavathetyarthaḥ |
vayamapyahamapi |
``asmado dvayośca'' ityekatve bahuvacanam |
gṛhasya paripālanādgṛhopalakṣitagṛhasthāśramasvīkārānnijaiḥ kāryai rājyaparipālanādibhiravaśyakartavyairvyāpārairvyagrā vyāsaktāḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ |
``vyagro vyāsakta ākule'' ityamaraḥ |
iti yat, tato hetoryaḥ samāgamo na babhūva na jātaḥ so'smābhirmanorathairutkaṇṭhāviśeṣairvāñchito viṣayīkṛtaḥ |
pākaṃ pacantītivannirdeśaḥ |
yadvā manāṃsyeva rathā manorathāstairaskhalitagamanasādhanatvena sādṛśyādrūpaṇam |
vāñchitaḥ prepsito bhavatāmasmābhiḥ saha samāgamaḥ |
asmābhirityapradhāne tṛtīyā svanaicyaṃ dyotayati |
asmābhirityubhayānvayi |
sucaritaparipākātsukarmaṇāṃ phalapradānaunmukhyāddhetoḥ cirasya prāpto'smābhiścireṇa labdhaḥ |
abhūditi śeṣaḥ||28||

 atra ceti |
asminsamāgame tvityarthaḥ |
kā ta iti |
stutipathātstutimārgādativṛttamatikrāntaṃ dhāma prabhāvo yasya sa tathoktastasya |
ativṛttiṃ pratyapādānatvavivakṣayā stutipathāditi pañcamī |
``ṛkpūrabdhūḥ'' ityādinā pathinśabdādakārapratyayaḥ |
``dhāmajanmaprabhāsthānaprabhāvasukhasadmasu'' iti ratnamālā |
te kā stutiḥ |
bhavediti śeṣaḥ |
vācāmagocaraguṇātiśaye tvayi guṇābhidhānātmikā stutirnocitetyarthaḥ |
``pratyakṣe guravaḥ stutyāḥ'' ityetattu śakyastutikaguruviṣayamiti bhāvaḥ |
avikalakṣitidāyinaḥ sadvīpāmityādipūrvoktarītyā saṃpūrṇabhūmipradasya te kiṃ dīyatām |
kiṃ vastu dānārhamityarthaḥ |
``puṣkalena dhanena vā'' ityetattvanīdṛśaviṣayamiti bhāvaḥ |
śāntasya vaiṣayikasukhebhyo niyamitamanaskasya muneḥ paramātmani mananaśīlasya tava parijanena kim |
parijanasādhyā śuśrūṣāpyanapekṣitetyarthaḥ |
``śaśrūṣayā vā parayā'' ityetadapyanyaviṣayamiti bhāvaḥ |
vasiṣṭhanābhitvājjāmadagnyasya gurutvābhisaṃdhineyamuktiḥ |
tathāpi stutidānaparijanānapekṣitatve'pi daśarathaḥ putraiḥ samaṃ rāmādibhiḥ sahitaḥ sannadya te vaśaṃvadaḥ |
tvadadhīna ityarthaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``priyavaśe vadaḥ khac'' iti khacpratyayaḥ |
jāmadagnyasya stutyādyanapekṣitatve'pi saputro daśaratho jāmadagnyādhīna iti taṭasthairanusaṃdhānājjāmadagnyaḥ kṛtātiśayo bhavati |
taduktaṃ nītividbhiḥ- manāgupakāritā ca sarvasyāsti tatparikaratvena jñāyamānatvāditi bhāvaḥ||29||

 yūyamīdṛśā iti kimāścaryam |
rāmādayastatvaṃ ca brāhmaṇādhīnā ityetannāpūrvamityarthaḥ |
preddhamiti |
munayastattvamananaśīlā yaṃ sūryaṃ preddhaṃ prakarṣeṇa dīptaṃ dhāma teja āmanantyadhīyate 
(
ṛ) |
agnīṣomatejomūlakatvarūpaḥ prakarṣaḥ praśabdena vivakṣitaḥ so'yaṃ jyotiṣāṃ nidhiḥ sarvatejasāmakṣayasthānabhūto devaḥ |
svāmī sūryo vaḥ kulasya yuṣmadvaṃśasya savitā janayitā |
``ṣu prasavaiśvaryayoḥ'' ityasmātkartari tṛc |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
iti yadato'smādanyadbhūtyai saṃpattaye ``bhūtirmānāṅgaśṛṅgārajanmasaṃpattibhasmasu'' iti ratnamālā |
praśaṃsāpadaṃ prakṛṣṭastutisthānaṃ kim |
na kimapītya6ḥ |
sūryo vaḥ kulasya janayitetyetadeva yuṣmākaṃ saṃpade paramapraśaṃsāsthānamityarthaḥ |
yeṣāṃ yuṣmākamaprameyamahimānavadhikamāhātmyo vasiṣṭho veda iva nityāpauruṣeyanirdoṣaśrutiriva dharme nityanaimittikādirūpeṣvalaukikaśreyaḥsādhaneṣu guruḥ saṃśayādinivartakaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
te tathāvidhā yajanaśīlā ikṣvākava ikṣvākudeśādhipatervaśyāḥ |
tadrājatvādapatyapratyayasya luk |
yadvā rājaviśeṣanāmadheyamikṣvākupadaṃ tadapatyeṣu lākṣaṇikamiti bahuvacanopapattiḥ |
yūyaṃ paramārtharājaṛṣayo vastubhūtarājarṣitvayuktāḥ bhavatheti |
anyatra tu rājarṣipadamaupacārikamityarthaḥ |
sūryo yuṣmākaṃ janmanā guruḥ, vasiṣṭhastu vidyaya[ā]gururityubhayavidhaśuddhiyuktānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ brāhmaṇavaśaṃvadatvaṃ nāpūrvamiti bhāvaḥ||30||

 saṃgrāmeṣviti |
diviṣadāṃ bharturindrasya saṃgrāmeṣu devāsurayuddheṣvabhayapradamasurabhayanivṛttiprāptipradaṃ dhanuḥ kārmukaṃ śāsanamājñā |
``nirdeśaḥ śāsanaṃ ca saḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
saptānāṃ dvīpānāṃ samāhāraḥ saptadvīpam |
pātrāditvātklībatvam |
tasminviśiṣṭāni sthitāni yūpāḥ paśuniyojanārthadārūṇi yajanāni ``same yajeta  |
prācīnapravaṇe vaiśvadevena yajeta'' ityādyuktayāgānuṣṭhānasthalāni teṣāṃ śreṇībhiḥ paṅktibhiraṅkitāścihnitā bhūmayastāstā dvīpabhūmayaḥ śaśvatkīrteḥ sadātanakīrteḥ |
``punaḥ sadārtayoḥ śaśvat'' ityarthaḥ |
nibandhanaṃ hetubhūtam |
nibandhanaśabdo nityanapuṃsakaliṅgaḥ |
kīrtirguṇavattāprathā |
yadvā audāryaguṇanimittā prasiddhiḥ |
athavā ``kṛtiryā ramayatyeva viśvaṃ sā kīrtirucyate |
kulamapyāgatā sā cedyaśa ityabhidhīyate/ |
yato viśvasya śamiti tasmādyaśa itīritam ||'' ityuktalakṣaṇalakṣitā vā kīrtirvivakṣitā |
bhagavatī paramapūjyā bhāgīrathī bhagīrathasaṃbandhinī gaṅgā sāgaraḥ sāgaraiḥ khātaḥ samudraśca |
prakhyātāni prasiddhāni tāni tāni nānāvidhānyāścaryakarmāṇi ca bhavatāmikṣvākuvaṃśyānāṃ yuṣmākaṃ bhūmānāṃ vaipulyam |
māhātmyamiti yāvat |
ācakṣate prakāśayanti |
atra māhātmyabodhane dhanurādīnāṃ khalekapotanyāyena kāraṇatvokteḥ samuccayaviśeṣo'laṅkāraḥ |
taduktam- ``khalekapotanyāyena bahūnāṃ kāryasādhane |
kāraṇānāṃ samudyogaḥ sa dvitīyaḥ samuccayaḥ||'' iti vaipulyavācino bahuśabdādbhāva imanicpratyaye bhūmaśabdo niṣpannaḥ||31||

 apavāryeti |
apavāryeti |
rāmajāmadagnyau pratyevetyarthaḥ |
etatkṣattriyāntakasya kṣattriyaguṇaślāghanaṃ vatsena rāmeṇa śikṣitam |
jāmadagnyasya vyutpāditamityarthaḥ/ |
araṇyagamanāya vanagamanārthamanumodasvānujñāpūrvakaṃ prīyasva/ |
bhavanto vasiṣṭhadaśarathajanakādayo'nujānantvanujñāṃ kurvantu |
anujñeyamarthamāha- raghujanaketi |
atra mayetyādiḥ |
mayā raghūṇāṃ janakānāṃ ca gṛheṣu bhavaneṣu |
``gṛhaṃ gṛhāśca bhūmni syāddvāvimau gehabhāryayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
garbhasyeva rūpaḥ svabhāvo yayostau tathoktau/tayoḥ |
sītārāmayorityarthaḥ |
``garbho'rbhake nāṭyasaṃdhau kukṣau panasakaṇṭake |
bhrūṇe'pavārake ca'' iti ratnamālā |
tatra rāmasya garbharūpatvamapahatapāpmano'pi svecchāmūlakam |
sītāyāstu tathāvidhānaurasatve'pi premātiśayeāvibhāgādgarbharūpatvam |
vyatikaro vivāharūpo'nyasaṃbandhaḥ sa eva maṅgalaṃ kalyāṇaṃ tasya vṛddhayo lakṣmaṇormilādīnāṃ vivāhā anubhūtāḥ sukhajanakasākṣātkāraviṣayīkṛtāḥ |
kiṃ ca bhṛgupateḥ paraśurāmasya vijayenonnataṃ vālirāvaṇayorapi jitaprāyatvenātyantavijayaśālinaṃ priyaṃ paramapremāspadaṃ vatsaṃ rāmamabhinandya sukhī sanniratiśayasukhayuktaḥ san |
bhūmani matvarthīya inipratyayaḥ |
gṛhānupeyāṃ prāpnuyām |
asminnarthe bhavanto'nujānantviti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
upeyāmiti prārthanāyāṃ liṅ |
puṣpitāgrā vṛttam |
taduktam-``ayuji nayugarephato yakāro yuji ca najau jaragāśca puṣpitagrā'' iti||32||

 vatseti |
te kauśikastvadekapriyaḥ kauśikaḥ prasthitaḥ |
gantumupacakrama ityarthaḥ/ |
ahamiti |
saumya somavatpriyadarśana, ahameva kevalo'ham |
vakṣyamāṇānuṣṭhānanityatvavirahito'hamiti yāvat |
tvāṃ moktuṃ notsahe na vyavasyāmi/``utsāhodhyavasāyaḥ syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
kiṃ tviti |
anuṣṭhānanityatvaṃ nityānuṣṭhānakatvamanuṣṭhānanaiyatyaṃ vā |
tacca pratyavāyaprayojakībhūtābhāvapratiyogitvamanuṣṭhānasya vivakṣitam |
svātanttryaṃ svairāvasthitimapakarṣati nāśayati |
kadācidaṅgīkṛtāmapi svairāvasthitiṃ prāyaścittānuṣṭhāpanena dviguṇāyāsajananena niṣphalatītyarthaḥ |
tadvivṛṇoti- saṃkaṭetyādi |
āhitāgnīnām |
``agnīnādadhīta'' ityuktādhānasaṃskṛtāgniyuktānāṃ puruṣāṇām |
idaṃ ca ``yāvajjīvamagnihotraṃ juhuyāt'', ``vasante jyotiṣā yajeta'' ityādyuktanityakratūnāmupalakṣaṇam |
gṛhasthaḥ gṛhasthāśramaḥ pratyavāyaiḥ svarāvasthitimūlakānanuṣṭhānaprayuktadoṣaiḥ saṃkaṭā niṣiddhā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
hi yasmādevaṃ tasmādanuṣṭhānityatvaṃ svātantryamapakarṣatīti saṃbandhaḥ||33||

 svagṛhāditi |
svagṛhatulyānmithilānagarāt svagṛhaṃ siddhāśramaṃ gantumāgantuṃ ca kāmacāraḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
gamanāgamanārthā kāmacārakriyā bhavatītyarthaḥ |
kāmenecchayā cāro vyāpāraḥ kāmacāraḥ/ |
bhagavanniti |
bhagavan, yadyanurudhyase yadi kāmayase |
``ano rudha kāme'' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ |
siddhāśramapadaṃ siddhāśramarūpaṃ sthānam |
tvāmiti |
ahamiti śeṣaḥ |
ahaṃ tvāṃ brahmarṣiśreṣṭhaṃ puraskṛtya gacchan san madhucchandaso māturmadhucchandā viśvāmitraputrastasya mātā viśvāmitrapatnī tasyāḥ satkārya ādaraṇīyo bhaviṣyāmi |
guṇavatputraprasūtvaprayuktagauravātiśayakhyāpanāya madhucchandaso māturityuktam |
pūrvaṃ bahuśo viprakṛtasyānantaraṃ mittrībhavataḥ svasyāpi brahmarṣitvapradasya paramapūjyasya vasiṣṭhasyātitherlābhāttasyāḥ prītyatiśayo bhavatīti bhāvaḥ/ |
kimiti |
etāvatyapyatyalpe'pi siddhāśramāgamananiyoge bhavān pūjyastasmāsu māṃ prati na prabhavati kim |
prabhurna bhavasi kimityarthaḥ/ |
rājānau daśarathajanakau ramaṇīyo hṛdyaḥ |
pāvano darśanaśravaṇādinā janānāṃ pāpahāro brahmarṣayorvasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayoḥ saṃgamaḥ samāgamaḥ |
anyonyeti |
anyonyamāhātmyavidoritaretaraprabhāvābhijñayoranyaiḥ svānyairaviditātmanordujñeyasvarūpayoḥ |
brahmarṣyoriti śeṣaḥ |
virodho'pi vidvaṣo'pi vibhrājate nāma paropakārahetutayā viśeṣeṇa dīpyata iti prasiddhameva |
paropakārahetutvaṃ ca triśaṅkuhariścandrādiviṣaye vyaktam |
snehe tu vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayoḥ parasparasauhārde tu viṣaye kā kathā |
vibhrājata iti kimu vaktavyamityarthaḥ |
viśvopakārahetutvāditi bhāvaḥ |
eṣeti |
rāmavadhūḥ sītā gurūn vasiṣṭhādīn vandate'bhivādayate |
ṛṣayo vasiṣṭhādayaḥ |
vīreṇeti |
he vatse jānaki, vīreṇa jagati mukhyena vīreṇa |
``mukhyasya nirviśeṣaśabdo'nyeṣāṃ viśeṣataḥ'' ityuktyā mukhyatvalābhaḥ |
maṅgalaṃ prayojanamasya māṅgalikaḥ |
vijaye sati māṅgaliko vijayamāṅgalikaḥ |
``saptamī'' iti yogavibhāgātsamāsaḥ |
ānuṣaṅgikeṇa paraśurāmavijayena saha tvatprāptirūpamaṅgalaṃ labdhavatetyarthaḥ |
te patyā |
``aprameyaṃ hi tattejo yasya sā janakātmajā'' ityuktarītyā tvadadhīnasarvotkarṣavatā rāmeṇa kartrā vṛtradruha indrasya mahāhaveṣu mahāyuddheṣu praśamiteṣu satsu nivartiteṣu satsu śacyapīndrāṇyapyūrjasvalāṃ prabalām |
``jyotsnātamisrā-'' ityādinorjasvalaśabdo nipātitaḥ |
kṣattraprakāṇḍagṛhiṇīnāṃ kṣattriyaśreṣṭhapatnīnāṃ yā bahumānapūjā gauravātiśayaprayuktapūjā tāṃ manasā vañcanayā vinā mānasapūrvakaṃ karotu |
yadvā kṣattraprakāṇḍagṛhiṇī iti pṛthakpadaṃ śacīviśeṣaṇam |
kṣattraprakāṇḍaṃ kṣattriyaśreṣṭha indraḥ |
``brahma bṛhaspatiḥ kṣattramindraḥ maruto vai devānāṃ viśaḥ'' iti śrutyendrasya kṣattratvamuktam |
tasya gṛhiṇī patnī śacyapyūrjasvalāṃ bahumānapūjāṃ karotviti pūrvavadarthaḥ||35||

 acirāditi |
rākṣaseṣu rāvaṇādiṣu samūlakāṣaṃ kaṣiteṣu mūlena saha nāśiteṣu satsvevaṃ syāt |
śacīkartṛkā bahumānapūjā syādityarthaḥ |
``nimūlasamūlayoḥ kaṣaḥ'' iti samūlaśabda upapade kaṣateḥ ``kaṣādiṣu yathāvidhyanuprayogaḥ'' iti ṇamul |
prakṛteḥ kaṣateranuprayogaḥ |
atra svagatatvokteḥ prakāśānuktiḥ prauḍhyeti vadanti/ |
svastīti |
bhagavanto vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrādevameva parasparasauhārdeneva svastyāsatāṃ yathā tathā tiṣṭhantu/ |
sthira iti |
sthiraḥ praśamo balavatāṃ karaṇenāprakampyā prakṛṣṭā śāntirantarindriyanagrahaste bhūyāt |
āśiṣi liṅ |
pratyagjyotiḥ svasmai svayaṃ prakāśamānamātmarūpaṃ jyotiste prakāśatām |
yogadṛṣṭyeti śeṣaḥ |
atra ``ātmajyotiḥ samrāṭ prāṇeṣu kṛtyaṃ tajjyotiḥ'' ityādikaṃ draṣṭavyam |
jyotiḥśabdena svayaṃprakāśakatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
tacca dharmabhūtajñāne'pyastīti pratyagityuktam |
pratyacatīti pratyak |
bhāvavācinā pratiśabdena prakāśaśeṣitvamuktaṃ tadeva svasmai bhāsamānatvaṃ naiyāyikābhimatajaḍātmatvavādavyudāsāya jyotirityuktam |
atra praśamoktirdamādīnāmupalakṣaṇārtham |
anena ``śāntodānta uparatastitikṣuḥ samāhito bhūtvātmanyevātmānaṃ paśyati'' iti śrutyartho darśitaḥ |
abhinno vinaṣṭaḥ śivaḥ maṅgalakaraḥ saṅkalpo mānasaṃ karma yasya tattathoktamantaḥkaraṇaṃ manaste'stu |
pūrvatrātra cāśiṣi loṭ||36||

 vatseti |
itastāvat |
āgantavyamiti śeṣaḥ/ |
ājñāpayeti |
kimitikartavyamiti śeṣaḥ/ |
yaditi |
kṣattravicchedāt kṣattradhvaṃsanādviśrāntenāpi viratenāpi mayā vyāpāraśūnyatāṃ dhatte ||37||

 paraśustvidhmādīnāmidhmasamitkāṣṭhānāṃ vraścanaṃ cchedanaṃ prayojanaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ sanmayyeva tiṣṭhatviti śeṣaḥ |
dhanuḥparaśvadhayormadhye paraśvadhasya saprayojanatvātsa mayi tiṣṭhatu |
dhanuṣastu mayi niṣprayojanatvāttvayi ca saprayojanatvāttavedamastvityāha- puṇyānāmityādinā |
daṇḍakāyāṃ vane puṇyānāṃ saritāṃ pāvanīnāṃ godāvaryādinadīnāṃ taṭeṣu tīreṣu bhūyāṃso bahutarā ye ṛṣayo nivasanti teṣu viṣaye |
laṅkāyāṃ sīdanti tiṣṭhantīti laṅkāsadaḥ |
``satsūdviṣa-'' ityādinā kvip |
vidhvaṃsāya vidhvaṃsaṃ kartum |
ṛṣīṇāṃ hāniṃ kartumityarthaḥ |
caranti saṃcaranti teṣāṃ rākṣasānāṃ vadhe tasya dhanuṣa upayoga upakārakatā bhavet |
sampratyamunā dhanuṣā saha eṣo'dhikāro rākṣasavadhe kartṛbhāvo vatsa eva sthitastvayyeva tiṣṭhati||38||

 iti dhanurarpayati |
ityuktvā dhanurdadātītyarthaḥ/ |
praṇamyeti |
iyamājñā paraśurāmeṇa kṛtā rākṣasavadhājñā gṛhītā |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
eṣa saṃcaritārthaprāptirūpaḥ krama uktaḥ/ |
āyuṣmanniti |
āyuḥśabdādbhūmni matuppratyayaḥ |
pratinivartasva |
alaṃ māmanugamya nivṛtto bhavetyarthaḥ |
ityuktvā niṣkrānta ityarthaḥ/ |
api nāmeti |
anyena bhārgavānugamanādanyenāpi pratiṣṭheyaṃ kim |
prasthitaḥ syāmityarthaḥ |
atra vitarkapratipādakavākyātmakaṃ rūpaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
kathaṃ ceti |
priyo rāmo yasya sa rāmapriyaḥ |
``vā priyasya'' iti priyaśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
gurujanāddhetorevaṃ daṇḍakāraṇyagamanaṃ kathaṃ syāt |
gurujano mama daṇḍakāraṇyagamanaheturna bhavati rāmapriyatvādityanumānamatra vivakṣitam |
ato'tra liṅgādyabhyūhanātmakamanumānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
nyasteti |
bhṛgupatau paraśurāme nyastāni tyaktāni śastrāṇyāyudhāni yena sa tathoktastathāvidhe sati |
yātudhānāvakāśahetutvena sādṛśye tathāśabdaḥ |
krūrairhiṃsakairyātudhānai rākṣasaiḥ kartṛbhiḥ |
tapa eva dhanaṃ prāṇebhyo'pi rakṣaṇīyaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktā ṛṣayaḥ tato bhaṅgabhiyā śaptumasamarthā ityarthaḥ |
utsāritā niṣkāśitāḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
kaṣṭaṃ duḥkhaṃ syāt |
``syātkaṣṭaṃ kṛcchramābhīlam'' ityamaraḥ||39||

 madhyamāyā iti |
he rāma, no'smākaṃ madhyamāyā mātuḥ |
kaikeyyā ityarthaḥ |
priyasakhī manthareti mantharānāmnī |
yāstīti śeṣaḥ |
seyaṃ mantharā tava didṛkṣayā tvaddarśanecchayāyodhyāyāḥ |
apādāne pañcamyantamidam |
prāptā |
āgatetyarthaḥ||40||

sādhviti |
asyāṃ pravṛttyāṃ mantharāhṛtāyāṃ vārtāyām |
``vārtā pravṛttirvṛāntaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
idamasmadapekṣitaṃ śiśvorāvayoḥ pravāsena deśāntarasthityā daurmanasyaṃ duḥkhitamanaskatvaṃ yadi vicchidyeta nāstītyupalabhyeta yadi sādhu samīcīnam |
tattasmātsamupasarpaya samīpaṃ prāpaya |
mantharāmiti śeṣaḥ/ |
susamāhitamiti |
kāryamiti śeṣaḥ |
nirvyākṣepamasmatkāryamityarthaḥ |
nirvarṇyālokya |
``nirvarṇanaṃ tu nidhyānaṃ darśanālokanekṣaṇam'' ityamaraḥ |
samagrasya saṃpūrṇasya saubhāgyasya saundaryasya lakṣmyāḥ samṛddheḥ parigrahaṇena svīkāreṇa hetunā locanānāṃ jagannayanānāṃ rasāyanaṃ rasāyanākhyauṣadhaviśeṣam |
``yajjarāvyādhividhvaṃsi bheṣajaṃ tadrasāyanam'' iti vāgbhaṭaḥ |
``draṣṭṝṇāṃ rāmasaundaryaṃ jarādoṣamapoha ti'' ityarthaḥ |
uktaṃ ca daśarathenāpi- ``nandāmi paśyannapi darśanena bhavāmi dṛṣṭvā ca punaryuveva'' iti |
anena śūrpaṇakhayā svakīyajarādoṣacodyaṃ pratyuktaṃ bhavati |
saumyaṃ somasaṃbandhi |
``somāṭṭyaṇ'' iti ṭyaṇpratyayaḥ |
``saumyaḥ pumānbudhe vipre triṣu syātsomadaivate |
ramye'nugre ca'' iti ratnamālā |
śarīranirmāṇaṃ śarīrasaṃsṛṣṭiḥ |
yadityādi |
yadrāmasya śarīranirmāṇamidānīmatyantajarāyāmapi cirakālavaidhavyaduḥkhena bahukālānuvṛttabhartṛmaraṇaduḥkhena |
ādikāle rāvaṇa evāsyāḥ patiṃ jaghānetyuttaraśrīrāmāyaṇe spaṣṭam |
pramuṣitaṃ praṇāśitaṃ saṃsārasaukhyamātmānaṃ dehaparigrahaprayojanībhūtayoṣitpuruṣasaṃsargasukhaṃ yasya sa tathoktastathāvidhasyāpi janasya |
mametyarthaḥ |
cāritraṃ  pātivratyātmakaṃ vṛttam |
dvitīyāntamidam |
``bhūvādigaṝbhyo ṇitran'' iti prakramya ``carervṛtte'' ityanena caradhātorṇitranpratyayena cāritraśabdo niṣpannaḥ |
``cāritraṃ śīlam/'' iti vaiyākaraṇāḥ |
hṛdaye manasi samākṣipati cālayati |
``kṣipu nirasane'' / |
api kuśalam |
kuśalaṃ pṛcchyata ityarthaḥ |
``garhāsamuccayapraśnaśaṅkāsadbhāvanāsvapi'' ityamaraḥ / |
kuśalaṃ sukhaṃ cet |
kuśalaṃ manaḥpriyam, sukhamātmasukham |
dhanadhānyaratnavastrādinā kuśalam, annapānādibhirārogyeṇa ca sukhamiti bhedaḥ |
``kuśalaṃ kṣemamastriyām'', ``śarmaśātasukhāni ca'' ityamaraḥ |
sadā prasnutastanī prasnutau putrasnehena kṣīraprasravayuktau stanau yasyāḥ seti vigrahe bahuvrīhiḥ |
``svāṅgācca'' ityādinā ṅīṣ |
pariṣvajyāliṅgya |
pratijñātau dāsyāmīti saśapathamuktau varau prasādau |
guṇādayaḥ śabdāḥ puṃsi klībe ca syurityarthakena ``guṇe gā'' iti prākṛtavyākaraṇasūtreṇa varaśabdasya prākṛte klībatvam |
saṃskṛte puṃstvam |
yadvā varaśabdo'yamiṣṭavācī saṃskṛte'pi napuṃsakaliṅgo bhavati |
``devaprasāde varaṇe jāmātari varaḥ pumān |
klībe tu kuṅkume śreṣṭhe triṣviṣṭe tvavyayaṃ varam||'' iti ratnamālā |
tatreti |
varadvayajñāpana ityarthaḥ |
vijñaptihārako mahārājaṃ prati vijñāpanāprakāraḥ |
bhavetyanantaramitiśabdo'dhyāhartavyaḥ |
bhavetyājñāpayatīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
kāryajñāpako lekhaḥ kāryalekhaḥ |
ityuktvā dadātītyarthaḥ/ |
vācayati paṭhati |
caurādiko'yaṃ vaciḥ svārthaṇijantaḥ |
lekhārthamāha- astvityādinā |
ekena vareṇa purā tvaddattavaradvaye prathamena pradānena vatsabharataḥ putrabharataste'dhirājyasya tvadīyasya rājakarmabhūtaparipālanaviṣayasya sarvabhūmaṇḍalasya |
adhirājasya karmādhirājyamityarthe saṃjñāpūrvakavidheranityatvādadhiśabde vṛddhyabhāvaḥ |
yadvādhikaṃ rājyamadhirājyamiti vā vigrahaḥ |
athavā ādhirājyamityeva padacchedaḥ kāryaḥ |
bhoktāstu |
prārthanāyāṃ loṭ |
anyeneti vareṇetyanuṣaṅgaḥ |
anyena vareṇa hetunā kālaharaṇaṃ vihāya kālakṣepaṃ vihāya |
kṣipramiti yāvat |
rāmo daṇḍakāṃ vanaṃ yātu |
atrāpi prārthanāyāṃ loṭ |
asau rāmastasyāṃ daṇḍakāyām |
``buddherātmā mahānparaḥ |
mahataḥ paramavyaktam'' ityatreva viśeṣaṇasya tacchabdena parāmarśaḥ |
cīradharaḥ sanvalkaladharaḥ san |
``suśusicimīnāṃ dīrghaśca'' iti medinī |
caturdaśasamāścaturdaśavarṣāṇi |
``samāḥ strī vatsare sādhu sarvatulyeṣu tu triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
tiṣṭhatu |
vasatvityarthaḥ |
taṃ punā rāmaṃ punaḥ |
sītā ca lakṣmaṇaśca sītālakṣmaṇam |
samāhāre dvandvaḥ |
sītālakṣmaṇameva sītālakṣmaṇamātram |
mayūravyaṃsakāditvātsamāsaḥ |
sītālakṣmaṇamātrameva sītālakṣmaṇamātrakaḥ |
svārthikaḥ kanpratyayaḥ |
tasmātparijanāt |
``anyārāditararte-'' ityādinā pañcamī |
anyaḥ parijano nānuvrajennānugacchet |
atrāpītiśabdo'dhyāhartavyaḥ |
iti vācayatīti parveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||4||

 prasādotkarṣo'nugrahātiśayaḥ |
aho |
vismayanīya ityarthaḥ |
tatreti |
mano yatra daṇḍakāyāṃ paryutsukaṃ rākṣasakṛtamunijanapīḍānusaṃdhānena gantuṃ sotkaṇṭham |
sthitamiti śeṣaḥ |
tatraiva daṇḍakāyāmeva gamanādeśo gamanājñā |
kṛta iti śeṣaḥ |
`` āṅpūrvo diśirniyoktṛprayojanavacanaḥ'' iti nyāsakāraḥ |
kiṃ ceṣṭasya priyasya viraho na jātaḥ |
kathamityatrāha- sa ceti |
anujo vatsaḥ sa lakṣmaṇo'nugaścānugantā ca |
ādiṣṭa iti śeṣaḥ||42||

 diṣṭyeti |
āryeṇa rāmeṇānumodito'haṃ madhyamāmbayādiṣṭavanānugamane prītiviṣayīkṛto'ham/ |
ārye iti |
mātuḥ priyasakhītvādāryaśabdaprayogaḥ/ |
nama iti |
prākṛte caturthyabhāvātsaṃsārasyeti ṣaṣṭhī |
saṃsāraḥ prāṇināṃ prapañcastasya svavañcanāsāphalyahetutvādbhagavata iti pūjānirdeśaḥ |
īdṛśāḥ pitṛvākyaparipālanādirūpaśraddhātiśayayuktarāmalakṣmaṇādirūpāḥ kalpadrumāḥ |
vanagamanābhyupagamena svābhīṣṭasaṃpādanāt kalpavṛkṣatvoktirūpaṇam |
prarohanti jāyanta ityarthaḥ |
``ruha bījajanmani prādurbhāve ca '' iti dhātupāṭhaḥ/ |
mātula iti |
mātulaḥ kaikeyyā bhrātā |
lakṣmaṇaṃ prati bharatasya jyeṣṭhatvādāryatvam |
tātaṃ daśaratham/ |
diṣṭyā |
kaṣṭaṃ ca |
ānando duḥkhaṃ ca yugapadbhavata ityarthaḥ |
kuta ityatrāha- apariṣvajyeti |
bharatamapariṣvajya bharatamanāliṅgya gacchato me daṇḍakāvanaṃ gacchato mama dhṛtirnāsti |
sattā na bhaviṣyatītyarthaḥ |
``dhṛtirdhāraṇadhairyayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
asmākaṃ lakṣmaṇasya mama ca pravāsena deśāntaragamanena duḥkhārthaṃ duḥkhapīḍitamenaṃ bharataṃ draṣṭum |
na tūtsahe naiva vyavasyāmi |
pariṣvaṅge karmatayopāttasya darśane karmatayopādānenānvādeśādenādeśaḥ |
prayāṇakāle bharatapariṣvaṅgādānandaḥ, prayāṇahetukabharatadarśanādduḥkhaṃ ceti sukhaduḥkhayoyaggapadyamiti bhāvaḥ||43||

 ekāyanībhūya rāmābhiṣekātmakābhīṣṭarūpaikādhikaraṇasthitā bhūtvā |
``ayanaṃ nilaye mārge dakṣiṇodaggatau raveḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
sarvāḥ prakṛtayaḥ samastāḥ paurāṇāṃ śreṇayaḥ |
``rājyāṅgāni prakṛtayaḥ paurāṇāṃ śreṇayo'pi ca'' ityamaraḥ |
trayyā iti |
trayyā vedasya trātā rakṣako yo'yaṃ tava tanūjaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
tena tathāvidhena |
trayīrakṣakatvāderbharatādisādhāraṇyaṃ mattvāha- rāmabhadreṇeti |
bhadraścāsau rāmaśca rāmabhadraḥ |
``śubhamaṅgalayorbhadraṃ syādramyaśreṣṭhayostriṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
bahulagrahaṇādbhadraśabdasya paranipātaḥ |
yadvā bhadra iva rāmo rāmabhadraḥ |
``upamitaṃ vyāghrādibhiḥ-'' ityādinā samāsaḥ |
``bhadraścāpi gajāntare'' iti ratnamālā |
rājñā mukhyarājatvvatā sarve lokā rājavantaḥ śobhanā rājayuktāḥ pūrṇakāmāśca viṣayasiddhibhiḥ paripūrṇābhilāṣāśca |
vidheyaviśeṣaṇamidaṃ dvayam |
svāminaste prasādātparameśvarasya tavānugrahātsantu bhavantu/prārthanāyāṃ loṭ |
iti vijñāpayantīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ||44||

 priyamiti |
kalyāṇaḥ śubhakaraḥ |
kāmo'bhilāṣo yāsāṃ tāstathoktāstābhiḥ prajābhiḥ kartrībhiḥ |
svānukūlatvamānandatvam |
svasyānyeṣāṃ cāviśeṣeṇānukūlatvaṃ kalyāṇatvamityabhiyuktāḥ |
vayamahaṃ priyaṃ sarvapriyaṃ rāmābhiṣekaṃ coditāḥ |
anuśiṣṭā ityarthaḥ |
codayaterdvikarmatvātpriyamityasyāpi karmasamarpakatvam |
kiṃ tu tathātve'pi rāmapriyau priyarāmau, maitrāvaruṇakauśikau vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrāvihāsmindeśe na sta ityarthaḥ |
tayoḥ sannidhānameva vilambaheturiti bhāvaḥ||45||

 parokṣa iti |
vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayoḥ sannidhau |
akṣṇaḥ paramiti vigrahe ``pratipara-'' ityādinā samāsāntavidhānasāmarthyādavyayībhāvaḥ |
``parokṣe liṭ'' iti nipātanāt parasyāderukārādeśaśca |
sukṛtaṃ suṣṭhu kṛtaṃ karma rāmābhiṣekātmakaṃ karma tayorvasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrayoḥ prītimānandaṃ kariṣyatyutpādayiṣyati |
atastayorasannidhāne'pi na doṣa iti bhāvaḥ |
nanu paurāhityādikaṃ kaḥ kuryādityatrāha- mantrajña iti |
mantrajño'bhiṣekarūpānuṣṭheyārthaprakāśakavedabhāgajño'bhiṣekārthaguptavādajñaśca |
``vedabhede guptavāde mantraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
bhagavān vāmadeva āsta eva hi |
ataḥ paurohityādikaṃ sulabhamityarthaḥ||46||

 yadyevamiti/evaṃ yadi pūrvoktaprakāro yadi tattasmādayam |
``pravartyantāṃ maṅgalāni''iti pūrvopakrānta ityarthaḥ |
jāmadagnyavijayotsavaḥ paraśurāmavijayaprayukta utsavo'bhiṣekamahotsavena cintitameva tanmadhyopanipatitena rāmābhiṣekātmakamahotsavena kartrā prasajyatāmupajīvanīyāṅgo bhavet |
paraśurāmavijayotsavāṅgānyeva rāmābhiṣekāṅgāni bhavantviti bhāvaḥ |
taduktaṃ tārkikaiḥ-``anyārthānuṣṭhitāṅgajanyopakārasyānyenopajīvanaṃ prasaṅgaḥ'' iti |
ya iti |
asminmahotsave rāmābhiṣekarūpamahotsave yaḥ prāṇī yadarthī yena vastunā sāpekṣo bhavati |
``tṛtīyā tatkṛta-''ityādinā samāsaḥ/tadvastu tasmai dīyatām |
vidhyarthe loṭ |
idaṃ ca rāmābhiṣekasya vaiśeṣikāṅgam |
yadyapi paraśurāmavijayotsave'pi vastrānnābharaṇādikaṃ dīyate, tathāpi tatra dātumicchayā dānam, atra tvarthināmanurodhena dānamiti bhavatīdaṃ vaiśeṣikāṅgam |
ahamiti |
tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthakaḥ |
tiṣṭhantvanye, arthināmahameva prathamo'rthī bhavāmītyarthaḥ/ |
keneti |
kena |
vastuneti śeṣaḥ/ |
yo'sāviti |
asāvadūraviprakṛṣṭo yo varadvayarūpo nyāso bhūyo'pi svīkāryatvena nikṣipto'rthaḥ |
``pumānupanidhirnyāsaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
taṃ varadvayaṃ nyāsaṃ me madhyamā mātā kaikeyī yatheṣṭamiṣṭaviśiṣṭamanatikramya nāthate yācate |
``nāthṛnādhṛ yāñcopatāpaiśvaryāśīḥṣu'' bhavatā sāmānyena dattaṃ varadvayaṃ svābhīṣṭaviśeṣe vyavasthāpya yācata iti bhāvaḥ |
he tāta, vayamahaṃ lakṣmaṇādayaśca tasyāḥ prasādena bhavato'nugraheṇārthinaḥ sāpekṣāḥ |
bhavāma iti śeṣaḥ||47||

 satyasaṃdhā iti |
raghavo raghuvaṃśajāḥ satyasaṃdhā yathārthapratijñāḥ |
``saṃdhāvadhau pratijñāyām'' iti ratnamālā |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
hiḥ prasiddhau |
uktaṃ ca purāṇaratne-``na hyarthinaḥ kāryavaśābhyupetāḥ kakutsthavaṃśe vimukhāḥ prayānti |
kiṃtvarthināmarthi'' tadā nadīkṣākṛtavrataṃ ślāghyamidaṃ kulaṃ te||'' iti |
kiṃ vicikitsasyasminnarthe kiṃ saṃśayavānasi |
``vicikitsā tu saṃśayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
he vatsa, tvayi tasyā dūte'pi mama prāṇabhūte tvayi tasyā kaikeyyāḥ sandeśahare'pi kaḥ prāṇānapi sarvāpekṣitān prāṇānapi dhanāyatyabhikāṅkṣati |
``aśanāyodanyadhanāyābubhukṣāpipāsāgardheṣu'' iti gardhaśabditākāṅkṣāyāṃ dhanāyeti nipātitāllaṭ |
matprāṇekṣiteṣvapi tatrābhikāṅkṣayā prāṇā api mayā dīyanta ityarthaḥ |
aprakaraṇe'pīṣṭajanātisaṃdhānātmakamadhibalamuktam||48||

 kathamiti |
anyadeva kimapi prakṛtarāmābhiṣekādanyadeva vanagamanaṃ manasāpi cintayitumanarham |
atrāpatitamiti śeṣaḥ |
hatāḥ smaḥ |
daiveneti śeṣaḥ |
hāśabdo viṣāde/ |
ikṣvākviti |
ikṣvākuvaṃśatilakasyekṣvākuvaṃśaśreṣṭhasya |
mukhālaṅkṛtivācinastilakaśabdasya gauṇyā śreṣṭhaparatvam |
nṛpasya daśarathasya patnī tatkartṛkakarmaphalabhoktrī ``patyurno yajñasaṃyoge'' iti ṅīp, nakāraśca |
viśuddhimati tasminrājakule kekayakule prasūtā janitā kaikeyīti śeṣaḥ |
āryā satī sādhvī satī |
``gauryāmāryā triṣu tveṣa kulīnanyāyasādhviṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
kimapi manasā cintayitumanarhamatyāhitaṃ lokabhayaṅkaram |
``atyāhitaṃ mahābhītiḥ karma jīvānapekṣi ca'' ityamaraḥ |
rākṣasakarma rakṣasāmasādhāraṇaṃ karma |
rakṣaśśabdāt ``tasyedam'' ityaṇ |
kathaṃ kuryāt |
kartuṃ nārhetyarthaḥ |
idaṃ no'smākaṃ mahadadbhutaṃ mahāścaryam |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ||49||

 satyeti |
tātapādā ityādi |
yūyaṃ satyapratijñā bhavatha cet |
sthetyasterlaṭi madhyamapuruṣabahuvacanam |
``satyasandhā hi rāghavaḥ'' ityanupadamevoktaṃ na vismaryate cedityarthaḥ |
nanu tvaṃ prāṇebhyo'pi priyatamaḥ |
tvadviyoge mama ca prāṇātyayo bhavet |
ataḥ prāṇātyaye'nṛtavadanaṃ na doṣa iti śāstramaryādetyatrāha- rāma iti |
rāmo yuṣmākaṃ priyo yadi |
iṣṭaścedityarthaḥ |
``tvayi dūte'pi'' ityādikamapyanupadamevoktaṃ na vismaryate cedityarthaḥ |
yuṣmābhirmama vanagamanānabhyanujñāne rāma eva yuṣmākaṃ durlabho bhavediti hṛdayam |
vāśabdadvayamapi vikalpe |
tattadā me prasīdata mama prasādaṃ kuruta |
kimarthamityatrāha- māteti |
ityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
me madhyamā mātā pūrṇakāmā viṣayasiddhyā paripūrṇamanorathāstu |
prārthanāyāṃ loṭ |
meśabdasya prasīdateti pūrvavākyāvayavattvānna vākyāditvam||50||

 evamastviti |
astvekenetyādinoktaprakāro'stvityarthaḥ |
kā gatiḥ |
etena vinā nānyo rāmalābhopāyo'stītyarthaḥ |
evaṃ hi sati kālāntare vāsmākaṃ rāmalābhaḥ saṃbhāvyate |
anyathā tu na tatheti hṛdayam/ |
putreti |
putrasaṃkrāntā āśrayaśaithilyātsvayamevāgatā |
na tu tairmanaḥpūrvakaṃ nihitetyarthaḥ |
tathāvidhā lakṣmīryeṣāṃ te tathoktāstairityarthaḥ |
uraḥprabhṛtitvātkap |
``lakṣmīḥ saṃpadramā śobhā'' iti ratnamālā |
vṛddhekṣvākubhiryadāraṇyakavrataṃ dhṛtam |
abhūditi śeṣaḥ |
pūrveṣāṃ hi putravattvamupabhuktarājyakatvaṃ ca yāvaccharīraśaithilyaṃ vārdhakaṃ cetyeteṣāṃ samuditatva evāraṇyakavratadhāraṇaṃ jātam |
tava tu teṣu kasyacidapyabhāve'pyāraṇyakavratadhāraṇaṃ jātamiti tebhyastava mahadvailakṣaṇyaṃ jātam |
vratamityasamañjasavṛttāntavyāvṛttiḥ||51||

 vatsa iti |
guruniyogata eva śvaśurājñayaiva / |
hā vatse iti |
``asmākaṃ tu janakasutā duhitaiva'' ityuktarītyā duhitṛtvābhisaṃdhinā vatse iti saṃbuddhiḥ |
kaṅkaṇadharaiva vaivāhikakaṅkaṇayuktaiva |
``nirvṛtte tvatha kalyāṇe'' ityādismṛtyā kalyāṇanirvartanānantaraṃ hi kaṅkaṇamocanaṃ vihitam |
kaṅkaṇasattve tu kalyāṇanirvartanānavagamātkalyāṇamadhya eva rakṣasāmupahārīkṛtāsīti duḥkhātiśayo hāśabdena dyotyate |
yadyapi dvitīyāṅke ``devyaḥ kaṅkaṇamocanāya militāḥ'' ityuktaṃ tathāpi kalyāṇakālātiśaye tātparyam |
``upāyanamupagrāhyamupahārastathopadā''/ |
atyāpanno'tiśayenāpadaṃ prāptaḥ |
etadāpannatvaṃ kathaṃ nāma kena prakāreṇa pariṇaṃsyata iti saṃbhāvyate |
``nāmaprākāśyasaṃbhāvyakrodhopagamakutsane'' ityamaraḥ |
kṣiprameva gurujanasya prāṇātyayānmama vanagamanavighnaḥ saṃbhāvyata iti bhāvaḥ/ |
īdṛśa iti |
āpāte tadātve |
``bhavettadātve'pyāpātaḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
bhavitavyatānusaṃdhānādarvāgityarthaḥ |
karuṇasyeṣṭajanaviyogajanyaduḥkhasya snehasya taddhetubhūtapremṇaśca saṃvegaḥ saṃbhramaḥ |
atiśaya iti yāvat |
``samau saṃvegasaṃbhramau'' ityamaraḥ |
īdṛśo mūrcchādiheturityarthaḥ |
kimatra kriyate'tra mūrcchāyāṃ kiṃ kriyate |
kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepe |
vanagamanavilambo na kārya iti bhāvaḥ |
kuta ityāha- pratiṣiddhamiti |
ambayā kaikeyyā kālaharaṇaṃ vanagamane kālavilambaḥ pratiṣiddham |
niṣiddhamityarthaḥ |
``yātvanyena vihāya kālaharaṇaṃ rāmo vanaṃ daṇḍakām'' iti hi lekhe sthitamiti bhāvaḥ |
pitṛmūrcchāmātṛniyogayorbalābalavicāre mātṛniyogo balīyān |
``piturdaśaguṇaṃ mātā gauraveṇātiricyate'' iti śāstrāt |
ata evāsminprakaraṇe rāmāyaṇe rāmavacanam- ``na nūnaṃ mama kaikeyi kiṃcidāśaṃśase guṇam |
yadrājānamavocastvaṃ mameśvaratarā satī||'' iti |
rājā hīśvaramātram |
kaikeyī tvīśvaratareti tadabhiprāyaḥ |
tadidamabhisaṃdhāyāha-tadalamiti |
tattasmāt |
ambāniyogasya balīyastvādityarthaḥ |
snehakātaryeṇālam |
pitṛsnehaprayuktādhīratayāsādhyaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ/ |
sādhviti |
saṃmatau dviruktiḥ |
ācāre mātṛniyogānuṣṭhāne niṣṭhā tātparyaṃ yasya sa tathoktastasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
``niṣṭhā tu nāśe niṣpattāvante tātparyayācñayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
sādhu sādhu |
samyaguktaṃ tvayetyarthaḥ |
amanuṣyeti |
te cittasārastava kāryākāryaviṣayabalābalānusaṃdhānamūlako dustyajagurujanatyāgaheturmanobalamamanuṣyasadṛśo manuṣyavisadṛśaḥ |
lokottara iti yāvat |
``sāro bale sthirāṃśe ca '' ityamaraḥ/ |
mātula mātuleti kutsane dviruktiḥ |
etatkaikeyyabhimataṃ vaḥ kulasya yujñaṃ sadṛśamanurūpamiti kṣapoktiriyam |
nānurūpamityarthaḥ |
yuktaṃ sadṛśamiti padadvayamekasyānurūpaśabdasyārthe vartate |
taduktamabhiyuktaiḥ- ``yogyatve sati prakṛṣṭatvaṃ sādṛśyayuktamanurūpatvam'' iti/ |
udbhrānta uccalitaḥ |
``bhramu calane'' ityasmāt ``gatyarthākarmaka- ityādinā kartari ktaḥ |
saṃpramugdho'sima saṃmūrcchito'smi |
pūrvavatkapratyayaḥ |
vatseti bharataṃ prati saṃbuddhiḥ |
patiriti |
svasurityādi |
svasuranena varadvayābhisandhinā patiḥ svabhartā daśaratho mṛtyorvaktraṃ vrajati maraṇaṃ prāpsyati |
vartamānasāmīpye laṭ |
etatsutayugaṃ rāmalakṣmaṇātmakaputradvayaṃ kartṛ |
vanaṃ vrajatītyanuṣaṅgaḥ |
gamiṣyatītyarthaḥ |
``rāme vā bharate vāpi viśeṣaṃ nopalakṣaye'' iti kaikeyyāḥ pūrvābhiprāyāpekṣayā sutayugamityuktam |
kiṃca vadhūṭī yauvanaprārambhavatī |
taduktaṃ śābdikaiḥ ``vadhūṭīciraṇṭīśabdau yauvanavācinau'' iti |
``jīvabhartrī kulasthā yā vadhūṭī sā stryapāṃsulā'' iti trikāṇḍaśeṣaḥ |
varākī dayanīyā |
``jalpabhikṣa-'' ityādinā vṛṅaḥ ṣākanpratyayaḥ |
ṣittvānṅīṣi rūpam |
sītetyarthaḥ |
rakṣobhyo rākṣasebhyaḥ |
``caturthī tadartha-'' ityādinā baliśabdena caturthyāḥ samāsavidhānādbaliśabdayoge caturthī |
balirivopahāra iva |
``karopahārayoḥ puṃsi baliḥ prāṇyaṅgaje striyām'' ityamaraḥ |
praṇihitā saṃkalpitā |
prakṣiptā bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``sītālakṣmaṇamātrakāt'' iti rāmānuvrajanapratyaṣedhe sīyāstayā paryudāsāditi bhāvaḥ |
kiṃca loko jano nirālambo nirāśrayaḥ |
kṛta iti śeṣaḥ |
``rājyaṃ cāhaṃ ca rāmasya'' ityukta bharatasvabhāvasamīkṣayā nirālamba ityuktam |
kiṃca naḥ kulamayaśasā kulakramāgatakīrtiviparītaprathayā |
``kulakramāgatā sā cedyaśa ityabhidhīyate'' ityukteḥ |
``tatsādṛśyamabhāvaśca tadanyatvaṃ tadalpatā |
aprāśastyaṃ virodhaśca nañarthāḥ ṣaṭ prakīrtitāḥ||'' ityuktatvānnaño viparītārthakatvam |
parivṛtam |
vyāptamabhūditi śeṣaḥ |
``aśvapatirvai bhagavanto yaṃ kekayaḥ saṃprati mamātmānaṃ vaiśvānaramadhye'' iti vedāntaprasiddhasyāsmakulasyāyaśo jātamiti bhāvaḥ |
yata evaṃ tato me svasa ḥ sahajāyā daurātmyaṃ durbuddhiravikalaṃ jagatsakalamapi lokaṃ viklabayati vihvalayati |
``viklavo vihvalaḥ syāttu'' ityamaraḥ |
svadoṣakhyāpanāya te māturityanuktvā me svasurityuktam||52||

 diṣṭyeti |
āryeṇa śvaśureṇa |
``āryayā'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
śvaśrvā kaikeyyetyarthaḥ/ |
iyamāryā jyeṣṭhabhrātuḥ patnītvādāryāśabdaḥ/ |
sītālakṣmaṇābhyāṃ saha sahitaḥ san |
eṣa iti |
asmiñchoke'smadviyogaduḥkhe eṣa tāto daśarathaśca tātaśca janakaśca priyāṇīṣṭānyapatyānyahaṃ lakṣmaṇaḥ sītā ca yāsāṃ tāstathoktāḥ |
sītāyā apyapatyatvaṃ pūrvoktadiśā draṣṭavyam |
mātaraḥ kausalyādayaḥ kaikeyyā apyanuśayaduḥkhamavarjanīyamiti mātara iti bahuvacanam |
avaśiṣṭāḥ paraḥśatadaśarathapatnyo vā vivakṣitāḥ |
bhavataivāśvāsanīyāḥ |
laghūkṛtaśokāḥ kartavyā ityarthaḥ |
nanu kimarthamāśvāsanaṃ tvayā tu na prayātavyamityatrāha- gatā vayamiti |
na nivartāmaha ityarthaḥ |
yadvā nanu bharataśatraghnāvāśvāsayatāmityatrāha- gatā vayamiti |
vayaṃ putrāścatvāro'pi |
atra bharataśatrughnayorakāryakṣamatvādgataprāyatvam ||53||

 vo āyuṣmān / |
kimidānīṃ karomīti kimanugacchāmyutānyadeva kimapi karomītyasminsaṃdehe nirṇayaṃ brūhītyarthaḥ/ |
rāmabhadreti |
pādaparicārakamaraṇyānugataṃ tvatpādaṃ paricaritumaraṇye tvāmanagitamityarthaḥ |
``tumunṇvulau kriyāyāṃ kriyārthāyām'' iti ṇvul |
avekṣasva |
manāgapāṅgavīkṣaṇenānumanyasvetyarthaḥ/ |
nanviti |
nanvityāmantraṇe'vadhāraṇe vā |
``praśnāvadhāraṇānujñānunayāmantraṇe nanu'' ityamaraḥ |
asya bharatasya varṇānāṃ brāhmaṇādīnāmāśramāṇāṃ brahmacaryādīnāṃ ca rakṣaṇe guruniyogaḥ piturādeśaḥ |
yathā mama vanagamane guruniyogastathā bharatasya varṇāśramarakṣaṇe guruniyoga iti samuccayasyārthaḥ/ |
lakṣmaṇasyeti |
tadvarṇāśramarakṣaṇam/ |
kimatreti |
atra pitṛniyukte'rthe kasyacitsvaruciḥ kim |
svecchā kā vyavasthāpiketyarthaḥ |
kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepe/ |
etāvatyeveti |
etāvati tvadanugamanamātre |
anyatra tu mama svarucirna prayojiketi bhāvaḥ |
śakyamiti |
mayi tiṣṭhati saprāṇe sati tvayānyena lakṣmaṇādinā pitṛniyuktaṃ samājñaptaṃ vastu pitṛniyogo vā |
bhāve ktaḥ |
pitṛniyuktamiti dvitīyāntaṃ prathamāntaṃ vā |
ādye  pitṛniyuktakarmakamullaṅghanaṃ śakyamityarthaḥ |
dvitīye pitṛniyuktaṃ vastu tanniyogo vā |
ullaṅghanagocaraśaktiviṣaya ityarthaḥ |
śakyamityatra kākvā nañartho'ntargataḥ |
tathā ca tavāpi madanugamanaṃ duḥśakam, lakṣmaṇāderapi tvadrucyā varṇāśramarakṣaṇaṃ duḥśakam |
pitṛniyogollaṅghiṣu triṣu mama daṇḍadharatvādityuktaṃ bhavati/ |
heti |
parityakto'smi |
anugamanapratiṣedhāditi bhāvaḥ |
mandabhāgyaḥ svalpapuṇyaphalaḥ kaikeyyā rājyagarddhikālambanatvādahaṃ svalpabhāgya ityarthaḥ/ |
vatseti |
evaṃ tāvaditi |
ayaṃ ca sāmānyato vācyanirdeśaḥ |
karṇe kathayitvā karṇe kathanamathinīya |
paścādasya prakāśo bhaviṣyati |
rāmabhadreti |
ayaṃ bharata evaṃ vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa |
bhagavatā ``utpattiṃ pralayaṃ caiva'' ityādyuktajñānātiśayayuktena śarabhaṅgeṇa śarabhaṅganāmnā maharṣiṇā preṣitaṃ tapanīyopānahorhemamayapādukayoryugam |
``tapanīyaṃ śātakumbham'' ityamaraḥ |
``pādukā |
pādūrupānatstrī'' iti cāmaraḥ |
tadupānadyugamāryo mama jyeṣṭhabhrātā rāmaḥ prasādīkarotu prasādena dattaṃ karotviti prārthanāyāṃ loṭ |
iti vijñāpayatīti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ/ |
taditi |
tadunmucya pādukāyugaṃ pādābhyāṃ viyojya |
gṛhāṇa svīkuru/ |
hā ārye iti |
pādukāpradānamātreṇa kathaṃ māṃ visarjayasīti duḥkhyāmītyarthaḥ/ |
vatseti |
matpādaspṛṣṭikayā |
spṛṣṭiṃ karotīti dhātvarthoddeśe ṇvul |
spṛṣṭikā sparśaḥ |
matpādayoḥ spṛṣṭikā matpādaspṛṣṭikā tayā nimittena |
yadi māmanugamanāya nirbadhnāsi tarhi mahyaṃ druhyasīti matkṛtena matpādasparśena nimittena pratinivartasvetyarthaḥ |
yadvā spṛṣṭau matpādau yasyāṃ śapathakriyāyāṃ sā matpādaspṛṣṭikā |
āhitāgnyāderākṛtigaṇatvānniṣṭhāntasya paranipātaḥ |
saṃjñāyāṃ kan |
matpādaspṛṣṭikā |
pratyayasthāt-'' itītvam |
matpādaspṛṣṭikayā śapathakriyayā nimittenetyarthaḥ |
sapadyasminkṣaṇe |
``sadyaḥ sapadi tatkṣaṇe'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃbhāvaya saṃbhavantau kuru |
tayorasaṃbhavaṃ pariharetyarthaḥ |
cirātpramūḍhau mūrcchitau/ |
nandigrāma iti |
āryo rāmo yāvannivartate tāvatparyantaṃ jaṭāṃ bibhratsannahamāryapāduke nandigrāme'bhiṣicya pṛthivīṃ pālayiṣyāmītyarthaḥ||54||

 bāṣpastambhamaśruṇaḥ stabdhatvakāraṇatāṃ nāṭayatyanukaroti/ |
nocchvasita ucchvāsaṃ na kurutaḥ |
śvasita ityatra ``rudādibhyaḥ sārvadhātuke'' iti laḍādeśasya tasa iḍāgamaḥ |
vījayati |
tathābhinayatītyarthaḥ/ |
hā heti |
muṣito'smyapahataprayojano'smi/ |
prāṇā iti |
mama prāṇāḥ prayānti gantumārabhante |
kiṃ ca tamasā'jñānenāvṛto'smi vyāpto'smi |
kiṃca marmacchidaḥ prāṇasthānabhedinyo'pūrvā navā rujo vyādhayaḥ |
``strī rugrujā copatāparogavyādhigadāmayāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
prasarantyavicchedenotpadyante |
yata evaṃ tataḥ putramukhenduṃ tava mukhacandramakṣṇorupadhehi mama netrasamīpaṃ prāpaya |
kiṃ ca giraṃ dehyahaṃ vanagamanānnivṛtto'smīti vācaṃ vada |
kiṃ ca tvaṃ sahasaivātarkitameva mayi tvadviyogaduḥkhite mayyakaruṇaḥ karuṇāśūnyo mābhūrna bhaveḥ |
``māṅi luṅ'' iti liṅarthe luṅ||55||

 sonmādam |
unmādaścittavibhramastamabhinīyetyarthaḥ |
kva viśāmi kutra praviśāmi |
mandabhāgadheyo'lpabhāgyaḥ |
``daivaṃ dittaṣṭaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ bhāgyaṃ strī niyatirvidhiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
 vihvalaḥ san/ |
ekībhūyeti |
he vatsa rāmabhadra, anekarasamapi bhinnarucyapi |
``raso rāge drave harṣe'' iti viśvaḥ |
ekībhūyaikarucirbhūtvā tvayaikābhilāṣaṃ bhūtvotsannam |
duḥkhitamiti yāvat |
ekakriyayaikavyāpāreṇonmukta uccārita ākrando rodanadhvaniryena tattathoktam |
kimidamityudbhrāntā niścayaśūnyā nārīnarāḥ strīpuruṣā yasmiṃstattathojñam |
nāryaśca narāśceti vigrahe itaretarayogadvandvaḥ |
``nṛnarayorvṛddhiśca'' iti śārṅgaravādigaṇasūtreṇa nṛśabdānṅīni vṛddhau ca satyāṃ nārīśabdo niṣpannaḥ |
``yatrordhvaṃ prakṛterviśeṣaḥ'' iti mahābhāṣyokteḥ |
ata eva ``naranārīmayo hariḥ'' iti prayogo'pi sādhuḥ |
etattvatpuraṃ tava puraṃ sarvamevātmaiśvaryaṃ jāmāturiti janakābhiprāyeṇa mithilānagaraṃ tvatpuramityuktam |
yadvā |
tvaditi bhinnaṃ padaṃ pacamyantam |
etatpuraṃ mithilānagaraṃ tvattvannimittaṃ sahasā śīghramanyathaiva saṃjātaṃ satprastutotsavaprayuktānandarūpaprakārātiriktaśokarūpaprakāraviśiṣṭaṃ jātaṃ sadāpadyata āpannaṃ bhavati |
``padḷ gatau'' upasargavaśāduktārthe vṛttiḥ |
yasminpure ghanaiḥ sāndrairbāṣpāmbubhirnetrajalaiḥ |
``bāṣpo netrajaloṣmaṇoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
viśiṣṭavācino'pi bāṣpaśabdasyāmbuśabdasamabhivyāhārādviśeṣaṇamātraparatvam |
kardamiteṣu saṃjātapaṅkeṣu |
``paṅko'strī śādakardamau'' ityamaraḥ |
vartmasu mārgeṣu yaddurdinam |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``meghacchanne'hni durdinam'' ityamaraḥ||56||

 vatseti |
anurudhyasva |
kāmayasvetyarthaḥ |
``āśaṃsāyāṃ bhūtavacca'' iti śamyaterniṣṭhā |
kuta ityatrāha- gurava iti |
anugantavyā mayānugamanārthā nānugantāraḥ |
māmanugantuṃ nārha ityarthaḥ |
hetvantaramapyāha- ātmanātṛtīyeneti |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
dvāvanyāvahaṃ ca tṛtīyaḥ |
tathāvidhena mayā gantavyamiti māturnirdeśa ityarthaḥ |
``sītālakṣmaṇamātrakātparijanādanyo na cānuvrajet'' ityukteriti bhāvaḥ |
``ātmanaśca'' ``pūraṇa iti vaktavyam'' iti sūtravārtikābhyāṃ tṛtīyāyā aluk/ |
kimahamiti |
kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepe |
nāhaṃ kevalo'nugacchāmītyarthaḥ |
``eke mukhyānyakevalāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
sabālavṛddhā bālavṛddhasahitāḥ |
``vopasarjanasya'' iti sahaśabdasya sabhāvaḥ |
prakṛtayaḥ paurajanāḥ |
skandheti |
he vatsa te te sāketā ayodhyāvāsinaḥ |
ime mahābrāhmaṇāḥ paramapūjyāḥ ṣaṭkarmacaritāśca viprāḥ |
``vipraśca brāhmaṇo'sau ṣaṭkarmā yāgādibhiryautaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vṛddhāḥ santo'pi |
``te dvijāstrividhaṃ vṛddhā jñānena vayasaujasā'' ityuktatrividhavṛddhatvayuktāḥ santo'pītyarthaḥ |
skandheṣu bhujaśiraḥsvāropitā yajñapātrāṇāṃ srakcamasādīnāṃ nicayāḥ samūhā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ santaḥ, anupatantībhiranudhāvantībhiḥ patnībhirbhāryābhirgṛhīto dhriyamāṇo'gnirgārhapatyādiryeṣāṃ te tathoktāśca santaḥ, prākpūrvameva prasthāpitā homadhenavo homārthadadhipayoghṛtāmikṣādihetubhūtā gāvo yeṣāṃ te tathoktāśca santaḥ |
dhenūnāṃ dhāvanāśaktyā pūrvameva prasthāpanaṃ maithilaiḥ saha mithilābhijanairbrāhmaṇaiḥ sārdham |
``so'syābhijanaḥ'' iti sāketamithilāśabdābhyāmaṇ |
sāketānāṃ sītākalyāṇāyāgamanāt ``sāketāḥ saha maithilaiḥ'' ityuktam |
vājapeyārjitairvājapeyayāgasaṃpāditaiḥ |
svairātmīyaiśchatraiḥ karaṇaiḥ |
vājapeyayājināṃ chatradhāraṇaṃ śāstrasiddham |
arkakiraṇāṃstava vārayituṃ tvayi yathā na patiṣyanti tathā kartuṃ dhāvanti śīghraṃ gacchanti |
``sṛ gatau'' ityatra ``pāghrā-'' ityādinā śīghragamane dhāvādeśaḥ |
``yāvajjīvaṃ na kaṃcana pratyavarohet'' ityetadapi tvadarthe parityajantīti bhāvaḥ |
tadidamuktaṃ śrīmati rāmāyaṇe- ``dvijaskandhādhirūḍhāstvamagnayo'pyanuyāntyamī |
vājapeyasamutthāni cchatrāṇyetāni paśya naḥ/ |
pṛṣṭhato'nuprayātāni meghānīva jalātyaye |
anāvṛtātapatrasya ravisaṃtāpitasya te/ |
ebhiśchāyāṃ kariṣyāmaḥ svaiśchatrairvājapeyakaiḥ |
yā hi naḥ satataṃ buddhirvedamantrānusāriṇī/ |
tvatkṛte sā kṛtā vatsa vanavāsānusāriṇī||'' iti||57||

 mātuleti |
gurubhiḥ pitṛmātulādibhiḥ |
``gururniṣekādikare purodhasi bṛhaspatau |
pitrādau nā bṛhatkhyātadurbharālaghuṣu triṣu||'' itiratnamālā |
dharmaloādguruvacanakartavyatvagurujanānugantavyatvarūpaśāstrārthabhaṅgāt |
``bhītrārthānāṃ-''iti pañcamī |
prasīda prasādaṃ kuru |
tatphalamāha- pratinivartyatāmiti |
mahājanaḥsāketamaithilamahābrāhmaṇapariṣat |
pratinivartyatām |
mattastvayeti śeṣaḥ |
iti praṇamati śaraṇaṃ gacchati |
śaraṇāgatalakṣaṇaṃ hi sarvasya durvāramiti hṛdayam/ |
uttiṣṭhottiṣṭhetyātag dviruktiḥ |
bodhayitveti |
pratinivṛttimiti śeṣaḥ |
kvāpi manasāpi cintayituṃ duḥśake sthāne |
tvāmiti mahābāho lakṣmaṇa, tvāmāmantraye |
tvāmapyanugamanamājñāpayāmi |
vaidehanandini sīradhvajaputri, tvāṃ cāmantraye |
pāpo'haṃ nivṛtto'smi nivṛttihetubhūtarāmapraṇāmakarmakatārūpaduritapūrṇo'hamanugamanānnivṛtto bhavāmi |
vāṃ yuvayoḥ kalyāṇamastu |
āśiṣi loṭ||58||

 rudanpratinivṛttya |
atra āheti śeṣaḥ |
aho nu khalu bhoriti vismaye nipātasamudāyaḥ |
pratimanvantaramiti |
lokānāṃ satyalokādīnāṃ pavitrā pariśuddhikarīyaṃ cāritrasya pitṛvacanaparipālanaprabhṛtisadvṛttasya |
``carervṛtte'' iti ṇitranpratyayaḥ |
pañcikā vistāraḥ, vyaktīkṛtirvā |
``paci vistāravacane'', ``paci vyaktīkaraṇe'' ityābhyāṃ dhātvarthanirderśe ṇvul |
``strībhāvādāvaniktiṇṇvul'' iti strītvam |
manvantare manvantare pratimanvantaram |
vīpsāyāmavyayībhāvaḥ |
``tṛtīyāsaptamyorbahulam'' iti bahulagrahaṇādvaikalpiko'mbhāvaḥ |
``manvantaraṃ tu divyānāṃ yugānāmekasaptatiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
bhūtairdevādiprāṇivargaiḥ |
``bhūtaṃ nyāyye same prāpte yathārthātītayorapi |
klībe sattve piśācādau bhūmyādau jantusattvayoḥ||'' iti ratnamālā |
dīyamānā satī śrīrāmāyaṇādinā kīrtyamānā satī cariṣyati vyāptā bhaviṣyati |
prāyaḥśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām |
``manye śaṅke dhruvaṃ prāyaḥ'' ityādyukteḥ |
saṃbhāvanā nāmotkaṭakoṭikaḥ saṃśayaḥ||59||

 kathamiti |
śṛṅgiberākhyanagaravāsakartretyarthaḥ |
``vasestavyatkartari ṇicca'' iti tavyatpratyayasya ṇittvādvṛddhiḥ |
niṣādānāṃ dhīvarāṇāṃ patyā guhena guhanāmnā tatpradeśaparyantānāṃ citrakūṭaparyantabhūbhāgānāmavaskandinaḥ pīḍayiturvirādharākṣasasya |
virādhaścāsau rākṣasaśceti karmadhārayātṣaṣṭhī |
sāmānyaśabdaṃ prati viśeṣaśabdasya viśeṣaṇatvāt ``viśeṣaṇaṃ viśeṣyeṇa-'' iti samāsaḥ |
ṣaṣṭhyāḥ samānādhikaraṇena samāsaniṣedhastu pariniṣṭhitavibhaktyā samāsaviṣayakaḥ |
durvilasitaṃ duśceṣṭitamāryasya kathitamiti lakṣmaṇena smāritam/ |
tatra rāmavacanam- tena hītyādi |
tena guhakathitena hetunā virādhahatakasya virādhākhyasya hataprāyasya |
``alpe'' iti kaḥ |
unmathanāya maraṇāya |
``ghātonmāthavadhā api'' ityamaraḥ |
sannikṛṣṭaḥ sannihitaḥ prayogo nāma kṣetraviśeṣo yasya sa tathoktastamanuṣaktayā samīpe saṃbaddhayā mandākinyā pavitrāḥ pariśuddhā mekhalāstaṭā yasya sa tathoktastam |
``mekhalā khaḍgabandhe syātkāñcīśailanitambayoḥ'' iti viśvaḥ |
citrakūṭācalamupetyetyasyottaratrānvayaḥ |
ṛṣibhiriti |
rakṣāṃsi hantumṛṣibhirmunibhirupajuṣṭamupasevitaṃ tīrthaṃ puṇyasalilaṃ yasyāḥ sā tathoktā tām |
ṛṣibhirupajuṣṭāmbhovācino'pi tīrthaśabdasya viśeṣyamātraparatvam |
daṇḍakāṃ prāpya sannihito gṛdhrarājo yasya tattathoktaṃ janasthānaṃ krameṇa pūrvoktasthāneṣu yaugapadyena yāyāṃ prāpnuyām/āśaṃsāyāṃ liṅ |
atrāpi pūrvāṅkāntapātreṇottarāṅkādau pravekṣyato gṛdhrarājarūpapātrasya sūcanādaṅkāsyamuktam||60||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vīrarāghavaḥ/
sa mahāvīracarite turīyāṅkaṃ vyavīvarat ||
ityaṇṇāppaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyākhyāyāṃ bhāvapradyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ caturtho'ṅkaḥ||

pañcamo'ṅkaḥ/

idānīṃ kaviḥ sugrīvavibhīṣaṇakathārūpapatākāṃ kharadūṣaṇādivadhaprayuktāpāyaśaṅkayā jaṭāyuvṛttāntarūpāpāyaśaṅkayā ca prāptyāśāṃ vadanpunarapi garbhasaṃdhimāha pañcamāṅkena- tatra prathamaṃ viṣkambhamāha- tataḥ praviśati saṃpātirityādinā |
saṃpātirjaṭāyoragrajaḥ |
nūnamiti saṃbhāvanāyām |
vatso jaṭāyuriti |
anujatvādvatsa ityuktiḥ |
malayakandara eva malayagiriguhaiva |
``darī tu kandaro vāstrī'' ityamaraḥ |
kulāyo nīḍaṃ tanmadīyam |
``kulāyo nīḍamastriyām'' ityamaraḥ |
upāsīdati sannikarṣeṇa prāpnoti |
paryāyāditi |
paryāyātkramāt |
``paryāyo'vasare krame'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃvartavistārayoḥ patatrāṇāṃ saṃkocavikāsayoḥ |
samityekīkāre'vyayam |
saṃvartanaṃ saṃvartaḥ |
saṃkoca iti phalitam |
kṣaṇaṃ dṛṣṭā naṣṭā adṛṣṭāśca kakubho diśo yābhistāstathoktāḥ |
``ṇaśa adarśane'', ``gatyarthākarmaka-'' ityādinā kartari ktaḥ |
``saṃkoce kṣaṇadṛṣṭatvaṃ vikāse kṣaṇanaṣṭatā |
patatrāṇāṃ gṛdhtapaterdiśāmiti vivekadhīḥ||'' kiṃca nīhārīkṛtebhyo himavattucchīkṛtebhyo meghebhyo mocitā vibhājitā dhutāḥ kampitāḥ ata eva vyaktaṃ sphuṭaṃ yathā bhavati tathā sphurantyaścalantyaḥ prakāśamānā vā vidyutastaḍito yābhistāstathoktāḥ |
pakṣavātyayā megheṣu khilīkṛteṣu nirāśrayatvādvidyutāṃ vyaktasphuraṇam |
kiṃca ārāddūrata eva kīrṇādvikṣiptāt |
``kaṝ vikṣepe'' karmaṇi ktaḥ |
``radābhyāṃ -'' iti niṣṭhātakārasya natvam |
kaṇādapi leśādapi |
``lavaleśakaṇāṇavaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
kaṇīkṛtā leśīkṛtā gurūṇāṃ grāvoccayānāṃ parvatānāṃ śreṇayaḥ paṅktayo yābhistāstathoktāḥ |
``acalaśailaśiloccayāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
tulyārthatvādgrāvoccayaśabdo'pi parvatavācī |
``pāṣāṇaprastaragrāvā-'' iti grāvaśabdo'tra śilāvācī |
śyaineyasya śyenīputrasya |
jaṭāyorityarthaḥ |
``pattrī śyenaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``jāterastrī-'' iti ṅīṣ |
``strībhyo ḍhak'' iti ḍhak |
``śyenaḥ śyenāṃśca gṛdhrāṃśca vyajāyata sutejasaḥ |
tasmājjāto'hamaruṇātsaṃpātistu mamāgrajaḥ/ |
jaṭāyuriti māṃ viddhi śyenīputramariṃdama ||'' iti hi śrīrāmāyaṇe jaṭāyunoktam |
bṛhatāṃ patattrāṇāṃ pakṣāṇāṃ dhutayaḥ kampanāni |
``garutpakṣacchadāḥ patraṃ patatraṃ ca tanūruham'' ityamaraḥ |
``dhūñ kampane'' iti svādau paṭhitādbhāve striyāṃ ktin |
āgamaṃ jaṭāyorāgamanaṃ prakhyāpayantyanumāpayanti |
``prajñāpayanti'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
liṅgābhyūhanamanumānaṃ nāmātra garbhasaṃdhyaṅgamuktam ||1||

 dūreti |
``śyaineyasya bṛhatpatatradhutināṃ'' ityādirjaṭāyorbṛhatpatatradhutināṃ vegamarutā ullolānyatyantacalitāni bhinnāni sthānādapakrāntānyambhāṃsi yasya sa tathoktastasya, dūramadhikamudvellita uccalito vāḍavo vāḍavāgniryasya sa tathoktastasya/vāyusakhatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
jaladheḥ samudrasya randhrairjalāpāyavaśātprakaṭaiḥ śvabhrairāpatitena praviṣṭena jaṭāyorvegamarutā vegajanyavāyunā pātālamādhmāyate pūryate |
yatpātālaṃ kartṛ |
vaikuṇṭhavarāhasyādivarāhasya kaṇṭhakuharādgalarandhrāt |
``kuharaṃ vivaraṃ bilam'' ityamaraḥ |
sphāramadhikaṃ yathā ``sphāraṃ bhūyaśca bhūri ca '' ityamaraḥ |
dhvāna iva śabda ivoccaṇḍaṃ krūraṃ yathā bhavati tathākāṇḍakālarajanīparjanyavadanavasarāpatitapralayarātrimahāmeghavadgarjati śabdati |
uktayacchabdastacchabdaṃ nāpekṣate  |
``kāṇḍo'strīdaṇḍabāṇārvavargāvasaravāriṣu'' ityamaraḥ |
caṇḍimātirekavivakṣayopamādvayam||2||

 kāverīti |
kāveryā valayitā mekhalā nitambo yasya sa tathoktastasya |
``mekhalā khaḍgabandhe'pi kāñcīśailanitambayoḥ'' iti viśvaḥ |
malayagireretasminsānau taṭe |
``snuḥ prasthaḥ sānurastriyām'' ityamaraḥ/divontarikṣātpatānyavarohāmi |
yatra malayagirisānāvāryo jyeṣṭabhrātā kāśyapaḥ kaśyapaputro vyavahito'pyapatyamiti pakṣeṇa |
kaśyapaśabdādapatye ṛṣyaṇ |
śakuntaḥ pakṣī |
saṃpātirityarthaḥ |
viprayuktapakṣaśchinnapakṣaḥ |
idaṃ viśeṣaṇamubhayānvayi |
aparaḥ śailendra ivāvāsabhūtamalayādanyaḥ śaila iva nivasati |
parvatopari parvata iva tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ |
viprayuktapakṣatve kāraṇamuttaratra vakṣyati||3||

 visraṃsayantīti |
pakṣau parigṛhya prāpya visraṃsayantī śithilayantyutpatanaśrameṇārtirutpatanārthaprayatnagauravaprayuktapīḍā mamāpi jātotpannā |
apiśabdastadanarhatvaṃ dyotayati |
tathāhi |
vibhoḥ padārthānāṃ pariṇāmāpakṣayādihetuśaktiyuktasya |
yadvā ``dikkālavīśvarādatiricyete'' iti matena vibhorityuktam |
athavā ``kālaḥ kalayatāmaham'' ityuktyā kālaśarīrakasya bhagavata ityarthaḥ |
jarākhyā vayohāniprayuktaśaithilyātmikā |
śaktitatkāryayorabhedāropaḥ |
śaktiḥ sāmarthyaṃ śaktyantarāṇāmanyāsāṃ śaktīnāmutpatanādisāmarthyānāṃ pratibandhahetuḥ kāryajananavaighaṭyakarī bhavatīti śeṣaḥ||4||

 taditi |
manvantareṣu purāṇaścirantanaḥ |
``manvantaraṃ tu divyānāṃ yugānāmekasaptatiḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
gṛdhrarājaḥ saṃpātistasmādutpattyāgamanāddhetorayaṃ saṃnihitaḥ |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``idamaḥ pratyakṣagataṃ samīpataravarti caitado rūpam |
adasastu viprakṛṣṭaṃ taditi parokṣe vijānīyāt||'' ityuktatvādidaṃśabdaḥ saṃnihitavācī |
aho bhrātṛsneho bhrātari mayi prītirvismayanīyā |
kuta ityatrāha- pureti |
asau mamāgrajaḥ saṃpātiḥ purākalpe pūrvasminbrahmadine |
``kalpastu pralaye nyāye śāstre brahmadine vidhau'' iti ratnamālā |
dūrotpatanasya khuralī lakṣyabandhanaṃ sūryamaṇḍalasaṃnidhirūpalakṣyasvīkāraḥ |
``khuralī lakṣyabandhanam'' iti keśavaḥ |
saiva kelirlīlā tayā janitādutpāditādatiprasaṅgādatyantasaṃnnikarṣāttapane sūrye gātrāṇi śarīrāvayavān |
``gātraṃ pratīke daṇḍāgrajaṅghābhāge vapuṣyapi'' iti ratnamālā |
paritapati sati śiśuriti karuṇayā māmavaṣṭabhya śiśutvahetumadviṣayadayayā māmavalambyopari tatapakṣaḥsanmamopari vistaritasvapakṣaḥ sansvapakṣābhyāṃ karaṇābhyāṃ māmavikalaṃ samagraṃ ploṣācchoṣaṇādarakṣadrakṣitavān |
rakṣaterlaṅ |
ato'tra bhrātṛsneho vismayanīya iti saṃbandhaḥ |
etena saṃpāteḥ pakṣaśoṣo jāta iti phalitam||5||

 ehyehītyādareṇa dviruktiḥ |
tvayeti vīreṇa garutmatā garuḍena naḥ pitāmahī vinateva gṛdhrāṇāṃ cakravartinā sārvabhaumena tvayāśyenī no mātā putravatī praśastaputrayuktā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
praśaṃsāyāṃ matup |
atra ``dvau putrau vinatāyāstu garuḍoruṇa eva ca |
tasmājjāto'hamaruṇātsaṃpātistu mamāgrajaḥ||'' ityetadanusaṃdheyam||6||

 vatseti |
kālaviprakarṣāttrayodaśavarṣātmakakālātipātāddhetoḥ |
rāmaḥ kiṃ mandībhūtapitṛśoka iti kākuranusaṃdheyā/ |
tasyeti |
tasya rāmasya vidyāyā adhyātmavidyāyāstapaso niyamasya vṛddhānāṃ trividhavṛddhānāṃ ca saṃyogaḥ saṃbandhaḥ sva svīyā dhīratā dhairyam |
aprakampyatvamiti yāvat |
nyāyyo nyāyādanapetaḥ |
``dharmapatyarthanyāyādanapete'' ityanapetārthe yat |
rakṣāyāṃ lokarakṣaṇe'dhikāraḥ kartṛbhāvaśca daurmanasyaṃ pitṛduḥkhaṃ vyapohati nāśayati |
bhinnāni vākyāni |
ataḥ kriyāpadasyaikavacanopapattiḥ||7||

 tṛptairiti |
virādhamāṃsānāṃ tṛptairvirādhamāṃsahetukatṛptiyuktaiḥ |
kāraṇasya śeṣatvavivakṣayā ṣaṣṭhī |
āveditārthānāha- citrakūṭādityādinā |
havyavāhe'gnāvupasedivānupasasāda |
saderliṭaḥ kvasuḥ |
dhātoretvābhyāsalopau |
ityāveditamiti ślokadvayamidamekānvayam||8||

9||

 bāḍhamiti |
saṃpātiśrutārthānumatau |
``bāḍhaṃ triṣu dṛḍhe klībamanumatyām'' iti ratnamālā |
pañcānāṃ vaṭānāṃ samāharaḥ pañcavaṭī tasyāṃ prativasati nivasati/ |
godāvarītaṭoddeśo godāvaryāstīrapradeśaḥ |
viṣayabāhulyaṃ smṛtiviṣayavastubhūyastvaṃ kālaviprakarṣaścānubhavakālasya cirātyayaśca smṛtiṃ pramuṣṇāti |
smaratyanayeti smṛtirbhāvanā tāṃ smṛtijanakasaṃskāraṃ pramuṣṇāti smṛtijananasāmarthyarahitāṃ karoti/ |
tadubhayamāha- kalpasyetyādinā |
kalpasyādāvasya brahmakalpasyārambhe cārugaṅgaiva patākā dhvajapaṭī yasya sa tathoktaścaraṇo daṇḍavat |
gaṅgādhvajapaṭādivaditi bhāvaḥ |
viṣṇoścaraṇo yāvadudasthādudgatastāvaduparyūrdhvapradeśe mama paricayo viśiṣyāvagama āsīt |
kiṃca tejasāṃ sūryādīnāṃ jyotiṣāmavadhivalayaḥ sīmāvalayabhūtaḥ saptamyasyāmburāśeḥ parisaragataḥ samīpagato lokālokaścakravālaparvataḥ |
yāvatsanniviṣṭa iti śeṣaḥ |
paryanteṣu pārśveṣu tāvatparicaya āsīdityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
ataḥ pūrvoktamubhayaṃ smṛtiṃ pramuṣṇātīti saṃbandhaḥ||10||

 tatreti |
radhuvṛṣaṃ ra3vaṃśajaśreṣṭhaṃ vṛṣasyantī ratyarthamicchantī |
``vṛṣasyantī tu kāmukī'' ityamaraḥ/ |
aneketi |
anekayugajīvinyā yasyāstretā tretāyugam |
``tretā tvagnitraye yuge'' iti ratnamālā |
trayodaśī trayodaśānāṃ pūraṇī |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
``tasya pūraṇe ḍaṭ'' iti ḍaṭaṣṭitvānṅīp |
sā śūrpaṇakhā kṣīrakaṇṭhakamatyantabālakaṃ vatsaṃ rāmaṃ vṛṣasyantī maithunārthamicchantī satī na lajjitā na trapate |
ato'sya maryādātikramo vismayanīya iti saṃbandhaḥ |
kiṃcidadhikadvādaśayugavayaskāyāstriṃśadvarṣādhikavayaskarāmakāmanā dharmaśāstrakāmaśāstrayoratyantānucitetyarthaḥ |
vṛṣasyantītyatra vṛṣaśabdasyecchārthakyajantasya ``aśvakṣīravṛṣalavaṇānāmātmaprītau kyaci'', ``aśvavṛṣayormaithunecchāyām'' iti sūtravārtikābhyāmasugāgame kāmukhyāṃ rūḍhiḥ||11||

 tasyāmiti |
lakṣmaṇastasyāṃ śūrpaṇakhāyām |
asya nyavīviśadityanenānvayaḥ |
karṇanāsoṣṭhasya |
samāhāradvandvaḥ |
kartanena chedena daśānanasya tiraskāreṇa praśastiṃ rāmakīrtiṃ nyavīviśanniveśayāmāsa |
niveśayaterlauṅi caṅi dvitvaṃ sanvadbhāvopādhinābhyāsadīrghaḥ||12||

 tannimitta iti |
tannimittaḥ śūrpaṇakhāvairūpyakaraṇahetukaḥ kaścidabhiyogo vairaheturabhigrahaḥ |
``abhiyogastvabhigrahaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anubaddha utpāditaḥ |
kaścidityanena hetuyogābhiyogavaividhyaṃ vyajyate/ |
ekeneti |
lakṣmaṇastu sītārakṣaṇe niyukta iti bhāvaḥ |
caturdaśeti |
samāsāntavidheranityatvāt |
``dvitribhyāṃ ṣamūdhnaḥ'' iti ṣapratyayābhāvena trimūrdhāna iti||13||

 āścaryamiti |
dāśarathau mahāśūradaśarathātmaje |
``pituḥ śataguṇaṃ putraḥ'' iti nyāyāditi bhāvaḥ |
daśarathaśabdādapatyatvavivakṣāyām ``ata iña'' |
mahaditi |
punaḥśabdaḥ kiṃtvityarthaḥ |
``punaraprathame bhede'' ityamaraḥ |
mahadvipulaṃ vairadvāraṃ vairahetureva dvāram |
śliṣṭarūpakam |
``dvāraṃ tu dvāryaupāye ca'' iti ratnamālā |
apāvṛtamiti vivṛtamiti manyamānaḥsaṃbhāvyasaṃpramugdho'smi kṛtyakṛtyavivekaśūnyo bhavāmi |
``muhavaicitye'' |
vaicityamavivekaḥ |
asminnavasare samaye na moktavyā na tyājyāḥ |
kuta ityatrāha- svasuriti |
madena madirādikṛtaharṣamohavyatikareṇāndhaḥ kṛtyādipramitiśūnyaḥ |
taduktam- ``madirādikṛto harṣamohavyatikaro madaḥ'' iti |
athavā madena śauryādiprayuktagarveṇāndhaḥ |
``gajadānaṃ garvaharṣaretomṛgamadā madāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
māyāvī māyāpracuro vañcanayā śāmbarīprabhṛtivismayanīyāstraśastrādisṛṣṭyā ca bhūyiṣṭha ityarthaḥ |
``asmāyāmedhā-'' ityādinā bhūmni matvarthīyo viniḥ |
prabhuḥ kośadaṇḍādimān |
``prabhuḥ śaktādhipau'' iti ratnamālā |
amitavīryo'saṃkhyeyaparākramaḥ |
``vīryaṃ parākrame śukle'' iti ratnamālā |
antikacaraḥ sapatno bhūmyanantaraḥ śatrurdaśamukho rāvaṇaḥ sodaryāyā ekodarajātāyāḥ |
``sodarādyat'' iti yatpratyayaḥ |
``samānodaryasodaryasagarbhyasahajāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
svasurbhaginyāḥ śūrpaṇakhāyāḥ |
``bhaginī svasā'' ityamaraḥ |
ḥnikāraṃ vairūpyam |
``nikāro viprakāraḥ syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
kathamiva sahate kathaṃ mṛṣyati |
ivaśabdenābhāso'pi sahanaheturnāstīti vyajyate |
tathā tadvadbhūyobhūyaḥ punaḥ punaḥ svajanānāṃ bandhūnām |
``bandhusvasvajanāḥ samāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
vinipātaṃ ca vadhaṃ ca kathamiva sahate |
subāhuvirādhavadhābhiprāyeṇa ca bhūyobhūya ityuktam |
kaṣṭaṃ duḥkhaṃ nipuṇaṃ ca yathā tathā śiśavaḥ sītārāmalakṣmaṇā no'smākamanupālyā rakṣaṇīyāḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
bandhubādayo vairadvāravivaraṇahetava iti bhāvaḥ |
bhūmyanantaralakṣaṇaṃ pūrvamevoktam |
asmiñloka upāyāpāyaśaṅkābhyāṃ prāptyāśāsphuṭā |
atra ca tattvānukīrtanarūpo mārgaḥ, vitarkapratipādakavākyātmakaṃ rūpam, apakārijanādbhayarūpa udvegaḥ, śaṅkātrāsarūpasaṃmaśca saṃdhyaṅgānyuktāni||14||

 ahamapīti |
kṛtāhnikaḥ kṛtasnānaḥ śivatātiṃ śivaṃkaraṃ vastvanusaṃdhāsyāmi dhyāyāmi |
``śivatātiḥ śivaṃkaraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atreṣṭārthopāyānusaraṇamākṣepo nāma saṃdhyāṅgamuktam/ |
gaganagamanamabhinīya |
svāvāsaṃ prāptumiti bhāvaḥ |
eṣo'smīti |
eṣo'haṃ pralayamarutaḥ pralayavātātpracaṇḍena tīvreṇa |
``caṇḍo daityaviśeṣe yamadāse triṣu tu tīvrakopanayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
raṃhasā vegena |
``raṃhastarasī tu rayaḥ syadaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃkṣiptaḥ saṃkocitaḥ prathimā vaipulyaṃ yasya tattathoktam |
``pṛthvādibhya imanijvā'' iti bhāve imanic |
antarikṣaṃ nabhaḥ pibanniva san |
antarikṣasya svacchatvājjalasādṛśyamabhisaṃdhāya pibannivetyuktam |
malayagirermalayaparvatātkṣepīyo drutaṃ yathā tathā nivāsabhūbhṛnnivāsaparvatastena saha saṃyuktaṃ kṣitiruhajālaṃ vṛkṣaṣaṇḍamabhyupeto'smi prāptobhavāmi||15||

 ayamiti |
ayamanokahānāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ vṛkṣāṇāṃ nivahaiḥ samūhairnirantaro nibiḍaḥ snigdhaścākacakyayukto nīlaḥ parisaraḥ samīpadeśo yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni tathāvidhairaraṇyairvanaiḥ pariṇadvā saṃśliṣṭā godāvarī mukheṣu dvāreṣu yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ kandarā guhā yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
abhiṣyandamānairvyāpnuvadbhiḥ |
``syandū prasravaṇe'' ityasyābhipūrvasya ``anuviparyabhinibhyaḥ syadnateraprāṇiṣu'' iti ṣatvam |
meghaimamedurito dviguṇito nīlimā nailyaṃ yasya sa tathoktaḥ |
dūramiti |
rāmaścitramṛgeṇa dūraṃ hṛtaḥ prāpitaḥ |
abhūditi śeṣaḥ |
lakṣmaṇastayā diśā rāmagatayā diśāgacchati |
tatastadanantaraṃ parivrāṭ saṃnyāsī |
``bhikṣuḥ parivrāṭ karmandī'' ityamaraḥ |
uṭajaṃ parṇaśālām |
``parṇaśāloṭajo'striyām'' ityamaraḥ |
praviṣṭaḥ prāptaḥ |
vyaktarūpo'yaṃ bhikṣurdaśakaṃdharaḥ |
jāta iti śeṣaḥ |
dhigiti nindāyām |
atra prastutopayogicchadmācaraṇādabhūtāharaṇaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||16||

 pramādo mama rāmalakṣmaṇayapaścānavadhānam |
aho vismayanīya ityarthaḥ |
kutsane dviruktiḥ |
paraḥsahasrairiti |
sahasrebhyaḥ pare paraḥsahasrāṇi taiḥ |
piśācānāmiva vadanāni yeṣāṃ te tathoktaiḥ kharairāyuktaṃ samantato yuktaṃ rathaṃ vadhūṭīmapāṃsinīṃ sītāmāropya eṣa pāpo rāvaṇaḥ kvāpi gacchati |
``jīvadbhartrī kulasthā yā vadhūṭī sā stryapāṃsinī'' iti trikāṇḍaśeṣaḥ||17||

 paulastya pulastyavaṃśya |
pulastyavaṃśya |
sābhiprāyamidaṃ viśeṣaṇam |
tamabhiprāyamāha-dhartāra iti |
vidyānāṃ sarvāsāmapi vidyānāmīśvarā nirvāhikāḥ pralayeṣvadhyetṛśūnyakāleṣu bhagavataḥ pūjyasya vedasya sarvapuruṣārthatatsādhanajñāpakasyāmnāyasya dhartāro dhārayitāraḥ |
sṛṣṭikāleṣu pravartanārthamiti bhāvaḥ |
ye pulastyādayaḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
teṣāmanvayaketanasya vaṃśadhvajasya |
tatprakhyāpakasyeti yāvat |
vedavrataiścaturvedāṅgaprājāpatyādiśirovratādibhiḥ snātasya vratāṅgasnānakartaurvitale bhavāni vaitalānyadhotalaviśeṣasthāni sadmāni yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ kālakeyādayastajjeturjitavataḥ |
tṛnnantatvādvaitalasadmana iti dvitīyāntam |
tapasā dīptasya prakāśamānasya rājñaḥ sato rañjakasya prabhoḥ sato bhavataste duścaritānāṃ duṣkarmaṇāmavatārasya saṃkramaṇasya jananī hetubhūtā durmatiḥ sītāpahārakartavyatāviṣayakadurbuddhiḥ kathaṃ jātā kena prakāreṇa jātā |
vaṃśavidyātapaḥ parākramaprabhāvādīni ceha cauryeṇa parakalatrāpahārapratibhaṭānīti bhāvaḥ |
atra prastutopayogisamānavacanātsaṃgraho nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||18||

 kathamiti |
avajñayā pakṣitvaprayuktānādareṇa na śrṛṇoti |
madīyaṃ vacanamiti śeṣaḥ |
ivaśabdaḥ saṃbhāvanāyām |
āḥ iti kope |
durātmandurbauddhe, rākṣasāpasada rākṣasādhama |
``nihīno'pasadaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
