tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti kope dviruktiḥ |
tuṇḍeti |
tuṇḍena madvaktreṇa protānāṃ chidrīkṛtānāṃ śirasāṃ karoṭerasthnaḥ |
``śiro'sthni tu karoṭiḥ strī'' ityamaraḥ |
vivarebhyo randhrebhya ākṛṣṭā bahiruddhṛtāḥ sphurantyaścalantyaḥ |
idaṃ viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ bahuvrīhighaṭakamālāśabdāntadvandve veditavyam |
tvaco'sṛgdharāḥ |
``striyāṃ tvagasṛgdharā'' ityamaraḥ |
vasā medāṃsi |
``medastu vapā vasā'' ityamaraḥ |
``sphuratprasphuṭa-'' iti ca kvacitpaṭyate |
kloma tilakam |
``tilakaṃ kloma'' ityamaraḥ |
plīhā gulmaḥ |
``gulmastu plīhā puṃsi'' ityamaraḥ |
yakṛtkālakhaṇḍam |
``kālakhaṇḍayakṛtī tu same ime'' ityamaraḥ |
drutāni ddhavībhūtānyuṣṇāni yuddhakrodhavaśāduṣṇasparśāni rudhirāṇi raktāni snāyavo vasnasāḥ |
``atha vasnasā |
snāyuḥ striyām'' ityamaraḥ |
āntramālā mālāsadṛśamāntraṃ purītat |
``āntraṃ purītat'' ityamaraḥ |
sasya sa tathoktastasya te tava, asya cāṅgairityanenānvayaḥ |
atyugro'titīkṣṇaḥ krakaca iva karapatrākhyaśastraviśeṣa iva pracaṇḍaistīkṣṇairnakharaiḥ |
``krakaco'strī karapatram'', nakho'strī nakharo'striyām'' iti cāmaraḥ |
utkartena samyagbhedanena |
``kṛtī cchedane'' ityasmādbhāve ghañ |
kvāṇanti śabdavanti kīkasānyasthīni yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni taiḥ |
``kīkasaṃ kulyamasthi ca'' ityamaraḥ |
khaṇḍitāścinnā grīvāṇāṃ kaṃdharāṇām |
``grīvāyāṃ śiro3ḥ kiṃdharetyapi'' ityamaraḥ |
dhamanayaḥ śirā yeṣāṃ taiḥ |
``nāḍī tu dhamaniḥ śirā'' ityamaraḥ |
aṅgaistavāvayavaiḥ karaṇaiḥ śyenīsuto gṛdhraḥ |
ahamiti yāvat |
tṛpyatu tṛptiṃ bhajatu |
prāptakāle loṭ |
atra roṣasaṃbhramavacanarūpaṃ toṭakaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||19||

 niṣkrānta iti |
jaṭāyuriti śeṣaḥ/ |
śuddhaviṣkambha iti kevalasaṃskṛtātmakatvāt |
vṛttānāṃ virādhaśūrpaṇakhākharadūṣaṇavadhādikathāṃśānāṃ vartiṣyamāṇānāṃ jaṭāyukathāṃśānāṃ ca saṃkṣepeṇa kathanācceti bhāvaḥ/ |
atha pañcamo'ṅkaḥ prastūyate- praviśya lakṣmaṇa ityādinā |
ārye |
sīte ityarthaḥ |
jyeṣṭhabhrātṛbhāryātvādāryāśabdaḥ |
kaṣṭaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ daśānāṃ krodhādyavasthānām |
``daśāvasthānekavidhā'' ityamaraḥ |
pariṇāmaṃ vikāram |
``paraṇāmo vikāraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anubhavati bhuṅkte mārīcaśatruḥ |
māyāmṛgahananāditi bhāvaḥ |
tamāha- eṣa iti |
mūrto mūrtimānkrodha iva sthitaḥ śatrukṛtāparādhaprayuktamanaḥprajvalātiśaya iti saṃbhāvanīyo jaṅgamaḥ saṃcariṣṇuḥ sākṣādagniriva sthitaḥ |
dāhahetutvenāgnitulyeṣṭajanaviyogādijanyaduḥkhātiśaya iti saṃbhāvanīyaḥ |
ubhatrotprekṣālaṃkāraḥ |
eṣa āryo hṛllekho jugupsāviśeṣaḥ |
``hṛdayasya hṛllekhayadaṇlāseṣu'' iti hṛdayaśabdasya hṛdādeśaḥ |
hṛdayaṃ likhatīti hṛllekhaḥ lekha ityaṇṇantam |
bhāryāpahāre doṣadarśanaprayuktamanovasthājugupsātra hṛllekhaḥ |
lajjā tu manaḥsaṃkocastābhyāṃ hetubhyāṃ saṃveginīṃ saṃbhramavatīm |
``samau saṃvegasaṃbhramau'' ityamaraḥ |
tanuṃ śarīraṃ kṛcchrādduḥkhādbibharti dhārayati||20||

 parvoktameva dṛṣṭāntoktyā viśadayati- tathā hīti |
tathaivetyarthaḥ |
ābhugneti |
eṣa ityanuṣajyate |
ābhugnayoḥ kuṭilayoḥ |
``ābhugnaṃ kuṭilam'' ityamaraḥ |
unnatatvādinā sāśṛśyaṃ vivakṣitam |
ghaṭanayānyonyamelanena saṃsūcito vyañjito'ntaḥsphuratā dhairyeṇāprakampyatvalakṣaṇasvabhāvaviśeṣeṇa stambhitaḥ pratihato'thāpi durvyavastho duḥkhena vyavasthā niyamanaṃ yasya sa durvyavasthaḥ, ata eva vitato viśīrṇaḥ proccaṇḍo'titīvraḥ kopātmako'nalo'gniragnisadṛśaḥ kopo yasya sa tathoktaḥ, ata evoddhūmāvalirudgatadhūmapaṅktirmadhye jvalandīpyamāno vāḍavo vaḍavāgniryasya sa tathoktaḥ, ambhasāṃ nidhiriva samudra iva sa eṣa āryo vidyutā vyañjito vajro dambholirgarbhe'ntaryasya sa tathoktastasya jaladasya chāyāṃ kāntiṃ samālambate |
dhārayatītyarthaḥ |
atra bhrukuṭīdhūmāvalyoḥ kopānalavāḍavayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvenābhinnayoḥ sādhāraṇadharmatvādupamālaṃkāraḥ |
evaṃ bhrukhuṭīvidyutoḥ kopānalavajrayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvenābhinnatvādupamālaṃkāraḥ |
tadanuprāṇitā ca padārthavṛttirūpā nidarśaneti saṃkaraḥ |
``padārthavṛttimapyeke vadantyanyāṃ nidarśanām'' ityukteḥ |
atra kopotkarṣābhidhānātprastutotkarṣābhidhānamudāhṛtirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||21||

 nyakkāra iti |
nyakkāro bhāryāpaharaṇabhavaḥ paribhavo me hṛdi vajrakīla iva durbhedyavajraśaṅkuriva |
``jvāle sthāṇau dvayoḥ kīlaḥ śaṅkuśaṅkulayoḥ pumān'' iti ratnamālā |
tīvraḥ sanduḥsahaḥ sanparispandate calati |
pratikriyānarhatvātsādṛśyam |
kiṃ ca mano lajjayānucitadaśāvartitvaprayuktasaṃkocona saṃmīlitaṃ svalpībhūtaṃ sadghorāndhe bhayaṃkare'jñānāvahe ca |
``andha dṛṣṭyupaghāte'' ityasmātpacādyac |
khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ |
tamasi majjatīva |
kartavyeṣu pratipattiśūnyaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
kiṃ ca tātasya pitṛsakhasya jaṭāyorvipattermaraṇājjāyata iti tātavipattijaḥ |
``pañcamyāmajātau'' iti janerlaḍaḥ |
śoko duḥkhaṃ kartṛ |
māṃ dahati bhasmīkaroti |
``daha bhasmīkaraṇe'' |
yasmiṃstātavipattijaśoke kriyā pratikriyā nāstyeva |
nyakkārādau tu pratikriyāstīti hṛdayam |
varākīṃ kṛpaṇāṃ sītāṃ prati karuṇā punaḥ sītāduḥkhanibandhanaduḥkhātibhūmistu |
``punaraprathame bhede'' ityamaraḥ |
marmāṇi prāṇasthānāni |
``tirīṭaṃ marma yojanam'' iti napuṃsakatvam |
chinattīva |
uktaṃ ca hanūmatā- ``iyaṃ sā yatkṛte rāmaścaturbhiḥ paritapyate |
kāruṇyenānṛśaṃsyena śokena madanena ca/ |
strī praṇaṣṭeti kāruṇyam'' iti prādhānyāttasyehopādānam||22||

 lokottarakarmāṇo lokeṣu śreṣṭhacaritāḥ kṛcchreṣu kaṣṭeṣu na pramuhyanti pramugdhā na bhavanti pramohastvavivekaḥ |
atrāpyudāhṛtirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ |
vatseti |
kākuratrābhipretā kiṃ lokottarāṇītyarthaḥ |
tadāha- yairiti |
yairnṛpatibhirbhuvanāni guptāni rakṣitāni santi akṛtobhayāni kṛtaścidapi bhayarahitānyāsannabhavan |
nāsti kuto bhayaṃ yasyeti vigrahe mayūravyaṃsakāditvātsamāsaḥ |
mahānto'bhīśavaḥ kiraṇā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāḥ |
``abhīśuḥ pragrahe raśmau'' iti śāśvataḥ |
sūryānvayasya sūryavaṃśasya ketavo dhvajavatprakāśakāḥ pūrve prācīnāste nṛpatayo rājāno mayā tiraskāritā anyaiḥ paribhavaṃ mayā prāpitāḥ |
kiṃ ca yo jaṭāyuḥ kalpānteṣvapi brahmadināvasāneṣvapi sthiro'naśvaraḥ sādhuḥ paropakāraśīlaḥ sa jaṭāyurmayā divaṃ gamito lokāntaraṃ prāpitaḥ |
kiṃ ca vane patnīṃ hārayatā rāvaṇakartṛkasītāpahāre prayojakakartrā |
mayā lokairyatkarmākṛtaṃ tatkṛtam |
ato lokottarāṇi rāmasya karmāṇītyarthaḥ |
yadvā lokaśreṣṭhānītyabhiprāyeṇa lokottarāṇīti lakṣmaṇenoktam |
rāmastvarthāntaraparikalpanārthaṃ tadevānuvadati- vatsa, lokottarāṇīti |
lokānuttārayantyullaṅghayanta iti lokottaraṇītyabhiprāyeṇa pūrve tiraskāritā ityuktam |
rājā rāṣṭramiti nyāyena lokaśabdastadrājaparaḥ |
kiṃ ca lokamāmuṣmikalokaṃ ut  atiśayena tārayanti prāpayantīti lokottarāṇi |
``vedāntaṃ tarati'' ityādāviva prāptyarthakāttaraterantarbhāvitaṇyarthātpacādyac |
ityabhiprāyeṇa jaṭāyurdivaṃ gamita ityuktam |
kiṃ ca lokebhya uttīrṇāni lokottarāṇītyabhiprāyeṇa ``yadakṛtaṃ lokaiḥ kṛtaṃ tanmayā'' ityuktam, ato lokottarāṇi rāmasya karmāṇīti suṣṭhūktaṃ tvayeti bhāvaḥ||23||

 tīrthabhūtasya guruvaddhitopadeṣṭuḥ |
``nipānāgamayostīrthamṛṣijuṣṭajale gurau'' ityamaraḥ |
``bhūtaṃ nyāyye same'' iti ratnamālā |
kva saṃbhava ityanvayaḥ/ |
paścimāvasthāmantimāvasthāṃ paśyāmīva |
smṛtivaiśadyāddarśane sāmyam |
yāmiti |
ayaṃ śrīrāmāyaṇaślokaḥ |
āyuṣman |
bhūmni matup |
āśāsyamānadīrghāyuṣyetyarthaḥ |
yāṃ sītāmoṣadhimiva mṛtasaṃjīnivīmoṣadhimiva mahāvane durlabhauṣadhīnāmapi saṃbhavasthānatvānmahati vane |
āṣadhirhi vane viceyā bhavati |
vicinoṣyanviṣyati |
sā sītā lokasaṃjīvinī sītā mama prāṇāścetyubhayam |
sāmānye klībatvam |
rāvaṇena hṛtaṃ prāpitaṃ nāśitaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
dvayamapi tantreṇa vivakṣitam||24||

 itīti |
vīralokānyuddhe hatānāṃ vīrāṇāṃ prāpyāṃllokān |
``adhiśīṅsthāsāṃ karma'' iti karmatvam |
lokābhinayena vīralokānityuktam |
``gaccha lokānanuttamān'' ityanuttamalokapradānāt/ |
vatseti |
hṛdayamarmāṇi vidhyantīti vigrahe ``nahivṛti-'' ityādinā marmaśabdasya dīrghaḥ |
kathāyā uddhātaḥ prastāvaḥ |
``syādabhyādānamudghātaḥ'' ityamaraḥ/ |
atha kimityaṅgīkāre/ |
kimiti |
paribhavātiprasaṅgasya paribhavātiśayasya yattulyamanurūpaṃ pratividhānaṃ syātkiṃ nāma kariṣyata ityanvayaḥ |
prāgeveti |
prāgeva sītāpahārādeḥ pūrvameva me matiricchā rākṣasavadhāya kṛtā |
yato yasmātte rākṣasā bahubhirnimittairmunijanakadanādibhiravyabhicaritahetubhirvadhyā eva |
tato hetoḥ prāgeva matiḥ sṛteti saṃbandhaḥ |
parata itaḥparaṃ sītāpahārādinimittotpattyanantaraṃ kṛtyaṃ kartuṃ śakyaṃ pratividhānaṃ kulasya śamanādanyannāsti rākṣasakulavidhvaṃsanādanyanna saṃbhavati |
iha tu sītāpaharaṇānimitteṣu tu tanmātrake tadeva tanmātramalpaṃ tanmātraṃ tanmātrakam |
atyalpameva rākṣasakulavidhvaṃsanam |
tasminkṛte'pi me mama śamo matkṛtoparamaḥ kutaḥ |
kasmādbhavediti śeṣaḥ |
ataḥ kiṃ nāma kariṣyata iti saṃbandhaḥ||25||

 tathā hīti |
anurūpapratividhānadaurlabhyādityarthaḥ |
pracaṇḍeti |
pracaṇḍo'tyantatīvraḥ paripiṇḍitaḥ saṃghātabhāvaṃ prāptaḥ stimitavṛttiḥ |
khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ |
antarmukho gūḍhaprasaraṇo māṃ muhurmuhuḥ pibanniva jhaṭityuccejarvalañśikhābhirjvālābhirniścaranniva bahirniḥsaranniva ca sthito manyuḥ krodho dāhyantaramanupalabhya svalpasya rākṣasakulavidhvaṃsanasyāsatprāyatvena madvyatiriktaṃ dagdhavyaṃ vastvaprāpya vāḍavaḥ payodhimiva māṃ dahati bhasmīkaroti |
ato'smānmanyormaā trāyatāṃ rakṣatu |
bhavāniti śeṣaḥ |
anurūpadāhyalambhanādinā māṃ tvaṃ rakṣetyarthaḥ |
``bhītrārthānāṃ-'' iti pañcamī |
iyameva vīrarasasya parā kāṣṭhā |
atra cāgnirhi prathamaṃ gomayapiṇḍādiṣu piṇḍībhūtaśaktyāvasthatayā stabdhavṛttirantareva sūkṣmam, anantaramūrdhvaṃ jvalan, tadanantaraṃ samantato jvālābhirvyāpnuvan, āśrayāntarābhāvena svāśrayameva dahatītyasya prasiddhāgnivṛttāntasyāprastutasya yat'' ityukteḥ||26||

 anurūpadāsyā lābhaṃ manvāno lakṣmaṇo vyāsaṅgāntarotpādanameva trāṇaṃ matvāha- etānīti |
mṛgāṇāṃ hariṇajātīyānāṃ yūthāni kulāni |
``sajātīyaiḥ kulaṃ yūthaṃ tiraścāṃ puṃsapuṃsakam'' ityamaraḥ |
śvāpadānāṃ duṣṭasattvānāṃ kulaiḥ samūhaiḥ |
``śuno daṃṣṭrā'' ityādinā saṃjñāyāṃ pūrvapadasya dīrghaḥ |
dakṣiṇāṃ diśamabhi dakṣiṇayā diśopalakṣaṇīyāḥ santaḥ pravartante paramparyeṇa tiṣṭhanti |
``abhirabhāge'' iti karmapravacanīyatvāttadyoge dvitīyā |
vibhāvayāmo vicārayāmaḥ/ |
vatseti |
adṛṣṭapūrvāḥ |
pūrvaṃ kadāpyadṛṣṭā ityarthaḥ/ |
nanvityavadhāraṇe |
āruṇimarūṇasya putram |
``ata iñ'' itīñ |
``tato'hamaruṇājjātaḥ'' ityukteragnisātkṛtya kṛtsnamagnimayaṃ kṛtvā dāhena saṃskṛtya |
``vibhāṣā sāti kārtsnye'' iti sātipratyayaḥ |
pañcavaṭyāśramānnirgatayorityanvayaḥ |
ko'pi kālo vartate |
dīrghakālo gataḥ |
vartamānasāmīpye laṭ |
tatra hetumāha- yata iti |
dūraṃ vicchinnāḥ samāptiṃ gatāḥ |
sīmāno'vadhibhūmayaḥ |
yathā ceti |
pratibhayaṃ bhayaṃ pratīti pratibhayam |
tathā tena hetunā nūnaṃ tarkayāmi |
``nūnamavaśyaṃ niścaye'' ityamaraḥ |
janasthānasya paścimaḥ pratīcyāṃ diśi sthitaḥ kuṃjaravāniti tasya saṃjñā |
danukabandhena danuputratvādapamūrdhakalevaratvācca danukabandhasaṃjñāvatā |
uktaṃ ca śrīrāmāyaṇe- ``śriyā virājitaṃ putraṃ danostvaṃ viddhi lakṣmaṇa |
indrakopādidaṃ rūpaṃ prāptametadraṇājire||'' iti/ |
draṣṭavya iti |
kāntāramaṇḍūkaḥ |
vanānnirgamanāśakta ityarthaḥ |
maṇḍūko jale eva tiṣṭhati |
tadvatayaṃ kāntāra eva tiṣṭhatīti bhāvaḥ |
draṣṭavyaḥ |
vikṛtaveṣatvāddraṣṭumāśaṃsita ityarthaḥ/ |
kaḥ ko'tra bhoḥ |
vidyata iti śeṣaḥ |
paritrāyatām |
bhavāniti śeṣaḥ |
ahaṃ hīti |
śabarī cāsau tāpasī ceti vigrahe karmadhārayaḥ |
``puṃvatkarmadhāraya-'' iti puṃvadbhāvaḥ |
mataṅgāśrame vāstavyā vāsakartrī |
siddhā siddhimatī||27||

 lakṣmaṇaniṣkramaṇenādya vyāsaṅgāntarābhāvādduḥkhodrekaṃ rāmasyāha-priye iti |
madhurāṃ priyām |
``svādupriyau tu madhurau'' ityamaraḥ |
vācaṃ prakira |
tattadarthavivakṣayā punaḥpunarvada |
atha ceti pūrvoktasya pratiṣedhaḥ |
parābhūtaiḥ paribhūtaiḥ 
(kartṛbhiḥ) vilapanavinodo'pi ``priye hā hā kvāsi'' ityādiparidevanena duḥkhalaghūkaraṇamapyasulabho durlabhaḥ paulastyo rāvaṇo'nindyo vrajatyahṛtabhāryatvena nindānarho yathecchaṃ carati |
mayi punaḥ parivādo hṛtabhāryatvena nindā |
sthiteti śeṣaḥ |
yato yasmādanena rāvaṇena 
(kartrā) vaire vidveṣe rūḍhe sati jāte sati |
tāṭakāvadhādinetyarthaḥ |
bahuguṇamabhyadhikaṃ yathā tathā pratikṛtam |
pratikriyā kṛtetyarthaḥ |
atra ca virā[ro]dhādimukhena pīḍanādrāvaṇavairānuguṇaṃ pratikṛtam |
sītāharaṇena dviguṇaṃ pratikṛtam |
jaṭāyuvadhena tu tadbahuguṇaṃ pratikṛtamiti bhāvaḥ |
jaṭāyuvadho hi sītāharaṇādapi garīyān |
tathā ca rāmavacanam- sītāharaṇajaṃ duḥkhaṃ na me saumya tathāgatam |
yathā vināśe gṛdhrasya matkṛte ca paraṃtapa||['']
ṛ iti |
guṇaśabda āvṛttivacanaḥ |
atrādhikyaṃ bodhayati-``maurvīsūdavṛkodaratattvāvṛttīndriyeṣu rūpādau |
sattvādau saṃdhyādau tyāgādāvapradhānake ca guṇaḥ||'' iti ratnamālā||28||

 taditi |
krūrāḥ karkaśāḥ |
``triṣu krūrāḥ karkaśoṣṇanṛśaṃsakruddhabhīṣaṇāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
dantā daśanā eva karapatrāṇi krakacāni |
``krakaco'strīkarapatram'' ityamaraḥ |
tairnikṛttānāṃ chinnānāṃ sattvānāṃ jantūnāṃ saṃghātebhyo niḥsaradbhiḥ pravahadbhirasṛgbhī raktaiḥ pluta ārdraḥ kūrcameva bhrūmadhyameva |
``kūrcamastrī bhruvormadhyam'' ityamaraḥ |
gucchaḥ stabako yasya tattathoktam |
yadvā kūrcasya bhrūmadhyasya gucchaḥ stabako yasya tattathoktam |
``gucchastu stabake stambe hārabhedakalāpayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā dīrghabāhoryojanabāhoḥ kabandhasya tadvaktraṃ vikṛtākṛti vapuśca rākṣasakutūhalinā nikṛtaveṣarākṣasadarśanecchāvatāryeṇa na dṛṣṭam |
``draṣṭavya eva sa durātmā'' ityuktavatā tvayā na dṛṣṭamityanuśayo lakṣmaṇasya sūcyate||29||

 atheti praśne |
asmatparyanveṣaṇe madanveṣaṇe |
rāmānveṣiṇyupāgateti hi taduktiḥ/ |
śṛṇoṣi |
śṛṇoṣi kiletyarthaḥ/ |
kastaṃ na śṛṇoti |
sarvo'pi lokastaṃ prasiddhaṃ śṛṇotyevetyarthaḥ |
kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepe/ |
sa ca vibhīṣaṇaśca |
daivādvidhivaśāt |
tadaiva kharādivadhānantarameva |
kasyāpi hetoḥ kenāpi hetunā |
``nimittakāraṇahetuṣu sarvāsāṃ prādarśanam'' |
``ṣaṣṭhī hetuprayoge'' iti ṣaṣṭhī |
bandhūnāṃ svasminmanovikārotprekṣaṇenetyarthaḥ |
uktaṃ hi mālyavatā- ``prājñaḥ khalvasāvutprekṣitavikāraḥ svayamevāpasarpet'' iti |
bandhubhyo'vagṛhya viśliṣya |
avagraho viśleṣaḥ |
apādāne pañcamī |
sugrīvasakhyāddhetoḥ |
idamapi mālyavatoktam ``bālyātprabhṛti'' ityādinā |
tasya vibhīṣaṇasyātmasamarpakaḥ svabharasya tvannirgrāhyatvābhisaṃdhiprakāśako lekho'yamityuktvā lekhamarpayatītyarthaḥ |
ātmanaḥ samarpaṇamātmasamarpakaḥ |
bhāve ghañ |
tasmādācaṣṭa ityarthe ṇic |
kartari ṇvul |
ayameva saṃdhyādiṣu caramaḥ samāśrayo nāma |
yathoktam- ``ariṇā vidhyamānasya balavadāśrayaṇaṃ samāśrayaḥ'' iti/ |
vācayati paṭhati |
svastīti |
astviti śeṣaḥ |
rāmadevam |
devaśabdo'yaṃ paramapūjyavācī |
``rājā bhaṭṭārako devaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
viśiṣṭeti |
viśiṣṭamatiśayitaṃ bhāgadheyaṃ bhāgyaṃ yeṣāṃ te tathoktāsteṣām |
``bhāgarūpanāmabhyo dheyaḥ'' iti bhāgyavācino bhāgaśabdāddheyapratyayaḥ |
no'smādṛśāṃ dvayī vakṣyamāṇaṃ dvayam |
``dvitribhyāṃ tayasyāyajvā'' ityajādeśasya sthānivattvāt ``ṭiḍḍhā-'' ityādinā dvayaśabdānṅīp |
paramā sarvātiśāyinī gatiḥ |
abhīṣṭārthasādhanaṃ bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kā sā dvayītyatrāha- dharma iti |
prakṛṣyamāṇaḥ sarvātiśāyī dharmo vā |
anāghrātāvadyagandhamalaukikaśreyaḥsādhanaṃ vā |
``dharmeṇa pāpamapanudati'' ityupakramya ``tasmāddharmaṃ paramaṃ vadanti'' iti śrutiriti bhāvaḥ |
dharmasya goptā pālakaḥ svarūkartṛdeśakālaparikaraphalatāsādhanabhāvādibhiḥ saha dharmasya sattānirvāhako bhavānvā sarvajñaḥ sarvaśaktaḥ sarveśvaraḥ kāruṇikaḥ parabrahmabhūtastvaṃ vā |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
vāśabdokto'yaṃ vikalpo vyavasthitavikalpaḥ |
tathāhi dharmaḥ prakṛṣyamāṇo veti mīmāṃsakamaryādā |
goptā dharmasya vā bhavāniti trayyantaniṣṇātānāṃ maryādā |
idaṃ ca ``dharmaṃ jaiminiḥ'' ``phalamata upapatteḥ'' iti brahmamīmāṃsāsūtrayorvyaktam |
kiṃ ca dharmaḥ prakṛṣyamāṇo veti kādhakānāṃ maryādā |
goptā dharmasya vā bhavāniti śaraṇāgatānāṃ maryādā |
tatra guṇāguṇaparīkṣāyāṃ taddhetorevāstu taddhetutvamiti goptā dharmasya vā bhavānityetadeva vibhīṣaṇasya siddhāntaḥ |
prakṛṣyamāṇa iti prapūrvātkarṣateḥ karmakartari yagātmanepade||30||

------------------
ḥpraˇˇkiṃ saṃdiśyatām |
kiṃ prativacanaṃ preṣyatāmityarthaḥ |
yadeti |
yena kāraṇena |
tattasmātsaṃdeśasya kimavaśiṣyate |
saṃdeśeṣu madhye kaḥ saṃdeśaḥ kartavyatayā tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ |
tvaṃ laṅkeśvaro'si, mama priyasuhṛdasītyeṣa eva saṃdeśaḥ phalita ityabhiprāyaḥ/ |
yatheti |
yathāha saumitriḥ |
tathāstviti śeṣaḥ/ |
anugṛhītāsmi |
vibhīṣaṇāya laṅkeśvaro'sītyādisaṃdeśena kṛtaprasādāsmi/ |
api vibhūṣaṇetyādi |
vibhīṣaṇasaṃparkādvibhīṣaṇasamāgamādāryāyāḥ sītāyāḥ kācitpravṛttiḥ kācidvārtā |
``vārtā pravṛttirvṛttāntaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
apyasti |
apiḥ praśne ``garhāsamuccayapraśnaśaṅkāsaṃbhāvanāsvapi'' ityamaraḥ |
vartamāna iti |
kāle iti śeṣaḥ |
saṃpratītyarthaḥ |
yaditi |
durātmanā durbuddhinā |
rāvaṇeneti śeṣaḥ |
apapriyamāṇāyā ityapādāne pañcamī |
srastamadhaḥpatitamanasūyādattatvādanasūyānāmacihnaṃ yatsrastaṃ tattairgṛhītamityanvayaḥ |
lakṣmaṇasya jijñāsodayārthaṃ śramaṇayā tairiti sāmānyenoktam/ |
heti |
mahāraṇyavāse priyasakhītyarthaḥ |
``ye tvayā kīrtitā doṣā vane vāstavyatāṃ prati |
guṇā ityeva tānmanye tava snehapariplutā||'' iti hi tayoktam |
videharājasya putrīti vigrahaḥ |
``ātmajastanayaḥ sūnuḥ sutaḥ putraḥ striyāṃ tvamī |
āhurduhitaram'' ityamaraḥ |
putraśabdātstrītvavivakṣāyāṃ śārṅgaravādigaṇapāṭhānṅīn |
``sūtograrājabhojakulamerubhyo duhituḥ putraḍvā vaktavyaḥ'' iti vārtikaṃ vyarthamiti śābdikāḥ |
saṃvaraṇaṃ mūrcchām/ |
tairiti śramaṇayā sāmānyenokte lakṣmaṇo viśeṣaṃ pṛcchati- ārye kenetyādinā |
vayodhikatvāttapotiśayavattvāccāryāśabdaḥ |
kena kartrā |
kasya hetoḥ |
kena hetunā |
``ṣaṣṭhī hetuprayoege'' iti ṣaṣṭhī |
taduttarīyaṃ kutra gṛhītamityabhipretam |
ṛṣyamūketi |
prativacanaṃ ṛṣyamūka ityuktiḥ |
rāmaguṇeṣu pūrvokteṣvābhigāmikeṣu rāmasya guṇeṣu pakṣapātātsvārasikaprītyā |
``pakṣaḥ sahāyagarudantikasādhyabhaktimitreṣu cullivivare nṛpakuñjare ca |
vṛnde kacāduparimāsadalau ca pakṣadvāre bale ca gajapārśvarodhayośca||'' iti ratnamālā |
pāto hi nimne salilasyeva svayameva pravaṇatā |
kasyetyasyottaraṃ rāmaguṇapakṣapātāditi |
``guṇe hetāvastriyām'' iti hetau pañcamī |
kenetyasyottaraṃ sugrīvetyādikam |
abhedaikatvavivakṣayā praśnavākye kenetyekavacanam |
prativacane tu sakhyaviśeṣavivakṣayā bahuvacanam/ |
vatseti |
niṣkāraṇapriyakāriṇo mattaḥ kiṃcitphalābhisaṃdhimantaraiva matpriyaṃkarā bhuvanairlokaiḥ |
``mañcāḥ krośanti'' itivanmahanīyaḥ pūjanīyo mahimā yeṣāṃ te tathoktāste sugrīvādayastasyāḥ sītāyā saṃstutamasmābhiściraparicitam |
``saṃstavaḥ syātparicayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
``abhijñāyate'nenetyabhijñānamuttarīyamṛṣyamūkamabhisaṃdhāyarṣyamūkamuddiśya draṣṭuṃ gacchāva ityanvayaḥ/ |
ita iti |
tarhi ṛṣyamūkasya ganvyatve  ita ita iti mārgadarśanam |
āgacchatviti śeṣaḥ/ |
vīrāṇāṃ vādo vīraireva gauravātiśayena punaḥpunaḥ kīrtanamatrabhavataḥ pūjyasya jāta eva jātamātrastasya yauvanātpūrvamevetyarthaḥ |
satataparibhrāntā bhayātiśayena saṃbhrāntā devāsurā yaistathoktānyāścaryāṇyapūrvakarmāṇi |
api ca kiṃ ca kileti prasiddhau |
yaditi |
vajralakṣaṇe devendre |
astīti śeṣaḥ |
mahābāhau vālini samunnataṃ yadvīryamasti tacceti cakāreṇa vāyuvālivīryayoḥ samuccayaḥ |
vidyata iti śeṣaḥ |
ityetatprasiddhamiti kilaśabdārthaḥ||31||

 evamiti |
evamuktaprakāraviśiṣṭa īdṛśa etatsadṛśairanyairapi prakārairviśiṣṭo hemagirau merau vāstavyasya vāsaṃ kartustatrabhavataḥ pūjyasya plavaṃgavānāṃ vānaraśreṣṭhānāmanīkasya senāyāḥ |
``cakraṃ cānīkamastriyām'' ityamaraḥ |
kesariṇaḥ kesarināmnaḥ kṣetrasaṃbhavaḥ patnyāmanyenotpāditaḥ |
``kṣetraṃ patnīśarīrayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
añjanāyā apatyamāñjaneyaḥ |
``strībhyo ḍhak'' |
anenāñjanā kesariṇaḥ patnītyuktam |
hanūmānnāma hanūmāniti nāmnā viśrutaḥ |
mātaryākāśe śvayati vardhata iti mātariśvā vāyuryasya hanūmato retodhā reto dhatte dhārayatīti tathoktaḥ |
``anyebhyo'pi dṛśyate'' iti kartari kvip |
dadhātirantarbhāvitaṇyarthaḥ |
bījapradaḥ |
pitetyarthaḥ |
anena kṣetrasaṃbhava ityatrābhipreto'nyo mātariśvetyuktaṃ bhavati tadviśiṣyakathanāddhetorvyañjitam |
kiṃ hanūmataikena |
hanūmadekasādhyaṃ na kiṃcidapītyarthaḥ |
api tu tādṛśā asaṃkhyātāḥ santītyarthaḥ |
tadāha- ambhodheriti |
ye vānarāḥ ambhodherapo jalāni nārikelīrasamiva nārikelaśalāṭusthitasalilamiva |
pippalyāditvānnārikelaśabdājjātivivakṣāyāṃ ṅīṣ |
tataḥ phalarūpavikārapratyayasya ``harītakyādibhyaśca'' iti lup |
lupi yuktavadbhāvātstrītvam |
``āsvādanadhvanisudhāmburasākhyadhātuṣviṣṭo rasaḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
culakaiḥ prasṛtibhiḥ |
``culukaḥ prasṛtau bhāṇḍe'' iti ratnamālā |
udvilumpanti nāśayanti ``luplṛ cchedane'' ``śe mucādīnām'' iti num |
yeṣāṃ vānarāṇāṃ śikhariṣu parvateṣūtkṣepa ūrdhvodañcanakriyāyāṃ helo'nādaraḥ |
``heḍṛ anādare'' ityasmādbhāve ghañ |
ḍalayoraikyāllakāraḥ |
utkṣepaviṣayānādara ityarthaḥ |
likucodumbaraprāyatāyā ambhodhibrahmastambaviṣayavyāpāreṇārthasiddhatvaṃ bodhayati |
ye brahmastambaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ nivāsadrumamiva viprakartuṃ vikārayituṃ dhūnanabhañjanādinā vikṛtaṃ kartuṃ kṣamāḥ samarthāḥ |
bhavantīti śeṣaḥ |
teṣāṃ vānarāṇāmasaṃkhyāḥ koṭyo'pyamarapateḥ sutaṃ vālinaṃ namanti |
vandanta ityarthaḥ||32||

 ārya iti |
dakṣiṇena dakṣiṇataḥ |
enabantamidam |
asthnāṃ saṃcayaḥ samūhaḥ |
hantaśabdo'nukampāyām |
kimetat |
kasyedamityarthaḥ/ |
yojanabāhoḥ kabandhasya |
abhisṛṣṭā nirmitā/ |
sauhityāditi |
suhitastṛptaḥ |
``suhi tṛptyarthe'' ityasmātkartari ktaḥ |
tasya bhāvaḥ sauhityam |
guṇavacanatvādbhāve ṣyañ |
``paryāptamupasaṃpannaṃ tṛptiḥ sauhityamucyate'' iti halāyudhaḥ |
tasmātsattvasaṃghātamāṃsabhakṣaṇena nityatṛptatvātkabandhasyeti bhāvaḥ |
pṛthavo vipulā rudhirotsekā raktapravāhāścamatkāriṇaḥ santo draṣṭṝṇāṃ darśanakutūhalahetavaḥ santaḥ |
camaditi rasārthakamavyayam |
kvathanti niṣpacyante |
``kvathe niṣpāke'' |
kiṃ ca nalakāḥ śaṅkhāsthīni |
``śaṅkāsthi nalakam'' iti halāyudhaḥ |
ardharcāderākṛtigaṇatvātpuṃliṅgo'pi |
nalakaśabdastvacāṃ māṃsānāṃ ca visraṃsanādagnisparśena bandhavigalanāddhetoścamatkāriṇaḥ santaṣṭaṅkāreṇotkaṭaṃ yathā tathoccaranti |
upariṣṭādgacchantityarthaḥ |
atha vilayanādagnisaṃyogena kārtsnyena dravībhāvāddhetorudbudbudā adhikabudbudayuktā medasāṃ vasānāṃ vīcayaḥ paṅktayastaraṅgā vā |
vīciḥ kuntalavakratve svalpe paṅktitaraṅgayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
utsarpantyūrdhvaṃ gacchanti |
itaḥśmaśānānalādevaṃbhūtāccitāgneḥ |
apādāne pañcamī |
ko'pyanirvācyo'yaṃ divyo devasadṛśa udetyuttiṣṭhati |
citraṃ citram |
samadhikamāścaryamityarthaḥ |
vīraśṛṅgārānyatarapradhānake nāṭake rasāntarāṇāvapyaṅgatayānupraveśasya vaktavyatvāccitāgnyādivarṇanaṃ kavinā kṛtam||33||

 danuriti |
śriyaḥ śrīnāmnyā apsarasaḥ putro danuriti pitṛprayuktaṃ nāma |
indrāstreṇa vajreṇa |
``astramāyudhacāpayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
kṛtaṃ kābandhyaṃ kabandhatvaṃ yasya sa tathokto bhavadāśrayādbhavatsaṃbandhātpūto'smi śuddho'smi||34||

 priyaṃ na ityādaradviruktiḥ/ |
mālyavatprayukteneti |
mālyavatā rāvaṇamātāmahena prayuktena preritena mayāraṇyaṃ yuṣmadāskandanāya yuvayorākramaṇāya dūṣitamāsījjīvahiṃsādidoṣayojitamabhūt |
alamiti |
vāśabdaḥ pūrvoktārthapratiṣedhe |
kaśmalasya smaraṇena prasaktimātreṇa mūrcchāhetoḥ pūrvoktasya smaraṇenālam |
phalaṃ nāstītyarthaḥ |
``gamyamānāpi kriyā kārakavibhaktīnāṃ prayojikā'' iti smaraṇena tṛtīyā |
saṃpratīti |
yuṣmatprabhāvādbhavanmāhātmyāddhetoḥ prādurbhūtaṃ prakāśitaṃ sahajaṃ svābhāvikaṃ jyotirjñānātmakatejo yasya sa tathoktastasya me'parokṣamiva pratyakṣamiva kiṃcidvastu pratibhāti kaścidarthaḥ prakāśate |
tadvastu kṛtamahopakārebhyo vo yuṣmabhyaṃ pratividhānāya pratyupakārāya kathyate |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
tadāha- prārthyetyādinā |
mālyavatā rāvaṇamantriṇā vālī prārthya, yuṣmadghāte yuvayorvadhe niyujyate preryate |
tenāpi vālināpi rāvaṇe maitrīmanurudhya rāvaṇasakhyamuddiśyābhyupeyate'ṅgīkriyate |
mālyavanniyoga iti śeṣaḥ |
itiśabdo'dhyāhartavyaḥ |
iti kathyata ityanvayaḥ||35||

 eṣa iti |
eṣa eva rāvaṇamaitryanurodhena mālyavanniyogābhyupagama eva cāritrasya vīrāṇāṃ sadvṛttasya panthāḥ |
mārga ityarthaḥ |
na tādṛśa iti |
tādṛśo mahāvīro vālī suhṛtkārye mittrakārye mādhyassthayamaudāsīnyaṃ nāvalambate nāśrayati |
mahāvīre'sminvālini mamāpi manaḥ sotkaṇṭhamiva yuddhecchāyuktamiva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ||36||

 itare |
lakṣmaṇaśramaṇādivyapuruṣāḥ |
rāmadevādanyatra kva rāmādanyasminkasminpuruṣe'mūnyakṣarāṇi ``na tādṛśaḥ'' ityādi pūrvaślokākṣarāṇi syuriti śeṣaḥ/ |
saujanyaṃ sujanasya kṛtajñasya karma pratyupakārakaraṇam |
``guṇavacana-'' ityādinā karmaṇi ṣyañ |
kṛtamanuṣṭhitam |
adhunādya mahābhāgo bhāgyātiśayayuktaḥ sveṣu lokeṣu nandatu samṛddhiṃ prāpnotu |
``ṭunadi samṛddhau'' kāmacāre loṭ |
tvaṃ svīyalokānbhuṅkṣvetyarthaḥ/ |
ārye śramaṇe, kiṃnibandhanā kiṃkāraṇikā maittrī sakhyam/ |
kailāsa iti |
kailāse rudrāvāse kailāsaparvate tulite satyutkṣipte sati |
``tula unmāne'' saṃjñāpūrvakavidheranityatvādguṇābhāvaḥ |
yadvā ``atulopamābhyām'' iti tulāśabdanipātanādguṇābhāvaḥ |
tulā ca tolanamupamā ca |
tatra tolavadvācinastulāvacchabdāt ``tatkaroti'' ityarthe ṇici karmaṇi kte ca tulite tulāvati kṛte tolanavati kṛte utkṣipte satīti paryavasyati |
athavā ``anityaṇyantāścurādayaḥ'' iti ṇijabhāve karmaṇi ktaḥ |
trayāṇāṃ bhuvanānāṃ samāhārastribhuvanam |
pātrāditvātstrītvābhāvaḥ |
pātālabhūsvargeṣvityarthaḥ |
jite sati dṛpyantaṃ darpayuktaṃ doryuddhāya bāhuyuddhāyābhyudyataṃ prayatamānaṃ daśāsyaṃ rāvaṇamindratanayo vālī kakṣāntare bāhumūlavivare |
``kakṣo gulme tṛṇe bāhumūle strī tūttarakrame'' iti ratnamālā |
prakṣipya niveśya saptasu nadīnātheṣu samudreṣu sāṃdhyaṃ saṃdhyābhavam |
saṃdhivelāditvādaṇ |
karmārghyadānādikaṃ samāpya nirvartyātho anantaram |
``maṅgalānantarārambhapraścakārtsnyeṣvatho atha'' ityamaraḥ |
muktavānvisṛṣṭavān |
indratanaya ityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
indratanayo vālī unmuktāya svena visṛṣṭāya natāya namrāya nāthitavate yācitavate |
``nāthṛ yācane'' ityādirdhātupāṭhaḥ |
tasmai daśāsyāya sakhyaṃ sakhitvam |
``sakhyuryaḥ'' iti phalitam||37||

 durātmanniti |
paulastyakulapāṃsana paulastyakuladūṣaṇa kṣatriyaparitāpinaste'naraṇyamaruttādikṣatriyasaṃtāpakāriṇastava vīryasyotkarṣo'tiśaya eṣa vālinyasatkalpaḥ |
jāta iti śeṣaḥ/ |
evamuktaprakārerṇettarādatiśayitāduttaro'tiśayito vīrabhāvo vīratvaṃ yasya sa tathokto vīraloko vīrasamūhaścitrīyate |
vismayaṃ janayatītyarthaḥ |
``namovarivaścitraṅaḥ kyac'' iti karaṇārthe kyac/ |
ārye iti |
śubhro dhavalaḥ/ |
nāyamiti |
ayaṃ śubhraḥ padārtho na giriḥ kiṃ tu vīrasya vālino yaśorāśiriva yaśorāśiriti saṃbhāvanīya eṣa śubhraḥ padārtho dundubhidaityendramahiṣasya dundubhināmno'surasya mahiṣākṛtitvānmahiṣasya vālinā hatasyāsthisaṃcayaḥ |
asthisamūha ityarthaḥ |
atrāpahnutirutprakṣā cālaṃkāraḥ||38||

 anenāsthisaṃcayenoparuddhāni niruddhāni vartmāni mārgāḥ |
tattasmānmārgoparodhātparihṛtya varjayitvā |
imamiti śeṣaḥ |
nanvityāmantraṇe |
kṣipati nirasyati/ |
yaditi |
saṃkrandananandana indrātmajaḥ kapivṛṣā kapīndraḥ |
``vāsavo vṛtrahā vṛṣā'' ityamaraḥ |
devadviṣo'surasya dundubheryadasthi karma |
doḥstambhayoḥ stambhasadṛśayorbāhvorvyāpāreṇa prayatnapūrvikayā kriyayā girivannirmathya giriṇā tulyaṃ pratihatya |
``mantha viloḍane'' |
nirāsthannirastavān |
``asu kṣepaṇe'' puṣāditvādaṅ |
``asyatesthuk'' iti thugāgamaḥ |
ayaṃ rājaputro rāmo nabho rundhatāvṛtya tiṣṭhadakāle varṣākāle śaradādau pāṇḍureṇa dhavalena ghanena meghena praspardhi saṃgharṣayuktaṃ tadadhikam |
dhavalonnataviśālamityarthaḥ |
tatkaṅkālaṃ śarīrāsthi |
karma |
``syāccharīrāsthi kaṅkālam'' ityamaraḥ |
pādasya |
na tu bāhustambhayorityarthaḥ |
aṅguṣṭhasya |
na tu pādasyetyarthaḥ |
vivartanādyādṛcchikaspandanāt |
na tu prayatnapuraḥsaravyāpārādityarthaḥ |
ito'smāddeśānnirvindhyaṃ vindhyānniṣkrāntaṃ yathā tathā |
``nirādayaḥ krāntādyarthe pañcamyā'' iti samāsaḥ |
yadvā vindhyasyābhāvo nirvindhyam |
arthābhāve'vyayībhāvaḥ |
``tṛtīyāsaptamyorbahulam'' iti saptamyā ambhāvaḥ |
vindhyābhāvadeśa ityarthaḥ |
na tu vindhyāntareveti bhāvaḥ |
āvidhyati nirasyati |
``vyadha tāḍane'' iti daivādiko'yamāṅpūrvakaḥ |
``grahijyā-'' ityādinā saṃprasāraṇam |
``āviddhau kuṭilakṣiptau'' iti ratnamālā |
ato vālinaḥ śataguṇabalo rāma ityāścaryātiśaya ityarthaḥ||39||

 praśānteti |
praśāntā gambhīrākṣobhyā nīlā vipulā ca śrīḥ śobhā yasyāḥ sā tathoktā araṇyagiryoḥ saṃbandhinī bhūmiḥ prasajyate prasaktā bhavati saṃnihitā bhavati |
prapūrvako'yaṃ sajjatiḥ samīpagamanārthaḥ |
ataḥ karmaṇi yagātmanepade |
asmābhiriti śeṣaḥ/ |
ṛṣyamūkapampayoḥ paryantabhūmayaḥ samīpadeśāḥ |
agrataḥ purato mataṅgāśramapadaṃ mataṅgāśramasthānam |
yatreti |
ciraśūnye'pi bahoḥ kālātpraṇibhiḥ śūnye'pi yatrāśramapade saṃnihitāḥ somacamasāḥ somabhakṣaṇapātraviśeṣā ādayo yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni vividhāni grahadhruvopabhṛjjuhūbhedena nānāvidhāni pāāṇyeva parikarā upakaraṇāni yasya sa tathokta āstīrṇabarhiḥ paristaraṇīkṛtasaṃskṛtakuśaḥ |
idhmavānsamidvān |
``idhmamedhaḥ samitstriyām'' ityamaraḥ |
ājyasya gandho leśo yasminsa tathoktaḥ |
``gandho gandhaka āmode leśe saṃbandhagarvayoḥ'' iti viśvaḥ |
gandhaśabdāntasya bahuvrīheḥ ``alpākhyāyām'' ityanena samāsānta ipratyayaḥ |
bhagavānpūjyo vaiśvānaro'gniradyāpi samidhyate dīpto bhavati |
antarbhāvitaṇyarthāt ``ñiindhī dīptau'' ityasmātkarmakartari yagātmanepade/ |
acintanīyārthā atarkyaprayojanāstapasāṃ viśeṣā bhedā atiśayā vā/ |
iheti |
samadairharṣayuktaiḥ |
``gajadānaṃ garvaharṣaretogṛgamadā madāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
śakuntaiḥ pakṣibhirākrāntebhya ārūḍhebhyo vānīrebhyo vañjalebhyaḥ |
``atha vetase |
rathābhrapuṣpavidulaśītavānīravañjulāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
surabhīṇi sugandhīni |
``surabhistu gavistriyām |
vasantacaiyoḥ puṃsi triṣu saumyasugandhinoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
śītāni svacchānyatyantanirmalāni toyāni yāsāṃ tāstathoktāḥ |
phalabharapariṇāmena hetunā śyāmeṣu jambūnikuñjeṣu skhalanena saṃghaṭṭanena murakhāṇi saśabdāni bhūrīṇi baiūni sratāṃsi pravāhā yāsāṃ tāstathoktāḥ |
``sroto'mbunirgamadvāre pravāhendriyayorjale'' iti ratnamālā |
nirjhariṇyo nadyaḥ |
``kūlaṃkaṣā nirjhariṇī'' ityamaraḥ |
iha vahanti pravahanti||40||

 dadhatīti |
āsminpradeśe'nurasitena saṃvṛtadeśavatkāṃsyadhvanivadanuraṇanena |
``rasitaṃ stanite dhvāne'' iti ratnamālā |
gurūṇi pracurāṇi kuharabhājāṃ guhāvartinām |
``kuharaṃ gahvare chidre'' iti ratnamālā |
bhallūkayūnāṃ taruṇānāmṛkṣāṇām |
``ṛkṣācchabhallabhallūkāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
ambūkṛtāni saniṣṭhīvanaśabdāḥ styānaṃ saṃghātabhāvaṃ dadhati dhārayanti |
bahirniḥsaraṇavilambāditi bhāvaḥ |
śiśirakaṭukaṣāyaḥ |
khañjakubjādivatsamāsaḥ |
sallakīnāṃ gajabhakṣyavṛkṣaviśeṣāṇām |
``-gajabhakṣyā tu suvahā surabhī rasā |
maheruṇā kundarukī sallakī hlādanīti ca||'' ityamaraḥ |
ibhairdalitānāṃ bhagnānāṃ viśīrṇānāṃ vāyunā viśakalitānāṃ granthīnāṃ parvaṇām |
``granthiḥ parvagadāntare'' iti ratnamālā |
niṣyandasya svarasadravyasya gandhaḥ styāyate saṃghātabhāvaṃ bhajati |
mūrtadravyayogyāyāḥ saṃghātabhāvabhajanakriyāyā amūrtena guṇena gandhena karaṇavarṇanāt ``kartari karmavyatihāre'' ityātmanepadam |
anyayogyakriyāyā anyena karaṇaṃ hi karmavyatīhāraḥ |
atiśayoktiralaṃkāraḥ||41||

 kimityādi |
paurastyamārutena purovātena |
``dakṣiṇāpaścātpurasastyak''iti puraḥśabdāttyakpratyayaḥ |
vitanyamānāni vikāsitāni kadambāni kadambapuṣpāṇi yeṣu tāni tathoktāni |
kadambaśabdāt ``puṣpamūleṣu bahulam'' iti vikārapratyayasya luk |
saṃgalitaṃ samyaggalitaṃ bāṣpapaṭalamaśrusamūho yasyāḥ sā tathoktā tathā parikṣipya samantato dṛṣṭvā dhanuravaṣṭambhena dhanuravalambanena dhīraṃ dhairyayuktaṃ yathā tathā dhāritaṃ śarīraṃ yeneti vigrahaḥ |
kiṃ sthīyate kimarthaṃ nirvyāpāreṇa bhūyate/ |
sthitamiti |
upanataṃ prāptaṃ jṛmbhārāmbhasya vikāsaprādurbhāvasya |
``jṛmbhastu jṛmbhaṇe |
triṣu nā tu vikāde syāt'' iti ratnamālā |
bimbaṃ cihnaṃ yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni taiḥ |
``pratibimbe tatprakṛtau pratikṛtyāṃ ca maṇḍale |
lāñchane'pi ca bimbo'strī'' iti ratnamālā |
kadambaiḥ kadambavṛkṣaiḥ kartṛbhiḥ sthitaṃ tasthe |
bhāve ktaḥ |
atiśayitaḥ kalo'vyaktamadhuradhvaniryeṣu tathoktāḥ kaṇṭhā yeṣāṃ tathoktairnīlakaṇṭhairmayūraistāṇḍavaṃ nāṭyaṃ kṛtam |
yadvātikalakaṇṭhaiḥ prakṛṣṭakokilayuktairnīlakaṇṭhairmayūraiḥ |
kokilagānānusāribhirmayūrairnṛtyata ityarthaḥ |
``prakarṣe laṅghane'pyati'' ityamaraḥ |
vighaṭamānāni vikasanti prauḍhāni bṛhanti tāpiñchāni tamālakusumānīva nīlaḥ |
``upamānāni sāmānyavacanaiḥ'' iti samāsaḥ |
``kālaskandhastamālaḥ spā
ṛttāpiñcho'pi'' ityamaraḥ |
adreḥ śikharaṃ śrayati bhajati |
ato varṣāgamo vihvalayatīti bhāvaḥ |
atra kadambakānanādīnyuddīpakāni virahātmako vipralambhaśṛṅgāraḥ |
taduktam- ``saṃbhogo vipralambhaśca śṛṅgāro dvividho mataḥ |
saṃyuktayostu saṃyogo vipralambho viyuktayoḥ||'' iti |
malayānilacandrādyāstaṭasthāḥ parikīrtitāḥ'' iti||42||

 tadetadavalokya lakṣmaṇasyānusaṃdhānamucyate- api nāmetyādi |
rasāntareṇa prakṛtādvipralambhaśṛṅgārarasādanyena vīrarasādināpi vikṣipyate vyāsaktaḥ kriyate |
vikṣepo hi manasā'nyathāpravṛttiḥ/ |
tvanniyogāditi |
tvanniyogāttvadājñayā sādhornyāyādanapetasya rāmasya |
kuta ityāha- nanu pūjyo'sīti |
tadapi kuta ityatrāha- mittrasya gururiti |
yo mittrasya gururmātāmahaḥ sa gurureva |
mamāpīti śeṣaḥ/ |
idaṃ vālino vākyam||43||

 bi
ṛbhrāṇa iti |
maghonendreṇa dattaṃ cārucāmīkarakamalamayaṃ hemapadmamayam |
``cāmīkaraṃ jātarūpam'' ityamaraḥ |
``nityaṃ vṛddhaśarādibhyaḥ'' iti vikārārthe mayaṭ |
dāma mālām |
``na puṃsi dāma saṃdānam'' ityamaraḥ |
aṅgenopalakṣito'ta eva taḍitvānvidyutvānsaṃdhyayā saṃdhyārāgeṇa churito rūṣito mahānambuvāha iva sthitaḥ kanakadāmapiśaṅgāṅgayostaḍitsaṃdhyā rāgayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvenaikībhāvaḥ |
kiṃ cotpātenāgnyutpātenāviddhamūrterāvṛtaśarīrasya |
``āviddhau kuṭilakṣiptau'' iti ratnamālā |
gairikāṅkasya dhātuviśeṣabhūṣitasya |
``svarṇe dhātau ca gairikam'', aṅko nāṭakavicchede saṃkhyāyāṃ yudhi bhūṣaṇe'' iti ca ratnamālā |
girerlaṣmīṃ śriyamupari dadhadvahan |
atrāpyutpātāviddhamūrtitvapiṅgāṅgatvayoḥ kanakadāmagaurikayośca bimbapratibimbabhāvaḥ |
gaganacaratvātprathamadṛṣṭāntaḥ, sthiratvādinā dvitīyadṛṣṭāntaḥ |
evaṃbhūta indrasūnurvālī javādvegādviyati nabhasyantarmadhye sīmantarekha keśapāśarekhām |
``rekhā tu śreṇirekhayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
tanotīva karotīva |
atropamānidarśanotprekṣāṇāṃ saṃkaraḥ||44||

 diṣṭyetyānande |
sa prasiddho vīrāṇāṃ goṣṭhyāḥ sabhāyāḥ |
``sabhāsaṃlāpayorgoṣṭhī'' iti ratnamālā |
vinode rasotpādakaḥ saṅgrāmastasya pradānena priyasuhṛdiṣṭaḥ sakhā māghavato maghavata indrasya putraḥ |
``tasyāpatyam'' ityaṇ |
vālī prāptaḥ |
āgata ityarthaḥ/ |
lokāloketyādi |
ahaṃ brahmastambaṃ gulmasadṛśaṃ brahmāṇḍaṃ karma |
``stambo gulmo'pyaśākhe'drau'' iti ratnamālā |
lokāloka eva cakravālaparvata eva |
``lokālokaścakravālaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
ālavālaṃ jalādhāraviśeṣaḥ |
``syādālavālamāvālamāvāpaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
tasya skhalanena saṃcalanena paripatataḥ saptamāmbhodheḥ pūrāḥ pravāhā yasminkarmaṇi tadyathā bhavati tathā |
kiṃ ca viśliṣyanti śithilībhavanti parvāṇi granthayo yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktam |
kiṃcākhilānyutkhātānyutpāṭitāni pātālasya mūlānyeva mūlāni yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktam |
kiṃcāvapatantyadhaḥ patanti bhūrīṇi bahūni |
``bhūri svarṇe bahau triṣu'' iti ratnamālā |
tārā eva nakṣatrāṇyeva prasūtāni puṣpāṇi yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktam |
``tārā syātsugrīvaguruyoṣitoḥ |
kanīnikarkṣayoḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
dhunīyāṃ kampayituṃ śaknomi |
``śaki liṅ'' ``dhūñ kampane'' kryādiḥ
ṛ ``kuḍikampyornalopaśca'' iti sūtreṇāvagartaṃ kampipadapravṛttinimittaṃ kampayitṛtvam |
mama tu brahmāṇḍamaviṣayaṃ bhavediti bhāvaḥ |
kiṃ tviha vidhau mālyavanniyukte kārye rāmadroharūpe tīvraḥ krūro viṣādaḥ khedo mamāsti |
rāmasya nyāyavṛttatvāditi bhāvaḥ |
atra brahmāṇḍe gulmatvādhyavasāyādrūpakātiśayoktiḥ |
tadaṅgāni tu lokālokālavāladīni rūpakāṇīti saṃkaraḥ||45||

 evamiti |
ayuktamanurudhyamānā anyāyyaṃ kāryaṃ kāmayamānāḥ |
ayuktagahvare'nyāyyakārya eva gahvare devakhātabile nipātyante |
karmakartari yagātmanepade |
svayameva patantītyarthaḥ |
yadyasmātpaulastyamaittrīpratiśravaṃ rāvaṇāya sakhyapratijñātam |
``aṅgīkārābhyupagamapratiśravasamādhayaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
anusmārya smārayitvā raghūdvahasya rāmasya nidhane vadhe |
aho graho vismayanīyo mālyavataḥ svakāryanirbandhaḥ |
``nirbandhoparāgārkādayo grahāḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
prātarārabhya prātaḥkālādārabhya māmanubadhnannanunayan |
kiṣkindhāyā ityapādāne pañcamī |
daurātmyāditi |
nijena svīyenāvināśinā vā |
``nijau svīyāvināśinau'' iti ratnamālā |
ārjavenākuṭilatvena śucau śuddhe'nupahate vā |
``śuśirnā śukraśṛṅgāracandrāṣāḍhaharāgniṣu |
upadhāśuddhasacive grīṣme śvetaguṇe ravau/ |
ayaṃ tu śvetaguṇini śuddhe'nupahate triṣu||'' iti ratnamālā |
māyāvibhirvañcanāpracurairaribhiḥ kartṛbhiḥ |
daurātmyātsahajaśāṭyalakṣaṇaduḥsvabhāvādvañcite dharmātmani dharmasvarūpe |
``rāmo vigrahavāndharmaḥ'' ityuktam |
atithau |
atithilakṣaṇamāpastambenoktam- ``agniriva jvalannatiyi[thi]rabhyāgacchati dharmeṇa vedānām'' ityārabhya ``dharmapuraskāro nānyaprayojano yaḥ so'tithirbhavati tasya pūjāyāṃ śāntiḥ svargaśca'' iti |
nirāgasi nirdoṣe niraparādhe vā |
``ago santau ca kilbiṣe'' iti ratnamālā |
jagatāṃ pūjye'vaśyapūjanīye parvoktātithipūjanasyoddeśyabhūte |
tathā ca smṛtiḥ- ``godaśahakālaṃ kāṅkṣeta gṛhadvāri samāgataḥ |
tithirviṣṇumuddiśya pūjanīyo viśeṣataḥ||'' iti |
gṛhānāgate'smābhirgantavye'pi svayameva gṛhaṃ prāpte |
yadyapi ``atithirnā gṛhāgate'' ityamaroktyā gṛhāgatatvamapyatithiśabdārthāntargataṃ tathāpi śrotriyatvādyatithilakṣaṇānākrānto'pi gṛhaṃ gata eva pūjanīya iti bodhayituṃ pṛthaguktiḥ |
etasminrāme ucitaṃ pūrvoktārjavādiguṇānurūpamarhaṇaṃ na vihitaṃ nāma na kṛtaṃ nāma |
nāmeti kutsāyām |
asminnucitavidhānābhāvaḥ kutsitaḥ ityarthaḥ |
ucitamityanena ``tasyābhimukho'bhyāgamya tasyāsanamāhārayettasya pādau prakṣālayettasyodakamāhārayetsāṃtvayitvā tarpayet'' ityāpastamboktaṃ vivakṣitam |
vācāpi nāveditam |
ucitakaraṇābhāve'pi vāṅmātreṇa nāveditamucitamityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
mukhyātithikalpānanuṣṭhāne'pyanukalpātithikalpo'pi na kalpita iti bhāvaḥ |
tadāha gautamaḥ- ``viparīte tṛṇodakabhūmisvāgatam'' iti |
pratyutocitakaraṇādiviparītaṃ dāruṇaṃ pāpakṛtāmapi bhayaṃkaraṃ pāpamaniṣṭācaraṇam |
vadha iti yāvat |
vyavasitaṃ kartavyatayā niścitam |
mameti śeṣaḥ |
etādṛśaṃ pūrvoktarītyā sarvadoṣāspadaṃ sakhyaṃ rāvaṇasakhyaṃ kim |
garhitamityarthaḥ |
kiṃśabdaḥ kṣepe||46||

 avaśyaṃ ceti |
mayā śrayasvināvaśyaṃ bhavitavyamityanvayaḥ |
yaśaḥsaṃtāna iti |
viśadaśaradindudyutinibhaprakāśo nirmalacandrikāsadṛśakāntiḥ |
yaśasaḥ śvaityaśaityādivarṇanaṃ samayasiddham |
viśvasminnantarviśvāntaḥ |
śauṇḍāditvādantaḥśabdena saptamyāḥ samāsaḥ |
visṛmaro vikasitaḥ |
``vikāsī tu vikasvaraḥ |
vikṛtvaro visṛmaraḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
ajayyo jetumaśakyaḥ |
``kṣayyajayyau śakyārthe'' iti nipātitaḥ'' indraḥ |
me pitetyarthaḥ |
tataḥ kīrtijanmavaśāddhetorabhirucitam |
iṣṭaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ |
``rucyarthānām'' iti caturthī||47||

 anatikramaṇīyamiti |
ubhayataḥpāśayobhayato'pi bandhanayantrayuktayā ubhayato'pi śastrayuktayāpi |
``pāśo bandhanaśastrayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
aho iti |
mahimā guṇotkarṣaḥ, anubhāvaḥ prabhāvaḥ, śaktayaḥ sāmarthyāni, aprākṛtaṃ prākṛtavijātīyaṃ paurāṇikeṣu purāṇe bhaveṣu |
``tatra bhavaḥ'' ityaṇ |
prasaṅgaprakaraṇeṣvanyaparoktiprastāveṣu |
etenāpakṣapātipramāṇapratipannatvaṃ vivakṣitam |
prakhyātaṃ cetyarthaḥ |
api ceti |
samuccayenācintyādbhutatvādeḥ saṃgrahaḥ |
satyametasyaiveti |
tejoleśasya vilāsarūpāṇi |
asya cottaratrānyaḥ |
atyugreti |
atyugrāṇyudagrāṇyadhikāni valgaccalat |
``valga calane'' |
dhagadhagitamiti jvālātiśayānukaraṇaṃ yeṣāṃ tāni tathoktāni jagaddāhe raudrādhikāreṇa bhayaṃkareṇa vyāpāreṇa krīḍanti kalpāntakālajvalanasya vilasantyuddāmānyucchṛṅkhalāni caṇḍāyitāni krūrācaraṇāni |
atyugretyādīnāṃ karmadhārayaḥ |
saṃvartārambhaḥ pralayārambhaḥ prāgbhāraḥ samūhaścaṇḍāyitānyujjṛmbhitāni ca vilāsarūpāṇīti saṃbandhaḥ||48||

 paradevatāpratipattiḥ paradevatātvapramitiḥ |
etheti |
ayaṃ rāmo dhyiātaḥ sansaṃbhāvito niścitaḥ paraḥsahasrāṇāṃ sahasrebhyaḥ pareṣāṃ brahmāṇḍānāṃ pralayaḥ saṃkṣayaḥ punarudbodhaḥ punaḥ sargaśca tāveva vibhavo'tiśayo yasya sa tathoktastathāvidho yathā yasmātsphurati prakāśate tathā tasmāddhetornārāyaṇaḥ |
``āpo nārāyaṇaḥ'' ityuktarītyānanyaparanārāyaṇaśabdābhidheyo bhagavāñśriyaḥ patirjagadavanahetorlokarakṣaṇātmakaprayojanāya sākṣādavataraṃ mukhyāvatāram |
``avatāraścāvataraḥ'' iti dvirūpakośaḥ |
gataḥ sanprāptaḥ sanbhuvi jayatīti manye |
nūnamiti niścaye |
vyayapūrvakatvātsṛṣṭeḥ pralayaḥ pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭaḥ |
``prasuptamiva sarvataḥ'' ityetadapekṣayā sargasyodbodhatvoktiḥ||49||

 dhigiti |
vinipāta ātmahāniḥ |
abhiniveśa utkaṭābhilāṣaḥ |
yato yenābhiniveśenādhyavasitaṃ niścitam |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
parityajyatāmavaśyaṃ tyājyaḥ |
mayeti śeṣaḥ |
sauhārdaṃ rāvaṇasakhyam |
``hṛdbhaga-'' ityādinobhayapadavṛddhiḥ |
``sauhārdaṃ sauhṛdam'' iti dvirūpakośaḥ |
pratyavāyo doṣaḥ |
adharma iti |
parameśvare rāme ādṛte satyadharmo mittradrohādirdharmatāṃ niḥśreyasasādhanatāmeti prāpnoti |
taṃ parameśvaraṃ rāmamanādṛtya bhāvitastadbhaktivirodhenānuṣṭhito dharmo maittrī pratiśravādiradharma eva syādātmāntanarakaheturbhavet||50||

 mahāvīrāṇāmiti |
samakṣaṃ saṃnidhau |
pratiśrutasaṅgrāmasya pratijñātayuddhasya |
``aṅgīkṛtamāśrutaṃ pratijñātam'' ityamaraḥ |
mahātmāno mahābuddhayaḥ |
viśvavyāpakaṃ trailokyavisṛmaram |
lupyate chinnaṃ bhavet |
karmakartari yagātmanepade |
saṃbhāvanāyāṃ liṅ |
apayaśo yaśoviparītaṃ doṣaprakhyānaṃ pracuraṃ bahulam |
``prabhūtaṃ pracuraṃ prājyamadabhraṃ bahulaṃ bahu'' ityamaraḥ |
atrāsminvirodhe samādhānaṃ kāryadvayavirodhaśamanam |
yuddhyate yuddhaṃ karotīti |
prasiddhiḥ prakhyātiḥ |
tathaiveti |
asādhyaṃ rāmeṇāśakyam |
pratyāyanaṃ samānabalatvapratyayotpādakam |
asādhyaṃ kiṃcidasti nāma |
aśakyaṃ kiṃcidastīti saṃbhāvanā vismayanīyetyarthaḥ |
``nāma prākāśyasaṃbhāvya-'' ityādyamaraḥ |
āviddhaḥ kṣiptaḥ |
sāvajñamatyalpatvādavamānayuktam |
kiṃcitpratyāyanaṃ pratyayakaraṃ svāvamānahetubhūte |
tanmātrārhatvasaṃbhāvanādvārā svaparibhavakare samīhatamabhilaṣitametaḥdṛśavacanamavamānahetupratyāyanaprārthanāvākyam |
pratyavāyasaṃvalinatena doṣayuktena |
saṅgrāmavivṛttau yuddhanivṛttiṃ prati |
ṛjurakṛṭilaḥ |
eṣa vakṣyamāṇaḥ |
jagatpūjya ityādi bahumānasūcakavakyapūrvakam |
sakhyaṃ maittrī |
yathā tathā vā guṇa iti vā doṣa iti vā |
protsāhanaṃ rāmeṇa saha yuddhakartavyatāvyavasāyajananam |
``utsāho vyavasāyaḥ syāt'' ityamaraḥ |
karmayoḥ panthāḥ karmapathaḥ |
``ṛkpūrabdhūḥ-'' ityādinā samāsāntaḥ |
rāvaṇasauhṛdaparityāginaṃ rāvaṇasakhyatyāginam |
ayaṃ vālī |
tasya rāmasya |
ajahikāmuṣmike etaddehānubhāvyaṃdehāntarānubhāvyaṃ ca śreyastayoḥ prasaṅgaḥ prastāvaḥ |
garīyo gurutamam |
triṣvekasya nirdhāraṇādīyasun |
na cetyādi |
sāvajñapratyāyanaprārthanayā rāmasya vaimukhyapakṣe rāvaṇakāryākaraṇānmittradrohiśaṅkārūpo'nāśvāsaprasaṅgaśca netyarthaḥ |
yata ityādi |
sarvāpekṣayā rāvaṇasakhyaparipālanādyapekṣayā |
satyapratijñatā pratijñāparipālanam |
hariścandrādibhiriti |
ādipadena daśarathādayaḥ parigṛhyate |
caṇḍāladāsyādikamityādipadena sarvāgṛhītarājyamapi vāsanāvaśādikaṃ
(
ṛ) gṛhyate |
na cedamiti |
idaṃ sāvajñapratyāyanaprārthanam |
prakopahetuḥ prakṛkopahetuḥ |
tasya rāmasya |
mahātmanāṃ kṣamālakṣaṇasvabhāvena mahatām |
mādṛśānāṃ tiraścām |
tenaiva vyājena sāvajñapratyāyanaprārthanārūpacchadmanā |
niḥsaramiti |
niḥsāraṃ niṣphalaṃ nirayānubandhi narakāvahaṃ cedaṃ sauhṛdaṃ rāvaṇasakhyaṃ nirākuryāṃ tyajeyam |
nijapadānnijasthānādvraśyantvadhaḥ patantu naśyantu vā hatā bhavantu vā |
mandaḥ svata eva pratibhāśūnyastadupari mālyavatā vimohitamanā bhrāmitahṛdayo mandodarīvallabho rāvaṇaḥ |
mandodarītyādinā tasyāḥ saumaṅgalyabalametāvaditi vyajyate |
mativibhramānmahāvīreṇa rāmeṇa saha vairāyate vairaṃ karoti |
``śabdavaira-'' ityādinā karaṇe kyaṅ |
iha rāvaṇasyānucitarāmavairakaraṇe kiṃ kurmaḥ |
buddhimanto vayaṃ na rāmavairaṃ kurma ityarthaḥ||51||

 svalpāntarālaṃ svalpāvakāśam/ |
mahāvīralābheneti |
mahāvīrasya tava lābheneti bhāvaḥ/ |
etāvantaṃ kālamityatyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
saṃprahāraḥ yuddham |
pratyayo balasāmyaniścayaḥ |
``pratyayo'dhīnaśapathajñānaviśvāsahetuṣu'' ityamaraḥ/ |
vṛkṣāntariti |
praṇihitamanasā sāvadhānena manasābhogayukte paripūrṇatāyukte |
``abhogaḥ paripūrṇatā'' ityamaraḥ |
gāḍho dṛḍhaḥ prahārastāḍanaṃ yena sa tathoktaṃ niśitaṃ śaraṃ tīkṣṇabāṇaṃ muñca |
tadvakṣo bhidyate ceddattaprasaramapi kṛtaraktādivisarpaṇaṃ vā yadi bhavet |
``prasarastu visarpaṇam'' ityamaraḥ |
yadva pravyatheta prakampeta cettena bhedādināśaṅkāyā nyūnabalatvasaṃdehasya nirāse'pagame tadanvanantaraṃ tulyabalaḥ |
iṣṭo bhavedityarthaḥ||52||

 vīrabandho kutsitavīra |
brahmabandhukṣattrabandhuśabdavadvīrabandhuśabdo'pi prakṛtakutsāparaḥ |
tṛṇaprāyaṃ tṛṇasadṛśam |
mahāndroho'pakāraḥ |
udāsīnaṃ niryuddhavyāpāramasamarthamaśaktam |
apavādo doṣaprathā |
manoriti |
prathitayaśasaḥ prakhyātakulakramāgatakīrtayaḥ |
``kulakramāgatā sācedyaśa ityabhidhīyate'' ityukteḥ |
eteṣāṃ bhūmipatīnāṃ caritaṃ vṛttamapavāde doṣaprathāyāṃ na patati |
trayāṇāṃ lokānāṃ samāhārastrilokī tasyāṃ vistāriṇaḥ prabalasya parivādasyaikajanako mukhyajananahetustṛṇīkāro'nādaro mano vyathayati duḥkhayati||53||

 devatādrohaḥ paradevatāpakaraḥ |
aho ityādi |
visaṃvāda āvayoritaretarabuddhyoranaikarūpyam |
asminrāme parameśvaradṛṣṭyā paramātmapramityā |
asya tu rāmasya tu paramotkarṣabuddhireva na tu nikṛṣṭabuddhiriti visaṃvāda ityarthaḥ |
ato mayyutkarṣabuddhyā svajanapratipattyā bandhubuddhyā |
prekṣāvantaḥ prajñāvantaḥ kiṃ parīkṣante |
na parīkṣanta eva |
dahanaśakteratiprasiddhatvādityarthaḥ/ |
madvacanānmadvākyaṃ nivedya |
lyablope pañcamī |
madhyemārgaṃ mārgasya madhye |
``pāremadhye ṣaṣṭhyā vā'' iti samāsaḥ |
vilambaḥ samāgamakālātipātaḥ/ |
āsphālayaṃścālayan |
drāgiti |
pratibhaṭasya śatroḥ |
prāgalbhyasya dhṛṣṭatvasya |
nirbhedanetyādi |
``dhṛṣṭe dhṛṣṇurviyātaśca pragalbhaḥ'' ityamaraḥ||54||

 dṛpyaditi |
mama bhujastambhayorgambhīro'kṣobhyaḥ sāro balaṃ kartṛ sphūrjatā nirghoṣavatojjṛmbhividyutodagrataṭidyuktena dambholinā vajreṇa saha sphardhinībhiḥ saṃgharṣaṇībhirmadiṣubhirmadbāṇaiḥ |
``iṣurdvayoḥ'' ityukteḥ strītvam |
vālina āyudhabhūteṣu parvateṣu bhīmaṃ yathātathābhyāhateṣu tāḍiteṣu satsu |
teṣāṃ vālināyudhīkṛtānāṃ madbāṇahatānāṃ girīṇāṃ dhāṭībhirvegitagatibhirbhagnānāṃ bhūbhṛtāṃ bṛhadupalānām |
``upalāśmānaḥ śilā dṛṣat'' ityamaraḥ |
dṛḍhāghātena yo vindhyāṇḍabhaṅgo vindhyāṇḍayoḥ sphoṭastena sphāyato vardhamānasya |
anudāttettvanibandhanamātmanepadamanityam |
bhāṅkārasya heturbhavatu |
prāptakāle loṭ||55||

 pūrvabuddhyā mayi pūjyatvabuddhyā |
saṃbhāvyata iti saṃśayyata iti |
paryākulaṃ duḥkhitam |
sarvaṃ yuddhavaimukhyaṃ tadābhimukhyaṃ vā |
itiniṣkrāntāḥ sarve||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vārarāghavaḥ/
sa mahāvīracarite pañcamāṅkaṃ vyavīvarat||
ityaṇṇāpaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyākhyāyāṃ bhāvapradyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ pañcamo'ṅkaḥ||
p.191 l.2

ṣaṣṭho'ṅkaḥ/

idānīṃ kaviḥ punarapi vimarśasaṃdhiṃ ṣaṣṭhāṅkena vadati- tatrāvyāpinyā āgastyakathāyāḥ prakaryā vālivadhādapāyābhāvataḥ kāryaniścayarūpaniyatāpteśca varṇanādvimarśasaṃdhiḥ |
taduktam- ``garbhasaṃdhau prasiddhasya bījārthasyāvamarśanam |
hetunā yenakenāpi vimarśaḥ saṃdhiriṣyate/ |
niyatāptiprakaryukteraṅgānyasya trayodaśa||'' iti |
tatra viṣkambhakaṃ racayati- tataḥ praviśati saṃpātirnāma vānara ityādinā |
pūrvāṅkaviṣkambhe gṛdhrasya saṃpāteḥ praveśāttadvyāvṛttaye vānara iti/ |
ādiṣṭo'smyājñapto'smi |
gaveṣayitumanveṣayitum |
``anveṣitaṃ gaveṣitam'' ityamaraḥ |
bhrātṛyaśaso nāśasaṃbhāvanayā yuddhamupagamya pracchanna ityapakhyātyā |
pūrvārjitaṃ yaśo vinaśyediti jñānenetyarthaḥ |
bhrātaraṃ vālinamanviṣyanmṛgayamāṇaḥ paryaṭati paritaścarati |
``aṭa gatau'' etasya mukhena mukhodgatavacanena/ |
evamiti |
pūrvopadarśitāṅgadavākyaprakāreṇa/ |
evamiti |
upadarśitarāmavākyaprakāreṇa/ |
yāvannivedayāmi nivedayiṣyāmi |
``yāvatpurānipātayorlaṭ'' iti laṭ |
adhijyamadhyārūḍhamaurvīkam |
parivāraṃ parikaram |
``syājjaṅgame parīvāraḥ khaḍgakośe paricchade'' ityamaraḥ |
kiṣkindhāṃ saṃpreṣya prasthāpyeti dvikarmakam |
ekākī ekaḥ |
na vidyate pratīkṣā paścādavalokanaṃ yasminkarmaṇi tattathoktam |
idaṃ cāgamane'nveti/ |
vibhīṣaṇa iti |
vibhīṣaṇavinābhāvānnāyaṃ sugrīvaḥ |
anucaravirahādayaṃ vālītyarthaḥ |
vālitve'nucaravirahasya hetutvamāha- pūrvedyuriti |
parvedyuścaikākī sthita iti/ |
na hantavya ityasya saṃbhrameṇa dviruktiḥ/ |
viparyayo'nyonyatādātmyabhramaḥ/ |
ekata evaikasyāṃ diśyeva |
sarvanāmno vṛttimātre puṃvadbhāvaḥ |
vayaṃ vibhīṣaṇo'hamanye ca/ |
amoghasyāvandhyasya |
lakṣyaṃ śaravyam |
na prayoktavyaḥ |
bāṇa iti śeṣaḥ |
vṛkṣakhaṇḍe vṛkṣasamūhe |
niraparādhe mṛge kathaṃ prayoktavya ityatrāha-mṛgayeti |
``ākheṭo mṛgayā striyām'' ityamaraḥ |
dharma evācāra eva |
sāvadhānena vyāsaṅgāntararahitena |
mṛgajātiśabdena jātyāśrayavyaktaya ucyante |
palāyate'nyatrāpagacchati |
``aya gatau'' ityasminpare upasargasya parāśabdasya rephasya ``upasargasyāyatau'' iti lakāraḥ |
enaṃ mṛgam palāyanakartṛtvenoktasya hananakarmatvenopādānādanvādeśe enādeśaḥ |
``dūrādhvānaṃ vadhaṃ yuddham'' ityādinā vadhasya dṛśyatvapratiṣedhādāha- niṣkrānta iti |
atra vadhoktervidravaḥ/ |
hanteti |
vaiśasaṃ hiṃsādoṣaḥ |
mayeti |
adrirājapramāṇena parvatavaraparimāṇenopalakṣitaṃ yadvālirūpaṃ vāliśarīraṃ tasya dharo dhārayitā||1||

 munirveti |
muniḥ śāpānugrahasāmarthyahetumananaśīlo devaḥ śaktiviśeṣayuktaḥ, tasmādapyadhiko jambhāririndraḥ, tato'pyadhiko bhūtānāṃ vidhātā sraṣṭā kamalabhūrbhagavānnābhipadmajanmā brahmā, teṣāṃ pūrvoktamuniprabhṛticaturmukhaparyantānāṃ ye mahimāno māhātmyāni teṣāṃ ko vātiśayo dehānte bhavatā svayamanugṛhītasya mamāgre prabhavati sthātuṃ śaknuyāt |
brahmādibhyo'pyatiśayitaprabhāvo muktaḥ śāstreṣu śrūyata iti bhāvaḥ |
atra svaśaktipraśaṃsanarūpavyavasāya uktaḥ||2||

 ka iti |
vijñāto na bhavati na vijñātaḥ |
naśabdasya samāsānnalopo na |
kutūhalena saṃkrāntaṃ harṣakartṛkasaṃkramaṇādhikaraṇamantastattvaṃ manaḥ padārthaḥ |
prasīdati prasannaṃ bhavati||3||

 vasuṃdhareti |
ahamityādi |
he vasuṃdharādhīśa bhūmipate rāma, mahābalānāṃ vanavānarāṇāṃ vineti niyāmakaḥ |
vaneti grāmyaprākṛtavānarebhyo vyāvṛttiḥ |
vālyaham |
kathamidaṃ kuraṅgatvamityatrāha- madeti |
madena garveṇa yo'ndhabhāvaḥ kṛtyākṛtyavivekaśūnyatā tena yo mataṅgaśāpastena kuraṅgabhāvo hariṇatvaṃ mayābhipannaḥ prāptaḥ||4||

 pūrvoktameva viśadayati- purākiletyādinā |
maryādāṃ vyavasthām |
tadīyāśramasya mataṅgasaṃbandhina āśramasya |
parisare samīpe |
vikṣiptavānvisṛṣṭavān |
paryukṣitaṃ paritaḥ kiktam |
vīradarpeṇa śauryagarveṇa |
vīreti bhāvapradhāno nirdeśaḥ |
atikrameṇa maryādātivṛttilakṣaṇenāparādhena |
vīradharmeṇa saha yuddhe maraṇarūpavīrapuruṣācāreṇa saha viruddhaṃ virodhavat |
anujagrāhānugrahaṃ kṛtavān |
anugṛhītārthamāha- vedānteti |
yaṃ paramapuruṣaṃ vedāntajālānāṃ chāndogyataittirīyakādyupaniṣajjālānāṃ pariśīlanena brahmasūtrapadāntasthahetubhirupabṛṃhaṇaiśca tātparyanirṇayābhyāsena labdhavarṇo vicakṣaṇaḥ |
anena śravaṇamanena ukte |
ananyabhāvo'nanyahṛdayaḥ |
anyānullekhidhyānayukta ityarthaḥ |
anena nididhyāsanamuktam |
antarāyeṇa mokṣavighnena svargādyabhiprāyeṇa vikalo rahitaḥ |
``vighno'ntarāyaḥ pratyūhaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
antarāyaśabdanirdeśena ``vāsudeve mano yasya japahomārcanādibhiḥ |
tasyāntarāyo maitreya devendratvādikaṃ phalam||'' iti purāṇaratnavacanaṃ sphoritam |
veda paśyati |
``tatastu taṃ paśyate niṣkalaṃ dhyāyamānaḥ'' iti śrutiḥ |
durūhatamena tatprasādena vinā tarkitumaśakyena tena paramapuruṣeṇānyaduravāpaṃ devādīnāmapi durlabhaṃ dehāntaṃ dehanāśamavāpsyasītyanujagrāheti pūrveṇa saṃbandhaḥ |
atra prakaraṇe madāndheti doṣaprakhyāpanātmako'pavādaḥ |
durātmanniti roṣasaṃbhāṣaṇarūpaḥ saṃpheṭaḥ |
maryādāmanavekṣyeti gurutiraskṛtirūpo dravaḥ |
śaśāpeti vadhabandhanādirūpo vidravaḥ |
anunīta iti virodhaśamanarūpaśaktiḥ |
vedāntajāletyādinā gurukīrtanarūpaḥ prasaṅga iti vimarśasaṃdheraṅgānyuktāni||5||

 sa evāyaṃ śāpa iti |
palāyita iti yatsa evāyaṃ śāpa ityanvayaḥ |
sa evāyamanugraha iti |
yattaveti |
ajaśvaryaṃ prāptavānasmīti yatsa evāyamanugraha ityanvayaḥ |
upanataṃ prāptam |
śāśvataṃ padaṃ mokṣasthānam/ |
kṛtottarakriyamanuṣṭhitaurdhvadaihikakarmāṇam |
bhrātṛvyasanarśitasya bhrātṛmaraṇaduḥkhapīḍitasya |
samāśvāsanāya duḥkhalaghūkaraṇāya |
paricaryamāṇaḥ kriyamāṇaśuśrūṣaḥ |
caturdaśa varṣāṇīti padadvayam |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
māsacatuṣṭaye |
``pakṣā vai māsāḥ'' iti dvayoḥ siṃhakanyāmāsayoścatuṣṭayam |
yadvā tulāvṛścikamāsayorapyanatyantayātrārhatvādvārṣikatvam |
idamevābhipretya ``catvāro vārṣikā māsāḥ'' ityuktam/ |
tatkathālāpeti |
tasya suhṛdaḥ kathālāpa eva pūrvavṛttapratipādakapraśnaprativacananirdeśa eva vinodaḥ sītāviyogaduḥkhalaghūbhāvastatra vyagratayā ca vyāsaktatayā ca/ |
hataṃ tāvaditi |
plavagakulamukhye vālini hate sati niśicarapate rāvaṇasya pauruṣabalaṃ puruṣasaṃbandhi balam |
mittrabalamityarthaḥ |
hataṃ dhvaṃsitam |
tāvacchabdaḥ kramārthakaḥ |
pauruṣabalamāyābalayormadhye prathamamidaṃ puruṣabalaṃ hatamityarthaḥ |
kravyādā iti kathāpi śabdo'pi mama ruṣā naṣṭā naṣṭaprāyaiva |
bhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
kharādivadhātirekavivakṣayā kṛtsnamiti viśeṣaṇam |
atra kathāpi mama ruṣā naṣṭeti svaśaktipraśaṃsanarūpavyavasāyātmakaṃ saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||6||

 māyaiva māyāmātram |
māyā ca śāmbarīprabhṛtiḥ |
sā cāpūrvasṛṣṭirvañcanā ca |
tatpratīkārāya tatparihārāya |
atra kāryagrahaṇarūpamādānaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
samyagvibhāvya nipuṇaṃ cintayitvā/ |
yaditi |
mayā yadyātudhānavadhādikam |
ādiśabdādvālivadhasaṃgrahaḥ |
adhiṣṭhitaṃ kartavyatvenādhyavasitam||7||

 bhavato viditam |
viditameva khalvityarthaḥ |
asmadīyatapaḥprabhāvena pracoditabuddhinā preritabuddhinā |
saṃkalpenaiva manovyāpāreṇaiva |
tathā pratyāyanaṃ tathā te pratyayo bhavediti viśvāmitreṇa saṃdiṣṭamiti saṃbandhaḥ |
ayamiti |
ayaṃ rāmaḥ kāmapratimayā manmathatulyayā tanusaundaryagarimaśriyā vigrahasaubhāgyasamudāyasamṛddhyā jaḍadhiyāṃ janānāmapi netrānandaṃ janayati |
ayaṃ rāmo mamatvantaścetasā yogasāmagryā pratyakpravaṇena manasā cirātparicitasya samādhiviṣayīkṛtasya cidānandasya svaprakāśānandasya paramaśriyā niratiśayasamṛddhyā brahmānandamānandavallyām ``yato vāco nivartante'' ityādinopapāditaṃ vāṅmanasāgocaramātmānubhavānandaṃ prathayati vistārayati |
yogadaśānubhūtānandasamṛddhyā saha muktidaśābhāvisākṣātkārānandaṃ janayatītyarthaḥ||8||

 māyābalamiti |
mahābala rāma, māyāvinaṃ prati māyāvinā saha spardhā virodhaṃ manāgapi svalpamapi na cintayeccintayituṃ na yuktam |
arhe liṅ||9||

 ata iti |
divyaṃ viprakṛṣṭārthadarśanakṣamam/ |
kiṃ paśyasi |
tvayā dṛśyamāno laṅkāvṛttāntaḥ ka ityarthaḥ/ |
``rāvaṇaḥ'' ityārabhya ``nanu puruṣadharmo bahumataḥ'' ityanto laṅkāvṛttāntaḥ |
sarvaprakṛtibhiramātyasenāpālādibhiḥ |
mantrayate kartavyaviṣaye guptaṃ bhāṣate |
``matri guptabhāṣaṇe'' iti curādiḥ/ |
mātāmahetyasya saṃbhramāddviruktiḥ/ |
tvayeti |
tvayā yaśyatkṛtyaṃ kapaṭavidhinā vañcanopāyena prayuktamanuṣṭhitaṃ tattadakhilaṃ durdaivāddurbhāgyādviparītaṃ pariṇataṃ saṃvṛttam |
virādhavyāpārādiko rāmavadhāya prayukto virādhādivadhasyaiva heturabhūditi bhāvaḥ |
tava paramaṃ pauruṣabalaṃ tathāvidhabalabhūto yuddhāyābhiyātastvaritaṃ vinihataḥ||10||

 asmākamiti |
vānarāṇāṃ rājā vālī |
kṣatriyāntako rāmaḥ paraśurāmaḥ||11||

 ekavāramekasminnavasare |
``nivahāvasarau vārau'' ityamaraḥ |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
sakṛdityarthaḥ |
tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍo vañcanātmako vadhopāyaḥ |
daivāyattaṃ bhāgyādhīnam |
prakāśadaṇḍastu senayā prakaṭābhiyānena yuddharūpaḥ/ |
cārairdūtairāveditamāha- ``ṛṣyamūke'' ityādinā ``vatsyati'' ityantena |
viprayuktau viśliṣṭau |
paraspareti |
parasparasahāyayostayoḥ parasparavinābhāve viśleṣe sati cchadmadaṇḍasya prayogo'nuṣṭhānaṃ cetsaphalo bhavitā bhaviṣyati |
bhavaterluṭ||12||

 kīdṛśaḥ kathaṃbhūtaśchadmadaṇḍa ityanuṣaṅgaḥ/ |
chadmadaṇḍaprakāramāha- ``yadā tāvat'' ityādinā ``jetavyaḥ'' ityantena |
mohamāpatsyate mūrcchaā prāpsyati |
saṅgrāmaprayojanasya sītāvāptirūpasya nivṛttyā dhvaṃsanena |
saṃpatsyate bhaviṣyati |
svajanaviṣaye bandhuviṣaye |
asya ca vinipāte'nvayaḥ |
ākasmiketi |
vinipāta ākasmikopasaṃpanne'kāṇḍa eva prāpte sati |
aṅgāre'ṅghriṇā pādenākrānte vākrānta iva |
``vā syādvikalpopamayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
atidhīrasyāpi puruṣasya mano vikalaṃ dhairyaśūnyaṃ jāyate bhavati||13||

 vidyujjihvasya vidyujjihvākhyasya māyayā vañcanaśaktyā nirmitaṃ sṛṣṭaṃ nikṣeptavyaṃ nidhātavyam |
samāśvāsātprāgevetyanvayaḥ |
pratyakṣāparādhena cākṣuṣeṇa sītāśiroharaṇarūpāniṣṭācaraṇena |
pratyakṣetyanena sītāpahāravyāvṛttiḥ |
dviguṇitakopāvapahārakopādapyadhikakopau |
tadavasthadhaiyag yathāvasthitadhaiyag tayoḥ saṃnidhānaṃ sāmīpyam/ |
rākṣasabalaṃ rākṣasasenā |
``sainyāsthirūparetaḥśaktisthaulayeṣu cedbalaṃ klībe'' iti ratnamālā |
kumbhakarṇa iti |
supta iti śeṣaḥ/ |
kveti |
nilīnaḥ pracchannaḥ |
jāgrati prakaṭe sati |
asya cottaratrānvayaḥ |
mātṛgarbhamiti |
mahāmṛtyormṛtyorapi mṛtyormokṣo bahirnirgamanam||4[14]/ |
audāsīnyaṃ daṇḍānuṣṭhānaśūnyatām |
vīrāṇāṃ dharmeṇa śreyaḥsādhanena śatrukṛtaprahāreṇa |
mṛtyurmaraṇam |
atra ca daṇḍasāmānye ca |
tūṣṇīṃdaṇḍa iti |
tasya siddhau niṣpattau |
daṇḍagatatūṣṇīṃbhāve siddhe satītyarthaḥ |
parairajñāte satīti yāvat |
prakāśadaṇḍe yuddhe sati mṛtyuśca maraṇaṃ ca, yaśo vīradharmasthairyaprathā, śreyaśca vīralokaprāptirūpeṣṭārthasiddhiśca, śāśvatam |
niścitamityarthaḥ |
śāśvataśca śāśvataṃ ca śāśvaṃ ceti sahoktau ``napuṃsakamanapuṃsakenaikavaccāsyānyatarasyām'' ityekaśeṣaikavadbhāvau||15||

 audāsīnye tu daṇḍasāmānyaprayoge tu mṛtyuḥ kutsimaraṇaṃ aśreyo vīralokavirahaśca |
sītāsteyapāpasya vadhena niṣkṛtāvapi vīraloko na sidhyatīti bhāvaḥ |
yathāsmadabhihitaṃ maduktam |
daṇḍavadhaprayogamanatikramya |
padārthānativṛttāvavyayībhāvaḥ |
jaya iti |
niyatamanyataradavarjanīyam |
idaṃ daṇḍadvayānuṣṭhānaṃ me mataṃ mameṣṭam |
ripau śatrau viṣaya audāsīnyaṃ daṇḍānanuṣṭhānaṃ kartṛ samādhānaṃ saṃśayanivṛttiṃ karma manasi nāyate na prāpnoti |
anārambhe daṇḍānanuṣṭhānarūpaudāsīnye vipanmṛtirnityā niścitā |
itarathā daṇḍaprayoge tu vipadaniyatā saṃdigdhā |
tadānīṃ daṇḍaprayogasyāniyatavipatkatve puruṣadharma udyogo vīrapuruṣasvabhāvabhūto daṇḍaprayogaprayatno bahumato nanu ślāghya eva |
evaṃ laṅkāvṛttāntaḥ pratyakṣitaḥ||16||

 viśvāso yāthārthyajñānam |
vijayasvābhibhūyāḥ |
``viparābhyāṃ jeḥ'' ityātmanepadam |
ādityahṛdayamādityahṛdayākhyaṃ sūryastatram/ |
unmūlanaṃ mūlena saha dhvaṃsanam/ |
avinābhāvena niyatasaṃnikarṣeṇa |
sarvaṃ rākṣasamāyāceṣṭitam |
rākṣasī rakṣaḥsaṃbandhinī |
rākṣasajātivaśena rākṣasatvānurodhena |
``nanu rākṣasaceṣṭitaḥ'' iti rākṣasakṛtyasyaiva pratiṣedhāt |
saṃnaddhaṃ sajjanam |
``saṃnaddhau sajjavarmitau'' iti ratnamālā |
bhūlokamavatīrṇaṃ vānarātmanā bhūmiṃ prāptam |
dīvatānīkaṃ devasenācakram |
``cakraṃ cānīkamastriyām'' ityamaraḥ |
saṃdiśyatāṃ vācikena bodhyatām |
hanteti harṣe |
tvayedānīmityasya cottaratrānvayaḥ |
laṅketi |
tvayā laṅkādvīpagataṃ kṛtsnaṃ niśācarakulaṃ parābhūyate śīghrameva jeṣyate |
vartamānasāmīpye laṭ |
evamuttaratrāpi |
diṣṭyetyānande |
vinirdhūyate nāśayiṣyate |
diviṣadāmīśiturindriyasya manasyullāso harṣa uccairadhikamupādhīyate janayiṣyate |
upādhānamiha jananam |
samyakpālanaṃ vidhivadrakṣaṇaṃ śīlanaṃ svabhāvo yasya sa tathoktastena tvaya jagatāṃ saukhyaṃ samāsthīyate kartavyatvenādhiṣṭāsyate |
atra siddhavadbhāviśreyaḥkathanarūpaṃ prarocanaṃ nāma virmaśasaṃdhyaṅgamuktam||17||

 aṭṭahāsā mahānto hāsāḥ |
``aṭṭahāso mahānhāso mukharāgādikṛt'' ityabhidhānāt |
diśaḥ prācyādayaḥ, vidiśa āgneyyādayaḥ, tā vidārayiṣyanti bhetsyanti |
bibhyaditi |
ardhadvayoktaṃ viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ pūrvoktāṭṭahāsaviśeṣaṇam |
bibhyatīnāmataeva bhūparirambhiṇīnāṃ bhūmau patitānāṃ kauṇapavadhūnāṃ rākṣasīnāṃ nirbhinneṣu vidīrṇeṣu garbheṣūdarāntardeśeṣu sthitānāmarbhakāṇāṃ bālānāṃ bhraṃśenādhaḥpātenodbhāvitasyopapāditasya bhīmabhāvasya bhayaṃkaratvasya garimṇātiśayena nyagbhūto'dharībhūto dambholiraśaniśabdo yaiste tathoktāḥ |
yajñabhreṣakṛtāṃ yajñavighnakarāṇāṃ rakṣorbhakāṇām |
``bhreṣo bhraṃśo yathocitāt'' ityamaraḥ |
udbhava evotpattidaśāyāmeva pravilayaprārambheṇa maraṇārambheṇa saṃbhāvitena svarbhogapratilambhena svargasukhaprāptyā saṃbhṛtaḥ saṃpāditastamaḥsaṃbhāraḥ śokātiśayo yasya sa tathoktastathāvidho jambhāririndro yaiste tathoktāḥ |
``tamo rāhau guṇe dhvānte śoke'' iti ratnamālā |
yajñavigghakāriṇāmapi svargasukhaṃ jātamitīndrasya duḥkhamabhūditi bhāvaḥ |
evaṃvidhā aṭṭahāsādiśo vidiśaśca vidārayiṣyantīti saṃbandhaḥ||18||

 idaṃ ceti |
idaṃ ca te dhanurityanvayaḥ |
udbhūteti |
yadbhūtāyā utpannāyāḥ pratibhūpānāṃ pratipakṣiṇāṃ bhūmnaḥ saṅghasya vibhutāyāḥ prabhutvasya vibhraṃśakṛdbhāvanāṃ nāśakarīṃ bhāvanāṃ saṃskāramābhibhraddadhānaṃ bhavadīyāyā nirbharāyāḥ sāmarthyapracurāyā bhujāyā bāhoḥ saṃbhāreṇa dhāraṇasāmarthyātiśayena saṃbhāvitaṃ dhṛtam |
``dordoṣā ca bhujā bhujaḥ'' iti dvirūpakośaḥ |
udyatodgacchatā bhairavabhāvena bhayaṃkaratvena saha bhāvitayotpāditayā bhavadbhrūbhaṅgabhaṅgyā tvadīyabhrūbhedaprakāreṇa |
``bhaṅgī prakāre tu'' iti ratnamālā |
miladyuktam |
gambhīro ghana udbhaṭo dīrgho bhūri vardhamāno bhīmaraṇitasya bhayaṃkarasya ṭaṅkārasya prāgbhāraḥ samūho garbhe yasya tattathoktamidaṃ te dhanurniśācaraśavairantarālamalaṃkariṣyatīti saṃbandhaḥ||19||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vīrarābhavaḥ/

sa mahāvīracarite ṣaṭṭhamaṅkaṃ vyavīvarat||

ityaṇṇāppaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyākhyāyāṃ bhāvapradyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ ṣaṭṭho'ṅkaḥ||

-------------------

śÿ »ktasaptamo'ṅkaḥ/

idānīṃ kaviḥ saptamāṅkena nirvahaṇasaṃdhiṃ prastauti |
tatsvarūpaṃ ca ``bījavanto mukhādyarthā viprakīrṇā yathāyatham |
ajakārthyamupanīyante yatra nirvahaṇaṃ hi tat/ |
phalāptikāryānuguṇyādaṅgānyasya caturdaśa |
saṃdhirvirodho grathanaṃ nirṇayaḥ paribhāṣaṇam |
prasādānandasamayāḥ kṛtirbhāṣopagūhane |
pūrvabhāvopasaṃhārau praśastiśca caturdaśa||'' iti |
atra vasiṣṭhavyāpārarūpasya phalanirvāhakātmakasya kāryasya, sītayā saha rāmapaṭṭābhiṣekātmakaphalāgamasya cārthaprakṛtervarṇanānnirvahaṇasaṃdhiḥ |
aṅgasvarūpanirūpaṇaṃ tu tattadavasareṣu vakṣyate |
tatra viṣkambhakamāha- tataḥ praviśatītyādinā |
ākāśayānena |
vimānenetyarthaḥ |
gaganagamanena vā |
manoratho'bhilāṣaḥ paripūrṇaḥ |
rāvaṇavadhādirūpaviśiṣṭasīmā jāta ityarthaḥ |
hatveti |
hanteti harṣe |
sānvayaṃ savaṃśam |
straiṇaṃ hitvā strīsamūhaṃ tyaktvā |
hananakriyāyāṃ karmatayā parihṛtyetyarthaḥ |
tarasā vegena |
tatastadanantaraṃ jātavedasi vahnau tattvākhyāyini sati sītāpariśuddhilakṣaṇasatyārthavādini sati tato'gneḥ sakāśātpriyāṃ sītāmādita svīkṛtavān |
āṅpūrvāddadāterluṅi sico lopaḥ||1||

 havyavāhena vahninā |
prasādo'nugrahaḥ |
ātmānaṃ svam |
vahniparāmarśyayamātmaśabdaḥ |
tadasyāṃ saivāvasthā yasyāḥ sā tathoktā tāṃ yathāpūrvaṃ sthitām/ |
jagatāmiti |
candra iva rāmo rāmacandraḥ |
pratikūle'niṣṭakare kaḥ pravartate |
ko'pi na pravartata evetyarthaḥ |
anukūle vā ko na pravartate |
sarvo'pi pravartata evetyarthaḥ |
atra kāryamārgaṇātmako virodho nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
tadīyapauruṣāvaṣṭambhena rāmaparākramāśrayeṇa |
itthamiti |
pauruṣātiśayaṃ bālādyatiśayam |
etāvadeva vakṣyamāṇamiyadeva |
araṇyavāsādvanavāsaṃ prāpya |
lyablope pañcamī |
kṣemeṇa sītālakṣmaṇātmakalabdhāṃśarakṣaṇena saha |
anena rāmeṇa |
dharma ekaḥ kevalaḥ pradhānamuddeśyaṃ yasya sa tathoktastathāvidhena |
ekaśabdo rājyamukhādikaṃ vyavacchinatti |
asya cottaratrānvayaḥ |
pureti |
tatkaikeyyāstādṛśyāḥ kaikeyyā vacanaparipāṭyā vākyānukrameṇa |
``paripāṭī anukramaḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
paravatā tadadhīnena |
puraṃdhryā kuṭumbinyā sītayā sahāñcati gacchatīti puraṃdhrīsadhryaṅ tena |
``syāttu kuṭumbinī puraṃdhrī'' ityamaraḥ |
``sahasya sadhriḥ'' iti sadhryādeśaḥ |
sītāsahāyenetyarthaḥ |
anena rāmeṇeto'yodhyāto gahanapadaṃ vanasthānamadhyāsitamadhyuṣitam |
``adhiśīṅsthāsāṃ karma'' iti sakarmakatvam |
imaṃ rāmaṃ kuśalinaṃ santaṃ punarayodhyāpuraṃ prāptaṃ dṛṣṭvā puruṣapariṇāmaṃ puruṣavadātmanā pariṇataṃ sucaritaṃ sarvalokasukṛtasamudāyaṃ punarjātaṃ manye punaravatīrṇaṃ saṃbhāvayāmi |
anvādeśasyāvivakṣaṇādimamityatra enādeśābhāvaḥ||2||

 vyavaharati vadasi |
pitāputrabhāvastava rāmasya ca janyajanakabhāvaḥ saṃbandho mahimānaṃ rāmasya māhātmyaṃ gopayati saṃvṛṇo'ti ``gupa gopane'' |
atra bījopagūhanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ |
samājaṃ rakṣantīti sāmājikāḥ |
``rakṣati'' iti ṭhak |
rāmacandrapaṭṭābhiṣekāya tvaryatāmityarthaḥ |
didṛkṣayā darśanecchayā |
sāketanagaramayodhyānagaraṃ tatsaṃnikṛṣṭe samīpagate pratīkṣate pratipālayati |
mahendreṇeti |
prāptaḥ praṇayabharaḥ premātiśayo yasminkarmaṇi tadyathā bhavati tathā ardhāsanagatastadīyasiṃhāsanasamāṃśagataḥ |
āsanasyārdhamardhāsanam |
``ardhaṃ napuṃsakam'' iti samāsaḥ |
mahābhāgo'tiśayitabhāgyavān |
surapariṣadā devagoṣṭhyodgītavibhava uccairgītaiśvaryaḥ |
mahānandasya mokṣasadṛśānandasya |
``muktirmokṣo mahānandaḥ'' iti nighaṇṭuḥ |
udreko vṛddhistasya plavaḥpravāhastasya bhareṇātiśayena parādhīnahṛdayo mahārājaḥ sākṣātpratyakṣabhūto dīptyā dyumaṇiriva sthito daśarathaḥ nabhomadhye pratīkṣata iti saṃbandhaḥ |
atra kāryopakṣepaṇarūpaṃ grathanaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||3||

 arundhatīpatirvasiṣṭhaḥ prakṛtivargamamātyādisamūhaṃ tvarayitvā |
ratnamaye ratnapracure |
niveśayatyupaveśayati |
tattasmānmaṅgalāni vighnanivartakāni cikīrṣitasaṃpādakāni ca gānanāṭyādīni prastūyantāṃ prakriyantām |
gandharvā iti |
dhanairgambhīrairāgabandhaiḥ sunirbandhanarāgairmadhuraṃ yathā tathā |
yadvā ghanarāgā nāma nāṭyagaulavarāliprabhṛtayasteṣvabhyarhitatvātprathamo nāṭyarāgastena madhuraṃ yathā tathā |
kāntasvaraṃ manojñasvaraṃ ca yathā tathā gandharvā gāyantu |
gambhīrasya nipuṇonneyabhāvarasasūcakasyābhinayasya sāttvikāṅgikāhāryādikasya caturvidhasya rasabhāvavyañjakavyāpārasya prayoge'nuṣṭhāne caturā nipuṇārambhādayo'psaraso nṛtyantu |
vidyādharāstvaritā vegayuktāḥ santo dīptāni puṣpāṇi kirantu varṣantu |
divyāḥ svargasthā dundubhayo bheryo gambhīraninadāḥ santo ghanadhvanayaḥ santaḥ kolāhalaṃ kalakalaṃ kurvantu |
atra bījānuguṇakāryaprakhyāpanānnirṇayo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam |
yathoktam- ``bījānuguṇakāryasya prakhyāpanaṃ nirṇayaḥ'' iti||4||

 diṣṭyetyānande |
vasiṣṭhādibhirityādiśabdena viśvāmitravāmadevādīnāṃ saṃgrahaḥ |
divyamālyetyādeḥ spaṣṭo'rthaḥ |
atra vāñchitaprāptirānando nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam/ |
ācāra ityutsava iti |
śiṣṭācāro'nuroddhavyaḥ, utsavaścāpratiṣedhya iti hetoḥ |
paṭṭābhiṣekaḥ |
rājāntarasyeva paṭṭābhiṣekeṇa rājyasvāmitvaṃ na kriyata ityarthaḥ |
anena rāmeṇāvalambitānāṃ parigṛhītānām |
ahaṃ tāvaditi |
prathamamahaṃ śatrūnhatvā svargarājye sthāpitaḥ||5||

 sugrīva iti |
avāpitaḥ prāpitaḥ |
dvikarmakamidam |
anyasya rāmādanyasya manasāpyasādhyam |
vāśabdaḥ karaṇāntaraṃ vikalpayati |
atra mithojalpanarūpaṃ paribhāṣā nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||6||

 ākalpāntamiti |
anantābhiḥ koṭībhirguṇitairāvartitairarthairvittaiḥ sādhitairanekādhvarairyāgairdivyānindrādīnadivyānbhūmiṣṭhāneśeṣavibudhānsamastadevān |
devānbhūdevāṃścetyarthaḥ |
ānandaṃ gamayanprāpayan |
ātmīyānkṣatriyānaviratairnityairaiśvaryaviśrāṇanairdānairupalālayanputrāniva prīṇayannasmākamāśīrvādaparamparāmanubhavannākalpāntaṃ kalpāntaparyantamāstām |
tiṣṭhatvityarthaḥ |
āśiṣi leṭ||7||

 ālokāloketi |
āśāpālānāṃ dikpatīnāmindradīnāmanubhāvasya prabhāvasya grasanena tirodhānena gurutarairabhyadhikairātmakīrtiprakāśairavanīṃ bhuvamālokālokaśailāllokālokaśailaparyantamañcitāmaṅkitām |
``añcū viśeṣaṇe'' curādiḥ |
ādadhānaḥ kurvāṇaḥ sannāttagandhānabhibhūtānaśeṣānapi ripūnnṛpatīnājñāmātrāt |
na yuddhādityarthaḥ |
apāsyannirasyandharitryāmādhirājyasya sārvabhaumatvasya pratiṣṭhāṃ sthairyaṃ bhavānācandrārkamanubhavatvityāśiṣi loṭ |
atra labdhasthirīkaraṇaṃ kṛtirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||8||

 paramārthavijñānānāṃ yathārthaviṣayajñānavatām |
na visaṃvadatyarthaśūnyaṃ na bhavati |
brahmaṇā caturbhukhena |
maheśvaro rudraḥ |
suhṛdau sugrīvavibhīṣaṇau |
bhrātaraḥ śatrughnādayaḥ |
yathocitaṃ yathāyogyam |
darśanīyatāṃ cārūtām |
śatrughna iti |
cāmare bālavyajanadvayaṃ bibhṛto dhārayataḥ |
bibharterlaṭi taso tviādguṇābhāvaḥ |
sumitrāsuto lakṣmaṇo vyajanena tālavṛntena vījayati mandamārutamudbhāvayati |
yuvā cāsau rājā ca yuvarājaḥ |
``rājāhaḥsakhibhyaṣṭac'' iti ṭac |
svīyopacāre svanurūpopacāre sthitastiṣṭhati |
atra paryupāsanātmakaḥ prasādo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||9||

 ananyasulabhamanyeṣāṃ durlabham/ |
suprītā iti |
caritādbhutena caritarūpeṇāścaryeṇa |
rakṣo rāvaṇa eva mṛtyustena grastamujjīvitaṃ saprāṇīkṛtam |
punariti |
punarjātā lokāḥ pātālabhūsvargādibhuvanāni |
prajā janāḥ |
trayo vedā ṛgyajuḥsāmātmakāḥ |
punarjātā ityanuṣaṅgaḥ |
yajñādikā ityādiśabdena dānahomayoḥ pūrtasya ca saṃgrahaḥ |
atra prāptakāryānumodanamābhāṣaṇaṃ nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||10||

 paripālena iṣṭaprāpaṇe'dhikāraḥ kartṛbhāvaḥ |
etāvantamiti |
etāvati kāle nairantaryeṇa yuddhavīro yuddhaprayojakavīrarasastvayāvalambitaḥ svīkṛtaḥ |
kālamityatyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
dharmaḥ śāstraikāvadheyaśreyaḥsādhanam, dānaṃ dīnebhyo vittotsargaḥ, dayā paraduḥkhaduḥkhitvam, vīraḥ pūrvoktavīrarasaśca |
tvāmupasthitā bhūyiṣṭhaṃ prāptāḥ |
pūrvaṃ dharmādīnāṃ vīropasarjanatvam |
itaḥparaṃ tu vīrasya tadupasarjanatvamityarthaḥ |
pūrvārdhe dharmādyanuktisteṣāṃ vīropasarjanavyañjanārthā |
atra vīrasya vīreti caramoktistasya dharmādyupasarjanatvavyañjanārthā |
dharmādeḥ sadaiva rāmasvarūpānubandhitvāt |
saṃbudhyantaṃ kvacitpaṭhyate||11||

 lokarakṣaivādhvaro yāgastatra dīkṣitaḥ saṃkalpitaḥ |
sarva iti |
janāḥ parasparaṃ sumanasaḥ priyahitaparahṛdayāḥ sāndrapramodā ghanaprītayaśca santu |
tatra viśeṣamāha- saṃtuṣyantvityādinā |
sādhavaḥ paropakārarasikāḥ samīhitasyābhīṣṭasya praṇayanaiḥ karaṇaiḥ saṃbhāvitāḥ satkṛtāḥ santaḥ saṃtuṣyantu viśeṣato harṣaṃ bhajantu |
sajjanamanasāṃ satkāryacintā satkāryacikīrṣā sadyaḥ siddhiṃ uttarakṣaṇa eva viṣayasiddhiṃ sadopaitu |
amī dvijāstavaiva vijayaṃ sarvotkarṣaṃ sāyaṃprātaḥ saṃprārthayantām |
svakīyayogakṣemanirvāhasya tvadbharatvāt |
vyāpārāntarānapekṣayā tvadutkarṣaprārthanaiva sarvadā dvijānāmuciteti bhāvaḥ |
atreṣṭakāryadarśanātkamaḥ pūrvabhāvo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||12||

 trailokyeti |
trayo lokāstrailokyam |
cāturvaṇyāditvātsvārthe ṣyañ |
tasya kuśalaṃ sukhaṃ yatnena yaddhādiviṣayodyogena hetunā tvayā kartrā sādhitaṃ niṣpāditam |
sukhī bhavetyādeḥ spaṣṭo'rthaḥ||13||

 saujanyeti |
sujanasya bhāvaḥ saujanyaṃ tvaccaraṇaparicaraṇaikasvabhāvatvaṃ tatraikarasena rāgāntarāsaṃvalitarāgayuktena |
``raso rāge drave harṣe'' ityabhidhānam |
sāndro ghano nibhṛtaḥ sthiraḥ snehaḥ premā yasya sa tathoktastathāvidhena |
karmasvaihikāmuṣmikavyāpāreṣu sāvadhānamapramattaṃ mano yasya sa tathoktastena saumitriṇā lakṣmaṇena sākaṃ sahitaḥ sansatyena bhūtahitaparatathyavacanena, dharmeṇāhiṃsanādinā ca sarvātmanānuktena tattadgocaraprayatnena ca |
sarveṣāmānandajanakaṃ sādhucaritaṃ sadācāraṃ sādhayan |
``yasyadācarati śreṣṭhaḥ'' ityādyuktaprakāreṇa svayamanuṣṭhāyānyānanuṣṭhāpayansaurājyasya śobhanarājabhāvasyātiśayena parayā kāṣṭhayā sarvaiḥ sujanaiḥ ślāghyaḥ praśaṃsanīyo yaśasvī parveṣāmapi durlabhaguṇaprathāpūrṇo bhava |
āśiṣi loṭ||14||

 yauvarājye'dhikṛtastvayā niyojitaḥ |
sakaleti |
svārājye svargaparipālanakarmaṇi |
``svaravyayam'' ityamaraḥ |
tasya rājā svārājaḥ |
``rori'' iti rephalope ``ḍhralope-'' iti dīrghaḥ |
tataḥ karmaṇi brāhmaṇāderākṛtigaṇatvātṣyañ |
pratiṣṭhāpito'smi sthirīkṛto'smi |
niścitāḥ |
rāvaṇakartṛkopamardābhāvanirṇayena nāśasaṃśayanivṛttyā nityasattāniścayagocarā ityarthaḥ |
``niścintāḥ'' iti pāṭhe rāvaṇopadravacintārahitā ityarthaḥ |
hṛdyāniti |
kṣoṇīdevā brāhmaṇā adyaprabhṛti muditamanasaḥ santaḥ sarvataḥ sarvadeśasarvakāleṣu hṛdyānrāvaṇabhayābhāvena manaḥpriyānyajñabhāgānyāgīyahaviraṃśānbhuvi bhūmau naḥ kalpayiṣyantatyasmabhyaṃ dāsyanti |
hanteti harṣe |
tvamapi rājñāṃ yogyaiḥ kṣatriyārhairbahubhiramitai rājasūyai rājā somalatā sūyate kaṇḍyate'sminniti vyutpattyā rājasūyanāmakairyāgaviśeṣairaśvamukhyakatvādaśvamedhaśabdavācyaiḥ kratubhiśca |
rājasūyaśabdasyārdharcāditvena napuṃsakatvena ``adhvaryukraturanapuṃkam'' iti samāhāraniyamapravṛtteritaretarayogadvandvaḥ |
yajñairiṣṭvāsmānsphāraṃ bahulamānandaṃ samadhigamaya saṃprāpaya||15||

 tvayeti |
putreṇeti śeṣaḥ||16||

 ekatastāvaditi |
tvīyāraṇyavāso mūlaṃ heturyasya sa tathoktaḥ |
etāvantaṃ kālaṃ caturdaśa varṣāṇi |
atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā |
idānīṃ paṭṭābhiṣekādanantaram |
taveti |
tavāraṇye vāse vanavāsakāle mahāśokena tatprayuktaniravadhikaduḥkhena hetunā vikale śithilite ma hṛdi tridaśapadalābho'pi sarvābhyarthanīyasvargasthānasthitirapi kiṃcitsvārasyaṃ manāgapi sukharūpatāṃ nālabhata nābhajat |
tvayīha pratyāvṛtte vanavāsādayodhyāṃ punarāgate sati |
punaḥśabdastuśabdārthakaḥ |
mahānandabharite mama manasi niḥśreyasamapi sarvātiśayitamokṣasukhamapi bhūmānaṃ vaipulyaṃ na dhatte na bhajati |
tvadāgamanasukhānmokṣasukhamalpamiti bhāvaḥ |
atra duḥkhapraśamanātmakaḥ samayo nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||17||

 ita iti |
iṣṭāpūrtairyāgānuṣṭhānataṭākanirmāṇādibhiḥ śāśvataṃ padaṃ nityaṃ sthānam |
śreyasaḥ śubhasya saṃtānasyāpatyasya ca siddhiṃ niṣpattim |
``saṃtānāḥsyuravicchittivaṃśāpatyasuradrumāḥ'' iti ratnamālā |
āśāsmaha icchāmaḥ |
``āṅaḥ śāsu icchāyām'' |
laṅketyādi |
laṅkāyāṃ yuddhasahāyānityarthaḥ |
saṃbhāvya pūjayitvā |
ādityairiti |
ādityasyāpatyaiḥ |
``dityadityāditya-'' ityādinā ṇyapratyayaḥ |
abhirūpaṃmanojñaṃ yathā bhavati tathā dhārito'nuṣṭhito mahyā bhūmisthaprāṇināṃ bhāra iṣṭaprāpaṇāniṣṭanivāraṇavyāpāro yaiste tathoktāstaiḥ |
``prāptarūpasurūpābhirūpā budhamanojñayoḥ'' ityamaraḥ |
udāraiḥ parebhyaḥ prāṇaparyantadāyibhirāvirbhūtaiḥ prakāśamāṇairguṇaiḥ śauryādibhiranubhāvaiḥ prabhāvaiśca gurubhirmahadbhirnṛpaiḥ kartṛbhirdharmeṇa śāstroktadharmayuktatayā |
prakṛtyāditvāttṛtīyā |
prajā guptāḥ pālitāḥ |
āptairyathādraṣṭṛtve sati yathārthavaktṛbhirātmanaḥ svasya |
ayamātmaśabdastatparo rāmaparo vā |
hitasya kālāntare'pi sukhaparyavasāyino'rthasyāvadhāraṇena prāgeva nyāyadṛṣṭyā niścayena prasiddheḥ |
``tena vittaḥ-'' iti caṇappratyayaḥ |
ārabdhanirvāhakaiḥ prārabdhakāryaniṣpādakaiḥ puruṣairādiṣṭenopadiṣṭenājñaptena vā pathā mārgeṇa ā cakravālācalaṃ deśataḥ kālataśca cakravālaparvatamavadhīkṛtya mahīmanupālaya rakṣa||18||

 vasiṣṭheti |
na mantrayitavyaṃ nālocayitavyam/ |
tayoriti |
maheśvaraḥ |
āheti śeṣaḥ |
vasiṣṭheti |
arundhatī jāyā yasya sa tathoktastasya saṃbuddhiḥ |
``jāyāyā niṅ'' iti niṅ |
yuktau saṃdaddhau |
atandritāvālasyarahitau |
syātaṃ bhavetam |
astaḥ sārvadhātukaliṅ||19||

 tau vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrau |
āhaturiti śeṣaḥ |
anugṛhītau kṛtaprasādau svo bhavāvaḥ |
asterlaṭyuttamapuruṣadvivacanam/ |
sarve bhavanto rāmādayaḥ sarve yūyam |
ihaivāyodhyāyāmeva |
nityotsavā avicchinnānandajanakavyāpārāḥ |
vayaṃ cāhaṃ caturmaukho mahendro daśarathaśca svaṃ svaṃ sthānaṃ kailāsaṃ satyalokaṃ svargaṃ ca/ |
rāmādaya iti |
ādiśabdena vasiṣṭhaviśvāmitrādīnāṃ saṃgrahaḥ |
yathocitaṃ guruśiybhāvādyanurūpaṃ yathā tathā |
kiṃ te bhūyaḥ priyamupaharāmi |
punaḥ kiṃ tava priyaṃ karavāṇītyarthaḥ/ |
itaḥparaṃ pa[va]kṣyamāṇādanyat |
hantavyā iti |
jagatāmātaṅkahetavo bhayaṃkarā ataeva hantavyā āvaśyakahananakarmībhūtā rākṣasā hatāḥ |
kiṃ ca śāśvatī cirakālasthāyinī prajāpālanadīkṣā ca yathā praviṣṭeti yat |
itaḥparaṃ priyamastīti saṃbandhaḥ |
atra kāryārthopasaṃhṛtirūpa upasaṃhāro nāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||20||

 bharatasya nāṭyācāryasya vākyaṃ vacanam |
sāhityeti |
yatra mahāvīracaritākhye saṃdarbhe sāhityasya samucitapadaracanāyā udayenāvirbhāvena saṃbhṛtaḥ saṃpāditaḥ, sahṛdayaiḥ kāvyakalākauśalayuktānasūyahṛdayayuktaiḥ puruṣaiḥ |
samantataḥ saṃbhāvanīyaḥ pūjanīya udaya āvirbhāvo yasya sa tathoktaḥ, sārasvataḥ sarasvatīsaṃbandhī sāra utkṛṣṭāṃśaḥ saṃbhūya militvā vijayate sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate |
tathāvidhaḥ samyagbhāvitena niṣpāditena saṃvidhānenetivṛttasaṃghaṭanena taralo vilasitaḥ |
yadvā saṃvidhānameva taralo madhyasthamaṇiryasminsa tathoktaḥ |
rūpakāṇāṃ mitho bhedakeṣu rasavastunāyakeṣu vastuśabditasya prakhyātotpādyamiśrabhedabhinnasyetivṛttāṃśasya madhye miśrasya cārutātiśayāvahatvāttaralatvarūpaṇam |
sudhāvadamṛtavatsundaro ramaṇīyaḥ |
``bhūtasaṃjīvanaṃ rāmavṛttāntaḥ'' ityuktarītyā sudhāvanmṛtasaṃjīvano'yaṃ saṃdarbho mahāvīracaritākhyo girāṃ gumpho viduṣāṃ vedanaśīlānāṃ paṇḍitānāmanandahṛdayānandāyānalpamanaḥsukhāya saṃdāyatāṃ samyakśodhito bhavatu |
``daip śodhane'' ityasmātkarmakartari lakāraḥ |
aghutvādītvaṃ na |
``saṃtāyatām'' iti ca pāṭhaḥ |
avicchedo bhavatu |
pūrvavallakāraḥ |
``tanoteryaki'' ityātvam |
atra śubhaśaṃsanarūpapraśastirnāma saṃdhyaṅgamuktam||21||

 evaṃ sāṅgaṃ nāṭakaṃ vṛttam||

śuddhaguṇalabdharūpā satpātrasnehasaṃbhṛtālokā/

dīpakalikeva sudṛśāṃ bhāvapradyotinī ruciṃ diśatu||
jitāmitro tittrapriyakṛdanurajyadbhiranujaiḥṣṭasamaṃ sītādevyā saha mudamasīmāmanubhavan/

kṛpātuṅgairasmānanukalamapāṅgairabhimṛśansudhoktipremāṇaṃ kalayatu maduktau raghuvaraḥ||

yo mahīsāravāstavyo vādhūlo vīrarāghavaḥ/

sa mahāvīracarite saptamāṅkaṃ vyavīvarat||

ityaṇṇāppaṅgāryanāmadheyena vādhūlavīrarāghaveṇa viracitāyāṃ bhāvaprapyotinīsamākhyāyāṃ mahāvīracaritavyākhyāyāṃ saptamo'ṅkaḥ||

samāptamidaṃ nāṭakam||
